### [1] [MiroBench: Benchmarking Realism in Agentic Simulation of Real-world Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14715)
*Yaoning Yu,Ye Yu,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MiroBench基准，用于评估LLM智能体模拟Reddit讨论的真实性。通过对4,292个真实Reddit线程的统计分析，发现当前LLM模拟器生成的讨论在多个维度上与现实讨论存在分布差异，轻量级提示优化仅带来有限改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被广泛用于模拟现实世界交互，但目前尚不清楚模拟行为是否保留了真实人类行为的内容模式和交互动态。现有评估方法碎片化，难以比较不同系统或衡量进展，因此需要一个针对真实世界社交模拟的系统性评估基准。

Method: 以Reddit讨论作为评估真实世界社交模拟的具体起点，构建包含4,292个真实Reddit线程的MiroBench基准。该基准使用统计测试方法，从四个主要维度比较生成讨论与真实讨论：重复性与语义一致性、叙述内容、毒性与攻击性、结构复杂性。实验覆盖五个领域和五个模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前LLM模拟器生成的讨论在分布上与真实Reddit线程存在不匹配。采用轻量级基于提示的改进程序只能提供有限的性能提升，说明现有方法在模拟真实社交交互方面仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: MiroBench为测量、诊断和改进基于LLM的社交模拟真实性提供了具体的基准。该研究揭示了当前LLM模拟器在复现真实社交行为模式和交互动态方面的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used to simulate real world interactions, but it remains unclear whether simulated behaviors preserve the content patterns and interaction dynamics of real human behaviors. Existing evaluations remain fragmented, which makes it difficult to compare systems or measure progress. In this paper, we focus on Reddit discussions as a concrete first step toward evaluating real-world social simulation. Reddit threads provide public, topic-grounded, multi-party interactions where people share experiences, debate, seek advice, express emotion, and collectively respond to products, events, and social issues. These discussions offer an observable window into broader social behavior, making them a useful setting for testing whether LLM agents can reproduce not only fluent text, but also the distributional patterns and interaction dynamics of real online communities. We introduce MiroBench, a benchmark for Reddit discussion simulation built from 4,292 real Reddit threads. MiroBench uses statistical tests to compare generated and real discussions across four major aspects: repetition and semantic uniformity, narrative content, toxicity and aggression, and structural complexity. Experiments across five domains and five models show that current simulators remain distributionally mismatched with real Reddit threads, while a lightweight prompt-based improvement procedure provides only limited gains. MiroBench offers a concrete benchmark for measuring, diagnosing, and improving realism in LLM-based social simulation.

</details>


### [2] [Selective Control under Noisy Perception: Governance Failures Hidden by Aggregate Metrics in Modular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14819)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 内容审核系统在标准准确率指标表现良好的情况下，仍可能因错误集中于连接不同社区的“桥接用户”而造成实际伤害。基于主体建模发现：噪声变化对整体有用性无显著影响（p=0.96），但伤害集中；提出单独定价两类错误的治理损失L_gov，其在假阳性偏重噪声下翻倍；用户度数可作为桥接性的近完美代理（r=0.96）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准聚合准确率指标掩盖内容审核系统对少数关键用户（桥接用户）的差异化伤害；需揭示并量化此类隐形危害，以支持更公平的治理。

Method: 在具有社区结构的网络上构建N=240的学习型智能体仿真，智能体发布无害、有益或危险内容；监管者使用含噪声的分类器进行惩罚或移除；比较不同噪声分布下的整体有用性，并量化对桥接用户的错误压制与漏放，引入治理损失L_gov并考察其与节点度数的关系。

Result: 噪声类型变化对系统整体有用性影响不显著（单因素ANOVA，p=0.96），聚合指标看似正常；但伤害高度集中于桥接用户；以假阳性为主的噪声下，L_gov翻倍以上；用户度数与定义桥接的中介中心性高度相关（r=0.96），可作为低成本审计指标。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率不足以揭示内容审核系统中的分配性伤害；应关注桥接用户受影响的两类错误，采用分误定价的治理损失指标；审计节点度数即可高效识别高风险用户，指导更公平的平台治理与监管。

Abstract: A content-moderation system can score well on every standard accuracy metric and still cause real harm, if its mistakes fall on the few users who connect otherwise separate communities. We show this in an agent-based model where N=240 learning agents on a community-structured network each post harmless, productive, or dangerous content, and a regulator removes or penalizes whatever a noisy classifier flags. Overall usefulness barely moves as the noise changes (one-way ANOVA, p=0.96): by aggregate measures, nothing looks wrong. The damage instead concentrates on these bridge users, whose useful posts are wrongly suppressed and whose dangerous posts are wrongly spared. A governance loss (L_gov) that prices these two mistakes separately from the cost of enforcement more than doubles under false-positive-heavy noise. Aggregate accuracy hides who is harmed, and the cheap quantity to audit is how many connections a user has (degree), a near-perfect proxy for the betweenness that defines a bridge (r=0.96).

</details>


### [3] [Hierarchical Generative Agents for Simulating Sequential Human Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14989)
*Maria G. Mendoza,Lucas Waldburger,Jin Lee,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型(LLM)和认知层次结构的动态灾害疏散仿真框架，通过模拟具有认知能力和个性化特征的智能体来预测人类在灾害中的疏散决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疏散计算模型假设人类行为是理性和同质的，导致预测结果不切实际且过于乐观；同时，在灾害场景中收集人类数据极具挑战性，亟需能够准确建模复杂认知、情感和社会过程的疏散模型。

Method: 构建了一个序贯决策疏散仿真框架：(1)建立基于网格的动态城市环境模型，模拟火灾等灾害演变；(2)设计三层认知结构的个性化智能体——高层疏散目标、中层路线推理、底层导航；(3)将大语言模型(LLM)与认知模块耦合，利用实证疏散数据进行校准，实现刺激驱动的动态决策建模。

Result: 开发了一个能够捕捉复杂人类疏散行为的仿真框架，该框架通过个性化和认知驱动的LLM智能体，能够更真实地模拟人类在灾害场景中的序贯决策过程。

Conclusion: 该研究为灾害疏散规划和资源配置提供了更有效、更真实的建模工具，克服了传统理性假设模型的局限性，为理解和预测人类疏散行为开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Complex cognitive, emotional, and social processes shape human evacuations during natural disasters. Accurate modeling and understanding of human behavior in disasters or emergencies can greatly impact the evacuation process by informing more effective planning and resource allocation. However, collecting human data in these situations is very difficult, and existing computational evacuation models assume rational, homogeneous behavior, leading to unrealistic, overly optimistic predictions. To address this gap, we present a simulation framework of sequential human decision-making during an evacuation scenario, introducing cognitively grounded, persona-driven agents. Our framework models evacuation behavior in a grid-based urban environment that evolves over time, capturing fire and other hazards. Human agents are modeled as personas that make sequential decisions in response to environmental stimuli with cognition structured in three levels: high-level evacuation goals, mid-level route reasoning, and low-level navigation. Decision-making is driven by large language models (LLMs) coupled with a cognitive module and calibrated with empirical human evacuation data. We propose a dynamic, stimulus-driven disaster simulation framework that models human evacuation decision-making using persona-conditioned LLM agents and a cognitive hierarchy.

</details>


### [4] [Resilient Consensus in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15024)
*Sribalaji C. Anand,George J. Pappas*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体在多智能体系统中难以达成经典理论保证的共识，但应用经典弹性共识过滤器可改善协议效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典弹性共识理论（针对确定性智能体开发）是否适用于可能表现出对抗行为的大语言模型（LLM）智能体，探索LLM在多智能体系统中的协调与决策安全问题。

Method: 将LLM协议问题建模为拜占庭共识博弈，在完全图和一般通信图上运行对照实验，测试不同温度和预测范围下的表现，并使用经典弹性共识过滤器包装智能体进行对比。

Result: 提示的LLM智能体未能达成理论上可实现的协议：即使在经典理论保证存在收敛算法的情况下，共识仍可能失败，且该失败在不同温度和预测范围下持续存在；同时，用经典弹性共识过滤器包装智能体可改善协议效果，其益处取决于底层拓扑结构本身提供的鲁棒性程度。

Conclusion: 经典弹性共识理论是评估智能体AI安全性的有效视角，可为LLM多智能体系统的协调机制提供理论指导和改进方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems where they must coordinate and agree on shared decisions. We ask whether classical resilient consensus theory, developed for deterministic agents, transfers to LLM agents that may behave adversarially. Framing LLM agreement as a Byzantine consensus game, we run controlled experiments on complete and general communication graphs. We find that prompted LLM agents fail to reach agreement that is achievable in principle: consensus can fail even in settings where classical theory guarantees that a convergent algorithm exists, and this failure persists across temperatures and horizons. At the same time, wrapping the agents with classical resilient consensus filters improves agreement. The benefit of filtering depends on how much robustness the underlying topology already provides. Our results suggest that classical resilient consensus theory is a useful lens for the safety of agentic AI.

</details>


### [5] [DeepRoot: A KG-Coordinated Multi-Agent System for Therapeutic Reasoning over Historical Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15931)
*Zijian Carl Ma,Sean J. Wang,Sijbren Kramer,Li Erran Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepRoot系统，这是一个多智能体大语言模型系统，能够将历史医学文献转化为可验证的药物发现线索，在《神农本草经》数据集上实现了47.6%的召回率，显著优于基线方法，同时大幅降低了幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史医学档案和传统医学对药物发现具有巨大潜力，但前本体论的散文文本和非标准分类法阻碍了数据的标准化和医学现代化。现有的大语言模型智能体系统（无论是工具调用、检索增强还是深度研究智能体）都无法大规模将此类文本转化为可验证的药物发现线索。

Method: 提出DeepRoot多智能体LLM系统，该系统联合构建和利用经过验证的知识图谱。该方法将通常被混淆的"基础"和"推理"分离为可组合的两个维度，用于治疗推理。系统通过知识图谱构建和大语言模型推理的结合，实现了对历史医学知识的系统性挖掘。

Result: 在《神农本草经》数据集上，DeepRoot在R@20指标下恢复了21个留出化合物-疾病治疗对中的10个（47.6%），显著优于原始语料库LLM（4.8%）和随机基线（约2.4%）。在LLM-as-judge审计中，DeepRoot在推理质量上优于基线LLM和具有直接工具调用访问权限的LLM。工具使用型LLM在87%的声明上产生幻觉证据，而DeepRoot仅为7-10%。仅使用图谱推理的幻觉率为0%但推理连贯性排名最低；DeepRoot的KG+LLM方法是唯一在两个维度上都获胜的条件。

Conclusion: DeepRoot成功弥合了历史医学文献与现代生物医学流程之间的鸿沟，通过分离和组合基础与推理能力，实现了对历史医学知识的系统性挖掘和重新利用，为药物发现和药物重定位提供了一条可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Historical medical archives and traditional medicines hold immense potential for drug discovery and remain a primary source for current drug development. However, pre-ontological prose and idiosyncratic taxonomies prevent the standardization and medical modernization of the data for use in current biomedical pipelines. Furthermore, no existing LLM agent system, whether tool-calling, retrieval-augmented, or agentic deep-research, can convert such text into verifiable drug-discovery leads at scale. We close this gap with DeepRoot, a multi-agent LLM system that jointly builds and utilizes a verified knowledge graph, showing that grounding and reasoning -- often conflated -- are separable axes the system can compose for therapeutic reasoning. Applied to the Shen Nong Ben Cao Jing, DeepRoot recovers $10$ of $21$ held-out compound-disease treatment pairs at R@$20$ ($47.6\%$ vs $4.8\%$ for a raw corpus LLM and $\sim\!2.4\%$ random) and dominates an LLM-as-judge audit for reasoning quality over baseline LLMs and LLMs with direct tool-call access to the same APIs DeepRoot itself queries. Tool-using LLMs hallucinate evidence on $87\%$ of claims, versus 7-10% for DeepRoot. Graph-only inference hallucinates $0\%$ but ranks lowest on reasoning coherence; DeepRoot KG+LLM is the only condition to win on both axes, pointing toward a route for systematic mining and repurposing of historical medical knowledge.

</details>


### [6] [Misinformation Propagation in Benign Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16710)
*Jonas Becker,Jan Philip Wahle,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中错误信息传播的风险，发现多智能体辩论比单智能体更能抵抗错误信息的影响，鲁棒性取决于群体组成和决策协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在高风险场景（医疗诊断、法律分析等）的部署日益增多，但单个智能体基于错误上下文的推理可能导致错误在智能体交互中传播，威胁系统可靠性，因此需要深入研究错误信息对多智能体系统的影响。

Method: 通过向良性单智能体和多智能体系统注入基于意图的错误信息，在推理、知识和对齐任务上进行实验，比较单智能体与多智能体辩论的表现，分析群体组成和决策协议（共识vs投票）对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 错误信息会降低单智能体性能并在多智能体辩论中持续存在，智能体常保留由受误导同伴引入的错误答案；但多智能体辩论能减少性能下降，尤其当多数智能体未暴露于错误信息时；共识机制比投票更能抵抗同伴压力，多数派常能引导受误导智能体回归正确答案。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统对错误信息的鲁棒性不仅取决于底层模型，还依赖于智能体间的信息交换方式和决策聚合机制，群体组成和决策协议是影响系统可靠性的关键因素。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems, in which multiple large language model agents solve problems through turn-based interaction, are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as medical diagnosis, legal analysis, and forensic decision-making. Their reliability can be at risk when single agents reason from incorrect or misleading context, e.g., from tool calls, since errors may propagate through agent interactions. This work studies this risk by injecting intent-based misinformation into benign single-agent and multi-agent systems across reasoning, knowledge, and alignment tasks. We find that misinformation can degrade single-agent performance and persists across multi-agent debate, with agents often retaining answers introduced by misinformed peers. Nevertheless, multi-agent debate reduces the resulting performance degradation compared to single-agent prompting, especially when most agents are not exposed to misinformation. Robustness depends on group composition and decision protocol. Consensus can be more stable than voting under peer pressure, while majorities can often steer misinformed agents back toward correct answers. Our results show that misinformation robustness in multi-agent systems depends on the underlying model and also on how agents exchange information and aggregate decisions.

</details>


### [7] [Human-on-the-Bridge: Scalable Evaluation for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16871)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Human-on-the-Bridge (HOB)框架，通过上游专家知识策展与下游LLM执行相结合，实现对AI Agent行为的可扩展评估，发现传统方法易遗漏的多种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent作为行为系统需要跨轮次推理、工具调用和上下文维护，现有评估方法信号碎片化：基准测试覆盖固定能力、人工审查难以扩展、LLM评估依赖设计、红队测试缺乏系统性、轨迹审计需要显式规则。缺乏可扩展且能保留专家判断质量的评估范式。

Method: HOB范式分两阶段：上游专家策展可复用评估知识（领域上下文、红队陷阱、评审人角色、评分指南、审计规则、后备策略）；下游ProofAgent Harness执行多轮对抗评估、轨迹捕获、多评审人评分和证据关联报告。支持对称和非对称设置（Harness LLM与被测Agent能力不对等）。

Result: 在金融、医疗、代码生成领域的23,500个Agent轮次评估中，小规模Harness LLM能有效挑战基于前沿LLM的Agent；发现静态基准和单一评估遗漏的失败模式：幻觉工具调用、缺失强制工具调用、策略漂移、操纵路径、安全但无解决能力的拒绝。

Conclusion: HOB实现了专家判断的编码前置与跨评估复用，证明了在评估质量不降低的前提下可大幅提升可扩展性，为AI Agent评估提供了将人类专业知识规模化应用的新范式。

Abstract: AI agents must be evaluated as behavioral systems, not as isolated response generators. They reason across turns, call tools, preserve context, follow policies, and act under uncertainty. Existing methods provide useful but fragmented signals: benchmarks measure fixed capabilities, Human-in-the-Loop review preserves expert judgment but does not scale easily, LLM-as-judge methods depend on evaluator design, red teaming is often episodic, and trace auditing requires explicit evidence rules. This paper introduces Human-on-the-Bridge (HOB), a scalable evaluation paradigm for agentic AI. HOB places human expertise upstream, where experts curate reusable evaluation intelligence before testing begins, including domain context, Red-Team Traps, Juror Personas, scoring guidelines, audit rules, and fallback policies. ProofAgent Harness then executes this curated intelligence repeatedly through multi-turn adversarial evaluations, trace capture, multi-juror scoring, and evidence-linked reporting. We evaluate HOB through symmetric and cost-efficient asymmetric settings across frontier LLM-based agents and Harness LLM tiers. The study covers 23,500 agent turns and produces evidence-linked findings across finance, healthcare, and code generation. The results show that HOB can amplify evaluation quality without requiring equally large evaluator models, allowing smaller Harness LLMs to challenge agents built on frontier LLM backbones. The evaluation surfaces failures often missed by static benchmarks and single-evaluator scoring, including phantom tool-call claims, missing mandatory tool calls, policy drift, manipulation paths, and safe but non-resolving refusals. These findings support HOB as a paradigm for scaling human-curated evaluation intelligence, where expert judgment is encoded upfront and reused across repeated agent evaluations rather than applied manually inside every run.

</details>

### [8] [Poster: EdgeCitadel -- Hybrid NATS-MQTT Orchestration for Edge Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14710)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EdgeCitadel，一个基于NATS 2.10与内置MQTT适配器的边缘多智能体编排平台，旨在解决边缘环境中AI智能体协调栈仍依赖云式传输或中继的问题，实现了跨ARM64、x64和Android客户端的跨设备测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端AI智能体（如家庭服务器、物联网网关、笔记本、手机等）日益普及，但其协调栈仍假设云式传输或依赖中央中继，无法有效适配异构、多设备的边缘环境，亟需一套原生的边缘多智能体编排机制。

Method: 设计以单一NATS 2.10服务器为核心的架构，内置MQTT适配器；结合MQTT连接性适配异构智能体、JetStream持久化与回放支持后端服务、在共享主题命名空间上实现直接对等委托，并部署被动聚合器以可视化并存储流量，但不参与消息传递路径。

Result: 成功构建了跨ARM64、x64和Android客户端的工作跨设备测试平台，验证了从MQTT中继原型向当前混合架构的迁移可行性，实现了异构边缘智能体的高效协调与流量可视化。

Conclusion: EdgeCitadel证明了基于NATS与MQTT混合架构能够在边缘环境中有效编排多智能体，突破了传统云式传输与中央中继的限制，为跨设备边缘AI智能体协同提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge-resident AI agents increasingly span home servers, IoT hubs, laptops, and phones, yet their coordination stacks still assume cloud-style transports or a central relay. We present EdgeCitadel, an edge multi-agent orchestration platform built around a single NATS 2.10 server with the built-in MQTT adapter. The design combines MQTT connectivity for heterogeneous agents, JetStream-backed persistence and replay for backend services, direct peer delegation over a shared subject namespace, and a passive aggregator that visualizes and stores traffic without sitting on the delivery path. Our poster highlights the migration from MQTT relay prototypes (common in IoT communication) to the current hybrid architecture and demonstrates a working cross-device testbed spanning ARM64, x64, and Android clients.

</details>


### [9] [SWARM-LLM: Collaborative Inference for Edge-based Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14711)
*Mostafa Dahshan,Quazi Mamun,Tanmoy Debnath*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SWARM-LLM是一个路由与协作层，协调边缘设备上的小型语言模型(SLMs)群与云端基础模型(FM)，通过不确定性估计和安全信号智能选择本地回答、群协作或调用云端模型，在保证性能的同时将云端查询限制在约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)性能强大但依赖中心化云基础设施，带来带宽、延迟和隐私成本；小型语言模型(SLMs)可在边缘设备运行但能力和鲁棒性有限。需要一种方案平衡性能与隐私、成本、延迟之间的权衡。

Method: 提出SWARM-LLM路由与协作层，对每个查询基于轻量级不确定性估计和安全信号做出决策：(1)本地回答、(2)与对等SLMs协作、或(3)调用云端基础模型。在商用硬件上部署三个异构SLMs，通过API访问70B参数云端FM，构建工作原型。

Result: 在包含简单、困难和安全性导向查询的受控工作负载上评估，SWARM-LLM在困难问题上相比纯边缘部署显著提升性能，同时将云端使用限制在约四分之一的查询量，实现了准确性、延迟和成本之间的实用平衡。

Conclusion: SWARM-LLM为隐私敏感的边缘部署提供了一种实用方案，通过智能路由在保持性能的同时有效控制云端使用，为边缘-云端协同架构提供了可行路径。代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are typically hosted on centralised cloud infrastructure, incurring significant bandwidth, latency, and privacy costs. In contrast, small language models (SLMs) can run on edge devices but have limited capability and robustness. This paper introduces SWARM-LLM, a routing and collaboration layer that coordinates a small swarm of edge-hosted SLMs with an optional cloud foundation model (FM). SWARM-LLM decides, for each query, whether to answer locally, collaborate with peer SLMs, or "summon" a cloud FM, using lightweight uncertainty estimates and safety signals. We implement a working prototype on commodity hardware with three heterogeneous SLMs and a 70B-parameter cloud FM accessed via API, and evaluate it on a controlled study workload of easy, hard, and safety-oriented queries. Our results show that SWARM-LLM substantially improves performance on hard questions compared to an edge-only deployment, while limiting cloud usage to roughly one quarter of queries, illustrating a practical trade-off between accuracy, latency, and cost for privacy-conscious edge deployments. The implementation code is available at the GitHub repository https://github.com/mdahshan/swarm_llm.

</details>

Conclusion: EdgeStream框架通过边缘计算有效解决了物联网环境中实时流数据分析的低延迟和带宽效率问题，证明了边缘-云协同架构的优越性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has led to large-scale continuous data streams that require realtime processing. Traditional cloud-centric architectures fail to meet low-latency and bandwidth efficiency requirements due to network delays and high data transmission overhead. This paper proposes EdgeStream, a lightweight edge-based framework for real-time streaming analytics in IoT environments. The system integrates edge nodes for local processing with a cloud backend for coordination and storage, using MQTT-based communication and distributed processing with anomaly detection. Analytical models for latency, throughput, and bandwidth are developed to evaluate performance. Experimental results, compared to cloudbased systems, show up to 92.8

</details>


### [11] [Evaluating Gemma4 Models as AI Teaching Assistants for Introductory Parallel Programming: A DataRaceBench Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14881)
*Sabbir Hussain Meraj,Riham Chowdhury,Shimul Debnath,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了两个Gemma4开源模型在调试OpenMP数据竞争中的表现，发现Gemma4-31B在解释和修复数据竞争方面接近完美表现，但额外的上下文信息并未如预期提升修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据竞争调试对并行编程学习者而言极具挑战性，源于并发执行的非确定性和共享内存语义的复杂性。虽然大型语言模型有潜力作为AI教学助手，但低成本开源模型在并行调试方面的能力尚不明确，需要对此进行系统性评估。

Method: 使用DataRaceBench基准测试套件中的OpenMP程序，评估两个Gemma4开源模型（Gemma4-E4B和Gemma4-31B）在识别、解释和修复数据竞争方面的能力。同时研究上下文提示（包括ThreadSanitizer报告和模型生成的解释）是否能改善修复质量。

Result: Gemma4-E4B正确解释了104个竞争条件程序中的82个，成功修复73个；Gemma4-31B实现了100个正确解释和98个成功修复。令人意外的是，额外上下文并未持续改善修复效果，有时反而降低了性能表现。

Conclusion: 开源大语言模型能够为学生自主调试提供有价值的支持，较大模型能实现对基准测试套件的近完全覆盖。这表明开源模型具有作为并行编程教学辅助工具的潜力。

Abstract: Debugging data races is a major challenge for students learning parallel programming due to the non-deterministic nature of concurrent execution and the complexity of shared-memory semantics. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that they could serve as AI teaching assistants, but the capabilities of lower-cost open-weight models for parallel debugging remain unclear. In this paper, we evaluate two Gemma4 open-weight models, Gemma4-E4B and Gemma4-31B, on their ability to identify, explain, and repair data races in OpenMP programs from the DataRaceBench benchmark suite. We also investigate whether contextual hints, including ThreadSanitizer (TSan) reports and model-generated explanations, improve repair quality. Our results show that Gemma4-E4B correctly explained 82 of 104 race-condition programs and successfully repaired 73, while Gemma4-31B achieved 100 correct explanations and 98 successful repairs. Surprisingly, additional context did not consistently improve repair effectiveness and sometimes reduced performance. These findings suggest that open-weight LLMs can provide valuable support for student self-debugging, with larger models offering near-complete coverage of the benchmark suite.

</details>


### [12] [NEURON-Fabric: CXL-Side Low-Bit Gradient Aggregation for Distributed Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15045)
*Ziqiang Wang,Changcheng Huang,Chung-Horng Lung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了NEURON-Fabric，一种CXL内存控制器架构，通过在CXL内存侧进行低比特梯度聚合来优化大模型分布式训练。在保持分类器头使用FP32精度的同时，将梯度通信流量降低至FP32基线的3.6-5.4%，同时保持相近的模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型分布式训练中，尤其是大语言模型工作负载，梯度All-Reduce操作对内存和通信路径造成越来越大的压力。现有方法缺乏灵活的精度控制机制，无法在保证训练精度的同时有效降低通信开销。

Method: 提出NEURON-Fabric CXL侧控制器架构，实现两种低比特梯度聚合方法：(1)梯度二值(G-Binary)符号计数聚合；(2)梯度三值(G-Ternary)门控聚合。提供控制接口以选择低比特或FP32路径，支持层级感知的精度分配策略。通过CXL内存旁路设计，在梯度缓存行通过时完成聚合操作。

Result: 周期级时序实验显示，5周期的低比特聚合数据通路在最坏情况下仅增加1.67%的运行时开销。功能测试确认了字节精确的身份读回和正确的聚合操作。在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18和SST-2/DistilBERT上，低比特聚合精度接近FP32。层级感知策略发现分类器头对低比特敏感，将其保持在FP32精度同时对backbone应用低比特聚合，可将梯度流量降至FP32基线的3.6-5.4%。硬件综合结果表明512位聚合数据通路的面积开销较小。

Conclusion: CXL内存控制器可以有效集成低比特梯度聚合功能，在保持可接受精度损失的前提下显著降低梯度通信开销。层级感知的精度分配策略能够识别敏感层并针对性优化，为大模型分布式训练提供了新的硬件-软件协同优化方向。

Abstract: In large-model distributed training, especially large language model workloads, gradient All-Reduce increasingly stresses the memory and communication path. This paper asks whether a Compute Express Link (CXL) memory controller can aggregate low-bit gradient signals as gradient cache lines pass through it, while preserving a 32-bit floating-point (FP32) path for workloads, layers, or phases that should not use low-bit approximation. We present NEURON-Fabric, a CXL-side controller architecture that performs packed gradient-binary (G-Binary) sign-count aggregation and gradient-ternary (G-Ternary) gated aggregation near CXL memory, with a control interface for selecting low-bit or FP32 paths. Cycle-level timing experiments show that the measured five-cycle low-bit aggregation datapath adds at most 1.67 percent exposed runtime overhead in the full last-level-cache miss regime; under bandwidth pressure, the same compute stage is hidden by CXL service time. Functional tests confirm byte-exact identity read-back, G-Binary sign-count aggregation, and G-Ternary gating. Training checks quantify the communication and accuracy tradeoff: low-bit aggregation remains close to FP32 on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 and SST-2/DistilBERT, while full-path low-bit aggregation fails on CIFAR-100/ResNet-18. Layer-aware admission identifies the classifier head as sensitive; keeping the head on FP32 while applying low-bit aggregation to the backbone recovers most accuracy and reduces gradient traffic to 3.6-5.4 percent of the FP32 baseline. Hardware synthesis and FPGA place-and-route estimates suggest that the 512-bit aggregation datapath is small enough to be treated as a near-memory datapath extension rather than a separate accelerator-scale block.

</details>


### [13] [StorRep: Storage Research Experiment Patterns on Chameleon Cloud and Trovi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16252)
*Ray A. O. Sinurat,Yuyang Huang,Nanqinqin Li,Mark Powers,Michael Sherman,Kate Keahey,Haryadi S. Gunawi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对存储实验可复现性极低的问题（仅0.5%易于复现），本文提出了StorRep框架，基于Chameleon基础设施提供了6个可扩展、可复现的存储实验制品及相关指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储实验对于推动存储研究至关重要，但创建可扩展和可复现的存储实验制品极具挑战性。研究表明，仅有1%的SSD模拟器实验被封装，0.5%易于复现，这种可复现性的缺失严重阻碍了存储研究的进步。核心挑战在于确保正确的实验环境以及创建可供未来研究扩展的实验。

Method: 提出了StorRep研究框架，基于Chameleon基础设施构建，提供了六个可扩展且可复现的存储实验制品作为后续研究的基础，同时提供了实验模式和指导原则，帮助研究人员创建透明、可靠的存储实验。

Result: 成功在多年来的多个社区和教育活动中进行了实验集成，产出了公开可访问的实验制品，这些制品可以在无任何限制下进行扩展和完全复现。

Conclusion: StorRep通过提供可复现的存储实验制品和指导原则，有效解决了存储实验可复现性低的问题，为存储研究领域建立了可扩展的实验基础，有助于推动存储研究的持续发展。

Abstract: Storage experiments are vital to advancing storage research, but creating extensible and reproducible storage artifacts can be a challenging task. Our research has shown that only 1% of SSD simulator-based experiences are packaged and 0.5% of them can be easily reproduced. The lack of such artifacts without proper reproducibility can significantly impede the advancement of storage research. The biggest challenges in these types of experiments are ensuring that we have the correct environment to conduct them and creating extensible experiments that can be built upon in future research. To address this issue, we introduce StorRep, a thorough study that provides six extensible and reproducible storage experiment artifacts that serve as the foundation for further storage research, utilizing the Chameleon infrastructure. Our study offers experiment patterns and guidelines that can help researchers create transparent and dependable storage experiments. We have successfully integrated our methods in several experiments in multiple community and educational events over several years and produced publicly accessible artifacts that can be extended and fully reproduced without any restrictions.

</details>


### [14] [Solyx AI Grid: Hardware-Telemetry-Aware Routing Across Geographically Distributed GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15050)
*Aleks Bernhard,Nithin Katla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Solyx AI Grid，一个跨站点LLM推理路由控制平面，通过整合GPU硬件遥测、vLLM应用指标和WAN信号，实现比轮询高1.56-1.75x的吞吐量，并将能力不匹配泄漏降至0.43%，故障重路由时间缩短至1,247ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU资源在地理上分散于多个站点，传统的单集群LLM推理路由假设已失效，需要一种能够跨站点协调GPU硬件状态、应用指标和网络条件的新型路由机制，以优化跨分布式基础设施的大模型推理性能。

Method: 开发了Solyx AI Grid跨站点推理路由控制平面，通过10信号加权压力评分器整合DCGM GPU硬件遥测数据、vLLM应用层指标以及实时WAN信号（RTT、抖动），基于这些多维信号进行逐请求的放置决策。系统在三个美国数据中心部署，覆盖H100/H200和RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell SE等15个GPU。

Result: 在八类工作负载和216单元SLO矩阵的实验中：tier-2 SLO下吞吐量较轮询提升1.56-1.75倍；能力不匹配泄漏从标准路由的32%降至0.43%；故障重路由p99延迟从4,226ms降至1,247ms；GPU硬件遥测平均领先应用层SLO违规11.2秒，支持主动流量迁移以避免用户感知的延迟影响。

Conclusion: 这是首个公开的融合硬件遥测、应用指标和主动WAN路径信号的物理多站点LLM推理路由实证研究，证明了硬件感知路由策略可有效提升分布式GPU基础设施的推理效率和可靠性，为跨地域GPU资源协同调度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As GPU capacity fragments across geographically distributed sites, single-cluster LLM inference routing assumptions break down in measurable ways. We present Solyx AI Grid, a cross-site inference routing control plane that integrates GPU hardware telemetry (DCGM), vLLM application metrics, and real-time WAN signals (RTT, jitter) into per-request placement decisions via a 10-signal weighted pressure scorer. Across two empirical campaigns--six H100/H200 SXM GPUs and nine RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell SE GPUs spanning three US datacenters, eight workload classes, and a 216-cell SLO matrix--Solyx AI Grid delivers 1.56--1.75x throughput at tier-2 SLO over round-robin across all eight classes, cuts capability-mismatch leakage to 0.43% (versus 32% for standard routers), and reroutes around failures at a p99 of 1,247 ms versus 4,226 ms. We further find that GPU hardware telemetry leads application-layer SLO breach by 11.2 seconds on average, enabling proactive traffic drain before user-facing latency impact. To our knowledge, this is the first public empirical study of live physical multi-site LLM inference routing combining hardware telemetry, application metrics, and active WAN path signals.

</details>


### [15] [CacheWise: Understanding Workloads and Optimizing KVCache Management for Efficiently Serving LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16824)
*Shubham Tiwari,Tapan Chugh,Nash Rickert,Simon Peter,Ratul Mahajan,Haiying Shen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CacheWise系统，通过前缀感知调度和重用感知驱逐策略优化编程代理的KVCache管理，将驱逐减少2-2.6倍，会话完成时间提升3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程代理作为快速增长的LLM应用，以长时间运行的闭环会话形式执行，但其服务行为尚未被充分研究。现有服务策略难以有效处理编程代理会话中重复重用大前缀和持续KVCache压力的特征。

Method: 收集真实编程助手轨迹数据集并分析其服务行为特征；设计CacheWise KVCache管理层，结合前缀感知调度与重用感知驱逐策略，利用工具调用元数据的轻量级预测指导缓存管理；在vLLM中实现该系统。

Result: KVCache驱逐次数减少2-2.6倍；编程代理会话总完成时间提升最多3.5倍。

Conclusion: 编程代理工作负载具有独特的KVCache重用模式，通过针对性的缓存管理策略可显著提升服务性能。CacheWise为编程代理等长时运行LLM应用提供了有效的KVCache优化方案。

Abstract: Coding agents are a fast-growing LLM application, executing as long-running closed-loop sessions in which LLM generations alternate with external tool calls. Yet, unlike chat workloads, their serving behavior has not been studied extensively. We address this gap by collecting a dataset of real-world coding assistant traces. Our analysis shows that coding agent sessions repeatedly reuse large prefixes and create sustained KVCache pressure that conventional LLM serving policies handle poorly.
  Based on our analysis, we present CacheWise, a KVCache management layer that improves KVCache reuse for coding agent workloads. CacheWise combines prefix-aware scheduling with reuse-aware eviction guided by lightweight predictions from tool call metadata. Implemented in vLLM and evaluated on the collected traces, CacheWise reduces KVCache evictions by up to 2-2.6x and improves total agent session completion time by up to 3.5x.

</details>


### [16] [A RAG-Enhanced Bi-Level Cognitive Orchestration Framework for LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15076)
*Yuhong Jiang,Zhishu Shen,Tong Yin,Qiushi Zheng,Yichao Jin,Fidan Mehmeti,Jiong Jin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对LEO卫星边缘计算中的时空资源碎片化问题，提出了一种RAG增强的双层认知编排框架，通过LLM和遗传调度器实现知识引导的多目标调度，显著降低了丢包率和时延，提升了能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络中遥感数据快速增长与有限的对地下行链路容量之间存在矛盾，卫星边缘计算虽然缓解了这一压力，但由于星上计算能力差异、能源受限以及间歇性的星间和星地连接，导致了时空资源碎片化问题，使传统静态路由和调度方法性能下降。

Method: 提出RAG增强的双层认知编排框架：上层利用大语言模型（LLM）结合离线蒸馏的专家知识库（EKB），根据实时网络状态的紧凑抽象描述符动态推断偏好权重；下层采用保真度感知的遗传调度器，利用推断的权重计算物理可行且无冲突的联合路由与任务卸载调度方案。

Result: 在高保真Walker-Delta网络测试平台上进行广泛评估，与最具竞争力的学习基线相比，丢包率降低30.7%，能效提升30%，端到端时延减少8.5%，并在级联节点故障场景下保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 该框架有效整合了碎片化资源，在混合关键性工作负载下实现了知识引导的多目标调度，为动态不确定的卫星边缘计算环境提供了一种有效的资源管理解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of remote sensing data in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks is increasingly constrained by limited downlink capacity to terrestrial networks. Satellite edge computing alleviates this pressure by enabling in-orbit data processing. However, it introduces a new challenge of spatio-temporal resource fragmentation. Variations in onboard computing capability, constrained energy availability, and intermittent inter-satellite and satellite-ground connectivity lead to highly dynamic and uneven resource distribution, which degrades the performance of conventional static routing and scheduling approaches. To address this, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-enhanced bi-level cognitive orchestration framework for knowledge-guided, multi-objective scheduling. The proposed framework explicitly decouples network control across two different operational scales: at the strategic upper level, a Large Language Model (LLM) leverages an offline-distilled Expert Knowledge Base (EKB) to dynamically infer preference weights based on a compact abstract-state descriptor of real-time network conditions. At the lower execution level, a fidelity-aware genetic scheduler utilizes these inferred weights to compute physically feasible, collision-free joint routing and task offloading schedules. Extensive evaluations on a high-fidelity Walker-Delta network testbed under mixed-criticality workloads demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively consolidates fragmented resources, achieving a 30.7% reduction in packet loss, a 30% improvement in energy efficiency over the most competitive learning-based baseline, and an 8.5% decrease in end-to-end latency, while maintaining robust performance under cascading node-failure scenarios.

</details>


### [17] [Coordinated Scheduling for MoE LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15177)
*Yifan Sun,Zhexiang Zhang,Jiantong Jiang,Gholamreza Haffari,Minxian Xu,Feng Liu,Rajkumar Buyya,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Gimbal系统，通过跨层协调调度解决MoE大模型服务中的动态负载与专家路由不平衡问题，显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE大模型服务系统存在前端调度器与后端专家均衡器决策隔离的问题，无法响应细粒度的引擎压力、后端MoE压力和源相关的专家流量，导致数据并行引擎不平衡和专家级热点问题。

Method: 提出Gimbal跨层协调调度系统：(1)细粒度DP引擎调度器，利用KV缓存使用量、剩余预填充工作、队列压力和MoE专家压力等在线后端压力信号来分发请求，并应用轻量级预填充感知队列排序策略；(2)扩展专家负载均衡，使用在线源-DP到专家路由统计，结合混合整数非线性规划(MINLP)启发式方法进行专家放置，联合考虑专家负载、源感知通信和迁移稳定性。

Result: 与最先进的vLLM系统相比，Gimbal将平均首令牌时间(TTFT)降低42.9%，平均每输出令牌时间(TPOT)降低33.3%，同时将高负载请求吞吐量提升3.0%。

Conclusion: 通过跨层协调调度机制，Gimbal有效解决了MoE大模型服务中的负载不平衡问题，实现了延迟和吞吐量的显著改善。

Abstract: Serving Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLMs) is challenging because dynamic request workloads interact with sparse expert routing, creating both data-parallel (DP) engine imbalance and expert-level hotspots. Existing LLM serving systems typically make these decisions in isolation: frontend schedulers route requests using coarse request counters, while backend expert balancers rely mainly on aggregate expert activation counts. This separation prevents the serving system from reacting to fine-grained engine pressure, backend MoE pressure, and source-dependent expert traffic. To address this gap, we propose Gimbal, a coordinated cross-level scheduling system for efficient MoE-based LLM serving. First, Gimbal presents a fine-grained DP-engine scheduler that uses online backend pressure signals, including key-value (KV) cache usage, remaining prefill work, queue pressure, and MoE expert pressure, to dispatch requests away from overloaded engines. Inside each engine, Gimbal further applies a lightweight prefill-aware queue ordering policy with aging to reduce head-of-line blocking without output-length prediction. Second, Gimbal extends expert load balancing with online source-DP-to-expert routing statistics and uses a heuristic guided by a mixed-integer nonlinear program (MINLP) to place experts while jointly considering expert load, source-aware communication, and migration stability. Our evaluation shows that Gimbal reduces average Time To First Token (TTFT) by 42.9% and average Time Per Output Token (TPOT) by 33.3% compared with the state-of-the-art serving system vLLM, while improving high-load request throughput by 3.0%.

</details>


### [18] [Generation Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware Inference Offloading for Multimodal LLMs in Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15210)
*Zhongxiao Wang,Yueshen Xu,Xinkui Zhao,Wei Shao,Rui Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了QLMIO框架，用于云边协同多模态大语言模型推理卸载，在生成质量和响应延迟之间实现最优平衡，延迟降低高达58.14%，同时保持了与纯云端执行相当的任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向边缘端部署发展，云边协同推理成为新范式。然而在云边协同MLLM系统中，用户关心的多个QoS属性（特别是内容生成质量和响应延迟）的联合优化极其困难，且缺乏预测生成质量和推理延迟的有效方法，也缺乏针对MLLM推理卸载问题的公开数据集。

Method: 提出Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware MLLM Inference Offloading (QLMIO)框架，通过推理卸载技术在生成质量和响应延迟之间进行决策优化。同时构建了真实的云边协同MLLM系统，并收集了MLLM推理卸载基准数据集MIOBench用于评估和促进该问题的研究。

Result: 实验结果表明，QLMIO框架相比基线方法将延迟降低高达58.14%，同时达到了与纯云端服务器执行所有请求情况下相当的任务完成率。

Conclusion: QLMIO框架能够有效解决云边协同MLLM系统中生成质量与响应延迟的联合优化问题，实现了显著的性能提升，为MLLM推理卸载研究提供了有效的解决方案和基准数据集。

Abstract: Beyond pure cloud, some efforts are being made to deploy Large Language Models (LLMs) in edge to accelerate inference response. So the deployment of LLMs in cloud-edge continuum becomes a promising paradigm, where the tasks involving multimodal data occupy a large part of requests. Under this continuum, users usually concern about multiple Quality-of-Service (QoS) attributes, but it is always intractable to jointly optimize them. In this paper, we propose to study the joint optimization of those attributes and focus on two key representatives, i.e., content generation quality and response latency. We propose to study the offloading technology to achieve a tradeoff between the two objectives in the cloud-edge collaborative Multimodal LLM (MLLM) system. However, it is highly difficult to predict generation quality and inference latency for MLLM inference tasks while optimizing this offloading process. To address these unprecedented difficulties, we propose a Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware MLLM Inference Offloading (QLMIO) framework to make decisions that optimally balance generation quality and response latency. Meanwhile, recognizing the absence of publicly available datasets tailored to the MLLM inference offloading problem, we constructed a real-world cloud-edge collaborative MLLM system and subsequently collected an MLLM Inference Offloading Benchmark (MIOBench) to comprehensively evaluate our framework and facilitate the study of this problem. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the QLMIO framework reduces latency by up to 58.14\% compared to baselines, while simultaneously matching the task completion rate achieved under the case that executes all requests exclusively on a cloud server. The dataset and codes are available at Github.

</details>


### [19] [Adaptive Resource Management and Quality Control for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15319)
*Yifei Xia,Hao Yuan,Suhan Ling,Haoran Sun,Hanke Zhang,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SlackServe系统，通过以播放缓冲时间为统一信号来动态调整资源分配和生成质量配置，实现实时流式视频生成的连续播放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩散变换器将视频生成从离线模式转变为实时流式模式，其服务级目标从固定延迟或吞吐量转变为保持播放连续性。一旦生成落后于播放时间线，用户会经历可见的卡顿，因此需要根据播放进度动态调整资源和生成质量。

Method: 提出SlackServe系统，使用播放缓冲作为统一信号：通过三级优先队列、重定位和弹性序列并行性在流之间重新分配资源；通过双模态帕累托路由在质量下限约束下为每个视频块选择保真度配置。

Result: 在16个H100 GPU集群上，SlackServe将连续播放比(CPR)提升1.64x-3.29x，将首个视频块时间(TTFC)减少1.61x-9.65x，同时保持相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: SlackServe成功实现了实时流式视频生成的播放连续性保障，通过播放缓冲驱动的资源分配和质量适应策略显著提升了用户体验质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive diffusion transformers (AR-DiTs) recast video generation from an offline paradigm to a real-time streaming one: the model generates video one chunk at a time, making each chunk available for playout once produced. The service-level objective (SLO) for this paradigm is no longer fixed latency or throughput but the preservation of playout continuity: generation must stay ahead of the playout timeline. Once generation falls behind, the remaining playable buffer (playout slack) is exhausted, and users experience visible stalls. This objective reveals two serving design insights. First, real-time video generation has a dynamic SLO that evolves with playout progress, so resources should move toward streams with lower playout slack. Second, an acceptable chunk delivered on time is preferable to a late high-fidelity chunk, so per-chunk fidelity configurations should adapt to available playout slack. Guided by these insights, we present SlackServe, a playout-slack-driven serving system that preserves playout continuity in real-time streaming video generation. SlackServe uses playout slack as a unified signal, reallocating resources across streams through three-tier priority queues, re-homing, and elastic sequence parallelism, while selecting per-chunk fidelity configurations within each stream through Bi-Modal Pareto Routing under a quality floor. On a 16-H100 GPU cluster, SlackServe improves Quality of Experience (QoE), measured by Continuous Play Ratio (CPR), by 1.64x-3.29x and reduces Time to First Chunk (TTFC) by 1.61x-9.65x over baselines, while preserving comparable generation quality.

</details>


### [20] [CoAgent: Concurrency Control for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15376)
*Hongtao Lyu,Dingyan Zhang,Mingyu Wu,Xingda Wei,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MTPO协议，通过利用LLM智能体的冲突判断与计划修复能力，将并发控制转变为建议性机制，在保持可串行化的同时实现了接近串行执行的5%正确率和1.4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（编码、DevOps、文档智能体）常并行访问同一git树、Kubernetes集群或文档，但经典并发控制机制（锁、OCC）不适用于LLM智能体：单个事务跨越数分钟推理、读集宽泛不透明、状态不允许分叉或缓冲导致写入立即生效，锁会阻塞长推理间隔，OCC冲突时丢弃数分钟工作。

Method: 提出MTPO（单调轨迹预序）协议：启动时固定串行化顺序，为每次读取提供顺序过滤值，推测性写入；当冲突发生时，单向通知受影响智能体重新判断并修补计划，框架通过工具注册的saga式逆操作机械撤销并重排错位写入。实现了CoAgent工具调用中间件，其特权ToolSmith在线生成具有占用声明和可撤销特性的工具。

Result: 在十个竞争工作负载上，CoAgent在串行正确率的5%以内达到1.4倍加速和接近串行的token成本，而2PL和OCC几乎丧失所有并发收益；在仅bash的目标系统上，在线生长25个工具库，将任务通过率从45/71提升至63/71，时间降至0.80倍、成本降至0.86倍。

Conclusion: MTPO通过利用LLM智能体的冲突判断与修复能力，成功解决了多智能体并发控制问题，在保持可串行化的同时实现了显著的性能提升和成本降低，为多智能体LLM系统提供了有效的并发控制解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems -- coding agents, devops agents, document agents -- now routinely run several agents in parallel against the same git tree, Kubernetes cluster, or document. As soon as two of them mutate shared state, they enter the regime classical concurrency control has studied for decades, but classical mechanisms fit LLM agents poorly. A single agent transaction spans minutes of inference, read sets are broad and opaque rather than statically inferable, and the live state agents act on admits neither fork nor buffer, so writes take effect the moment they execute. Locks block long inference intervals; OCC abort-and-retry discards minutes of work on every conflict.
  This paper builds concurrency control on a capability classical transactions lack: the LLM inside each agent can judge whether a conflicting write invalidates its plan, and can repair exactly the operations that depended on it. Control therefore turns advisory: the runtime informs, the agent repairs. Our protocol, MTPO (Monotonic Trajectory Pre-Order), fixes a serialization order at launch, serves each read the order-filtered value, and applies writes speculatively in place; a one-way notification asks an affected reader to re-judge and patch its plan, while the framework mechanically undoes and reorders misplaced writes through the saga-style inverse each tool registers in advance. At quiescence the run is serializable in the pre-decided order. We realize MTPO as CoAgent, toolcall middleware whose privileged ToolSmith grows footprint-declared, undoable tools online. On ten contended workloads, CoAgent stays within 5\% of serial correctness at a $1.4\times$ speedup and near-serial token cost, where 2PL and OCC surrender nearly all concurrency gains; on a bash-only target system, it grows a 25-tool library online and lifts the task pass rate from 45/71 to 63/71 at $0.80\times$ the time and $0.86\times$ the cost.

</details>


### [21] [Is RISC-V Ready for Massively Parallel Astrophysical Codes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15490)
*Jenny Lynn Almerol,Nitin Shukla,Federico Ficarelli,Geray S. Karademir,Andrea Bartolini,Emanuele Venieri,Giacomo Madella,Elisabetta Boella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了三款天体物理应用在RISC-V处理器上的性能与移植性，发现RISC-V较x86和ARM系统性能落后3-9倍，主要受限于内存带宽、缓存、向量单元和编译器技术，但仍具备科学计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RISC-V架构的快速发展，评估其在科学计算领域的性能和移植性具有重要意义。本文旨在研究RISC-V处理器在代表性天体物理生产代码中的表现，识别性能瓶颈，并与成熟的x86和ARM平台进行对比，为RISC-V在科学计算领域的应用提供参考。

Method: 选取三款典型的天体物理生产代码（iPIC3D、PLUTO、OpenGGCM），分别代表内存受限、计算受限和混合负载类型。在Sophgo SG2044 RISC-V处理器上运行，并与AMD EPYC 9554（x86）和NVIDIA GH200 Grace（ARM）系统进行对比。首先验证数值正确性以确保移植成功，然后进行性能测试，分析不同MPI+OpenMP配置下的性能表现。

Result: 所有应用成功移植到RISC-V平台，数值正确性得到验证。RISC-V性能持续落后：相对x86慢3-6倍，相对ARM慢5-9倍。性能差距主要原因包括：有限的内存带宽、共享缓存限制、较窄的128位向量单元、较低的时钟频率，以及GNU编译器套件不够成熟的自动向量化能力。内存受限内核受影响最大，带宽过早饱和和L2缓存争用在更高线程数时降低可扩展性。混合MPI+OpenMP配置显示内存争用与通信开销之间的权衡，中间配置实现最佳性能。

Conclusion: RISC-V处理器能够支持科学计算工作负载，移植性良好，但目前性能与成熟架构存在显著差距。要实现竞争力性能，需要在硬件和编译器技术方面进行改进，特别是自动向量化能力的提升。研究结果为RISC-V在科学计算领域的发展指明了改进方向。

Abstract: We present a performance and portability evaluation of three well-established astrophysical production codes, namely iPIC3D, PLUTO, and OpenGGCM, on a Sophgo SG2044 RISC-V processor (part of the Monte Cimone cluster), with comparisons to AMD EPYC 9554 (x86) and NVIDIA GH200 Grace (ARM) systems. These applications represent memory-bound, compute-bound, and hybrid workloads, respectively. Numerical correctness is verified across all platforms, confirming portability. RISC-V shows consistently lower performance, with slowdowns of about $3-6\times$ relative to x86 and $5-9\times$ relative to ARM. The gap is mainly due to limited memory bandwidth, shared cache constraints, narrower 128-bit vector units, and lower clock frequency, but also less-mature auto-vectorization capability of the GNU compiler suite. Memory-bound kernels are the most affected, where early bandwidth saturation and L2 cache contention reduce scalability at higher thread counts. Hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations reveal a trade-off between memory contention and communication overhead, with intermediate configurations achieving the best performance. These results suggest that RISC-V is capable of supporting scientific workloads; however, additional improvements in both hardware and compiler technology, particularly in auto-vectorization, are required to achieve competitive performance.

</details>


### [22] [Quantifying the Impact of Lossy Compression on Neural Generative Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15959)
*Zhimin Li,Harshitha Menon,Charles Jekel,Valerio Pascucci,Peter Lindstrom*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有损压缩对生成式代理模型质量的影响，提出了一种量化压缩误差容忍度的方法，在保证模型质量的前提下实现高达39倍的数据压缩和3倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真的生成式代理模型需要大量训练数据，这带来了存储和I/O挑战。虽然有损压缩可以缓解这一问题，但压缩误差会以微妙的方式影响模型质量，难以量化其影响程度。

Method: 首先刻画神经网络训练过程中固有的不确定性（相同训练配置会产生不同模型），利用这种变异性提出一种方法来估计代理模型在不影响精度的情况下能够容忍多大的压缩诱导误差。

Result: 在两个应用仿真上的评估表明：该方法实现数据存储节省达23.7倍和39倍，同时不影响代理模型质量；减小训练数据集规模提升了数据加载速度，训练时间缩短最多3倍。

Conclusion: 有损压缩可有效应用于生成式代理模型的训练数据压缩，在显著降低存储需求和加速训练的同时保持模型质量，为科学发现中的大规模训练数据管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are used as generative surrogate models for scientific discovery, which are trainable approximations of scientific simulations. These models enable users to replace time-consuming numerical simulations with learned alternatives, providing quick solutions. However, high-fidelity generative surrogate models require massive training datasets, which can create storage and I/O challenges. Lossy compression is a promising way to reduce this burden, but compression errors may affect the model quality in subtle ways, making it challenging to quantify their impact.
  In this work, we examine how lossy compression of training data impacts the quality of generative surrogate models. We begin by characterizing the uncertainty inherent in training neural networks, showing that identical training configurations can produce different models. By exploiting this variability, we propose a method to estimate how much compression-induced error a surrogate model can tolerate without affecting its accuracy. Evaluation of two application simulations demonstrates that our approach significantly reduces memory/storage requirements and speeds up training while producing high-quality surrogate models. These results show that lossy compression saves data storage up to 23.7x and 39x with negligible impact on the quality of the surrogate model. Meanwhile, reducing the size of the training data set also enhances the data loading speed and reduces the training time by up to 3x.

</details>


### [23] [PreLort: Prefix-Nested LoRA for Federated Fine-Tuning under Rank Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15963)
*Muhammad Waseem,Nurbek Tastan,Andrej Jovanovic,Nicholas D. Lane,Nils Lukas,Karthik Nandakumar,Samuel Horvath*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PreLort方法，通过前缀嵌套的低秩层级组织方式，解决联邦学习中不同客户端适配器秩不一致导致的聚合问题，使低秩客户端能从高秩客户端学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA方法在处理异构硬件资源时，无法控制信息在秩维度上的分布，导致共享低秩表示的次优利用；低秩客户端无法有效利用高秩客户端贡献的信息。

Method: 提出PreLort框架：(1) 前缀嵌套的低秩公式化，将适配器维度组织为前缀层级；(2) 分段聚合规则，仅对贡献每个秩段的客户端平均；(3) 前缀嵌套训练策略，在多种秩截断下优化适配器。

Result: 在多个基础模型上实验表明，该方法在准确率和ROUGE-L指标上持续优于现有异构联邦LoRA方法，同时实现更低或相当的困惑度。

Conclusion: PreLort通过确保低秩前缀维度捕获最任务相关信息，实现了低秩客户端从高秩客户端的丰富信息中受益，为异构联邦学习中的参数高效微调提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models using parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA enables privacy-preserving adaptation of foundation models. Heterogeneous hardware resources introduce challenges, as clients with different adapter ranks cannot be directly aggregated. While existing methods enable aggregation under heterogeneous ranks, they fail to control how information is distributed across rank dimensions, leading to suboptimal use of shared low-rank representations. Instead, we propose PreLort: a nested low-rank formulation for federated LoRA that organizes adapter dimensions into a prefix hierarchy. Our approach ensures that lower-rank dimensions encode task-relevant information, while higher-rank dimensions capture additional capacity. Building on this, we introduce (i) a segment-wise aggregation rule that averages only over clients contributing to each rank segment, avoiding dilution from zero-padded lower-rank clients, and (ii) a prefix-nested training strategy that optimizes each adapter under multiple rank truncations, encouraging useful signal to concentrate in low-rank prefix dimensions. Together, these components encourage a consistent low-rank prefix capturing the most task-relevant information, while higher-rank dimensions learn additional capacity. This allows low-rank clients to benefit from richer information contributed by higher-rank clients, as prefix dimensions are consistently learned and aggregated. Experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms prior heterogeneous federated LoRA methods in accuracy and ROUGE-L, while achieving lower or comparable perplexity across multiple base models.

</details>


### [24] [Raiders of the Lost Log: Synchronous Parallel In-Place Models and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15969)
*Michael T. Goodrich,Vinesh Sridhar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了同步PIP模型族，填补了现有异步PIP模型在中间处理器数量下无法保证原地性的空白，并设计了高效的同步并行算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PIP模型假设异步处理，且在中间处理器数量下没有原地保证，限制了其在并行和分布式计算中的应用。

Method: 提出Synchronous PIP模型族，引入parallel-augmented sweep技术，确保算法在所有处理器数量下保持高效且严格原地。

Result: 设计了无需辅助共享内存、每个处理器仅需常数私有内存的高效同步并行算法，证明了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: Synchronous PIP模型成功解决了异步模型的局限性，为嵌入式系统和物联网应用提供了严格原地的并行计算框架。

Abstract: Embedded systems and Internet of Things (IoT) applications motivate in-place parallel algorithms, which avoid allocating additional shared memory past the input. Work by Gu, Obeya, and Shun [APOCS '21] defines a family of PIP (parallel in-place) models and parallel algorithms that eschew auxiliary memory at high processor counts while remaining in-situ when run sequentially. However, their models assume asynchronous processing and have no in-place guarantees for intermediate processor counts. We address this gap in the literature by proposing a Synchronous PIP family of models for in-place parallel and distributed computation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our new model by giving efficient and synchronous parallel algorithms in this model that require no auxiliary shared memory and only constant private memory per processor. Importantly, we show how to leverage a new parallel-augmented sweep technique to ensure that Synchronous PIP algorithms remain efficient and strictly in-place at all processor counts.

</details>
