*Rowdy Chotkan,Bulat Nasrulin,Johan Pouwelse,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Beware框架，通过过滤虚假延迟报告、计算鲁棒的权重分布和应用机器学习，解决动态配置优化中的拜占庭攻击漏洞，将共识延迟降低最多45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链BFT-SMR系统通过动态配置优化（如基于网络延迟分配领导权和调整投票权重）来提升性能，但这一过程存在三个可被拜占庭节点利用的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出Beware重配置框架，包含三个核心机制：1）过滤虚假延迟报告；2）计算鲁棒的权重分布；3）应用机器学习技术收敛到拜占庭弹性配置。

Result: 实验评估表明，与现有解决方案相比，Beware可将共识延迟降低最多45%，有效提升了系统性能。

Conclusion: Beware成功解决了BFT-SMR动态配置优化中的安全漏洞，在保证拜占庭容错性的同时显著提升了共识性能，证明了安全感知的配置优化方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Distributed systems handle adversarial nodes through redundancy, which imposes a significant performance overhead. In blockchain systems, Byzantine fault-tolerant state-machine replication (BFT-SMR) is the replicated service that totally orders client transactions before execution. While prior research has primarily focused on designing novel consensus algorithms with improved performance, recent studies have shown that further gains can be achieved through configuration optimization. More precisely, replicas can monitor network latency to dynamically assign the leader role and tune voting weights, thereby improving consensus performance. However, we identify three vulnerabilities in this process that Byzantine nodes can exploit. To address these weaknesses, we propose Beware, a reconfiguration framework that filters out falsified latency reports, computes robust weight distributions, and applies machine learning to converge towards Byzantine-resilient configurations. Our evaluation shows that Beware reduces consensus latency by up to 45% compared to existing solutions.

</details>


### [26] [SwiftCache: Efficient LLM Serving for Multi-turn Conversations with Heterogeneous KV Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16135)
*Jianmin Hu,Minxian Xu,Sa Wang,Chong Ma,Min Shen,Kejiang Ye,Lin Qu,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Swiftcache协作推理系统，通过异构模型共享闲置GPU内存和NVLink带宽，解决多轮对话中KV缓存导致的显存不足和重载延迟问题，将P99 TTFT降低69%，上下文长度提升3.98倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景中历史token的KV缓存不断累积，有限的GPU显存容量迫使KV缓存卸载至CPU或SSD，导致随着上下文增长，KV缓存重载延迟显著增加。同时显存容量限制了最大推理长度，进而限制了对话轮次。

Method: 提出SwiftCache协作推理系统，使异构模型共享服务器内闲置的GPU内存和NVLink带宽。低KV缓存需求的模型将闲置显存捐赠给高需求模型存储前缀缓存，通过NVLink实现跨模型KV缓存共享以避免慢速PCIe传输。此外，仅在本地GPU显存中保留当前活动层的KV缓存以降低显存压力。

Result: 在真实工作负载上的实验表明，相比vLLM和SGLang，SwiftCache将P99 TTFT降低高达69%，最大上下文长度提升高达3.98倍，且对共置模型的干扰极小。

Conclusion: SwiftCache通过协作式内存共享和层间缓存优化，有效缓解了多轮对话场景下的显存瓶颈问题，显著提升了推理性能和上下文长度，证明了异构模型间资源共享的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multi-turn conversation is a fundamental scenario in LLM applications, widely used in chatbots and AI agents. As the conversation evolves, historical tokens accumulate continuously. Existing systems cache their key-value (KV) pairs to avoid redundant computation. However, limited GPU memory (HBM) capacity often forces these KV caches to be offloaded to CPU memory or SSD, making KV cache reloads increasingly costly in terms of latency as the context grows. Meanwhile, the constrained HBM capacity also limits the maximum inference length, thereby restricting the number of turns that can be supported in a conversation.
  To address these two challenges, we propose SwiftCache, a collaborative inference system that enables heterogeneous models to share underutilized GPU memory and NVLink bandwidth within a server. Specifically, models with low KV cache demand donate idle GPU memory to store the prefix cache of high-demand models, allowing cross-model KV cache sharing over NVLink and avoiding slow PCIe transfers. SwiftCache further reduces memory pressure by keeping only the KV cache of the currently active layer in local GPU memory, thereby enabling longer-context inference. Our experiments on real-world workloads show that SwiftCache reduces P99 time-to-first-token (TTFT) by up to 69% and extends maximum context length by up to 3.98x compared to vLLM and SGLang, with minimal interference to co-located models.

</details>


### [27] [A Unified Constant-Time Switch Rule for Constructing Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16892)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的闭式构造方法，能够将高斯网络的所有边划分为两个边不相交的哈密顿回路，覆盖了所有情况（包括互质和非互质生成元、奇数和偶数d），无需分情况讨论表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯网络是基于高斯整数剩余类定义的四度对称互联网络。先前的研究表明，当生成元α=a+bi满足gcd(a,b)=1时，实部和虚部维度可以直接形成两个边不相交的哈密顿回路。后续工作将结果扩展到非互质情况gcd(a,b)=d>1，但其证明使用了冗长的节点序列表，并对d的奇偶性分别处理。本文旨在提供一个统一的闭式构造方法，覆盖所有情况，避免分情况讨论。

Method: 在具有d行和r=(a²+b²)/d列的矩形表示中，构造方法对每个q=1,2,...,d-1在列a_q=q-1处使用常数时间的局部交换规则。每次交换移除两条水平边并插入两条垂直边。交换后的水平结构形成第一个哈密顿回路，而其在高斯网络中的边补集形成第二个哈密顿回路。该方法需要O(d)的交换生成时间和O(N)的时间列出两个回路，其中N=a²+b²。

Result: 成功构造了一个统一的闭式方法，将高斯网络的完整边集划分为两个边不相交的哈密顿回路。该方法覆盖了d=1和d>1的情况，以及奇数和偶数d的情况，无需分情况表格。对1≤a≤b≤100范围内的所有情况（仅排除退化的N=2网络）进行穷举验证，以及大规模验证至N=3,250,000，均确认了构造的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一闭式构造方法有效地解决了高斯网络中边不相交哈密顿回路的构造问题，简化了先前需要分情况处理的复杂证明，为高斯网络的理论研究提供了更优雅的解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian networks are degree-four symmetric interconnection networks defined over residue classes of Gaussian integers. Earlier work showed that when the generator $α=a+bi$ satisfies $\gcd(a,b)=1$, the real and imaginary dimensions directly form two edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. A later construction extended the result to the non-coprime case $\gcd(a,b)=d>1$, but its proof used long node-sequence tables and separate odd/even cases for $d$. This paper gives a unified closed-form construction that covers both $d=1$ and $d>1$, and also covers both odd and even $d$, without separate case tables. In the rectangular representation with $d$ rows and $r=(a^2+b^2)/d$ columns, the construction uses a constant-time local switch rule for each $q=1,2,\ldots,d-1$ at column $a_q=q-1$. Each switch removes two horizontal edges and inserts two vertical edges. The switched horizontal structure forms the first Hamiltonian cycle, while its edge-complement in the Gaussian network forms the second Hamiltonian cycle. Thus, the full edge set is partitioned into two edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. The construction requires $O(d)$ switch-generation time and $O(N)$ time to list the two cycles, where $N=a^2+b^2$. Exhaustive validation for all $1\leq a\leq b\leq 100$, excluding only the degenerate $N=2$ network, and large-scale validation up to $N=3{,}250{,}000$ confirm the construction.

</details>


### [28] [Efficient Data Availability Sampling via Coded Distributed Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16200)
*Dang Pham Minh,Hung Vuong Huu,Duc A. Tran*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDA，一种基于编码分布式阵列的新型数据可用性采样(DAS)设计，利用网络编码提升区块链的扩展性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链系统采用全复制模型，要求节点下载完整区块以验证数据可用性，导致随着区块大小增加出现传播延迟、数据传输冗余和共识达成缓慢等问题。尽管以太坊已采用DAS缓解此问题，但现有研究主要关注密码学基础，而支持拜占庭容错和可扩展的P2P网络层机制仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CDA(编码分布式阵列)设计，基于网络编码技术构建数据可用性采样系统，确保在节点仅存储数据小片段的情况下实现鲁棒性和高效性，重点关注P2P网络层的发现和路由机制。

Result: 评估研究显示，CDA相比以太坊最新的DAS方案RDA，在性能上实现数倍的提升，证明了该方法在可扩展性和效率方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: CDA为区块链网络提供了一种高效、鲁棒的数据可用性采样解决方案，通过优化网络层机制有效解决了现有DAS系统的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Data availability is a fundamental bottleneck in modern blockchain networks. Most blockchain systems rely on a full-replication model, which requires downloading of a full block to verify its availability. This model does not scale with block size because every node must handle large volumes of data, leading to slower block propagation, duplicated data transfer, and longer consensus agreement. This issue is well-known in Ethereum, where layer-2 rollups publish data directly into the chain. To overcome, Ethereum adopts Data Availability Sampling (DAS) to let nodes keep only a small fragment of the data while still ensuring availability. Prior work on DAS has focused on cryptographic foundations. Meanwhile, the peer-to-peer network layer that provides Byzantine-tolerant and scalable mechanisms for discovery and routing of DAS fragments is underexplored. We propose CDA, a new design for DAS based on coded distributed arrays that leverages network coding to ensure both robustness and efficiency. Our evaluation study compares CDA to RDA, the latest DAS development of Ethereum, showing an improvement of several times better.

</details>


### [29] [Re-Rooting-Based Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16954)
*Bader Albader,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla,Galal Hassan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态源节点重定位的轻量级容错广播方法，通过将故障节点重新定位为广播树的叶节点来避免故障节点转发，在单故障和双故障情况下保证广播完成，最多需要2k步完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稠密高斯网络中，节点故障可能破坏广播过程，特别是当故障节点位于内部转发位置时。现有的容错方法通常需要构建冗余生成树或备份路由结构，开销较大。因此需要一种轻量级的容错广播方法。

Method: 提出基于动态源节点重定位（re-rooting）的轻量级容错广播方法。对于单故障情况，直接从故障节点的距离k边界选择新源节点；对于双故障情况，证明了在G(k+(k+1)i)中任意一对故障节点存在一个距离恰好为k的节点作为新源节点。源节点选择过程需要O(k)时间。

Result: 在单故障和双故障情况下，该方法能够保证完成广播，最多需要2k步（原始广播需要k步，重定位距离最多k步）。通过反例证明三故障情况下该保证不成立。仿真结果验证了该方法在单故障和双故障场景下能够完成对所有非故障节点的消息传递。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法无需构建冗余结构即可实现容错广播，在单故障和双故障情况下具有理论保证和实际有效性，是一种高效的轻量级容错广播解决方案。

Abstract: Dense Gaussian networks provide degree-4 interconnection topologies with small diameter and regular structure, making them suitable for efficient one-to-all broadcasting. However, node failures can disrupt the broadcast process when faulty nodes occupy internal forwarding positions. This paper proposes a lightweight fault-tolerant broadcasting method based on dynamic source relocation, or re-rooting. Instead of constructing redundant spanning trees or backup routing structures, the proposed method selects a new source node so that the faulty nodes are located at graph distance \(k\), the network diameter, from the new source. Consequently, faulty nodes become leaf-level nodes in the broadcast process and are not required to forward the message. For the single-fault case, the new source is selected directly from the graph-distance-\(k\) boundary of the faulty node. For the two-fault case, we prove that for any pair of faulty nodes in \(G(k+(k+1)i)\), there exists a node whose graph distance from both faulty nodes is exactly \(k\). The source-selection procedure requires \(O(k)\) time. Since the original one-to-all broadcast completes in \(k\) parallel steps and the relocation distance is at most \(k\), the proposed method completes in at most \(2k\) steps in the worst case. We also show that the two-fault guarantee does not generally extend to arbitrary three-fault configurations by giving a counterexample in \(G(3+4i)\). Simulation results confirm complete delivery to all non-faulty nodes under the tested one- and two-node failure scenarios, while the baseline broadcast may fail when faulty nodes occur at internal forwarding positions.

</details>


### [30] [Tropical: Enhancing SLO Attainment in Disaggregated LLM Serving via SLO-Aware Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16264)
*Jinming Ma,Jiefei Chen,Xiuhong Li,Jiangfei Duan,Haojie Duanmu,Xingcheng Zhang,Chao Yang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Tropical系统，通过SLO感知的多路复用策略平衡排队时间和干扰，在LLM服务中同时优化了TTFT和TPOT两个关键性能指标，在90% SLO达成率下请求处理量提升2.09倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务架构在TTFT和TPOT两个延迟约束上存在权衡：非 disaggregated 架构排队时间长但干扰小，disaggregated 架构排队时间短但干扰大，无法同时在两个指标上达到最优。

Method: 设计并实现Tropical系统，引入SLO感知的多路复用策略，动态平衡排队时间和预填充-解码之间的干扰，充分利用两种架构的优势。

Result: 在真实数据集评估中，Tropical在90% SLO达成率下处理请求数量提升2.09倍；相比disaggregated系统，P90 TTFT提升9倍，P90 TPOT仅降低15%；相比非disaggregated系统，P90 TPOT提升2.8倍，P90 TTFT保持不变。

Conclusion: Tropical成功结合了非disaggregated和disaggregated LLM服务的优点，首次实现了TTFT和TPOT双指标的同时优化，为LLM服务提供了新的架构设计思路。

Abstract: To guarantee service quality in transformer based large language model (LLM) serving, it is essential to meet the latency constraints of both the prefill phase (measured by Time-to-First-Token, TTFT) and the decode phase (measured by Time-per-Output-Token, TPOT). Non-disaggregated serving places prefill and decode on the same worker, while disaggregated serving places the prefill and decode on isolated workers. However, no single architecture excels in both TTFT and TPOT metrics. After conducting a root cause analysis, we concluded that indisaggregated LLM serving, prefill execution has minimal interference with decode execution but result in high queuing times. In contrast,non-disaggregated LLM serving effectively reduces queuing times but introduces significant interference between prefills and decodes.
  In order to leverage the best aspects of both non-disaggregated anddisaggregated LLM serving, we have designed and implemented Tropical.Tropical introduces an sevice-level objectives (SLO)-aware multiplexing strategy that balances the queuing time and the interference, enabling the LLM serving to achieve high TTFT and TPOT SLOs simultaneously. Our evaluation of real-world datasets reveals that Tropical outperforms both state-of-the-art non-disaggregated and disaggregated LLM serving systems, achieving up to 2.09 more requests within a 90% SLO attainment. Specially, compared to the disaggregated LLM serving system, Tropicalimproves P90 TTFT performance by 9 with only an 15% reduction in P90 TPOT. Against the non-disaggregated LLM serving systems, Tropicaldelivers a 2.8 performance improvement in P90 TPOT while maintaining the same P90 TTFT.

</details>


### [31] [SMEPilot: Characterizing and Optimizing LLM Inference with Scalable Matrix Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16332)
*Feiyang Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMEPilot，一个针对支持SME（可扩展矩阵扩展）的CPU的LLM推理引擎。通过基于屋顶线模型的特征分析，为每个算子动态选择CPU-only、SME-only或SME+CPU协同执行模式，在多种平台上实现了最高3.94倍的推理性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CPU集成了矩阵扩展单元（如Arm SME），为LLM推理提供了高吞吐量矩阵运算能力。然而，这些单元并不能完全替代传统CPU核心：LLM推理中的prefill、decode、attention和KV-cache操作具有不同的算术强度、向量行为和布局要求，同时SME单元和CPU核心竞争共享内存带宽，存在性能不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 通过基于屋顶线模型对SME支持的CPU进行特征分析和建模；2) 设计SMEPilot推理引擎，为每个算子形状选择最优执行方式（CPU-only、SME-only或SME+CPU协同）；3) 在tile粒度上将矩阵工作分配给SME和CPU核心；4) 在attention中重叠SME适合的矩阵阶段和CPU适合的向量阶段；5) 维护布局状态以便在关键路径上复用而非重复构建打包张量表示。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-30BA3B模型上，跨越手机、PC和服务器平台进行测试，SMEPilot实现了端到端推理性能最高3.94倍的提升。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过深入理解SME单元与CPU核心的特性和限制，并针对性地设计执行策略，可以有效利用现代CPU的矩阵扩展能力，显著提升LLM推理性能。SMEPilot为异构CPU架构上的LLM推理优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern CPUs increasingly integrate matrix extensions, such as Arm Scalable Matrix Extension (SME), that provide high-throughput matrix execution within the CPU. For LLM inference, however, these units are not a universal replacement for conventional CPU cores: prefill, decode, attention, and KV-cache operations expose different arithmetic intensities, vector behavior, and layout requirements, while SME units and CPU cores still compete for shared memory bandwidth. This paper studies this mismatch through a roofline-based characterization of SME-enabled CPUs and uses the resulting model to guide operator-level execution choices. We present SMEPilot, an LLM inference engine that selects CPU-only, SME-only, or cooperative SME+CPU execution for each operator shape. SMEPilot partitions matrix work across SME and CPU cores at tile granularity, overlaps SME-suitable matrix stages with CPU-suitable vector stages in attention, and maintains layout state so packed tensor representations are reused rather than repeatedly rebuilt on critical paths. Across Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-30BA3B on phone, PC, and server platforms, SMEPilot improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 3.94$\times$.

</details>


### [32] [Generated, Parallel, Scalable? A Study of Agentic AI-Generated Julia Code on Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16534)
*Linus Bantel,Anna-Lena Roth,Jonas Posner,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次研究基于智能体（agentic）的LLM生成并行Julia代码，发现商业模型在Base.Threads和MPI.jl上表现尚可，但在Dagger.jl任务并行中存在任务依赖、粒度和调度等关键缺陷，大规模HPC系统的鲁棒并行代码生成仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Julia在HPC领域日益普及，但实现可扩展的高性能并行仍需并行编程专业知识。现有LLM代码生成研究多关注单次提示，缺乏对智能体自主规划、生成和优化代码场景的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究智能体生成并行Julia代码的能力与局限。

Method: 基于OpenCode智能体扩展Julia文档MCP服务器，评估GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.7和Qwen3-Coder-Next三款LLM生成Dagger.jl任务并行代码的能力。测试三种并行结构问题：π近似、分块矩阵乘法和分块Cholesky分解。对比Base.Threads和MPI.jl基线，共享内存扩展至192核，分布式内存实验在两节点上进行。

Result: 智能体能可靠生成小规模输入的可执行代码，但在大规模下因死锁、过度订阅或内存溢出而失败，开源模型问题最严重。商业模型在Base.Threads和MPI.jl上扩展性相当，但其Dagger.jl实现暴露出任务依赖、粒度控制和调度策略等重复性弱点。

Conclusion: 智能体AI在生成并行Julia代码方面具有潜力，但生成适用于大规模HPC系统的鲁棒、性能感知的并行实现仍是未解决的挑战，特别是在任务并行框架中存在显著的正确性和可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Julia is increasingly used in hpc as a single-language alternative to combining high-level scripting with low-level systems languages, but achieving scalable performance still requires expertise in parallel programming. llms are increasingly used for code generation and are advancing rapidly with each new version. Yet, existing studies focus on single-shot prompting rather than agentic settings, in which an llm autonomously plans, generates, and refines code through tool use.
  Using an OpenCode-based agent extended with a Julia-documentation mcp server, we study agentic generation of parallel Julia code, focusing on task-based execution with Dagger.jl. We evaluate three llms, OpenAI GPT-5.5, Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7, and the open-weight Qwen3-Coder-Next, on three problems with distinct parallel structures: π approximation, tiled general matrix multiplication, and tiled Cholesky decomposition. The generated Dagger.jl implementations are compared against agent-generated Base.Threads and MPI.jl baselines, with shared-memory experiments scaling to 192 cores and distributed-memory experiments on two nodes.
  The agents reliably produce executable code for small inputs but fail at larger scales due to deadlocks, oversubscription, or out-of-memory errors, with the open-weight model affected most severely. The two commercial models scale comparably on Base.Threads and MPI.jl, while their Dagger.jl implementations expose recurring weaknesses in task dependencies, granularity, and scheduling. Agentic AI is promising for producing parallel Julia code, but generating robust, performance-aware implementations for large-scale hpc systems remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [33] [Incentives and Evidence in Learned Service Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16555)
*Syed Izhan Khilji,Alireza Furutanpey,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过实证评估发现，强化学习在服务编排中难以规模化部署的原因并非仅是技术层面的性能退化，更多源于制度性问题：现有研究偏向于与弱势基准比较来夸大收益，缺乏对生产环境的可复现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在服务编排领域研究已逾十年，但仍未在生产环境中大规模应用。通常解释是学习型控制器在延迟/噪声遥测、工作负载变化及不受控租户下性能退化。本文旨在验证现有证据是否支持该解释，并揭示文献中存在的系统性问题。

Method: 评估三个高影响力的强化学习编排系统（覆盖资源分配、DAG调度和自动扩缩容），采用预注册预测方法，在生产相关扰动下比较性能退化，使用配对推断并控制族错误率。进行诊断分析以区分真实优势与人为现象。

Result: 大多数预测的性能反转并未发生。诊断显示结果常反映基准崩塌、构件局限或评估选择，而非学习控制器真能容忍扰动。一个看似的观测滞后优势约为Kubernetes HPA等效控制器的40倍。另一个广泛引用的结果无法从发布构件复现，最强的可复现优势远小于发表结果。结论在扰动幅度和评估模式改变时发生反转。

Conclusion: 问题本质是制度性而非纯技术性：发表与评审激励偏向于与便利基准比较获取收益，即使这些收益不能证明部署性能。需引入生产级基准、注册扰动模型、独立运营指标及奖励可复现运营证据的发表标准，否则文献增长将无法确立学习是否真正改进编排。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for service orchestration has been the subject of sustained research for over a decade, yet it is not used in production at scale. The usual explanation is that learned controllers degrade under delayed and noisy telemetry, workload shifts, and uncontrolled tenants. We test whether existing evidence supports that explanation. We evaluate three highly influential RL-based orchestration systems spanning resource allocation, DAG scheduling, and autoscaling, using pre-registered predictions about comparative degradation under production-relevant perturbations and paired inference with family-wise error correction. Across the tests, most predicted performance reversals do not occur. Diagnostic analyses show that these outcomes often reflect comparator collapse, artefact limitations, or evaluation choices rather than evidence that learned controllers tolerate the perturbations. One apparent advantage under observation lag is roughly fortyfold compared to a Kubernetes HPA-equivalent controller. Another widely cited result cannot be reconstructed from its released artefact, and the strongest reproducible margin is far smaller than the published results. Conclusions also reverse under changes in perturbation magnitude and evaluation mode. Based on these results and broader patterns in the literature, we identify an institutional problem. Publication and review incentives favour benchmark gains against convenient comparators, even when those gains provide little evidence of deployment performance. We argue that the problem is not solely technical. Rather, it is institutional, so learned orchestration needs production-grade comparators, registered perturbation models, separate operational metrics, and publication criteria that reward reproducible operational evidence. Without these changes, the literature can grow without establishing whether learning improves orchestration.

</details>


### [34] [DRIFT: Risk-Constrained Diffusion with Imitation Priors for Mixed-Autonomy Traffic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16589)
*Yaoshen Yu,Minghui Liwang,Wenbo Zhu,Xinlei Yi,Yiguang Hong,Yuhan Su,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRIFT框架，一种面向人机混驾交通场景的风险约束扩散生成方法，通过统一异质性条件编码、条件扩散轨迹生成和风险感知的长尾反馈机制，实现了在闭环执行管道中迭代生成、筛选和验证多样化可执行交通行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来智能交通系统将长期处于人机混驾范式，人工驾驶车辆（HVs）与自动驾驶车辆（AVs）共存带来了显著的异质性、放大的不确定性以及日益复杂的交互动态。在此背景下，同时捕捉由动态AV渗透率变化引起的异质性行为分布偏移、在强车辆耦合约束下生成多样化且可执行的轨迹，以及对罕见但高影响事件进行可靠的闭环安全性与稳定性诊断，仍是根本性挑战。

Method: 提出风险约束扩散与模仿先验（DRIFT）框架，该方法统一了三个核心组件：（1）异质性感知的条件编码；（2）基于条件扩散的可执行轨迹生成；（3）由风险感知长尾反馈增强的渐进式对抗对齐。该框架使交通行为能够在闭环执行管道中被迭代生成、过滤、选择和验证。此外，还开发了统一的评估协议，联合表征代表性交通场景和AV渗透率 regime下的安全性、效率和闭环稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，DRIFT在闭环混驾基准测试中实现了良好的安全-效率权衡，同时揭示了候选可执行性、在线选择和长尾反馈对可执行交通演化的重要影响。该方法能够有效生成多样化的可执行轨迹，并通过风险约束确保安全性。

Conclusion: DRIFT框架成功解决了人机混驾交通场景中的核心挑战，为未来智能交通系统的发展提供了有效的交通生成与评估方法。研究强调了可执行性约束、在线筛选机制和长尾反馈在闭环交通演化中的关键作用，为混驾环境下的安全性与效率平衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Future intelligent transportation systems are envisioned to evolve toward a long-term mixed-autonomy paradigm, where human-driven vehicles (HVs) and autonomous vehicles (AVs) coexist within highly coupled traffic ecosystems. Such coexistence introduces pronounced heterogeneity, amplified uncertainty, and increasingly intricate interaction dynamics. In this context, it remains fundamentally challenging to simultaneously capture the heterogeneous behavioral distribution shifts arising from dynamic AV penetration, generate diverse yet executable trajectories under strong inter-vehicle coupling, and conduct reliable closed-loop safety and stability diagnostics for rare but high-impact events. To this end, we present risk-constrained diffusion with imitation priors (DRIFT), a mixed-autonomy traffic generation framework which unifies heterogeneity-aware conditional encoding, conditional diffusion-based executable trajectory generation, and progressive adversarial alignment enhanced by risk-aware long-tail feedback, thereby enabling traffic behaviors to be iteratively generated, filtered, selected, and validated within a closed-loop execution pipeline. In addition, a unified evaluation protocol is developed to jointly characterize safety, efficiency, and closed-loop stability across representative traffic scenarios and AV penetration regimes. Experimental results demonstrate that DRIFT achieves a strong safety-efficiency trade-off in closed-loop mixed-autonomy benchmarks, while further revealing the critical influence of candidate executability, online selection, and long-tail feedback on executable traffic evolution.

</details>


### [35] [From the NYU Ultracomputer to Modern Exascale: A Historical and Architectural Survey of In-Network Computing and Scalable Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16819)
*Lars Warren Ericson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大规模并行系统四十年发展的历史与技术综述，深入分析了硬件架构、互连网络和同步原语的演进，涵盖了从早期共享内存系统到现代深度学习硬件的广泛内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性地梳理和分析过去四十年间大规模并行系统中硬件架构、互连网络和同步原语的发展历程，通过对比早期与现代设计，揭示技术演进规律和设计权衡。

Method: 采用历史案例分析和技术对比相结合的方法，包括：分析NYU Ultracomputer和IBM RP3的Fetch-and-Add硬件实现；对比IBM SP分布式内存架构与现代NVIDIA SHARP、HPE Slingshot网络计算模型；对MPI操作频率进行完整性能剖析；研究现代深度学习在异构硬件上的软件-硬件边界；评估基于Inmos Transputers的主动合并交换机可行性。

Result: 提供了MPI操作频率的完整性能剖析数据；详细阐述了一侧RMA原子操作到PCIe Atomics和GPU缓存的底层硬件映射；揭示了HIP翻译、Triton编译和W4A16量化在现代异构硅片上的执行机制；追溯了group lock原语在组互斥(GME)和房间同步中的历史影响。

Conclusion: 通过对早期细粒度共享内存硬件合并与现代分布式内存架构的对比，揭示了并行系统设计理念的演变，同时反映了美国系统工程与欧洲形式方法之间的历史和哲学分歧，为未来并行系统设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: This paper presents a historical and technical survey of the hardware architectures, interconnection networks, and synchronization primitives that have shaped massively parallel systems over the past four decades. We examine the design of the NYU Ultracomputer and the IBM Research Parallel Processor Prototype (RP3), focusing on the hardware implementation of the Fetch-and-Add primitive in multistage interconnection networks. We contrast these early attempts at fine-grained, shared-memory hardware combining with the distributed-memory architectures of the IBM SP series and the modern in-network computation models found in NVIDIA SHARP and HPE Slingshot.
  We provide a technical analysis of message-passing synchronization, presenting a complete profiling of MPI operation frequencies and detailing the low-level hardware mapping of one-sided RMA atomics to PCIe Atomics and GPU caches. We investigate the software-hardware boundary in modern deep learning, detailing how HIP translation, Triton compilation, and 4-bit quantization (W4A16) execute on modern heterogeneous silicon.
  To evaluate alternative network node designs, we present a historical hardware case study analyzing the feasibility of implementing active combining switches using message-passing Inmos Transputers programmed in Occam. Finally, we contextualize the evolution of concurrent software synchronization by examining Isaac Dimitrovsky's parallel "group lock" primitive, tracing its downstream echoes in group mutual exclusion (GME) and room synchronization, and reflect on the historical, philosophical divide between American systems engineering and European formal methods.

</details>


### [36] [Tangram: Hiding GPU Heterogeneity for Efficient LLM Parallelization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16907)
*Yanda Tao,Pedro F. Silvestre,Marcel Wagenländer,Peter Pietzuch*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Tangram是一个面向异构GPU集群的LLM训练并行化系统，通过将同构GPU组成“岛”并复用现有并行规划器，生成负载均衡的流水线并行计划，实现比现有异构并行器高达2.3倍的吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM训练集群规模扩大，集群中GPU类型和互联方式的异构性日益增强，现有自动并行规划器在异构环境下面临搜索空间爆炸问题，常被迫忽略专家并行或ZeRO等技术，导致规划质量下降。

Method: Tangram将并行规划与GPU异构性解耦，核心思路包括：(1) 利用批量采购形成的同构GPU集合作为“同构岛”，暴露给现有并行规划器；(2) 将模型分片与GPU岛匹配后，组合成负载均衡的流水线并行计划。通过狭窄API集成现有并行器，并对枚举计划进行剪枝以控制复杂度。

Result: 在异构GPU集群上，Tangram相比当前异构并行器（Metis和Sailor）实现了高达2.3倍的训练吞吐提升，并能扩展到大规模GPU集群。

Conclusion: Tangram有效解决了异构GPU集群中LLM并行规划搜索空间爆炸问题，在不牺牲并行化技术（如专家并行、ZeRO）的前提下，显著提升了训练性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: The scale of LLM training jobs requires parallelization planning over large GPU clusters. Due to different GPU types and interconnects added over time, these GPU clusters are increasingly heterogeneous. Automatic LLM parallelizers can search for parallelization plans but face an exploding search space with heterogeneous GPUs. To make search tractable in heterogeneous GPU clusters, parallelizers often omit types of parallelism (e.g., expert parallelism) or memory-saving techniques (e.g., ZeRO), which results in worse plans.
  We describe Tangram, a system that enables the use of existing heterogeneity-unaware LLM parallelizers in heterogeneous GPU clusters by decoupling parallelization planning from GPU heterogeneity. For this, Tangram exploits two insights: (1) since bulk purchases result in sets of GPUs with similar compute, memory, and connectivity, Tangram can expose such homogeneous GPU islands to existing parallelizers; and (2) parallelizers commonly first partition models and then parallelize partitions. Tangram can compose such model slices, assigned to GPU islands, into work-balanced pipelines for high throughput. Tangram integrates with existing parallelizers through a narrow API, which relies on the enumeration of model-slice/island pairs. Tangram achieves up to 2.3x higher training throughput than current heterogeneous parallelizers (Metis and Sailor) and scales to large GPU clusters by pruning enumerated plans.

</details>

### [37] [A Definition of Good Explanations and the Challenges Explaining LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14838)
*Louis Mahon,Elliot Ford,Callum Hackett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合反事实解释和对话者先验信念的解释定义，并探讨了其对AI可解释性的影响，特别是为何LLM难以生成优质解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的可解释性对其在诸多场景中的采用至关重要，但如何定义"好的解释"在哲学上长期争论且在AI领域重新受到关注；为构建有效的解释方法，需首先明确什么是优质解释。

Method: 受反事实解释（counterfactual explanations）概念启发，提出一种解释定义，该定义在传统反事实框架基础上引入了对话者对解释中可能涉及的各事实的先验信念。

Result: 推导并分析了该定义在AI可解释性领域的含义与约束，揭示了其如何限制或塑造解释生成过程，并指出大型语言模型（LLM）输出特别难以据此定义生成优质解释。

Conclusion: 将对话者先验信念纳入解释定义，为评估与生成AI系统解释提供了新视角；该框架下LLM解释性较弱，提示未来研究需关注信念因素与模型输出结构的适配问题。

Abstract: How to define a good explanation is a long-standing philosophical debate which has found recent renewed interest in the context of AI outputs. Explainability is crucial for AI adoption in many contexts, but in order to produce good explanations of AI systems, we must first have an understanding of what good explanations are. In this paper we propose a definition inspired by the notion of counterfactual explanations, however we argue that one must also take into account the interlocutor's prior beliefs in each fact that could be offered in an explanation. We explore the ramifications of this definition for AI explainability and, in particular, why LLM outputs are difficult to produce good explanations for.

</details>


### [38] [Dr-DCI: Scaling Direct Corpus Interaction via Dynamic Workspace Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14885)
*Yi Lu,Zhuofeng Li,Ping Nie,Haoxiang Zhang,Yuyu Zhang,Kai Zou,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-DCI框架，通过将检索器作为可调用动作来构建动态工作空间，在检索器的召回能力与DCI的精确操作之间取得平衡，有效解决了大规模语料库上代理搜索的可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理搜索系统依赖检索器（如BM25或ColBERT）提供的排序结果或受限文档视图，限制了代理重新组织材料和跨文档验证约束的能力。直接语料库交互（DCI）虽然提供了灵活的操作，但在大规模语料库上会变得缓慢且不稳定，导致性能和效率下降。

Method: DR-DCI框架将检索器视为代理可调用的动作，用于扩展本地工作空间。代理不再直接对整个语料库进行操作，而是动态地将相关文档拉入不断演进的工作空间，并在其中执行DCI操作。这种设计结合了检索器级别的召回能力与DCI风格的精确性：检索保持探索的可扩展性，而DCI保留了有效证据解析所需的本地操作能力。

Result: 在Browsecomp-Plus上，DR-DCI达到71.2%的准确率，比原始DCI和消融变体提升高达8.3个百分点，同时减少了工具使用、运行时间和预估成本。通过工作空间保留的上下文重置，准确率进一步提升至73.3%。在语料库规模实验中，DR-DCI在10万到1000万文档规模下保持有效，而原始DCI变得不稳定，BM25表现显著较差。在2000万规模的Wiki-18 QA设置中，DR-DCI在六个基准测试上平均得分为63.0，超越了基于检索和训练的搜索代理基线。

Conclusion: DR-DCI成功地将检索器的召回能力与DCI的精确操作相结合，实现了在大规模语料库上既有效又高效的代理搜索。消融分析进一步表明，排序预览和跨文档DCI操作是性能的关键因素。该框架为大规模语料库上的代理搜索提供了一个可扩展且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic search over large corpora relies on retriever-mediated interfaces (e.g., BM25 or ColBERT) for scalable candidate discovery. While effective at ranking relevant documents, these interfaces expose evidence only as ranked results or bounded document views, limiting agents' ability to reorganize material and verify constraints across documents. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) addresses this limitation by exposing shell-executable corpus operations for flexible search, filtering, comparison, and verification. However, full-corpus terminal commands become slow and unstable as the corpus grows, degrading performance and efficiency. We introduce DR-DCI, a retriever-steered DCI framework that treats retrieval as an agent-callable action for expanding a local workspace. Rather than operating directly over the full corpus, the agent dynamically pulls relevant documents into an evolving workspace and conducts DCI operations within it. This design combines retriever-level recall with DCI-style precision: retrieval keeps exploration scalable, while DCI preserves the local operations needed for effective evidence resolution. Experiments show that DR-DCI is both effective and efficient across scales. On Browsecomp-Plus, DR-DCI reaches 71.2\% accuracy, improving over raw DCI and ablated variants by up to 8.3 points while reducing tool usage, wall time, and estimated cost. With workspace-preserving context reset, accuracy further improves to 73.3\%. In corpus-scaling experiments, DR-DCI remains effective from 100K to 10M documents, whereas raw DCI becomes unstable and BM25 performs substantially worse. DR-DCI also scales to a 20M-scale file-per-document Wiki-18 QA setting, achieving an average score of 63.0 across six benchmarks and outperforming retrieval-based and trained search-agent baselines. Ablation analysis further shows that ranked previews and inter-document DCI are key to performance.

</details>


### [39] [Relational Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14892)
*Adiba Ejaz,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系结构因果模型，将结构因果模型扩展到对象和关系变化的场景，研究了在未见过的对象组合下因果查询的可识别性问题，并提出了关系神经因果模型方法，在模拟交通场景实验中优于非关系基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能需要具备因果性和组合性的环境模型，前者支持干预和反事实推理，后者支持对未见过的对象组合进行泛化，但目前缺乏对何时及如何学习这种模型的形式化研究。

Method: 提出关系结构因果模型，扩展结构因果模型至对象和关系变化的场景；定义关系因果图并推导符号识别准则；提出关系神经因果模型，以处理未观察混杂因素并实现识别。

Result: 在模拟交通场景（包含变化的车辆、信号和行人）实验中，关系神经因果模型方法在性能上超越了非关系基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的关系结构因果模型和关系神经因果模型为在对象和关系变化的环境中进行因果推理和泛化提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: An artificial intelligence must have a model of its environment that is causal, supporting reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, and also combinatorial, supporting generalization to unseen combinations of objects. In this work, we formally study when and how such a model can be learned. We develop relational structural causal models, extending structural causal models (Pearl 2009) to settings where objects and their relations vary. First, we show how answers to not only causal but also observational queries about unseen combinations of objects can not be identified without further assumptions. To enable such identification--including in the presence of unobserved confounding--we define relational causal graphs and derive symbolic identification criteria. Finally, we propose relational neural causal models, a provably correct approach that outperforms non-relational baselines on simulated traffic scenes with varying cars, signals, and pedestrians.

</details>


### [40] [Trust Between AI Agents: Measuring Formation, Breakage, and Recovery, with Implications for Governing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14923)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于有代价验证的行为测量框架，用于量化AI代理间的信任。研究发现前沿模型在与可靠队友合作时会减少60-85%的验证行为，而信任的建立快于恢复，且信任倾向直接影响多智能体系统的决策效率与收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型代理越来越多地以团队形式协作，每个代理需要评估对队友的信任程度，但目前缺乏衡量AI代理间信任的标准方法。为了解决这一问题，作者希望提出一种可观察、可量化的信任测量框架。

Method: 作者设计了一个合作生存游戏框架，基于“有代价验证”的行为测量方法。在该游戏中，检查队友的工作需要消耗资源，而信任错误答案可能导致致命后果。通过与无记忆版本模型的对比，减少验证的行为作为信任的可观察度量。研究考察了六个前沿模型快照在信任形成、破裂和恢复过程中的表现。

Result: 当与一致可靠的队友配对时，四个模型快照（Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.1和Gemini 3.1 Pro）减少了约60-85%的验证行为，而两个较小的模型快照几乎没有这种调整。失败会逆转这种信任折扣，但模型反应各异：部分模型集中审查过错方，其他模型则对整个团队更加谨慎。信任恢复慢于形成，集中发生的失败比分散的失败更能长期维持怀疑。能够形成信任的模型验证更少、决策更快、获得更高收益，而持续过度验证与优柔寡断而非安全性相关。

Conclusion: 研究表明信任倾向可以在部署前进行测量，对多智能体AI系统的治理而言，校准而非最大程度的怀疑应成为核心关切。

Abstract: As language-model agents increasingly work in teams, each agent must decide how much to trust its teammates. Yet we lack a standard way to measure trust between AI agents. We propose a behavioral measure based on costly verification. In a cooperative survival game, checking a teammate's work consumes resources, while trusting a wrong answer can be fatal. Relative to a memoryless version of the same model, reduced verification provides an observable measure of trust. Using this framework, we study trust formation, breakage, and recovery across six frontier model snapshots. When paired with a consistently reliable teammate, four snapshots (Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.1, and Gemini 3.1 Pro) reduce verification by roughly 60-85%, whereas two smaller snapshots show little or no such adjustment. Failures reverse this discount, but models differ in how they respond. Some concentrate renewed scrutiny on the culprit, while others become more cautious toward the entire team. Recovery is slower than formation, and clustered failures sustain suspicion far longer than the same number of failures spread apart. These differences have practical consequences. Models that form trust verify less, decide more quickly, and achieve higher payoffs in our environment. By contrast, persistent over-verification is associated with indecision rather than safety. Our results show that trust dispositions can be measured before deployment and suggest that calibration, rather than maximal suspicion, should be the central concern in the governance of multi-agent AI systems.

</details>

Abstract: Frontier reasoning-tuned language models still fail on deductive tasks at depth, and the cost of improved performance through extended internal reasoning scales poorly. We introduce PrologMCP, a task-agnostic, open-source server that exposes Prolog as a stateful tool through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). We evaluate a formalizer agent enhanced with PrologMCP against standard and reasoning LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4.1, and o4-mini) on two subsets of PARARULE-Plus: a general-purpose sample and a more challenging one targeting a specific failure mode of natural-language reasoning. On the general sample, the formalizer matches or exceeds reasoning LLMs (accuracy 1.00 vs.\ 1.00 / 0.998), with the largest gains over standard models (0.762 for GPT-4.1). On the challenging subset, the formalizer remains near-perfect (1.00 / 0.99) while reasoning LLMs drop to 0.95 / 0.94. These results suggest that delegating inference to Prolog via MCP is a robust and inspectable alternative to extended natural-language reasoning.

Abstract: Time series forecasting models often benefit from historical patterns. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent research explored retrieving relevant historical time series segments to enhance forecasting. However, relying solely on time series similarity is often insufficient for retrieval under non-stationarity. To address this, we propose a multimodal approach: a \textbf{S}emantics-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{R}etrieval-\textbf{A}ugmented Time Series \textbf{F}orecasting framework, SERAF. Unlike mainstream approaches that depend only on time series similarity, SERAF conducts dual retrieval over the time series and their self-generated textual descriptions. It retrieves two complementary sets of historical patterns and corresponding futures, which are selectively and jointly used to guide future predictions. Experiments across seven real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SERAF in bridging numerical and semantic views of time series compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架来识别深度神经网络中的“AI记忆印迹”（AI engrams），实现了对神经网络学习记忆的精准定位和手术式编辑（组合或擦除），无需迭代优化。

Method: 作者引入了一个几何框架，将神经科学中的特异性（specificity）、再激活（reactivation）、充分性（sufficiency）和必要性（necessity）四个标准形式化为一个约束逆问题，推导出一个闭式估计器，能够从全局纠缠的参数中分离出单个记忆痕迹，并证明该生物学导出的解对应于参数流形上的自然梯度更新。

Abstract: Memory formation is fundamental to intelligence, yet whether deep neural networks preserve identifiable memory traces analogous to biological memory units remains an open question. This work introduces a geometric framework to identify such "AI engrams" by formalizing the neuroscientific criteria of specificity, reactivation, sufficiency, and necessity into a constrained inverse problem. We derive a closed-form estimator that isolates individual memory traces from globally entangled parameters, and show that this biologically-derived solution corresponds to a natural gradient update on the parameter manifold. AI engrams enable surgical manipulation of learned knowledge: any subset of memories can be composed or erased through linear arithmetic, without iterative optimization. Experiments ranging from simple MLPs to LLMs demonstrate the causal validity and substantial scalability of AI engrams. Together, these results bridge theories of biological memory and artificial representation learning and offer geometric insight into how deep networks simultaneously support functional specificity within distributed storage.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: LLM judges are used to reduce the need for costly human labor in evaluating open-ended text generation. However, the reliability of these judges depends critically on their alignment with human raters -- a property that itself depends on costly human annotations. In this work, we develop a method (Metric Match) for estimating correlation-based reliability metrics of LLM judges from limited annotations. Metric Match selects a subset of samples for human annotation such that the subset matches the population reliability metric with respect to acquired synthetic labels. We empirically show that Metric Match achieves a win-rate of 0.838 against random subset selection across four different correlation metrics and 15 datasets, with an 18.7% decrease in average estimation error and reduces annotation needs by 32.5%. We provide a cost model and highlight a medical case study where our method saves $1,041.67 compared to random selection for expert annotation. Further, we shift our task from reliability estimation to reliability classification of whether a given judge is above a deployment threshold, outperforming random selection with Metric Match. All project code is publicly available, and we additionally provide an installable package for ease of use.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 双粒度设计能更精确诊断模型识别不安全动作的能力，并评估其作为护栏部署时提升全任务安全的实际效果。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated by whether they complete realistic desktop and web tasks. However, task success alone can miss failures in which an agent reaches the nominal goal through an unsafe shortcut. We introduce OSGuard, a dual-granularity benchmark suite for evaluating safety in computer-use agents under benign, unchanged user instructions. OSGuard contains an action-level benchmark for local guardrail decisions and a risk-augmented execution suite for end-to-end evaluation. The action-level benchmark consists of contextualized proposed actions labeled as allowed, unrelated, or unsafe, each judged relative to the original instruction and current interface state. The execution suite contains manually constructed OSWorld-derived task variants in which the original task remains achievable, but the environment is modified to introduce latent hazards such as destructive overwrites, etc. Each variant is paired with augmented evaluators that retain the original task-success criterion while adding explicit state-based safety invariants, allowing us to distinguish safe completions from unsafe completions that satisfy the nominal task objective. Our experimental results on OSGuard show that current multimodal guardrails can perform well on isolated action judgments, while risk-augmented execution exposes remaining gaps between local oversight and reliable end-to-end safety. This dual-granularity design enables more precise diagnosis of whether models can both recognize unsafe proposed actions and improve full-task safety when deployed as guardrails.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了"合成反向适应"概念，描述人类与AI系统通过相互适应策略和行为而共同进化的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在多智能体环境中的广泛应用，人类与AI之间存在复杂的相互影响和适应关系。作者旨在建立一个理论框架来理解这种递归、共同进化的交互动态。

Method: 通过分析多个领域的案例，包括围棋博弈、混合动机社会互动以及地缘政治模拟，展示AI系统发展新策略或社会协议后，人类如何从中提取洞见并调整自身行为。

Result: 展示了合成反向适应如何在多智能体环境中产生新的智能体交互动态，证明了人类与AI系统之间存在递归的共同进化关系。

Conclusion: 合成反向适应为理解人机交互的复杂性和共同进化本质提供了有效的理论框架，有助于深入认识多智能体环境中的人类-AI协同演化机制。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the concept of synthetic counteradaptation, a process where human and AI systems co-evolve by adapting to each other's strategies and behaviors. Synthetic counteradaptation occurs when AI systems develop novel strategies or social protocols, prompting humans to extract insights and adapt their own behaviors in response, leading to the emergence of new agent interaction dynamics. To illustrate these dynamics, we analyze examples from various contexts, including the game of Go, mixed-motive social interactions, and geopolitical simulations. By exploring these cases, we demonstrate how synthetic counteradaptation provides a framework for understanding the recursive and co-evolutionary nature of human-AI interactions in multi-agent environments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多模态数据的时间-事件预测面临两大核心挑战：模态不平衡和分布偏移。现有方法在处理CT影像与电子健康记录(EHR)数据融合时，缺乏对模态不平衡情况下融合行为的系统性分析，且跨机构和跨任务泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出基于基础模型的跨模态表征对齐框架：(1) 使用领域特定基础模型独立编码CT和EHR两种模态；(2) 在共享潜在空间中通过四种融合策略进行对齐：后期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力和共注意力机制；(3) 在大规模多机构队列上进行评估(PE: 训练集3,099例、内部验证1,098例、外部验证435例；CVD: 训练集2,951例、内部验证837例、外部验证682例)。

Result: (1) 当模态贡献相当时，融合策略一致性地将C-index提升1.5-5.4%；(2) 对比式多模态融合(特别是CLMBR表征)在PE死亡率预测中提供最一致且统计显著的改进；(3) 对于MACE预测，交叉注意力(one-hot编码)在内部验证中表现最佳，图像引导的共注意力在外部验证中表现最优。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统分析了模态不平衡条件下TTE预测中的融合行为，建立了任务感知的多模态对齐作为稳健泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则，为跨机构、跨任务的临床预测模型开发提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [48] [Minimal Oversight: Uncertainty-Aware Governance for Delegated AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15563)
*Carlos R. B. Azevedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了最小充分监督原则（MSO），这是一个用于AI系统自主权委托的变分原理，在Fisher信息流形上最小化治理负担，为不确定性感知的AI治理提供了可计算的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益将决策委托给专门的模型、评估器、工具和监督控制器，核心问题不再仅仅是模型准确性，而是不确定性感知的治理：包括应授予多少自主权、哪些证据应校准信任、委托AI系统能维持怎样的性能上限以及何时需要人工干预。

Method: 提出了最小充分监督原则（MSO），在Fisher信息流形上最小化治理负担并满足交付约束；基于揭示行动的治理委托信道模型，证明了平稳逐符号审查策略的容量定理；推导了工作流复杂性与质量退化关系的局部一阶近似，以及干预时机与有效容量、复杂性和漂移相关的漂移主导自主时间标度律。

Result: Euler-Lagrange解产生了一种在任务空间上的注水式治理委托分配；发现了掩蔽现象作为一种结构性AI治理病理：纠正后的性能可能隐藏校准信任所需的能力信号；合成仿真和半真实重构工作流验证了上游优先纠正、基于敏感性的干预以及自主权扩展前的显式可行性检查等设计建议。

Conclusion: 建立了一个用于委托AI系统中不确定性、规划和监督的可计算框架，并提供了配套的Python软件包，为AI系统的自主权委托和治理提供了理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly delegate decisions to specialized models, evaluators, tools, and supervisory controllers. The central AI problem is no longer only model accuracy, but uncertainty-aware governance: how much autonomy to grant, which evidence should calibrate trust, what performance ceiling a delegated AI system can sustain, and when human intervention becomes necessary. We propose the Minimum Sufficient Oversight Principle (MSO), a variational principle for principled autonomy delegation: minimize governance burden on the Fisher information manifold subject to a delivery constraint. The resulting Euler-Lagrange solution yields a water-filling allocation of governed delegation across the task space. Building on a revealed-action governed delegation channel model, we prove a capacity theorem for stationary symbolwise review policies, derive a local first-order approximation relating workflow complexity to quality degradation, and give a drift-dominated autonomy-time scaling law linking intervention timing to effective capacity, complexity, and drift. Within this framework, masking appears as a structural AI-governance pathology: corrected performance can hide the competence signal needed to calibrate trust. Synthetic simulations and a semi-real reconstructed workflow support design prescriptions including upstream-first correction, sensitivity-based intervention, and explicit feasibility checks before autonomy is expanded. The result is a computable framework for uncertainty, planning, and oversight in delegated AI systems. A companion Python package is available at https://github.com/crbazevedo/delegation-lab.

</details>

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven framework for retrieving remote sensing data from cloud-based geospatial catalogues using natural language queries. The system converts user intent into structured API calls, enabling efficient access to satellite imagery and environmental datasets. The architecture integrates three agents: Guardrail for safety and policy enforcement, General-QA for intent interpretation, and Recommender-Analyst for schema-aware API call generation. This coordinated design ensures reliable, semantically aligned interaction with external data services. The modular framework is portable across platforms through API schema substitution and supports applications in environmental monitoring, disaster response, and climate analysis. It establishes a scalable interface between user intent and geospatial infrastructure, enabling streamlined and automated Earth observation workflows. Preliminary experiments under adversarial multi-turn settings show that prompt-level safety instructions improve robustness, although rare high-impact failures persist in API manipulation scenarios and highlight the need for adaptive, system-level defenses that balance safety, usability, and cost efficiency, which motivates the use of our intercept-level Guardrail agent.

*Sidi Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Base Sequence Analysis框架，将LLM智能体的运行行为编码为XEPV四字母序列，发现高风险模式P-X-P和验证缺失问题，设计的Governor系统使任务成功率提升6.2%且token消耗降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体研究缺乏对其运行时行为的系统性分析方法，无法有效识别失败模式并进行干预优化。受基因组序列分析启发，作者提出将智能体的细粒度运行行为编码为紧凑的符号序列，以实现模式挖掘、统计分析和预测建模。

Method: 提出Base Sequence Analysis框架，使用四字母字母表（X探索、E执行、P规划、V验证）编码智能体运行时行为；收集生产环境ReAct智能体8天内347条真实执行轨迹，应用n-gram模式挖掘、马尔可夫转移矩阵和点二列相关分析；设计三层运行时干预系统Governor（规则引擎+统计累加器+卡方阈值适配器）；在SWE-bench的2000条SWE-agent轨迹上进行跨系统验证。

Result: 发现三个关键结果：（1）三元组P-X-P是唯一统计显著的高风险模式，降低成功率10.4%；（2）P-ratio是成功率最强的负向预测因子（r=-0.256，p<0.0001）；（3）E→V转移概率仅2.1%，表明系统性验证缺失。Governor系统部署后实现任务成功率绝对提升6.2%，同时平均token消耗减少44%。跨系统验证证实探索螺旋和E→V验证缺失在独立系统中复现。

Conclusion: Base Sequence Analysis框架成功揭示了LLM智能体的行为模式和失败根源，Governor干预系统证明了基于序列分析的优化方法有效性。该方法具有良好的跨系统泛化能力，为智能体行为理解、优化和跨智能体行为指纹识别等六个研究方向奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose Base Sequence Analysis, a framework that encodes the runtime behavior of LLM-powered autonomous agents into compact symbolic sequences using a four-letter alphabet: X (Explore), E (Execute), P (Plan), and V (Verify). Drawing an analogy to genomic sequence analysis, we apply n-gram pattern mining, Markov transition matrices, and point-biserial correlation to 347 real-world execution traces collected from a production ReAct agent system over 8 days. Our analysis reveals that (1) the trigram P-X-P is the only statistically significant high-risk pattern, lowering success rate by 10.4%; (2) P-ratio is the strongest negative predictor of success (r=-0.256, p<0.0001); and (3) the E->V transition probability is only 2.1%, indicating a systemic verification deficit. Based on these findings, we design Governor, a three-layer runtime intervention system comprising a rule engine, a statistical accumulator, and a chi-square-based threshold adaptor. In a natural before/after deployment evaluation (N=101 vs. N=246), Governor achieves a +6.2% absolute increase in task success rate while simultaneously reducing average token consumption by 44%. To validate cross-system generality, we apply the XEPV encoding to 2,000 public SWE-agent trajectories on SWE-bench, confirming that exploration spirals and the E->V verification deficit replicate in an independent system. We outline six research directions including base sequence language models, cross-agent behavioral fingerprinting, and reward shaping, and release an open-source toolkit for reproducibility.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的广泛应用，越来越多的个体将AI作为认知替代品而非补充工具，这可能导致未经验证的推理义务累积，形成"认知债务"。作者旨在建立正式理论框架来分析这种现象的系统性风险和社会后果。

Method: 构建包含两个状态变量（认知资本和认知债务）的理论模型，采用乘性生产技术，其中认知资本作为抵押品决定AI采用的回报。通过六个命题推导，分析理性代理人的决策行为、系统性风险累积机制以及分散均衡与社会最优的偏离。

Result: 理性代理人会承担正向认知债务；平静期会降低主观风险评估、提高AI替代强度并复合杠杆，导致"认知明斯基时刻"——主观风险下降而真实系统脆弱性上升；预期危机损失与总杠杆呈凸关系；危机后可能出现用更多AI修补AI失败的"虚假修正循环"；分散均衡下AI替代采用过度；高认知资本代理人更密集采用AI，可能最终侵蚀其非辅助认知资本至低于初始低技能代理人。

Conclusion: 认知债务理论揭示了AI替代认知的隐性成本和系统性风险；分散均衡因系统性风险、认知公共物品和军备竞赛外部性而导致AI过度采用；需要考虑政策干预以实现社会最优。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [52] [Agentic Retrieval and Reinforcement Learned Equation Chains: A Controlled Generation Framework for Complex and Novel Physics Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15591)
*Tirthankar Mittra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ARVRE框架，通过强化学习构建有效物理方程链并指导LLM生成高质量物理应用题，解决了现有方法生成题目存在歧义、不可解或过于简单的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理应用题生成方法多改编自数学应用题生成，常产生歧义、不可解或结构简单且语言多样性有限的问题，高质量、新颖、复杂且可解的物理应用题生成仍是一个挑战性且研究不足的领域。

Method: 提出ARVRE两阶段框架：第一阶段使用离线时序差分学习构建有效的物理方程链，结合智能体检索增强生成(RAG)框架动态选择特定主题的概念和词汇；第二阶段利用大语言模型将方程链和检索到的概念转换为自然语言物理问题。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了结合强化学习、检索技术和大语言模型在教育物理内容可靠生成方面的潜力，为教育内容生成提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Generating high-quality Physics Word Problems (PWPs) that are novel, complex, and solvable remains a challenging and underexplored problem in educational content generation. Existing approaches, many adapted from Math Word Problem (MWP) generation, often produce ambiguous, unsolvable, or structurally simple questions with limited linguistic diversity. We introduce ARVRE (Agentic Retrieval Value Reinforced Equation-chain), a two-stage framework for generating diverse and mathematically valid PWPs. In the first stage, a form of offline temporal-difference learning is used to construct valid chains of physics equations, while an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework dynamically selects topic-specific concepts and vocabulary. This design enables explicit control over problem structure and difficulty. In the second stage, a Large Language Model (LLM) converts the equation chain and retrieved concepts into a natural-language physics question. By grounding generation in valid equation chains, our method preserves mathematical correctness while promoting linguistic diversity and contextual richness. Human and automated evaluations demonstrate that ARVRE generates PWPs that are more complex, novel, and solvable than those produced by existing approaches. These results highlight the potential of combining reinforcement learning, retrieval, and LLMs for reliable generation of educational physics content.

</details>


### [53] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文应用可验证的增长物理变压器与递归自改进系统（VGPT-RSI）研究黎曼猜想，成功生成两个经过形式化验证的有限证书，并明确识别出阻碍完整证明的数学瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼猜想仍是数学界未解决的核心难题之一。作者不直接宣称证明，而是探索可验证的AI辅助推理系统能否生成可靠的、经形式化检验的部分进展，同时显式识别剩余的数学障碍。

Method: 应用VGPT-RSI系统处理两个与黎曼猜想相关的认证任务：（1）构建并验证参数化安全下界曲线上的有限RH边界证书，使用向外舍入区间算术和Arb/FLINT球算术进行审计，并在Rocq/CoqInterval中检验参数化定理；（2）启动形式化Lagarias路径证书，将有限量形式化并生成Coq检验的有限证书。

Result: 成功生成两个经形式化验证的有限证书：一是参数化区域上的RH边界证书，二是基于Lagarias准则的有限证书。系统精确识别出未解决的数学瓶颈：Lagarias等价性的形式化、超越有限截断的全局尾部定理证明，以及将反例归约至极大丰富数或相关极值整数。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI系统能够生成经认证的RH相关形式化进展，有效组织证明依赖关系，并在剩余障碍确属数学本质时避免过度宣称。该方法为形式化数学研究提供了可验证的AI辅助推理框架。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [54] [AI-Driven Framework for Adaptive Water Network Management with Proof-of-Concept Implementation: Addressing Non-Revenue Water in Jordan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15709)
*Mohammed Fasha,Nahel Al-Maayta,Bilal Sowan,Mohammad Athamneh,Husam Barham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成EPANET水力建模、数字孪生、SCADA系统和LLM智能体的智能框架，用于持续监测配水网络并降低非营收水（NRW）。在安曼1164节点网络上验证了系统可行性，能在2分钟内完成异常检测并零API成本生成健康报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约旦面临严重的水资源短缺问题，50%的水因泄漏、盗窃和计量问题（即非营收水NRW）而流失，传统被动式方法无法持续有效地减少NRW，亟需一种主动、智能的水网络管理方案。

Method: 整合EPANET水力建模、数字孪生技术、SCADA系统与大语言模型（LLM）智能体，结合实时数据流与基于物理的仿真进行异常检测；采用检索增强生成（RAG）进行政策解读，利用函数调用实现网络控制；通过EPYT和离线LLM（Ollama运行的llama3.1:8b）在安曼1164节点区域网络上进行概念验证。

Result: 成功实现自动化水力仿真和基于流量的异常检测，与分区计量（DZ）实践一致；AI生成的健康报告响应时间低于2分钟，API成本为零；30.1 L/s模拟泄漏在15根管道中产生可测流量重分布，精确定位至15节点簇，验证了爆管检测能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过分阶段实施适应约旦的间歇供水模式和有限自动化水平，为水资源匮乏地区提供了一条可扩展的智能自动化路径，可有效降低NRW并提升运营效率。

Abstract: Jordan faces severe water scarcity with 50\% of water produced is lost to leakage, theft and metering issues also known as non-revenue water (NRW). Traditional reactive approaches have proven insufficient for sustained NRW reduction. This paper proposes an intelligent framework integrating EPANET hydraulic modeling, digital twin technology, SCADA systems, and large language model (LLM)-based AI agents for continuous network monitoring and adaptive decision-making. The system combines real-time data streams with physics-based simulation to detect anomalies, employing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for policy interpretation and function calling for network control. A proof-of-concept implementation validates technical feasibility using EPYT with offline LLMs (llama3.1:8b via Ollama) on a 1,164-junction Amman district network. The system demonstrates automated hydraulic simulation, flow-based anomaly detection aligned with water distribution zone (DZ) practice, and AI-generated health reports with response times under 2 minutes and zero API costs. Burst detection relies on local flow anomaly analysis: a 30.1~L/s simulated leak produces measurable flow redistribution in 15 pipes, flagging a 15-junction cluster that localises the burst -- confirming alignment with water distribution zone (DZ) monitoring practice. The framework accommodates Jordan's intermittent supply patterns and limited automation through phased implementation, offering a scalable pathway for water-scarce regions to leverage intelligent automation for NRW reduction and operational efficiency.

</details>


### [55] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IRTS-ToolBench，这是首个针对不规则时间序列的问答基准，包含跨13个领域、10种任务类型的1700个问题，填补了现有TSQA基准假设规则采样的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列数据具有高度不规则性（观测异步、缺失值具有信息性、采样频率多变），但现有的时间序列问答（TSQA）基准大多假设输入为规则采样，导致LLM和AI代理在不规则条件下的性能评估存在根本性空白。

Method: 构建了IRTS-ToolBench基准数据集，包含1700个问题，覆盖13个领域和10种任务类型；提供了标准化输入和可复现的评估协议，使研究者能够独立评估LLM在不规则时间序列分析中的表现。

Result: 成功创建了首个针对不规则时间序列问答的综合性基准测试，并开源了代码（https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench），为LLM-based不规则时间序列分析研究提供了标准化的评估工具。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench填补了不规则时间序列问答评估的关键空白，为研究社区提供了独立可用的标准化基准，推动了对LLM在真实复杂时间序列场景下能力的理解和改进。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [56] [CONCORD: Asynchronous Sparse Aggregation for Device-Cloud RAG under Document Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15179)
*Xuedong Hu,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONCORD框架，通过异步稀疏聚合解决文档隔离双端RAG中的同步延迟问题，在保持性能的同时将吞吐量提升1.66-2.15倍，通信量降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在设备-云协同推理场景下，隐私和政策约束导致私有文档留在设备端、公共知识位于云端，形成文档隔离的双端RAG设置。现有方法依赖频繁的远程同步和密集证据传输，在现实延迟和带宽条件下严重限制了系统吞吐量。

Method: 提出异步稀疏聚合框架CONCORD，将云端视为异步到达的证据源而非持续同步的协同生成器。引入等待债务控制机制，根据观察到的等待回报决定每步解码是否继续等待远程参与；设计证书引导的最小补充机制，仅请求确定当前贪心决策所需的远程证据。部分步骤查询云端以保持与密集双端聚合相同的贪心token，其余步骤则基于本地证据完成。

Result: 在Natural Questions和WikiText-2数据集上的实验表明，CONCORD相比基线方法分别将端到端吞吐量提升1.66倍和2.15倍，每token通信量降低超过两个数量级，同时保持可比的答案质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: CONCORD成功解决了文档隔离双端RAG设置中的同步延迟和通信开销问题，通过异步机制在保证生成质量的前提下显著提升了系统效率，为隐私敏感场景下的知识增强生成提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [57] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogGuard框架，通过解耦离线LLM画像构建和在线SLM预测，并采用前缀对齐KV缓存复用和长度感知分布式微调策略，实现了边缘智能服务的高效主动预警，在教育和运营场景中显著降低了时间开销并提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能服务的主动预警需要预测任务完成情况，依赖于长期静态属性和短期动态状态。现有方法面临两大挑战：(1)画像方法通常特定于领域，缺乏跨服务场景的可复用抽象；(2)在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型时，由于输入序列长度差异导致高同步开销。

Method: 提出CogGuard框架，通过共享的静态-动态画像到评分流水线，将离线LLM画像构建与在线SLM评分预测解耦。设计场景特定的画像方法与前缀对齐KV缓存复用以减少重复编码开销；提出带对比正则化的长度感知分布式微调策略以缓解异构集群的工作负载不平衡。

Result: 在教育和运营数据集上的实验表明，CogGuard将画像构建时间减少高达48%，分布式微调时间减少19%，在100分制预警任务上MAE分别为13.4和5.9。在最大规模教育场景中，与最强基线相比预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard框架成功解决了边缘智能服务主动预警中的画像复用和异构集群微调效率问题，通过LLM与SLM的协同工作实现了高效准确的预警服务，具有良好的跨场景适用性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [58] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对定向广告中的隐私风险，提出了一种基于噪声预言机模型的属性推断攻击框架。通过可复现的基准测试发现，在160次广告活动后，攻击者AUC可达0.64-0.65，而聚合报告是最有效的隐私保护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向广告系统可以将广告商选择的受众与展示可见用户行为的广告单元配对。当交互与引发该交互的营销活动保持关联时，广告商可能获得与用户相关的观察数据，而不仅仅是聚合报告。这种机制可能泄露用户敏感属性信息，但目前缺乏对其隐私风险的系统建模和量化评估。

Method: 1. 将定向广告渠道建模为属性推断的噪声预言机，分离目标定位谓词、曝光、交互和披露四个边界，捕获资格与投放之间、交互与广告商可见性之间的差距；
2. 构建可复现的基准测试：使用公共数据校准的合成人群，每个包含已知敏感标签，生成营销活动语义层提供主题变体和响应先验；
3. 模拟器生成基本事实、事件轨迹、披露观察和度量指标；
4. 在通用营销活动和披露定义下，比较贝叶斯、监督学习、正无标签(PU)和自适应攻击方法。

Result: 在四种主题变体、七个模拟器种子和两种交互设置的最终评估中：重复的具有身份暴露的营销活动产生可测量但有限的推断信号；在160次营销活动时，贝叶斯和监督学习攻击在主要设置中达到约0.64 AUC，在更高交互设置中达到约0.65 AUC。聚合报告消除了与用户相关的预言机输入，是最强的控制措施；类型过滤和随机化披露可减少释放的信号。

Conclusion: 本文建立了交互式定向广告隐私保护的模型、制品和防御评估方法。研究结果表明，身份暴露式的广告交互会带来可测量的隐私风险，而披露策略是最有效的控制手段，特别是聚合报告能显著降低隐私泄露风险。代码已开源以供进一步研究使用。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [59] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Visual-Seeker，一个视觉原生的多模态深度搜索智能体。通过主动视觉推理和细粒度视觉细节关注，结合5K高质量多模态轨迹数据训练，在五个多模态搜索基准上达到最先进性能，甚至超越部分闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在复杂开放场景中存在事实基础不足的问题。当前多模态深度搜索智能体主要依赖语义明确的简单图像和纯文本证据轨迹，缺乏多跳跨模态推理和搜索能力，视觉原生搜索范式仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker智能体，将视觉视为动态输入而非静态输入。设计主动视觉推理数据管道，主动关注细粒度视觉细节，在搜索过程中动态收集视觉证据。合成5K高质量多模态轨迹用于模型训练，解锁视觉原生潜力。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上实现了最先进的性能表现，超越了多个闭源模型，验证了在真实网络环境中稳健的视觉原生推理和搜索能力。

Conclusion: Visual-Seeker成功实现了视觉原生的多模态深度搜索，通过主动视觉推理机制有效提升了模型在复杂场景下的事实基础能力和跨模态推理性能，为多模态搜索智能体的发展提供了新的方向。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


### [60] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mask-Proof框架，将真实数学证明转化为可自动评估的掩码步骤任务，构建了包含292个跨领域问题的Mask-ProofBench基准，实现了对数学证明中步骤级推理能力的可扩展、可复现评估，评估器与专家标注的一致性达96.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学问题求解和研究级证明方面能力日益增强，但缺乏可扩展且可复现的方法来测量长证明中的步骤级推理能力。现有评估方法要么强调最终答案，要么依赖昂贵的专家人工评分，而端到端证明生成仍难以自动验证，这限制了对AI辅助证明的科学进展的信任。

Method: 提出Mask-Proof流水线：(1)将真实证明转化为自动可检查的掩码步骤任务；(2)掩码关键公式步骤，提供必要上下文；(3)使用基于LLM的等价性判断器，通过重复投票机制确保稳定性，评估模型对掩码步骤的重建能力。构建了包含292个跨领域研究问题的Mask-ProofBench基准数据集。

Result: 在17个模型上的实验表明：(1)推理增强型模型相比标准模型性能提升12%-27%；(2)评估器与专家标注者的一致性达到96.8%，实现了对步骤级数学推理的忠实、可复现和可比较的测量。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof提供了一种可扩展、可复现的步骤级数学推理评估方法，填补了证明级AI辅助评估的空白，为数学证明领域的AI辅助科学研究提供了可靠的评估基准，有助于推动该领域的科学进展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [61] [Feature Attribution in Directed Acyclic Graphs Using Edge Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15273)
*Qiheng Sun,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Haocheng Xia,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAG-SHAP方法，通过基于边的干预来解决现有Shapley值特征归因方法在复杂特征交互和因果关系场景下的局限性，能够同时捕获特征的外部性和外生影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在涉及复杂特征交互和因果关系的场景中面临挑战，即使提供了因果结构。这些方法通常采用以节点为中心的视角，仅将重要性归因于单个特征，因此往往无法同时捕获特征的外部性和外生影响，导致不合理的解释。

Method: 提出了一种名为DAG-SHAP的新型特征归因方法，该方法基于边干预。DAG-SHAP将每个特征边视为单独的归因对象，确保能够适当地捕获特征的外部性和外生贡献。此外，还引入了一种近似方法来高效计算DAG-SHAP。

Result: 在真实数据集和合成数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了DAG-SHAP的有效性。

Conclusion: DAG-SHAP通过将归因对象从节点转向边，成功解决了现有方法无法同时捕获特征外部性和外生影响的问题，为复杂因果结构下的特征归因提供了更合理的解释方法。

Abstract: Shapley value-based feature attribution methods face challenges in scenarios involving complex feature interactions and causal relationships, even when a causal structure is provided. Existing methods typically adopt a node-centric view, attributing importance solely to individual features. Consequently, they often fail to simultaneously capture the externality and exogenous influence of features, leading to unreasonable interpretations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel feature attribution method called DAG-SHAP, which is based on edge intervention. DAG-SHAP treats each feature edge as an individual attribution object, ensuring that both externality and exogenous contributions of features are appropriately captured. Additionally, we introduce an approximation method for efficiently computing DAG-SHAP. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets validate the effectiveness of DAG-SHAP. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DAG-SHAP.

</details>


### [62] [A Formal Framework for Declarative Agentic AI in Business Process Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15291)
*Mohammad Azarijafari,Luisa Mich,Michele Missikoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGO方法论，为智能体业务流程分析建立了基于集合论和数理逻辑的形式化框架，支持自动生成BP工作流并保证推导路径的可靠性与完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI为业务流程自动化带来新机遇，可实现自主决策与动态适应，但要实现这一潜力，需要对业务流程实体及其交互进行形式化精确定义。

Method: 提出AGO方法论，从"谁在行动"（智能体）、"为何行动"（目标）、"相关实体是什么"（对象）三个建模视角出发，基于集合论和数理逻辑形式化定义AGO实体类型及其交互，将所有定义组织到业务流程知识库（BPKB）中。

Result: 构建的BPKB支持结构化查询、增量更新以及业务流程工作流的自动生成，同时确保推导路径的可靠性与完备性。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架为智能体业务流程的实现提供了坚实的理论基础，使自动化决策与动态适应成为可能，并通过知识库的组织方式保证了系统的可维护性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Agentic AI opens new opportunities for automating Business Process (BP), enabling autonomous decision-making and dynamic adaptation. However, realising this potential requires BP entities and their interactions to be defined with formal precision. This paper presents a formal framework for Agentic BP analysis through the AGO methodology. AGO captures the modelling perspective in terms of who is acting (Agents), why it is carried out (Goals), and what the relevant entities are (Objects). Grounded in set theory and mathematical logic, we formally define the AGO entity types and their interactions, organising all definitions into a BP Knowledge Base (BPKB). The resulting BPKB supports structured querying, incremental updates, and automatic generation of BP workflows, while ensuring soundness and completeness of the derived paths.

</details>


### [63] [CODA-BENCH: Can Code Agents Handle Data-Intensive Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15300)
*Yuxin Zhang,Ju Fan,Meihao Fan,Shaolei Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CODA-BENCH，这是首个在数据密集型环境中联合评估代码和数据智能的基准测试，包含1,009个任务，揭示了先进代理在数据密集型任务中的成功率仅为61.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试通常将代码能力与数据处理能力分开评估，无法反映真实开发场景中代码与大规模数据（文件系统）紧密结合的复杂性。先进代理正逐渐具备自主工程师的潜力，因此亟需能够同时考察代码生成与数据发现能力的综合性评估基准。

Method: 基于Kaggle生态系统构建数据密集型Linux沙箱环境，包含数百个数据集。代理需在平均包含980个文件的复杂文件层次结构中主动探索、识别相关资源，并生成代码完成数据驱动的分析任务。基准涵盖31个社区的1,009个任务，模拟真实数据规模与噪声。

Result: 对先进代理的评估显示，即使是最优系统在有效整合数据发现与代码执行方面仍存在显著困难，整体成功率仅为61.1%。这一结果揭示了当前代理在数据密集型任务能力上的明显不足。

Conclusion: CODA-BENCH填补了代码与数据智能联合评估的空白，评估结果突显了当前代理在数据密集型场景中的能力差距，为未来研究指明了重要的改进方向。
