We showcase the SNA as a "solve once, query anywhere" physical world model via two engineering case studies: a 7D parametric manufacturing simulation and an experimental thermal-to-property inversion pipeline for Inconel 718. The VSNA executes a 1,000,000-query Monte Carlo sweep in 102s on a standard laptop CPU, yielding a 150,000x speedup over a full-grid finite element baseline hosted on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. It further enables real-time generative inverse-mode reconstructions under 100ms. These results demonstrate that the SNA serves as a compact mathematical substrate for continuous parameter manifolds to enable real-time inversion, optimization loops, and rapid uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [166] [Remember, Don't Re-read: Stateful ReAct Agents for Token-Efficient Autonomous Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14945)
*Faramarz Jabbarvaziri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将无状态的自研究模式重构为有状态的ReAct智能体，通过LangGraph实现持久化状态管理，在超参数调优和代码优化任务上分别减少了90%和52%的token消耗，同时保持优化质量不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自研究模式的无状态设计在每次迭代时需从头重建实验上下文，导致每次迭代产生O(n)的token成本，总计O(n²)的高昂开销。这种重复读取完整历史记录的方式造成资源浪费，亟需一种更高效的状态管理机制来降低token消耗。

Method: 采用LangGraph框架将自研究模式重构为有状态的ReAct智能体，通过类型化持久状态跨迭代传递实验历史，利用工具调用接口管理状态更新。有状态智能体在固定大小的对话窗口内运行，每次迭代仅需O(1)成本，而非无状态方式的O(n)成本。

Result: 在超参数调优任务（15次迭代，单次观察数据小）上，有状态智能体token消耗减少90%（2,492 vs 24,465）。在代码性能优化任务（40次迭代，单次观察数据大，包含完整源代码和基准测试结果）上，token消耗减少52%（627K vs 1,275K）。两个任务均达到可比的优化质量。

Conclusion: 有状态架构相比无状态架构实现了结构性的token节约，根本原因在于：无状态智能体以O(n)成本重读完整历史，而有状态智能体在O(1)成本的固定对话窗口内运行。本文提供了充分的架构细节，使从业者能够为自身工作流实现有状态自研究智能体。

Abstract: The autoresearch pattern enables autonomous experimentation by having a large language model (LLM) iteratively modify code to optimize a target metric. Its stateless design, however, reconstructs experimental context from scratch at every iteration, incurring $O(n)$ token cost per iteration and $O(n^{2})$ total. This work reformulates the pattern as a stateful ReAct agent using LangGraph, where typed persistent state carries experimental history across iterations via a tool-calling interface. Two benchmarks are evaluated: hyperparameter tuning (15 iterations, small per-iteration observations) and code performance optimization (40 iterations, large per-iteration observations containing full source code and benchmark results). On hyperparameter tuning, the stateful agent consumes 90\% fewer tokens (2{,}492 vs.\ 24{,}465). On code optimization, the stateful agent consumes 52\% fewer tokens (627K vs.\ 1{,}275K) while achieving comparable optimization quality on both tasks. The token reduction is structural: the stateless agent re-reads the full history at $O(n)$ cost per iteration, while the stateful agent operates within a fixed-size conversation window at $O(1)$ cost. This paper describes the architecture in sufficient detail for practitioners to implement a stateful autoresearch agent for their own workflows.

</details>


### [167] [A Comparative Study of Graph Neural Network Layer Selection for Interaction Modelling in Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14956)
*George Daoud,Mohamed El-Darieby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了19种图神经网络层在轨迹预测任务中的表现，发现ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现最佳，并总结了聚合方式、注意力机制和权重分配等实用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖精确的轨迹预测，图神经网络(GNN)已成为建模道路代理时空交互的有前途的方法。然而，轨迹预测的GNN架构设计缺乏标准化，缺乏关于哪些图层能有效捕获空间交互和时间动态的指导。

Method: 对19种图神经网络层类型进行详细的比较研究，重点关注其空间和时间处理能力，在探索的超参数设置下测试各种图层组合在轨迹预测任务中的表现。

Result: 识别出5种突出的图层组合，其中ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层的表现持续优于其他层。发现：基于求和的聚合比基于均值的方法更有效；多头注意力机制能实现更丰富的交互；对不同跳数距离分配不同权重可显著提高预测精度。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计更具可解释性和有效性的轨迹预测模型提供了实用指导原则，有助于推动自动驾驶系统中GNN架构的标准化设计。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely on precise trajectory prediction to plan safe and efficient movement. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become a promising approach for modelling spatiotemporal interactions among road agents. However, designing GNN architectures for trajectory prediction remains non-standardized, with little guidance on which graph layers effectively capture spatial interactions and temporal dynamics. This paper offers a detailed comparative study of 19 graph layer types, focusing on their spatial and temporal processing capabilities to discover the most effective architectures for trajectory prediction. Within the explored hyperparameter setting, we highlight five standout layer combinations, with ARMA, Chebyshev, and topology-aware layers consistently performing better than others. Beyond performance metrics, our findings yield practical design principles: sum-based aggregation is more effective than mean-based methods, multi-head attention mechanisms enable richer interactions, and assigning different weights to different hop distances significantly improves prediction accuracy. These findings offer useful guidance for designing more interpretable and effective trajectory prediction models.

</details>


### [168] [Leveraging Physiological Signals to Predict Exam Outcomes with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14960)
*Lala Yamazaki,Ramchandra Rimal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用考试期间采集的生理数据（电皮肤活动、心率、皮肤温度），通过对比逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM、LSTM、GRU及Transformer等多种机器学习模型，预测考试成绩并分析生理信号与学业表现的关系，发现简单模型（如随机森林）在性能、效率与可解释性上具优势，Transformer在数值数据处理上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究如何利用考试过程中的生理压力指标预测学业表现，揭示生理信号与学习成果之间的关联，从而理解影响学生心理健康的压力因素，为通过生理数据提升学生福祉和学业成果提供依据。

Method: 采集考试期间的电皮肤活动、心率和皮肤温度等生理数据；采用多样化的机器学习模型进行分析，包括传统模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、支持向量机）和深度学习模型（LSTM、GRU、Transformer）；重点评估Transformer处理数值数据的适应性；使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数等标准指标比较模型效能。

Result: 深度学习模型在捕捉生理数据的复杂关系方面表现优异；简单模型（如随机森林）在保持计算效率和可解释性的同时，有时能取得更优的预测性能；Transformer展现出良好 versatility，其性能与LSTM、GRU模型相当。

Conclusion: 强调了根据具体问题目标广泛实验不同模型类别的重要性，需在精度、效率和可解释性之间取得平衡；阐明了生理信号与学业表现之间的关系，为利用生理数据监测和改善学生心理健康与学业成果提供了研究基础。

Abstract: This study investigates the application of machine learning models to predict exam outcomes using physiological data collected during examination sessions. Physiological stress indicators, including electrodermal activity, heart rate, and skin temperature, were analyzed to uncover their association with academic performance. A variety of machine learning approaches were employed, ranging from standard models like logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machines to more advanced architectures, including transformers, long short-term memory (LSTM), and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models. This diversity aimed to capture the complex interactions within the data effectively. A key focus was assessing the adaptability of transformers in processing numerical data and evaluating their performance in this novel context. Standard performance metrics, such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, were used to compare model efficacy. The experimental results demonstrate that while deep learning models generally excel at capturing complex relationships in physiological data, simpler models like random forests can sometimes achieve superior performance while offering computational efficiency and interpretability. Furthermore, transformers demonstrated notable versatility, showcasing performances comparable to those of the LSTM and GRU models. This research underscores the importance of experimenting with a broad class of models that align with the objectives of the problem at hand, balancing precision, efficiency, and interpretability. By elucidating the relationships between physiological signals and academic performance, this study contributes to understanding stressors affecting students' mental health. It further promotes leveraging physiological data to enhance student well-being and academic outcomes.

</details>


### [169] [Benchmarking Instance-Dependent Label Noise with Controlled Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14965)
*Shadman Islam,Agustinus Kristiadi,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CILN框架，通过控制输入损坏生成实例相关标签噪声(IDN)基准数据集，使噪声来源和严重程度明确可控。实验表明该框架能产生真实的IDN，揭示现有噪声标签学习方法在传统噪声设置下未观察到的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成实例相关标签噪声(IDN)基准主要通过不完美的标注器或分类器评分者生成噪声，噪声来源模糊且不可控。需要一个框架使噪声源和严重程度明确、可控，以便更好地评估噪声标签学习方法。

Method: 提出CILN框架，通过控制输入损坏生成IDN：1)对输入实例施加可控的损坏；2)使用多样化投票池对损坏实例进行标注；3)基于CIFAR-10、MNIST和Adult数据集，构建了90个涵盖多种损坏类型和严重程度的基准设置。

Result: 生成的基准数据集展现出真实的实例相关噪声特性，提供多样化的混淆结构。在CIFAR-10上，标签分布比现有合成IDN基准更接近人类不确定性。进一步揭示了Co-Teaching和DivideMix等流行方法在传统噪声设置下未观察到的失败模式。

Conclusion: 噪声结构(而不仅是噪声率)在基准难度和算法行为中起重要作用。CILN通过使模糊性生成明确且可控，为研究多样化实例难度来源下的噪声标签学习提供了互补的基准框架。

Abstract: Synthetic instance-dependent label noise (IDN) benchmarks are widely used to evaluate noisy-label learning methods, yet existing approaches typically generate noise through imperfect annotators or classifier raters, leaving the source of ambiguity implicit. We introduce CILN, a benchmark generation framework that creates IDN through controlled input corruptions. A diverse voter pool labels corrupted instances, producing benchmark datasets in which both the source and severity of ambiguity are explicit and controllable. Using CIFAR10, MNIST, and Adult, we construct 90 benchmark settings spanning multiple corruption families and severity levels. Our experiments show that the resulting benchmarks exhibit genuine instance-dependent noise, provide diverse confusion structures, and, on CIFAR-10, can produce label distributions that are closer to human uncertainty than an existing synthetic IDN benchmark. We further demonstrate that corruption-mediated IDN can expose failure modes of popular noisy-label learning methods, including Co-Teaching and DivideMix, that are not observed under comparable levels of rater-fallibility noise. These findings suggest that noise structure, not only noise rate, plays an important role in benchmark difficulty and algorithm behavior. By making ambiguity generation explicit and controllable, CILN provides a complementary benchmarking framework for studying noisy-label learning under diverse sources of instance difficulty.

</details>


### [170] [Conflict-Aware Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15625)
*Yijun Lu,Zihan Fang,Pengpeng Qiao,Zheng Lin,Jing Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Por Lip Yee,Zhe Chen,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FC-MoE框架，通过重要性感知加权和梯度共识投影解决联邦学习中MoE微调的专家冲突问题，在非独立同分布环境下提升收敛速度和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中集成MoE进行高效微调时，数据异构性导致不同客户端的相同索引专家在优化时面临不一致甚至冲突的特征-标签相关性，这种不匹配在聚合时产生破坏性干扰，破坏优化稳定性并降低模型性能。

Method: 提出FC-MoE框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）重要性感知加权机制，优先考虑可靠的本地更新；（2）梯度共识投影，抑制冲突更新以保持全局优化路径稳定；（3）本地知识保留机制，通过重新锚定领域特定残差来保持客户端的专业知识。

Result: 在非独立同分布的联邦环境中进行大量实验，证明FC-MoE能够加速收敛，同时提升全局模型和本地模型的性能。

Conclusion: FC-MoE成功解决了联邦学习中MoE微调面临的专家优化冲突问题，为在隐私保护的协作优化场景下实现高效的大语言模型微调提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The continuous scaling of large language models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational costs, making Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) a scalable alternative for efficient fine-tuning via sparse activation. While federated learning (FL) emerges as the paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative optimization, integrating MoE into FL under data heterogeneity may trigger conflicting expert optimizations. Client-specific data distributions force same-indexed experts to optimize under inconsistent or even conflicting feature-label correlations. This mismatch induces destructive interference during aggregation, thus destabilizing the optimization trajectory and degrading model performance. To address this issue, we propose FC-MoE, a federated conflict-aware framework for MoE fine-tuning. It employs an importance aware weighting scheme to prioritize reliable local updates and utilizes gradient consensus projection to suppress conflicting updates, ensuring a stable global optimization path. Moreover, a local knowledge retention mechanism further preserves specialized client expertise by re-anchoring domain-specific residuals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FC-MoE accelerates convergence and enhances both global and local model performance in non-IID federated environments.

</details>


### [171] [Zero-order Parameter-free Optimization for LMO-based Methods: Novel Approach for Efficient Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14970)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AdaNAGED方法，结合无参数优化、零阶优化和非欧几里得几何更新，高效解决了大语言模型微调中零阶优化对参数敏感的问题，实现了无需人工调参的高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调中，反向传播的内存开销巨大（需存储激活值、梯度和优化器状态）。零阶（ZO）优化虽能降低内存需求，但其性能对步长和平滑参数高度敏感，需要针对每个任务进行昂贵的参数调优。现有的无参数优化方法虽能自动调节参数，但尚未与能处理参数异构结构的线性最小化预言机（LMO）方法相结合。

Method: 提出AdaNAGED方法，统一了三个关键组件：（1）零阶优化实现梯度无关训练；（2）无参数自适应机制自动调节算法参数，无需预知问题相关常数；（3）基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的非欧几里得几何更新，考虑参数块的异构结构。该方法在无参数优化框架下实现了LMO驱动的零阶优化。

Result: 建立了理论收敛保证，并在OPT-1.3B模型的大规模语言模型微调任务上验证了方法的有效性，证明了AdaNAGED能够在无需人工参数调优的情况下实现高效的微调性能。

Conclusion: AdaNAGED成功地将梯度无关训练、自适应参数调节和非欧几里得几何更新统一到一个框架中，为零阶优化在大规模语言模型微调中的应用提供了理论保证和实践验证，解决了传统零阶优化方法参数敏感和调参成本高的问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) has become a central application of modern optimization, enabling pretrained models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks and domain-specific data. A major obstacle in large-scale fine-tuning is the memory overhead of backpropagation, which requires storing activations, gradients, and optimizer states. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative, but its performance is highly sensitive to the stepsize and smoothing parameter, often requiring costly task-specific tuning. Parameter-free (PF) optimization addresses this issue by adapting algorithmic parameters without prior knowledge of problem-dependent constants. Moreover, large-scale fine-tuning can benefit from geometry-aware updates that account for the heterogeneous structure of parameter blocks, which can be modeled through methods that exploit linear minimization oracle (LMO). In this work, we study PF adaptation for LMO-based ZO optimization and introduce $\texttt{AdaNAGED}$, a method that unifies gradient-free training, adaptive tuning, and non-Euclidean update geometry. We establish convergence guarantees and validate the method on large-scale LLM fine-tuning task with $\texttt{OPT}-1.3\mathrm{B}$ model.

</details>


### [172] [FastMix: Fast Data Mixture Optimization via Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14971)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Jun Xia,Ruobing Xie,Bin Xia,Xingwu Sun,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTMIX是一个自动化数据配比发现框架，通过将数据配比选择重构为双层优化问题，仅需训练单个代理模型即可高效优化预训练和后训练的数据混合比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模和多样化数据集推动了大型模型的发展，但在预训练和后训练中识别最优数据配比仍是一个重要的开放性问题。现有方法依赖预定义启发式规则或资源密集型模拟，效率低下且可扩展性差。

Method: 将数据配比选择重构为双层优化问题，证明了优化配比比例在数学上等价于在均匀源采样下分配每源损失权重。这将配比系数直接嵌入到可微迭代优化目标中，实现了配比和模型的梯度优化。采用近似迭代优化流程：内循环根据当前配比更新模型参数，外循环根据验证反馈更新配比比例。

Result: 在预训练和后训练任务中，FASTMIX均超越基线方法，同时大幅降低搜索成本。相比现有方法，仅需训练单个代理模型即可完成数据配比优化，显著提升了效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: FASTMIX通过双层优化方法有效解决了数据配比优化问题，为大规模模型训练中的数据选择提供了高效、可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: While large and diverse datasets have driven recent advances in large models, identifying the optimal data mixture for pre-training and post-training remains a significant open problem. We address this challenge with FASTMIX, a novel framework that automates data mixture discovery while training only a single proxy model. Instead of relying on predefined heuristics or resource-intensive simulations, FASTMIX jointly optimizes mixture coefficients and model parameters, substantially improving efficiency and scalability over prior approaches. At the core of FASTMIX is a reformulation of mixture selection as a bilevel optimization problem. Under this reformulation, we show that optimizing mixture ratios is mathematically equivalent to assigning per-source loss weights under uniform source sampling. This embeds the mixture coefficients directly into the differentiable iterative optimization objective, enabling efficient, gradient-based optimization of both mixture and model. To solve the optimization problem, FASTMIX implements an approximate iterative optimization procedure, alternating between (i) updating model parameters on data sampled according to current mixture ratios (inner loop) and (ii) updating mixture ratios based on validation feedback (outer loop). Across pre- and post-training, FASTMIX outperforms baselines while drastically reducing search cost. Code (https://github.com/hrtan/fastmix)

</details>


### [173] [Rational Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14990)
*Naiyu Yin,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了有理稀疏自编码器（RSAE），通过用可训练的有理函数替换固定编码器激活函数，在保持特征级可解释性的同时，在重建指标和下游行为指标上严格优于现有的SAE家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的稀疏自编码器（SAE）家族受限于固定的编码器非线性函数（如ReLU、JumpReLU和TopK），这种硬编码的稀疏机制会扭曲重建与稀疏性之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出有理稀疏自编码器（RSAE），用可训练的有理函数替换固定编码器激活函数。采用两阶段流程实现：第一阶段通过初始化程序复制预训练基线SAE权重，插入通过松弛Remez交换获得的合成数据有理系数，并校准尺度参数；第二阶段在标准稀疏正则化重建目标下进行微调。

Result: 在三个开源语言模型的残差流激活上，针对所有三个基线激活家族，RSAE在微调后在重建指标和下游行为指标上均严格优于基线SAE，同时在稀疏探测下不牺牲特征级可解释性。这些改进在不同宿主语言模型、基线激活家族和测试的基线稀疏性范围内保持一致，每个自编码器仅增加少量标量参数，在单个消费级GPU上数分钟即可运行。

Conclusion: RSAE提供了一种灵活且高效的方法来改进稀疏自编码器，通过可训练的有理激活函数实现了比固定非线性函数更好的性能，同时保持了可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are standard tools for mechanistic interpretability, but current SAE families are constrained by fixed encoder nonlinearities such as ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK. This hard-codes a particular sparsity mechanism into the model and can distort the reconstruction-versus-sparsity trade-off. We introduce the Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE), which replaces the fixed encoder activation with a trainable rational function. Rational activations are flexible enough to uniformly approximate the activation primitives used by existing SAE families on compact domains (for TopK, the thresholded gate obtained after a separating top-k threshold is supplied), while also providing a richer function class for adapting to the observed pre-activation geometry. We realise this idea through a two-stage pipeline: an initialisation procedure that copies the pre-trained baseline SAE weights, plugs in rational coefficients obtained by the relaxed Remez exchange on synthetic data, and calibrates the scale parameters along with the rational coefficients; followed by a fine-tuning step under the standard sparsity-regularised reconstruction objective. Empirically, on residual-stream activations of three open-weight language models and across all three baseline activation families, the RSAE strictly improves on it after the fine-tuning step, both on reconstruction-side metrics and on downstream-behaviour metrics, without sacrificing feature-level interpretability under sparse probing. These gains are consistent across host language models, across baseline activation families, and across the full range of baseline sparsity we tested, while the upgrade itself adds only a handful of scalar parameters per autoencoder and runs in minutes on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [174] [Unlocking Latent Dimensions: Exploring Representations of Large-Scale X-ray Scattering Data using Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14999)
*Monika Choudhary,Xiaoya Chong,Runbo Jiang,Wiebke Koepp,Petrus H. Zwart,Damon English,Gregory M. Su,Eric Schaible,Chenhui Zhu,Mostafa Nassr,Noah P. Wamble,Kelvin Kam-Yun Li,Jonathan M. Chan,Jose Carlos Diaz,Cameron McKay,Lynn Katz,Benny Freeman,Guillaume Freychet,Yevgen Matviychuk,Eliot Gann,Daniel B. Allan,Benedikt Sochor,Frank Schluenzen,Stephan V. Roth,Ethan Crumlin,Dylan McReynolds,Tanny Chavez,Alexander Hexemer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对X射线散射数据处理滞后问题，训练了一个领域专用的卷积变分自编码器(C-VAE)，实现了对海量数据的低维表征学习，支持离线数据探索和在线实时分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学用户设施产生的X射线散射数据速度远超传统工作流的处理能力，迫切需要开发能够快速处理和分析这些海量数据的新方法。

Method: 在150万张X射线散射图像上训练了一个领域专用的注意力机制卷积变分自编码器(C-VAE)，学习能够捕捉不同实验条件下结构变化的低维表征，并将该方法集成到MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer组件中。

Result: 学习到的潜空间展现出组织良好的聚类和反映实验进展的平滑轨迹，能够支持可控的合成散射图像生成；在两个同步辐射装置的时间分辨薄膜形成实验中，模型无需重新训练即可将实验组织成可解释的潜空间结构；与通用视觉基础模型DINOv3 (ViT-7B)的基准测试表明，领域专用训练能产生更具可解释性的散射数据潜空间组织。

Conclusion: 该方法成功应用于离线数据集探索和实时在线分析两种场景，通过领域专用训练获得了比通用模型更具可解释性的潜空间组织，为X射线散射数据的高效处理和结构探索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific user facilities generate X-ray scattering data faster than traditional workflows can process them. We address this challenge across two settings, offline dataset exploration and live on-the-fly analysis. We train a domain-specific attention-based Convolutional Variational Autoencoder (C-VAE) on 1.5 million X-ray scattering images to learn low-dimensional representations capturing structural variation across diverse experimental conditions. The learned latent space reveals well-organized clusters and smooth trajectories reflecting experimental progression. It further supports controlled synthetic scattering image generation across diverse structural states. When deployed without retraining, the model organizes time-resolved film formation experiments at two synchrotron facilities into interpretable latent structures. Benchmarking against DINOv3 (ViT-7B), a general-purpose vision foundation model, demonstrates that domain-specific training yields more interpretable latent organization for scattering data. Both workflows are integrated within Latent Space Explorer, a component of the MLExchange platform, supporting interactive structural exploration across archived datasets and live experiments.

</details>


### [175] [How Should World Models Be Evaluated? A Decision-Making-Centric Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15032)
*Yang Yu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifei Sheng,Haoxiang Ren,Haoxin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于世界模型评估的综述论文。文章指出当前"世界模型"概念被泛化用于指代多种不同对象，导致评估指标多样化且出现主张与证据不匹配的问题。作者提出L0-L7评估层级框架，认为评估应当以决策用途为中心，重点关注模型在反事实推理、策略评估、规划和策略优化方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究领域存在概念混淆和评估标准不统一的问题。术语"世界模型"被用于指代动作条件环境模型、潜在想象模型、未来视频预测器等多种不同对象，相应的评估指标也从视频真实感知、物理合理性扩展到策略排序、规划成功等多个维度。这导致论文经常做出超出其评估能力范围的声明，形成了主张与证据不匹配的问题。作者认为核心问题应当回归到模型的实际用途，特别是当模型被呈现为用于具身决策的世界模型时，关键不在于生成视觉上吸引人的视频，而在于能否支持可靠的反事实推理和策略优化。

Method: 作者提出L0-L7评估层级框架来组织现有文献：(1) L0-L3层级：主要作为生成产物的诊断性评估，关注视觉合理性；(2) L4层级：通常是第一个真正的干预性测试；(3) L5-L7层级：提供决策有用性最直接的证据。基于这一诊断，作者提出了一个以决策为中心的评估框架和基准协议，核心评估维度包括：反事实动作保真度、闭环滚出有效性、奖励/价值预测、策略排序一致性、优化提升、模型可利用性和不确定性校准。

Result: 论文通过系统梳理现有文献，揭示了世界模型评估中的核心问题：评估指标与模型声称用途之间的错配。L0-L7框架清晰地展示了从视觉质量到决策效用的评估层级关系，其中L5-L7层级（涉及策略评估和优化）才是真正验证世界模型决策有用性的关键。研究识别出了现有评估中的不足，特别是对反事实推理、策略诱导分布偏移和长时域滚出能力的测试不足。

Conclusion: 世界模型的评估必须以用途为导向。对于旨在支持具身决策的世界模型，评估应从视频生成的视觉质量转向对决策过程的核心支持能力测试。作者提出的决策为中心评估框架强调了七个关键评估维度，为标准化世界模型评估提供了系统性的方法论指导。这一框架有助于解决当前领域中的主张-证据不匹配问题，推动世界模型研究向更具实用价值的方向发展。

Abstract: World models have rapidly become one of the central abstractions in modern AI. Yet the term now refers to several different objects: action-conditioned environment models, latent imagination models, future-video predictors, interactive neural simulators, latent predictive representations, and synthetic-data engines. Evaluation has broadened with the term. Recent papers measure video realism, perceptual similarity, instruction following, physical plausibility, policy ranking, executability, planning success, and downstream policy improvement. The result is not only metric diversity but also a recurring problem of claim/evidence mismatch: papers frequently make a stronger claim about what their model is useful for than their evaluation can actually establish.
  This paper surveys the recent literature and argues that the central question is use-dependent. When a model is presented as a world model for embodied decision-making, a more decisive issue is not whether it generates visually compelling videos, but whether it supports reliable counterfactual reasoning, policy evaluation, planning, and policy optimization under intervention, policy-induced distribution shift, and long-horizon rollout. We organize the literature using an L0--L7 ladder that ranges from visual plausibility to policy optimization utility. In our interpretation, L0--L3 are most naturally read as diagnostics of generated artifacts, L4 is often the first genuinely interventional test, and L5--L7 provide the most direct evidence of decision usefulness. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a decision-making-centric evaluation framework and a benchmark protocol that foreground counterfactual action fidelity, closed-loop rollout validity, reward/value prediction, policy-ranking agreement, optimization lift, model exploitability, and uncertainty calibration.

</details>


### [176] [Transformers Learn the Mestre-Nagao Heuristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15036)
*Pranav Venkata Konda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性方法，成功逆向工程了一个用于预测椭圆曲线秩的两层Transformer模型，发现模型自主学习到了解析数论中的Mestre-Nagao启发式算法，实现了超过99%的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在数学领域的应用日益增多，但其内部工作机制往往不透明。研究者希望通过对一个成功预测椭圆曲线秩的简单Transformer模型的逆向工程，理解模型究竟学到了什么数学规律，以及模型的决策过程是否与已知的数学理论相吻合。这有助于建立对AI数学推理能力的信任，并可能发现新的数学洞见。

Method: 研究者训练了一个两层Transformer编码器，使用前128个归一化Frobenius迹作为输入，对导数≤10000的有理椭圆曲线进行秩0或秩1分类。随后采用多种机械可解释性技术：注意力分析、线性探测、激活修补、logit归因和神经元级电路分析，对模型进行逆向工程。研究还比较了50个独立训练的模型，分析它们在函数空间和权重空间的一致性。

Result: 模型在两类上均达到>99%的准确率，在训练集中无同构或二次扭曲关系的测试曲线上准确率基本不变。逆向工程发现：模型学习到的输入权重与Mestre-Nagao求和启发式权重高度一致（Spearman相关系数0.997，Pearson相关系数0.952）；仅需20个（占512个）第1层MLP神经元即可实现AUROC为0.992的秩预测；CLS注意力集中于质数位置的频率是合数位置的2-50倍；CLS嵌入能以R²=0.962±0.011编码log L(E,1)；50个独立训练模型在函数空间几乎相同（成对一致率>98.8%）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了机械可解释性方法可以有效揭示神经网络学习到的数学算法。令人惊讶的是，模型仅从Frobenius迹数据中自主学习到了解析数论中的Mestre-Nagao启发式结果，这表明神经网络能够重新发现已知的数学理论。研究还发现注意力权重与因果信息流是分离的，且多个训练解在函数空间高度一致，为理解神经网络的数学推理能力提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We train a two-layer transformer encoder to classify rational elliptic curves $E/\mathbb{Q}$ of conductor $\leq 10000$ as either rank 0 or rank 1 from the first 128 normalized Frobenius traces. We achieve >99% accuracy on both classes, and accuracy is essentially unchanged on test curves with no isogeny or quadratic-twist relative in the training set. We then apply techniques from mechanistic interpretability such as attention analysis, linear probing, activation patching, logit attribution, and neuron-level circuit analysis to reverse-engineer the algorithm the (centroid in function space) model learned. We find that a sparse circuit of 20 out of 512 layer-1 MLP neurons is sufficient for rank prediction under a linear probe with an AUROC of 0.992 at plateau, implementing a push-pull detector architecture of rank-0 and rank-1 detectors with a one-sided readout. However, we notice that the model has sub-optimal readout problems indicating a mismatch in rank-order between the readout pathway and the discriminative circuit. Critically, the learned input weights of the top discriminating neuron match the Mestre-Nagao sum heuristic weights $\log(p)/(p\cdot \log{B})$ with a Spearman coefficient $r = 0.997$ and Pearson coefficient $r = 0.952$: the model has learnt a result from analytic number theory from the Frobenius trace data alone. We additionally find that all 50 independently trained models concentrate CLS attention on prime positions at 2-50$\times$ the rate of composite positions. The CLS embedding encodes $\log{L(E,1)}$ with $R^2 = 0.962\pm 0.011$ across the 50 models (after controlling for the conductor). Activation patching analysis reveals that attention weights are dissociated from causal information flow. Additionally, the 50 solutions from training are near-identical in function space (with pairwise agreement $>$98.8%) despite large weight space barriers.

</details>


### [177] [Temporal Difference Learning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15048)
*Qizhen Ying,Yangchen Pan,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Junfeng Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间差分(TD)目标函数，通过将扩散过程重构为马尔可夫奖励过程并借鉴强化学习中的策略评估方法，解决了传统扩散模型训练中缺乏跨时间步一致性的问题，从而显著提升了少步采样场景下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型的训练目标仅关注单个时间步（或相邻时间步对）的局部去噪目标，并未强制要求沿去噪轨迹的预测之间保持一致性。这种跨时间一致性的缺失会导致模型性能下降，特别是在使用少步采样器时问题更为严重。

Method: 将扩散过程重构为马尔可夫奖励过程，并将去噪问题转化为强化学习中的策略评估问题，从而推导出一种统一的时间差分(TD)方法，适用于离散时间和连续时间扩散模型。同时提出了一种基于样本的重加权方法来稳定训练过程。

Result: 实验表明，使用TD训练可以显著改善由FID衡量的样本质量。当采样步数较少时优势更为明显，突显了该方法在低计算预算场景下的实用价值。消融研究验证了成对损失重加权、正则化权重和单步步幅等设计选择的合理性。

Conclusion: 该TD方法是一种通用的即插即用解决方案，能够强制跨时间一致性并提升不同扩散生成模型的生成质量，具有重要的实践意义和广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Diffusion models are typically trained with objectives that focus on local denoising targets at individual time steps (or adjacent pairs), which do not enforce consistency between predictions along the denoising trajectory. This lack of cross-time consistency can degrade performance, especially for few-step samplers. We introduce a temporal difference (TD) objective that penalizes inconsistency of the model's multi-step progress along the denoising path. By reformulating the diffusion process as a Markov reward process and casting denoising as a policy evaluation problem in reinforcement learning, we derive a unified TD approach that applies to both discrete- and continuous-time diffusion formulations. We further propose a principled sample-based reweighting method that stabilizes training. Empirically, we show that using our TD training can significantly improve sample quality measured by FID, with stronger advantages when the number of sampling steps is small, highlighting its practical utility under low-computation-budget scenarios. We provide ablation studies to justify our design choices, including pairwise loss reweighting, regularization weight, and one-step stride. Overall, our TD approach can be a general drop-in that enforces cross-time consistency and improves generation quality across different diffusion generative models.

</details>


### [178] [Physics-conforming Latent Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15053)
*Matthias Chung,Yutong Bu,Deepanshu Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Physics-conforming Latent Twins框架，通过在潜空间设计中强制物理约束（守恒律、不变量、耗散结构等），构建满足物理原理的代理模型，解决了时依赖问题中传统代理模型忽视物理结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中的代理模型在时依赖问题上，仅能准确插值训练轨迹是不够的；可靠的代理模型还应遵守守恒律、不变量、容许性条件和耗散结构等赋予轨迹物理意义的基本原理。

Method: 基于Latent Twin公式，联合学习编码器、解码器和潜流映射（连接任意时间索引状态），同时约束潜动力学以保持或耗散指定的结构量；发展约束转移视角，将原始状态空间的物理结构与潜空间的相容约束联系起来，推导保持线性/二次不变量或强制耗散不等式的代数条件。

Result: 证明了结构保持界限，表明潜空间约束可改善解码后对物理缺陷的控制；在代表性ODE和PDE基准上的数值实验表明，该方法在保持准确代理预测的同时，显著改善了约束满足、结构保真度和长期定性行为。

Conclusion: Physics-conforming Latent Twins成功地将物理约束融入潜空间动力学设计中，实现了物理一致性与预测精度的统一，为物理系统的代理建模提供了结构保持的理论保证和实用框架。

Abstract: Surrogate models are central to scientific machine learning, where they enable fast prediction, simulation, inference, and control for complex physical systems. For time-dependent problems, however, accurate interpolation of training trajectories is not sufficient: reliable surrogates should also respect the conservation laws, invariants, admissibility conditions, and dissipative structures that give those trajectories physical meaning. We introduce Physics-conforming Latent Twins, a framework for learning latent surrogate solution operators whose dynamics satisfy selected physical principles by design. The method builds on the Latent Twin formulation by jointly learning an encoder, a decoder, and a latent flow map between arbitrary time-indexed states, while constraining the latent dynamics to preserve or dissipate prescribed structural quantities. We develop a constraint-transfer viewpoint that connects physical structure in the original state space with compatible constraints in latent space, and prove structure-preservation bounds showing how latent enforcement improves control of physical defects after decoding. We also derive algebraic conditions for latent flow maps that preserve linear and quadratic invariants or enforce dissipative inequalities. Numerical experiments on representative ODE and PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction, structural fidelity, and qualitative long-time behavior while maintaining accurate surrogate prediction.

</details>


### [179] [Size Doesn't Matter: Cosine-Scored Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15054)
*Silen Naihin,Lev Stambler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用余弦相似度替代内积作为稀疏自编码器（SAE）的评分函数，解决了高范数token在BatchTopK下不当占用字典槽位的问题，学习到更多人类可识别的概念特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE使用内积检测特征，激活值同时依赖于方向对齐和输入范数。在BatchTopK机制下，高范数token会同时推高所有预激活值，不恰当地占用字典槽位，而子层归一化已将幅度信息丢弃，导致编码器检测到的量与模型实际读取的不一致。

Method: 提出用余弦相似度与输入幅度的学习组合替代内积评分，让优化器自主选择范数的利用程度；进一步提出逐特征扩展版本，允许每个特征独立决定其范数依赖程度。

Result: 在相同重构性能下，余弦编码器学习到的特征与人类可识别概念对齐的频率远高于标准方法，填补了内积方法浪费在范数检测器上的字典槽位。梯度重加权几乎无法缩小差距，证实前向传播评分几何结构是关键杠杆。优化器从不选择完全恢复内积，没有任何特征选择超过半范数依赖。

Conclusion: 余弦评分的优势并非在所有任务或深度上通用，但作者认为对于归一化表示上的字典学习，余弦评分应成为默认选择。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) detect features via inner product, so a feature's activation scales with both its directional alignment and the input's norm. Under BatchTopK, high-norm tokens inflate all pre-activations simultaneously, claiming dictionary slots regardless of content alignment. This matters because sublayer normalization has already discarded the magnitude the score measures, so the encoder detects a quantity the model does not read. We replace the score with a learned blend of cosine similarity and input magnitude, letting the optimizer choose how much norm to use; a per-feature extension lets each feature decide independently. In both regimes, training is free to recover inner product but never does, with no feature ever choosing more than half-magnitude dependence. At matched reconstruction, the cosine encoder learns features that align with human-recognizable concepts far more often than standard, filling dictionary slots that inner product wastes on norm detectors. Loss reweighting that equalizes gradients barely closes the gap, confirming forward-pass score geometry as the lever. The advantage is not universal across tasks or depths, but we believe cosine scoring should be the default for dictionary learning on normalized representations.

</details>


### [180] [Machine Learning and the Random Walk Puzzle: Forecasting the CAD/USD Exchange Rate with Expanding Window Evaluation and SHAP Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15058)
*Louis Agyekum,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Kofi Acheampong,Emmanuel Boadi,Priscilla Yaa Amakye,Fafa Shalom Tchorly,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估机器学习模型预测USD/CAD汇率的能力，发现仅线性回归统计显著优于随机游走基准，随机森林在扩展窗口框架下实现最低MAPE(1.17%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨机器学习模型是否能够在月度USD/CAD汇率预测中超越传统的朴素随机游走基准，应对汇率预测的固有挑战。

Method: 使用2017年1月至2026年5月的日度数据重采样为113个月度观测值，评估五种ML模型(线性回归、随机森林、梯度提升、XGBoost、AdaBoost)，以随机游走和ETS模型为基准。采用扩展窗口框架保持严格的样本外完整性，使用Diebold-Mariano检验评估预测精度差异，通过结构断点检测识别四个显著断点，并应用SHAP分析解释最佳模型的预测驱动因素。

Result: 朴素随机游走模型仍是强劲基准。仅线性回归统计显著优于随机游走(DM统计量3.0585，p值0.0071)；ML集成模型仅显示边际差异。随机森林在扩展窗口框架下实现除随机游走外所有模型中的最低MAPE(1.17%)。SHAP分析确认短期滞后(特别是lag1和lag2)及近期滚动均值主导预测，与汇率的近随机游走行为一致。

Conclusion: 尽管机器学习技术在众多领域表现优异，但在汇率预测领域，朴素随机游走模型仍是难以超越的基准。仅线性回归展现出统计显著的优越性，而更复杂的ML集成模型未能提供明显改进，反映了汇率时间序列的近随机游走特性。

Abstract: This study examines whether machine learning (ML) models can outperform the naive random walk benchmark in forecasting the monthly USD/CAD exchange rate. Using daily data from the Bank of Canada spanning January 2017 to May 2026, resampled into 113 monthly observations, five ML models are evaluated: linear regression, random forest, gradient boosting, XGBoost, and AdaBoost. These models are benchmarked against the naive random walk model and exponential smoothing with Holt-Winters seasonality (ETS). All models are evaluated using an expanding-window framework to maintain strict out-of-sample integrity, and forecast-accuracy differences are assessed using the Diebold-Mariano (DM) test. Structural break detection identifies four significant breakpoints in the series, corresponding to the escalation of the US-China trade war in 2018, the COVID-19 economic recovery in 2020, the peak of the Bank of Canada rate-hiking cycle in 2022, and the start of the Bank of Canada rate-cutting cycle in 2024. SHAP, or Shapley Additive Explanations, analysis is applied to interpret the drivers of the best-performing ML model. The results show that the naive random walk model remains a formidable benchmark. Linear regression is the only model that statistically outperforms the naive random walk model, with a DM statistic of 3.0585 and a p value of 0.0071, whereas the ML ensemble models show only marginal differences. Random Forest with an expanding-window framework achieves the lowest MAPE of 1.17 percent among all models except the random walk. SHAP analysis confirms that short-term lags, particularly lag1 and lag2, and recent rolling means dominate predictions, consistent with the near-random-walk behavior of exchange rates.

</details>


### [181] [HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17028)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HAMON，一种被动衍射光学预测核心，通过将历史值编码到光学孔径并利用级联可训练相位掩模和自由空间衍射直接生成预测。在标准基准测试中，HAMON在ETTm2的所有预测范围和ETTh2的大多数范围上超越了最强的数字基线，MSE最高提升14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现简单线性和频域模型在长序列时间预测中仍具有竞争力，且标准预测基准可能不需要Transformer中密集叠加的表示能力。这引发了根本性问题：如果核心预测算子通常是低复杂度且近似线性的，是否必须通过学习型数字时序混合来实现？作者旨在探索被动光学器件是否可以直接实现预测功能。

Method: HAMON将历史值编码到光学孔径上，未来位置保持暗态，通过级联可训练相位掩模配合自由空间衍射在输出场中直接形成预测。推理时仅需单次被动光学传播，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。方法基于标准傅里叶光学原理，使用相位编码、强度兼容读取，并通过TorchOptics交叉模拟器进行验证。

Result: HAMON在ETTm2的所有预测范围上超越最强数字基线，在ETTh2上除最长范围外均超越基线，MSE最高提升14%，且在不同预测范围上表现一致。在Weather数据集上具有竞争力，在其余ETT设置以及高通道数的Traffic和Electricity数据集上落后于最强基线。消融实验证实预测来源于承载数据的光场而非数字预测头。

Conclusion: HAMON证明了被动光学器件可以直接执行时间序列预测，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。由于核心使用标准傅里叶光学，HAMON为光学硬件和被动物理序列混合定义了具体实现目标，为低复杂度预测任务提供了全新的物理计算范式。

Abstract: Simple linear and frequency-domain models remain surprisingly competitive in long-horizon time-series forecasting, and recent mechanistic evidence suggests that standard forecasting benchmarks may not require the dense superposed representations that make transformers powerful in other domains. This raises a substrate-level question: if the core forecasting operator is often low-complexity and approximately linear, does it need to be implemented as learned digital temporal mixing? We introduce HAMON, a passive diffractive optical forecasting core in which historical values are encoded onto an optical aperture, future positions are left dark, and cascaded trainable phase masks with free-space diffraction shape the forecast directly in the output field. At inference, prediction is performed by a single passive optical propagation pass with no trainable digital sequence-mixing layer.
  Across standard benchmarks, HAMON outperforms the strongest digital baselines considered on ETTm2 at all horizons and on ETTh2 at all but the longest horizon, improving MSE by up to 14\% and doing so consistently across horizons rather than at isolated points. It is competitive on Weather and trails the strongest baselines on the remaining ETT settings and on the high-channel-count Traffic and Electricity datasets. Phase encoding, intensity-compatible readout, and phase-scrambling ablations, together with a TorchOptics cross-simulator check, indicate that the forecasts arise from the data-bearing optical field rather than from a digital forecasting head. Because the passive core uses standard Fourier optics, HAMON defines a concrete target for optical hardware and for passive physical sequence mixing.

</details>


### [182] [Phase-Localized Curation Does Not Help: A Negative Result on Per-Phase Metric Selection for Demonstration Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15064)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分段指标聚合策略在演示数据筛选中表现不佳，不如全局或统一指标策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假设操作演示具有时间阶段结构，因此数据筛选指标应在各阶段内局部应用而非全局应用，以更有效地检测和剔除缺陷演示。

Method: 将轨迹分割为阶段，在各阶段内使用局部最优指标评分并聚合（阶段门控筛选），在三个接触丰富的LIBERO抓取任务上测试，并与统一指标应用及单一全局指标策略对比。

Result: 阶段门控筛选从未成为最佳策略，在其中两个任务上表现最差（任务1：86.0 vs 92.0；任务3：22.7 vs 48.0）。失败机制在于当缺陷信号集中于单一阶段时，跨阶段排序聚合会用无信息分数稀释缺陷信号。此外，各阶段的最优指标选择无法跨任务迁移。

Conclusion: 结果否定了阶段分解筛选的假设，建议从业者应优先识别单一的缺陷信息指标，而非按阶段分解筛选流程。

Abstract: Manipulation demonstrations have temporal phase structure, and a natural hypothesis is that demonstration-curation metrics should be applied within phases rather than globally. The idea is to segment each trajectory into phases, score each phase with the metric that is locally most informative, and then aggregate. This follows directly from prior work showing that a single global metric can be the best detector of a defect and yet the worst curator of the resulting policy. We test the per-phase hypothesis on three contact-rich LIBERO pick-and-place tasks with a controlled early-release structural defect, comparing phase-gated curation against the same metrics applied uniformly and against a strong single global metric. Across all three tasks and five random seeds per condition, phase-gated curation is never the best curation strategy, and it is the worst of the three on two of the three tasks (Task 1: 86.0 vs. 92.0 for global; Task 3: 22.7 vs. 48.0 for uniform). We trace the failure to a concrete mechanism. When the defect signal is concentrated in a single phase, rank-aggregating across phases dilutes that signal with uninformative scores from defect-free phases, selecting a worse demonstration subset than simply applying the defect-informative metric everywhere. We further show that the per-phase metric selection does not transfer across tasks, since no phase shares a winning metric between any two tasks, so the selection cannot be reused and must be re-derived per task from a noisy sweep. These results bound a plausible and previously untested method, and they argue that practitioners should prefer identifying a single defect-informative metric over decomposing curation by phase. We release the full pipeline, all metric implementations, and per-seed results.

</details>


### [183] [TriAdReview: Triangular Adversarial Review Architecture for Multi-Model Technical Document Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15074)
*Zhiqiang Zhou,Junliang Dai,Xu Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TriAdReview三角对抗评审架构，通过双评审模型和判断机制迭代改进技术文档生成，在五个任务上实现10.1%整体提升，但发现对抗评审存在简化偏向，在完整性导向任务上产生负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一大语言模型在技术文档生成中常存在过度工程化、安全盲点和覆盖不完整的问题，需要多模型协作机制来提升生成质量。

Method: 提出TriAdReview三角对抗评审架构，采用工程视角和边界视角两个独立评审模型，通过三角判断机制迭代改进生成器模型的输出；在五个基准任务上对比单模型基线、双模型（单评审）和三模型（完整系统）三种配置。

Result: 三模型配置相比单模型基线实现10.1%整体提升（26.2 vs. 23.8，p<0.05），其中安全审计提升27.6%、代码生成20.8%、架构设计15.6%；但在需求分析任务上下降7.5%，揭示对抗评审架构对简化存在结构偏向，在完整性导向任务上适得其反。

Conclusion: 首次实证刻画了多模型对抗评审何时有效何时有害，发现对抗评审存在简化偏向的结构性限制，通过任务类型框架分析边界条件，证明评审提示词适应可部分缓解该问题，为协作AI系统设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for technical document generation, yet single-model outputs often suffer from over-engineering, security blind spots, and incomplete coverage. We propose TriAdReview, a triangular adversarial review architecture that employs two independent reviewer models (engineering and boundary perspectives) and a triangular judging mechanism to iteratively improve a generator model's output. We evaluate TriAdReview across five benchmark tasks - architecture design, code generation, proposal review, security audit, and requirements analysis - using three configurations: single model (baseline), dual model (single review), and triple model (full system). Results across 75 experiments (n=5 per cell) show that the triple model configuration achieves a 10.1% overall improvement over the single model baseline (26.2 vs. 23.8 out of 50; p<0.05, paired t-test), with particularly strong gains on security audit (+27.6%), code generation (+20.8%), and architecture design (+15.6%). A second scorer (mimo-v2.5-pro) confirms the direction with a smaller effect (+2.7%), suggesting moderate inter-rater agreement. However, the system shows a -7.5% degradation on requirements analysis, revealing that adversarial review architectures have a structural bias toward simplification that is counterproductive for completeness-oriented tasks. We analyze this boundary condition through a task-type framework and demonstrate that reviewer prompt adaptation partially mitigates the issue. Our findings provide the first empirical characterization of when multi-model adversarial review helps versus harms, with implications for the design of collaborative AI systems.

</details>


### [184] [An Integrable Token Mixing Layer from the Generalized Yang Baxter Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15085)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自由费米子和广义Yang-Baxter结构的序列令牌混合层——YB Mixer，通过Ising交换代数实现了保持正交性和可交换性的稳定序列处理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将可积系统中的局部代数约束与全局计算稳定性原理应用于序列处理，旨在构建一个数学上严谨、具有保范性质的架构，以解决序列建模中的稳定性与泛化性问题。

Method: 利用Ising交换代数构造自由费米子结构，实现精确保持范数的正交映射；通过谱循环生成器维持系统的正交性与传递矩阵的可交换性，使推理过程无序依赖且适应可变计算预算。

Result: YB Mixer实现了高度稳定的序列处理能力，具备正交保范性与可交换传递矩阵特性，能够有效泛化到更长的序列长度。

Conclusion: YB Mixer为序列处理提供了一个数学基础扎实、稳定性强的架构，其源自可积系统的代数约束为深度学习模型设计提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: The YB Mixer is a sequence token mixing layer derived from free fermion and generalized Yang Baxter structures. It applies a core principle from integrable systems where a local algebraic constraint guarantees global computational stability. By using the Ising exchange algebra the mixer creates a free fermionic structure that acts as an exactly norm preserving orthogonal map. This algebra also produces commuting transfer matrices which allow inference to be order free and adaptable to any variable budget. To ensure the model can generalize to longer sequence lengths it uses a spectral circulant generator. This generator maintains the crucial orthogonal and commuting properties of the system. The result is a highly stable and mathematically grounded architecture for sequence processing.

</details>


### [185] [High-Dimensional Random Projection for Activation Steering in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15092)
*Minh-Hieu Pham,Bach Do,Laziz Abdullaev,Tan Minh Nguyen,Khoat Than*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HiDRA，一种无需训练的激活引导方法，通过在高维投影空间中进行操作，突破了现有线性方法的局限，实现了更优的大语言模型行为控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于均值差异的激活引导方法仅能捕获类别激活之间的均值差异，无法恢复在叠加假设下非线性特征子空间中自然存在的判别信号。

Method: 提出高维随机投影激活引导方法（HiDRA），这是一种无需训练的方法，可与现有激活引导方法无缝集成。通过在投影的高维空间中执行激活添加操作，能够捕获线性方法无法触及的更好的判别结构。

Result: 在多种大语言模型家族和基准测试上的实验表明，HiDRA持续优于基线方法，实现了更强的行为控制能力，且没有显著的计算开销。

Conclusion: HiDRA证明了通过高维投影可以有效捕获非线性特征子空间中的判别信号，为大语言模型的行为控制提供了一种更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a key methodology for controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Existing difference-in-means based methods, however, are fundamentally limited: they capture only mean differences between class activations and fail to recover discriminative signals that naturally exist in the nonlinear feature subspace under the superposition hypothesis. Motivated by that, we propose High-Dimensional Random-projection for Activation Steering (HiDRA), a training-free approach that integrates seamlessly with existing activation steering methods. By performing activation addition in the projected high-dimensional space, HiDRA can provably capture a better discriminative structure beyond the reach of linear methods. Experiments across diverse LLM families and benchmarks demonstrate that HiDRA consistently outperforms baseline counterparts, achieving stronger behavioral control without significant computational overhead.

</details>


### [186] [Diversity-Driven Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Nested Pareto Set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15115)
*Yiyi Zhu,Yaolin Wen,Xiang Xia,Xin An,Hanyi Si,Xiang Shu,Yangde Fu,Liang Dou,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DOMOO方法，通过累积风险控制、嵌套Pareto集学习策略和多样性驱动选择策略，解决了离线多目标优化中的分布外问题和解集多样性不足的问题，在收敛性和多样性上均达到最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线多目标优化中，由于无法访问真实目标函数，代理模型在未见设计上的不准确会导致分布外（OOD）问题，使优化器偏向选择真实Pareto前沿极端的低质量解，且缺乏多样性。

Method: 提出DOMOO方法，包含三个核心组件：(1)累积风险控制模块，估计候选解的潜在风险并缓解OOD问题；(2)嵌套Pareto集学习策略，联合学习偏好和PSL参数以适应多样化的Pareto前沿几何形状；(3)多样性驱动选择策略，利用新设计的IGD_offline指标同时考虑多样性和收敛性，提取代表性且分布良好的最终解集。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试上的大量实验表明，DOMOO在收敛性和多样性两个维度上均取得了所有对比方法中的最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: DOMOO有效解决了离线多目标优化中的OOD问题和极端解偏差问题，通过风险控制和多样性驱动机制实现了高质量、多样化的Pareto集，为离线优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has emerged as a powerful approach to solving complex optimization problems involving multiple objectives. In many practical scenarios, function evaluations are unavailable or prohibitively expensive, necessitating optimization solely based on a fixed offline dataset. In this setting, known as offline MOO, the goal is to find out the Pareto set without access to the true objective functions. This setting suffers from the out-of-distribution (OOD) issue, where the surrogate model is not accurate for unseen designs. Due to the OOD issue, surrogate errors may cause the optimizer to select solutions that do not lie on the true Pareto front and are biased toward its extremes. To address this, this paper proposes Diversity-driven Offline Multi-Objective Optimization (DOMOO), which aims to find out a diverse and high-quality set of solutions. First, DOMOO incorporates an accumulative risk control module that estimates the potential risk of candidate solutions and alleviates the OOD issue between the training data and the generated solutions. In addition, a nested Pareto set learning (PSL) strategy is proposed to jointly learn preference and PSL parameters, then optimize them, enabling adaptation to diverse Pareto front geometries. To further enhance solution quality, we design a diversity-driven selection strategy that extracts a representative and well-distributed set of final solutions. To achieve this diversity-driven selection strategy, we propose $\text{IGD}_\text{offline}$, a tailored indicator for the offline setting that considers both diversity and convergence, and avoids the bias of hypervolume indicator. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that DOMOO achieves the best average rank across tasks in both convergence and diversity among the compared methods.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Accuracy: Measuring Bias Acknowledgment in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Responsible AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15127)
*Xian Sun,Wei Gao,Yingshuo Wang,Lingdong Kong,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang,Wenlong Dong,Zhiyuan Zheng,Hrishikesh Paranjape,Abhishek Mandal,Johnny R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出仅用准确率评估推理模型存在盲点，提出了追踪层面的诊断方法来测量模型对注入偏见的易感性和确认率，发现GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4在易感性相近但确认率差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要审查推理过程的教育工具、决策支持系统和审计流程等场景中，仅评估最终答案准确率无法区分模型是否在推理过程中明确标记了注入的偏见内容。这种评估盲点阻碍了负责任的模型评估。

Method: 提出了一个最小化的追踪层面诊断框架，包含两个维度：易感性（偏见是否破坏了原本正确的答案）和确认率（推理追踪是否包含对注入内容的明确定义表面引用）。在GSM8K数据集上进行了数千次偏见注入实验。

Result: GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4具有相似的易感性率（1.3% vs. 1.2%），但在相同评分标准下确认率差异显著（13.0% vs. 75.0%），表明两个模型在最终准确率相近的情况下，对偏见内容的显式识别能力存在巨大差异。

Conclusion: 仅依赖准确率的评估方法无法捕捉模型对偏见内容的处理差异，需要引入追踪层面的诊断指标来实现更负责任的推理模型评估，这对需要透明度和人类监督的应用场景尤为重要。

Abstract: Reasoning models are increasingly used in settings where the final answer is not the only object of review: educational tools may show students intermediate steps, decision-support systems may require human oversight, and audit workflows may inspect traces for misleading or biased input. In such settings, two responses can receive the same final-answer score while differing in whether the trace explicitly flags injected biasing content. Accuracy-only evaluation collapses these cases. We study this gap as a measurement blind spot for responsible evaluation and introduce a minimal trace-level diagnostic with two axes: \emph{susceptibility} (whether the bias breaks a previously correct answer) and \emph{acknowledgment} (whether the trace contains a rubric-defined surface reference to the injected content). Across thousands of biased GSM8K trials, GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4 have similar susceptibility rates ($1.3\%$ vs.\ $1.2\%$) but substantially different acknowledgment rates ($13.0\%$ vs.\ $75.0\%$) under the same rubric.

</details>


### [188] [Contextual Bandits for Maximizing Stimulated Word-of-Mouth Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15146)
*Ahmed Sayeed Faruk,Elena Zheleva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文多臂老虎机的新型框架，通过学习社交网络中用户的个体级溢出概率来优化激励式口碑传播，实验证明该方法能显著提升目标用户选择的精确度和整体奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激励式口碑传播需要识别和定位最容易产生溢出效应的关联用户。然而，溢出概率在个体及其连接之间存在显著异质性，现有方法未能准确估计和利用这种个体间的溢出概率差异，导致目标用户选择不够精准。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的上下文多臂老虎机（contextual multi-armed bandit）框架。该框架能够学习个体级的溢出概率，并基于学习到的概率对关联用户进行排序，以最大化激励式口碑传播带来的奖励。

Result: 在真实网络数据集上的实验表明，考虑溢出异质性的方法能够提高对top-k关联用户的定位精度，在奖励提升方面优于不学习个体溢出效应的基线方法。

Conclusion: 准确理解和估计社交网络中用户间的溢出概率对于提高激励式口碑传播的有效性至关重要，通过学习个体级溢出效应可以显著提升营销策略的性能。

Abstract: Stimulated word-of-mouth is a strategy that promotes information sharing through prompts or incentives. Optimizing stimulated word-of-mouth through social networks requires identifying and targeting connected users who are most susceptible to spillover, a phenomenon where the influence of recommendations extends beyond the immediate audience to impact their connected users. The probability of spillover varies across individuals, and their connections, leading to heterogeneity. Understanding and accurately estimating the spillover probabilities among users in social networks is crucial for improving the effectiveness of stimulated word-of-mouth. To address this, we present a novel contextual multi-armed bandit framework that learns individual spillover probabilities and ranks connected users to maximize rewards from stimulated word-of-mouth. Experiments on real-world network datasets demonstrate that accounting for spillover heterogeneity enhances the targeting precision of top-$k$ connected users, boosting rewards and outperforming baseline methods that do not learn individual spillover effects.

</details>


### [189] [False Sense of Safety in Selective Signal Classification: Auditing Bound Tightness and Exchangeability for Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15153)
*Jingwen Zhou,Mingzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了选择性预测中分布自由风险控制在信号域检测器上的有效性，发现常用方法存在严重超预算问题，而认证方法在破坏交换性假设的分组部署中也会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性预测中的分布自由风险控制承诺在置信度1-δ下误差率低于预算α，但该承诺在实际信号域检测器（机器异常声音检测和AI生成图像取证）中的有效性尚待验证，需要审计不同校准规则的实际表现。

Method: 在信号域检测器上审计四种校准规则：未认证的经验阈值法（NAIVE）以及认证的Hoeffding、Clopper-Pearson（CP）和投注（WSR）上置信界，通过合成试验和真实数据分割进行系统评估。

Result: 发现三个关键问题：(i) NAIVE阈值法在49-73%的合成试验和高达68%的真实数据分割中超预算；(ii) CP和WSR能认证显著覆盖率而Hoeffding无法认证，且在可交换分割下零超预算；(iii) 在分组部署中认证规则有9-30%超预算率，远超δ，表明交换性前提被破坏。

Conclusion: NAIVE方法提供虚假安全感而非真正的理论保证；紧致的置信界（CP、WSR）很重要；在分组部署场景下，交换性假设失效是根本问题，需要保守的分组阈值以恢复有效性但会损失覆盖率。

Abstract: Selective prediction with distribution-free risk control promises that, with confidence 1-delta over the calibration draw, the error rate of accepted inputs stays below a user budget alpha. We audit this promise on signal-domain detectors -- machine anomalous-sound detection (ASD) and AI-generated-image forensics -- for four calibration rules: uncertified empirical thresholding (NAIVE) and certified Hoeffding, Clopper-Pearson (CP), and betting (WSR) upper confidence bounds. We report three findings. (i) NAIVE thresholding, common in practice, exceeds its declared budget in 49-73% of synthetic trials (n=200 calibration points) and in up to 68% of real-data splits: a false sense of safety rather than a broken theorem, since the rule never had a certificate. (ii) Tightness matters: CP and WSR certify substantial coverage where Hoeffding certifies none, with zero observed budget overruns under exchangeable splits. (iii) Under grouped deployment (unseen machine types or generators), certified rules overrun in 9-30% of trials -- far above delta -- showing the failure lies in the broken exchangeability premise, not in the bounds; a conservative per-group threshold restores validity at a severe coverage cost.

</details>


### [190] [Semantic Reasoning in Medicine: The Role of Knowledge Graphs Across Five Key Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15155)
*Haniye Sherafatmandjoo,Mohammad Akbari,Zahed Rahmati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于知识图谱在医疗领域应用的综述，系统回顾了知识图谱在临床决策支持、疾病预测、精准医疗等应用方向以及基于EHR、文献等数据构建医疗知识图谱的方法研究，分析了当前面临的知识覆盖不全、数据异构性、隐私伦理等挑战，为未来医疗AI系统的发展提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱作为整合和推理复杂生物医学与临床数据的有前景的解决方案，在医疗决策、预测、推荐和个性化护理中发挥重要作用。尽管已有研究展示了其在多种医疗应用中的价值，但仍面临知识覆盖有限、数据源对齐困难、推理方法脆弱以及隐私偏见等重大挑战，亟需系统梳理研究现状以指导未来发展。

Method: 从应用导向和方法导向两个维度对医疗领域知识图谱研究进行回顾与分类。应用方面涵盖临床决策支持系统、疾病与治疗结果预测、健康推荐系统、精准医疗和医学问答系统；方法方面关注从EHR、临床文本、生物医学文献和网络资源构建知识图谱的框架，涉及本体、语义网技术、深度学习信息抽取和混合神经符号流水线等技术基础。

Result: 知识图谱在医疗应用中能够增强可解释性、语义连贯性和患者特异性推理能力。当前研究已展示其在多样化医疗场景中的实用性。然而仍存在知识覆盖有限且碎片化、异构数据源对齐困难、稠密多关系图上的推理与表示学习方法脆弱、隐私保护与偏见及责任归属等未解决问题。

Conclusion: 通过分析发展趋势、系统架构和评估实践，本文为知识图谱驱动的医疗AI系统的未来发展提供指导，支持其安全有效地集成到医疗环境中，并指出开放研究方向以应对当前技术局限性与伦理挑战。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) have emerged as a promising solution for integrating and reasoning over complex biomedical and clinical data in healthcare. By representing structured relationships among entities such as diseases, drugs, symptoms, and patient records, KGs provide a semantic backbone for decision-making, prediction, recommendation, and personalized care. Recent advances have demonstrated their utility across diverse medical applications--including clinical decision support systems, disease and treatment outcome prediction, health recommender systems, precision medicine, and medical question answering--where KGs often enhance interpretability, semantic coherence, and patient-specific reasoning. In parallel, a growing body of work focuses on medical KG generation itself, proposing frameworks that construct graphs from EHRs, clinical narratives, biomedical literature, and web resources using ontologies, semantic web technologies, deep-learning-based information extraction, and hybrid neuro-symbolic pipelines. Despite this progress, significant challenges remain, including limited and fragmented knowledge coverage, difficulties in aligning heterogeneous data sources, the fragility of current reasoning and representation-learning methods on dense multi-relational graphs, and unresolved issues related to privacy, bias, and accountability. This survey reviews and categorizes current research on KGs in medicine along both application-oriented and methodology-oriented dimensions, discusses their benefits and technical foundations, and outlines key limitations and open research directions. By analyzing trends, architectures, and evaluation practices, this work aims to guide future developments in KG-driven medical AI systems and support their safe and effective integration into healthcare environments.

</details>


### [191] [PolyKV: Heterogeneous Retention and Allocation for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15157)
*Chao Fei,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PolyKV框架，通过层级别的KV压缩方法选择与非均匀预算分配，在相同缓存预算下显著恢复长文本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法对所有Transformer层采用统一的压缩策略和缓存预算，忽略了不同层在预填充和解码阶段可能扮演不同角色，因而需要不同的驱逐策略和缓存容量。

Method: 提出PolyKV层级KV缓存优化框架：(1)基于层级信号将每层路由至适合的KV压缩策略；(2)在固定总预算下分配非均匀缓存预算；(3)实现现有KV缓存方法的异构组合。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B上，512-token平均KV预算下，PolyKV分别恢复了54.5%和25.7%的性能差距（相比最强单策略基线与FullKV之间）。在128-1024预算范围内，持续超越最强基线1.7%-6.4%，对应恢复FullKV性能差距的40.0%-54.5%。

Conclusion: 层级别的异构KV缓存压缩策略组合与非均匀预算分配能有效提升长文本任务的性能表现，验证了考虑层级差异的重要性。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for reducing the memory cost of long-context large language model inference. Existing approaches, however, typically apply a single compression policy and a uniform cache budget across all transformer layers. This uniform design ignores the fact that different layers can play different roles during prefill and decoding, and may therefore require different eviction strategies and cache capacities. We present PolyKV, a layer-wise KV cache optimization framework that considers design space with method selection and budget allocation. PolyKV routes each layer to a suitable KV compression policy based on layer-level signals, while assigning non-uniform budgets under a fixed total budget. This formulation enables heterogeneous compositions of existing KV cache methods. Experiments on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B show that, under the same 512-token average KV budget, PolyKV recovers 54.5% and 25.7% of the LongBench performance gap between the strongest single-policy baseline and FullKV, respectively. Across 128-1024 budget sweep, PolyKV consistently improves over the strongest baseline by 1.7%-6.4%, corresponding to 40.0%-54.5% recovery of the FullKV gap.

</details>


### [192] [Towards a Unified Generative Model for Scarce Time Series with Domain Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15172)
*Zihao Yao,Qi Zheng,Jiankai Zuo,Yaying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TimeMoDE框架，这是一种将扩散Transformer与专家混合机制相结合的新方法，专门用于解决数据稀缺场景下的时间序列生成问题，在少样本设置下表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列生成方法大多假设训练数据充足，这严重限制了它们在数据稀缺场景下的有效性。现实应用中经常面临数据不足的问题，因此需要开发能够在低数据环境下有效工作的时间序列生成方法。

Method: 提出TimeMoDE框架，集成了扩散Transformer与专家混合机制(MoE)：1)在大规模多领域数据集上预训练以提取领域无关的时间表示和领域特定信息；2)设计领域提示(Domain Prompts)来调节专家分配，缓解捕捉数据集间关系的局限性；3)融合扩散时间步信号，使专家具备对时间序列退化变化的感知能力，适应不同去噪阶段的需求。

Result: 大量实验表明，TimeMoDE在各种低数据设置下都优于现有方法，成功建立了先进的时间序列少样本生成的创新范式。

Conclusion: TimeMoDE为数据稀缺场景下的时间序列生成提供了一个有效的解决方案，通过领域适应性和扩散阶段感知的结合，实现了更好的泛化性能和生成质量。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic time series with generative models has wide-ranging applications in real-world scenarios. Despite recent progress, most existing methods are trained under the assumption of abundant training data, which substantially limits their effectiveness in data-scarce settings. In this paper, we propose TimeMoDE, a novel framework that integrates Diffusion Transformers with Mixture-of-Experts to exploit both domain adaptability and diffusion-stage awareness for time series generation under data scarcity. It is pre-trained on a large-scale collection of multi-domain datasets to extract domain-agnostic temporal representations and domain-specific information benefiting generalization during fine-tuning. We propose Domain Prompts to condition expert assignment for indistinguishable noised tokens, mitigating the limitations of capturing inter-dataset relationships. Moreover, we incorporate diffusion timestep signals to equip the experts with awareness of time series degradation variations, facilitating adaptive calibrate to stage-dependent denoising requirements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TimeMoDE outperforms existing methods under diverse low-data settings. It establishes an innovative paradigm for advanced time series few-shot generation.

</details>


### [193] [StarOR: Synergizing Tree Search and Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15197)
*Jiajun Li,Yu Ding,Shisi Guan,Ran Hou,Wanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了StarOR框架，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)与测试时强化学习，通过在搜索过程中更新临时LoRA适配器，实现了优化建模的最优性能，在5个优化基准测试中超越现有方法和前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的优化建模方法依赖大规模标注数据，适应新问题分布成本高；一次性生成在层次化建模中脆弱，早期符号错误会传播导致无效公式；现有基于搜索的测试时扩展方法使用固定策略，重复展开会继承相似的建模偏差，中间决策的信用分配有限。

Method: 提出StarOR框架，将建模过程分解为四个阶段，结合MCTS与测试时强化学习；在每个非终端节点通过GRPO更新临时LoRA适配器；使用MCTS生成的兄弟节点作为局部比较集，将搜索时探索转化为实例特定的策略优化；设计无监督多面奖励系统为中间公式决策提供细粒度反馈，无需真实标签。

Result: 在五个优化基准测试上达到最优性能；即使使用4B骨干网络也能超越现有方法和前沿大模型。

Conclusion: StarOR通过协同搜索与自适应机制成功解决了层次化优化建模中的符号错误传播和建模偏差问题，证明了测试时强化学习在优化建模任务中的有效性，为自动化优化建模提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Optimization modeling is inherently hierarchical, requiring a precise sequence of symbolic commitments. Traditional learning-based automated optimization modeling methods improve modeling policies through large-scale annotated or curated training data, but are costly to adapt to new problem distributions. Meanwhile, one-shot generation remains brittle in hierarchical modeling, where early symbolic errors can propagate into invalid formulations. Test-time scaling offers a promising alternative by enabling structural exploration with additional instance-level computation; however, existing search-based methods typically rely on a fixed policy, causing repeated rollouts to inherit similar modeling biases and providing limited credit assignment for intermediate decisions. To address these limitations, we propose StarOR, a synergistic search-and-adaptation framework that couples MCTS with Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for optimization modeling. StarOR decomposes the modeling process into four stages and updates a transient LoRA adapter via GRPO at each non-terminal node. By using MCTS-generated siblings as local comparison sets, StarOR transforms search-time exploration into instance-specific policy refinement. Moreover, an unsupervised multi-faceted reward system provides fine-grained feedback for intermediate formulation decisions without ground-truth labels. Experiments across five optimization benchmarks show that StarOR achieves state-of-the-art performance even with a 4B backbone, outperforming existing methods and the frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [194] [Controlled Dynamics Attractor Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15207)
*Cheng Zhang,Minnan Luo,Zesheng Yang,Ming Li,Yong-Jin Liu,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出受控动力学吸引子Transformer（CDAT），通过融合Mo-vMF注意力能量、Hopfield细化能量以及CANN启发的兴奋-抑制调制，将吸引子动力学与现代能量注意力结合，在图异常检测和图分类任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的自注意力机制与联想记忆框架分别擅长表示学习和可解释检索，但后者的连续时间推理动力学缺乏经典连续吸引子神经网络（CANN）的生物合理性，需填补此理论空白。

Method: 提出CDAT架构：将混合von Mises-Fisher注意力能量与Hopfield细化能量耦合，引入CANN启发的兴奋-抑制调制增强能量下降，构建拓扑约束动力系统，通过构造性耗散分析形式化建立受控推理动力学。

Result: CDAT在图异常检测和图分类的多个基准测试上取得了最先进的性能，验证了结构化吸引子动力学在图任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: CDAT成功桥接了吸引子式动力学与现代能量注意力模型，为可解释、生物合理且高性能的Transformer变体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have dramatically advanced representation learning and inference in deep models through self-attention mechanisms. In parallel,associative memory (AM) frameworks map representations onto energy landscapes, offering interpretable retrieval mechanisms. However, their continuous-time inference dynamics lack the biological plausibility of classical Continuous Attractor Neural Networks (CANNs). To bridge this gap, we propose Controlled Dynamics Attractor Transformer (CDAT), which couples a mixture von Mises-Fisher (Mo-vMF) attention energy with a Hopfield refinement energy, while augmenting energy descent with a CANN-inspired excitation-inhibition modulation. CDAT instantiates a topology-constrained dynamical system whose couplings encode relational structure among tokens, thereby linking attractor-style dynamics to modern energy-based attention. We further provide a constructive dissipation analysis to formally establish their controlled inference dynamics. Benefiting from these robust and structured dynamics, CDAT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks in graph anomaly detection and graph classification.

</details>


### [195] [Can Neural Networks Achieve Optimal Computational-statistical Tradeoff? An Analysis on Single-Index Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15219)
*Siyu Chen,Beining Wu,Miao Lu,Zhuoran Yang,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度的算法，证明了神经网络在高斯单指数模型学习中可以达到最优的计算-统计权衡，样本复杂度匹配SQ下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神经网络是否能通过基于梯度的方法在学习高斯单指数模型时达到最优的计算-统计权衡。已有研究显示，在统计查询（SQ）框架下，任何多项式时间算法需要Ω(d^{s^\star/2}∨d)样本，但神经网络能否达到这一复杂度尚不清楚。

Method: 受标签变换和景观平滑等技术启发，提出了一种统一的基于梯度的算法来训练两层神经网络。该方法适应于多种损失和激活函数，覆盖广泛的现有方法。对于稀疏设置，引入了新颖的权重扰动技术来利用稀疏结构。

Result: 算法学习到的特征表示与未知信号θ^\star高度对齐，样本复杂度为Õ(d^{s^\star/2}∨d)，对于所有生成指数s^\star≥1，在多对数因子内匹配SQ下界。对于k-稀疏设置，方法在多对数因子内匹配Õ̃(k^{s^\star})的下界。

Conclusion: 证明了神经网络可以高效地学习高斯单指数模型，达到理论最优的样本复杂度。提出的框架，特别是权重扰动技术，具有独立的研究价值，并为其他问题如稀疏张量PCA提供了潜在的基于梯度的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we tackle the following question: Can neural networks trained with gradient-based methods achieve the optimal computational-statistical tradeoff in learning Gaussian single-index models? Prior research has shown that any polynomial-time algorithm under the statistical query (SQ) framework requires $Ω(d^{s^\star/2}\lor d)$ samples, where $s^\star$ is the generative exponent representing the intrinsic difficulty of learning the underlying model. However, it remains unknown whether neural networks can achieve this sample complexity. Inspired by prior techniques such as label transformation and landscape smoothing for learning single-index models, we propose a unified gradient-based algorithm for training a two-layer neural network in polynomial time. Our method is adaptable to a variety of loss and activation functions, covering a broad class of existing approaches. We show that our algorithm learns a feature representation that strongly aligns with the unknown signal $θ^\star$, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O} (d^{s^\star/2} \lor d)$, matching the SQ lower bound up to a polylogarithmic factor for all generative exponents $s^\star\geq 1$. Furthermore, we extend our approach to the setting where $θ^\star$ is $k$-sparse for $k = o(\sqrt{d})$ by introducing a novel weight perturbation technique that leverages the sparsity structure. We derive a corresponding SQ lower bound of order $\widetildeΩ(k^{s^\star})$, matched by our method up to a polylogarithmic factor. Our framework, especially the weight perturbation technique, is of independent interest, and suggests potential gradient-based solutions to other problems such as sparse tensor PCA.

</details>


### [196] [Edu-Theater: A Data-Efficient Agent Framework for Scalable Learner Behavior Simulation through Staging Roll-Call](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15225)
*Weibo Gao,Qi Liu,Linan Yue,Zheng Zhang,Yichao Du,Fangzhou Yao,Ao Yu,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于群体感知的学习者模拟范式（Edu-Theater），通过构建群体水平先验知识并进行少量诊断性查询，在减少LLM调用次数的同时实现了更高的模拟精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习者模拟方法以个体为中心，需要为每个学习者构建密集的交互历史并迭代推断潜在知识状态，这种方法不仅数据和计算密集，而且在冷启动场景下表现脆弱；同时大规模学习者-任务交互数据的收集成本高昂，且受隐私和学习者参与度限制。

Method: 提出了群体感知点名模拟范式，首先构建群体水平的熟练度先验，然后通过少量针对性诊断查询细化个体学习者状态；基于此范式开发了Edu-Theater系统，这是一个由LLM驱动的智能体系统，包含教师智能体和对学习者日志的回溯点名探测机制。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Edu-Theater在显著减少LLM调用次数的情况下实现了更高的模拟精度，生成的合成数据能够有效增强自适应测试等下游应用。

Conclusion: Edu-Theater证明了群体感知模拟范式的有效性，能够在不需要密集的个体历史数据的情况下实现可扩展的未来行为模拟，为解决数据稀缺和隐私约束下的学习者模拟问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large-scale learner-task interaction data are crucial for intelligent educational systems but are costly to collect and constrained by privacy and learner engagement. Learner simulators play a critical role in simulating scalable learner behavior without the need for continuous involvement of real learners. However, existing methods are predominantly \textbf{individual-centric}, pairing a simulator with each learner to iteratively infer latent knowledge states from dense interaction histories, which is both data- and computation-intensive, and fragile in cold-start scenarios. We propose a \textbf{cohort-aware roll-call simulation paradigm} that first constructs cohort-level proficiency priors and refines individual learner states through a small number of targeted diagnostic queries. Based on this paradigm, we introduce \textbf{Edu-Theater}, an LLM-powered agent system that performs cohort-aware learner simulation via a teacher agent and retrospective roll-call probing over learner logs. Edu-Theater enables scalable future behavior simulation without the need for dense per-learner histories. Experiments on two real-world datasets demonstrate that Edu-Theater achieves higher simulation accuracy with significantly fewer LLM calls, producing synthetic data that enhances downstream applications such as adaptive testing.

</details>


### [197] [EnvShip-Bench: An Environment-Enhanced Benchmark for Short-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15240)
*Kun Ma,Qilong Han,Chengjing Song,Jingzheng Yao,Hao Wang,Changmao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EnvShip-Bench，一个统一的船舶短期轨迹预测基准，基于DMA和NOAA的大规模AIS数据构建，支持纯轨迹、环境感知和交互感知预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开海事AIS资源存在预测协议不一致、数据质量不均、缺乏基准可用的上下文标注等问题，阻碍了公平比较和上下文感知建模，因此需要建立统一的基准。

Method: 基于丹麦海事局和NOAA的大规模原始AIS数据，通过统一处理流程构建基准，采用标准化预测协议（10分钟观测+10分钟预测，20秒采样，船舶中心局部坐标），提供大规模核心基准、质量优先的紧凑子集，以及同步的环境和邻近船舶上下文扩展。

Result: EnvShip-Bench支持在统一评估框架下进行纯轨迹、环境感知和交互感知预测，大量基准统计和分析证明了其作为标准化、可扩展、上下文感知的海事轨迹预测研究基础的有效性。

Conclusion: EnvShip-Bench为海事轨迹预测研究提供了标准化、可扩展和上下文感知的基础平台，解决了现有资源的关键限制，有望推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction is important for intelligent shipping, maritime surveillance, and navigation safety. However, existing public maritime AIS resources are often limited by inconsistent forecasting protocols, uneven data quality, and the lack of benchmark-ready contextual annotations, which hinder fair comparison and context-aware modeling. To address this gap, we present EnvShip-Bench, a unified benchmark for short-term vessel trajectory prediction built from large-scale raw AIS data from the Danish Maritime Authority (DMA) and NOAA through a common processing pipeline. EnvShip-Bench adopts a standardized forecasting protocol with 10 minutes of observation, 10 minutes of prediction, and 20-second sampling in vessel-centric local metric coordinates. Beyond the large-scale core benchmark, it provides a quality-first compact subset for efficient and reproducible experimentation, together with synchronized environmental and nearby-vessel context extensions. As a result, EnvShip-Bench supports trajectory-only, environment-aware, and interaction-aware forecasting under a unified evaluation framework. Extensive benchmark statistics and analysis demonstrate that EnvShip-Bench offers a standardized, extensible, and context-aware foundation for maritime trajectory forecasting research.

</details>


### [198] [M-CTX: Exact and Scalable Spatial Context Retrieval for Trajectory Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15244)
*Kun Ma,Qilong Han,Chengjing Song,Jingzheng Yao,Xiao Han,Yuee Zhou,Changmao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出M-CTX框架，将轨迹预测中的空间上下文构建重构为空间数据库查询问题，在500万锚点的数据集上将17 CPU天的构建时间缩短至1.8小时（226倍加速），并实现精确检索与可选的64倍SDF压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代轨迹预测器依赖外部空间上下文（地图几何、有符号距离场SDF、邻近移动物体）来提升预测质量，但为每个训练锚点构建空间上下文已成为隐藏的系统瓶颈。在代表性海事AIS流水线中，548万锚点的空间上下文构建需要约17个CPU天，主导了下游预测器的成本。

Method: 提出M-CTX框架，将上下文构建重构为"一次摄入、多次查询"的空间数据库工作负载，用可组合的索引支持算子替换三个暴力阶段：OSM范围检索、SDF计算和移动船只邻居查找。核心包括：(1)学习的范围索引后端BR-LZ，提供召回完整的MBR重叠范围检索；(2)将候选放大相对于全局扩展基线减少1.1x-2.7x；(3)可选存储模式支持SDF压缩。

Result: 在四个海事区域、八个基线系统、高达4000万空间特征的合成工作负载和1000万条AIS流上验证，M-CTX精确复现参考上下文。在548万锚点语料库上，将上下文构建从约17 CPU天减少至1.8小时，实现226倍端到端加速。可选存储模式将SDF上下文压缩64倍，ADE变化仅0.04米。

Conclusion: 研究确立了精确空间上下文检索作为现代轨迹分析中的一类数据库问题，证明通过数据库方法可以显著缓解轨迹预测系统的性能瓶颈，同时保持预测精度。

Abstract: Modern trajectory predictors increasingly condition on external spatial context, such as map geometry, signed distance fields (SDFs), and nearby moving agents. While this context improves prediction quality, constructing it for every training anchor has become a hidden systems bottleneck. In a representative maritime AIS pipeline, spatial context construction requires roughly 17 CPU-days for a 5.48M-anchor corpus, dominating the cost of the downstream predictor. We present M-CTX, an exact and scalable spatial context-retrieval framework for trajectory analytics. M-CTX recasts context construction as an ingest-once, query-many spatial database workload and replaces three brute-force stages -- OSM range retrieval, SDF computation, and moving-vessel neighbour lookup -- with composable, index-backed operators. Its learned range-index backend, BR-LZ, provides recall-complete MBR-overlap range retrieval and reduces candidate amplification by 1.1x--2.7x relative to global-expansion one-curve baselines. Across four maritime regions, eight baseline systems, synthetic workloads with up to 40M spatial features, and 10^7-record AIS streams, M-CTX reproduces the reference context exactly. On the 5.48M-anchor corpus, it reduces context construction from about 17 CPU-days to 1.8 hours, a measured 226x end-to-end speed-up. An optional storage mode further compresses SDF context by 64x with only a 0.04 m ADE change. These results establish exact spatial context retrieval as a first-class database problem in modern trajectory analytics. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/mark000071/M-CTX-Traj.

</details>


### [199] [Exploring Starts Are Not Enough: Counterexamples and a Fix for Monte Carlo Exploring Starts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15247)
*Octave Oliviers,Glenn Vinnicombe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建反例证明MCES算法（包括初始访问和首次访问两种变体）可能收敛到次优解，并为初始访问MCES提出了收敛性修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛探索起点（MCES）的渐近行为是强化学习中一个长期存在的开放性问题，即使在表格设置下其收敛性也未被证明。作者旨在通过构造反例来研究MCES的收敛性质。

Method: 作者构建了新的反例来展示初始访问和首次访问MCES都会收敛到次优解。对于初始访问MCES，提出了基于状态逐一调整学习率的方法（与更新频率成反比），而不是使用传统的均匀化方法。

Result: （1）证明了即使贪婪动作比非贪婪动作更新更频繁，初始访问MCES仍可能存在稳定的次优解；（2）通过按状态逐一调整学习率可保证收敛到最优；（3）首次访问MCES也可能收敛到次优解；（4）证明了单靠探索起点不能保证收敛到最优性。

Conclusion: 这些结果基本解决了一个基础性的开放问题，表明收敛性关键取决于应用于不同动作的更新的相对大小和频率。这使得学习率的选择以及探索与利用之间的平衡成为MCES分析和可扩展蒙特卡洛控制方法实现的核心考虑因素。

Abstract: The asymptotic behaviour of Monte Carlo Exploring Starts (MCES) is a long-standing open question in reinforcement learning, even in the tabular setting. We investigated the convergence properties of tabular MCES by constructing examples in which the algorithm converges to suboptimal solutions. This paper presents new counterexamples for both initial-visit and first-visit MCES and gives a convergence-restoring modification for the initial-visit case. We show that stable suboptimal solutions may exist for initial-visit MCES with sample-average updates even when greedy actions are updated more often than non-greedy actions on average. However, by scaling learning rates inversely to update frequencies on a state-by-state basis, convergence to optimality is guaranteed. Unlike previous uniformisation methods, this modification is applicable to large-scale problems that require approximating the estimated value function. We then extend the example to show that sample-average first-visit MCES may also converge to suboptimal solutions. This largely settles a fundamental open problem and shows that exploring starts alone do not guarantee convergence to optimality. More broadly, these results highlight that convergence depends critically on the relative size and frequency of updates applied to different actions, making the choice of learning rates and the balance between exploration and exploitation central to the analysis of MCES and the implementation of scalable Monte Carlo control methods.

</details>


### [200] [AI for Social Good: An Investigation of the Causal Relationship Between Environmental Regulations and Their Effects on Air Pollution in London, UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15257)
*Yang Han,Jacqueline CK Lam,Victor OK Li,Yiu-Wai Man*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个不确定性感知的贝叶斯深度学习框架，用于评估2010-2020年伦敦空气污染法规对PM$_{2.5}$浓度的影响，发现法规平均降低PM$_{2.5}$浓度1.88 μg/m³（相对降低12.35%），证明了因果AI在环境治理中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染法规评估面临两大挑战：一是政策实施非随机，二是污染轨迹受气象、社会经济变化、时间趋势和重叠干预等多重因素影响。传统的效应估计方法难以处理这些复杂的混杂因素和政策实施的选择性偏差，因此需要开发新的方法框架来准确评估法规的因果效应。

Method: 研究提出了一个不确定性感知的贝叶斯深度学习框架，包含：(1)贝叶斯LSTM捕获环境和社会经济协变量的时间依赖性；(2)贝叶斯嵌入层表示时间和法规状态输入；(3)法规状态预测分支支持基于倾向得分的调整以处理非随机政策实施。框架整合了伦敦监测站的每日PM$_{2.5}$观测数据、气象协变量、年度社会经济指标、时间指标以及32项政策措施的法规状态数据。通过比较观测的PM$_{2.5}$浓度与假设无法规情景下的反事实预测来估计法规效应，并通过重复贝叶斯训练运行和Bootstrap重采样来量化不确定性。

Result: 伦敦的空气污染法规与PM$_{2.5}$平均降低1.88 μg/m³相关（相对降低12.35%），95%置信区间为1.64-2.12 μg/m³。效应估计显示：2013年之前效果有限，2013-2017年逐渐明显，2018和2019年最强。研究发现持续和累积的监管干预对改善伦敦空气质量有可测量的贡献。

Conclusion: 研究证明了不确定性感知的因果AI方法可以支持环境问责、公共健康保护和循证治理。该框架能够有效处理政策非随机实施和多重混杂因素，为环境决策提供了科学依据，展示了AI在环境政策评估中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Air pollution regulation is central to urban public health governance, but estimating its effects is difficult because policies are implemented non-randomly and pollution trajectories are shaped by meteorology, socioeconomic change, temporal trends, and overlapping interventions. This study develops an uncertainty-aware Bayesian deep learning framework to estimate the aggregate effect of air pollution regulations on PM$_{2.5}$ concentrations in London from 2010 to 2020.
