Conclusion: MosaicQuant证明了通过内点-离群点分离和统一4-bit表示，可以在不牺牲精度的情况下实现高效量化。ZipperEngine的设计解决了稀疏残差计算与密集计算的融合问题，为LLM量化提供了一个既保持精度又保证实际加速效果的实用解决方案。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization significantly reduces the memory footprint and accelerates the inference of large language models (LLMs). However, its limited bit-width representation struggles to faithfully capture both dense common values (\emph{inliers}) and rare large-magnitude values (\emph{outliers}), causing substantial accuracy degradation. Existing mixed-precision methods mitigate this by retaining outliers in high precision, but at the cost of breaking the uniformity of low-bit execution, introducing precision conversion and extra data movement that undermine practical speedup. We propose \textbf{MosaicQuant}, a unified 4-bit LLM quantization paradigm built on a novel principle of \emph{inlier--outlier disaggregation}. Rather than elevating outlier precision, MosaicQuant quantizes the full weight matrix into a dense 4-bit base component, where inliers are captured faithfully while outlier are inevitably quantized. A sparse 4-bit residual component is then introduced to compensate for these quantization errors, selectively targeting the most error-critical weight blocks where output distortion is shown to be concentrated. However, a unified representation alone is insufficient, as naïvely executing the sparse residual as a separate kernel still breaks the unified low-bit inference pipeline. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{ZipperEngine}, which fuses sparse block computation into the dense 4-bit GEMM kernel via an overlapped pipeline, unifying not only the representation but also the execution into a single coherent low-bit inference pipeline. Extensive experiments on LLaMA3 and Qwen3 demonstrate that MosaicQuant preserves near-FP16 accuracy while achieving up to $1.24\times$ speedup over the W16A16 baseline.

</details>


### [237] [Z-Plane Neural Networks: Bounded Geometric Activation Replaces ReLU and LayerNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15669)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Z-Plane神经网络，通过几何激活函数Radial Bounding将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的2D相量束，从而在无需ReLU和LayerNorm的情况下实现深层网络的稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度神经网络依赖于欧几里得标量激活（如ReLU）和全局归一化技术（如LayerNorm）来防止梯度不稳定，但这些机制本质上会导致神经元死亡、丢失关键的方向信息，并破坏特征表示的正交性。受生物轴突频率调制传输机制的启发，作者旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的2D相量束。引入几何激活函数Radial Bounding（x / max(1, ||x||₂)），该函数限制能量幅度同时保留相位（方向）信息，数学上证明了其保持1-Lipschitz连续性并通过保留切向梯度防止梯度消失。

Result: 100层的Z-Plane MLP完全不需要ReLU和LayerNorm，在MNIST数据集上成功收敛，达到98.34%的准确率，并展现出绝对的数值稳定性，证明仅靠有界几何激活就足以实现稳定的深度学习。

Conclusion: 有界几何激活函数单独使用就足以实现深层网络的稳定训练，无需传统的标量激活函数和归一化技术，为深度神经网络的设计提供了新的思路和方向。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks rely on Euclidean scalar activations (e.g., ReLU) and global normalization techniques (e.g., LayerNorm) to prevent gradient instability in deep architectures. However, these mechanisms inherently cause dead neurons, discard critical directional information, and destroy the orthogonality of feature representations. Inspired by the frequency-modulation transmission of biological axons, we propose the Z-Plane Neural Network, which maps hidden states into 2D phasor bundles on a hypersphere. We introduce a novel geometric activation function, Radial Bounding($\mathbf{x} / \max(1, \|\mathbf{x}\|_2)$), which limits the energy magnitude while preserving the phase (direction). We demonstrate mathematically that this isotropic activation maintains 1-Lipschitz continuity and prevents gradient vanishing by preserving tangential gradients. Empirically, a 100-layer Z-Plane Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-entirely devoid of ReLU and LayerNorm-successfully converges on the MNIST dataset with 98.34% accuracy and absolute numerical stability, proving that bounded geometric activation alone is sufficient for stable deep learning.

</details>


### [238] [The Reservoir Attention Network: Cross-Pass State in Pretrained Transformers via Content-Addressable Reservoir Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15678)
*Emma Leonhart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reservoir Attention Network (RAN)，通过在预训练transformer中间层的attention中注入固定的随机初始化reservoir来在forward passes之间传递状态。实验从GPT-2到Qwen2.5，证明未经训练的循环动态本身就足以传递可用的跨pass状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究是否仅靠未经训练的循环动态（固定随机reservoir）就足以在forward passes之间传递有用状态，将训练后的recurrence作为更昂贵的互补研究方向。这为compute-limited场景提供了一种轻量级的跨pass状态传递方案。

Method: 在预训练transformer的中间层attention中注入一个固定的、随机初始化的reservoir。该reservoir在训练过程中保持固定（不训练），用于在forward passes之间携带状态。实验覆盖GPT-2 (124M, 355M)到Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B)，在单个消费级GPU上运行，使用最小化探测任务来隔离单个机制。

Result: 证明了固定随机reservoir能够成功在forward passes之间传递状态，未经训练的循环动态本身就足以传递可用的跨pass状态。实验结果为RAN架构的可行性提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: RAN架构证明了未经训练的recurrent dynamics可以有效地在forward passes之间传递状态，这为未来compute-limited场景下的always-alive agent研究奠定了基础，同时将训练recurrence作为独立的、更昂贵的互补研究方向。

Abstract: A feasibility and dynamics study of the Reservoir Attention Network (RAN), an architecture that injects a fixed, randomly-initialized reservoir into the mid-layer attention of a pretrained transformer to carry state across forward passes. Experiments span GPT-2 (124M, 355M) to Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) on a single consumer GPU. The tasks are minimal probes chosen to isolate individual mechanisms; the broader always-alive agent vision is treated throughout as compute-limited future work, not a claim of this paper. The reservoir is left untrained (fixed random) by design: this isolates whether untrained recurrent dynamics alone suffice to carry usable cross-pass state, leaving trained recurrence as a complementary, more expensive direction.

</details>


### [239] [ReQAT: Achieving Full-Precision Reasoning Accuracy with 4-bit Floating-Point Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15682)
*Janghwan Lee,Sihwa Lee,Jinseok Kim,Yongjik Kim,Jieun Lim,Jinwook Oh,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReQAT框架，通过针对低熵符号token的量化训练策略，首次实现了大推理模型的FP4全量化（W4A4KV4）部署，在恢复精度的同时提供了最高3.9倍的吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型通过长链式思考实现强问题求解能力，但全精度推理成本高且KV缓存占用大。FP4格式可实现高效部署，但全量化（W4A4KV4）会导致严重的推理性能退化，现有PTQ和QAT方法均无法有效恢复。

Method: 提出ReQAT推理中心FP4训练框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ)通过重访相同推理轨迹聚焦关键低熵决策；(ii) Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM)在低熵位置增强置信度；(iii) Q-FIT量化友好初始化联合校准RoPE一致的KV缓存变换以稳定QAT。

Result: 在相同训练预算下，ReQAT不仅恢复而且超越了BF16微调精度，在NVIDIA DGX Spark上实现最高3.9倍吞吐量加速，在B200上实现3.1倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过识别FP4量化失败集中在低熵符号token（如数字和运算符）这一关键洞察，ReQAT成功解决了全量化导致的推理退化问题，为大推理模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong problem-solving through long chain-of-thought, but their deployment is constrained by the high cost of full-precision inference and growing KV cache footprints. Microscaled FP4 formats enable efficient FP4 deployment; however, fully quantizing weights, activations, and KV caches (W4A4KV4) causes severe reasoning degradation that existing PTQ and QAT fail to recover. We identify that FP4 failures concentrate on low-entropy tokens--precise symbolic commitments such as digits and operators--where quantization noise inflates sampling errors that cascade through reasoning traces. Based on this insight, we propose ReQAT, a reasoning-centric FP4 training framework with three components: (i) Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ), which revisits identical reasoning traces to focus updates on critical low-entropy decisions; (ii) Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM), which reinforces confidence at low-entropy positions; and (iii) Q-FIT, a quantization-friendly initialization that jointly calibrates RoPE-consistent KV cache transformations to stabilize QAT. Under the same training budget, ReQAT not only recovers but surpasses BF16 fine-tuning accuracy, while delivering up to 3.9x throughput speedup on NVIDIA DGX Spark and 3.1x on B200.

</details>


### [240] [Multi-Fidelity SINDy: Sparse Discovery of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems with Fidelity-Weighted Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15690)
*Filippo Zacchei,Ana Larrañaga,Attilio Frangi,Andrea Manzoni,Steven L. Brunton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加权集合-弱SINDy方法，能够从不均匀噪声和多保真度数据中准确识别非线性动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际仿真与实验数据通常含有噪声，且噪声水平在不同观测、设备或实验中表现出异质性（非均匀方差），传统方法难以有效处理此类非均匀数据，限制了动力系统发现的准确性。

Method: 将集合SINDy与弱SINDy相结合，在广义最小二乘框架下引入加权回归策略，为不同噪声水平的观测数据分配相应权重，并给出权重策略的统计理论依据。

Result: 在常微分方程与偏微分方程等多个基准系统上验证，表明该方法有效缓解了异方差噪声的不利影响；整合低成本、低质量的重复测量可改善模型恢复效果，部分情况下匹配甚至优于仅使用高保真数据的结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了对非均匀噪声数据的鲁棒建模，展示了多保真度数据融合在动力系统发现中的价值，为低成本测量与异质数据的科学应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Data from simulations and experiments are rarely noise-free and often exhibit heterogeneous levels of fidelity. Measurement uncertainty may vary across repeated observations, sensing devices, or even within a single experiment. This work addresses the problem of discovering nonlinear dynamical systems from such inhomogeneous data. We extend the Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems (SINDy) framework to account for variable noise levels by combining Ensemble SINDy and Weak SINDy within a weighted regression formulation derived from generalized least squares. A statistical justification for the weighting strategy is also provided. The methodology is validated on several benchmark systems, including ordinary and partial differential equations. In addition, we show the benefit of multi-fidelity integration for forecasting the dynamics of a double pendulum system. The results confirm that the proposed approach mitigates the adverse effects of heteroscedastic noise and that repeated, low-cost, low-quality measurements can improve model recovery, in some cases matching or outperforming reconstructions obtained using only high-fidelity data.

</details>


### [241] [When Generator Replay Degrades: Projected Rehearsal Orchestration for Heterogeneous Federated Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15695)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Khoa D. Doan,Binh T. Nguyen,Danh Le-Phuoc,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRO和PRO-MAX框架，通过投影排练编排替代传统的输入空间合成重放，有效解决联邦类增量学习中异构任务流和监督不平衡问题，在图像、文本和图基准上显著提升了知识保留和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦类增量学习（FCIL）在客户端观察到不同标签子集、以不同阶段推进任务、对相同语义概念提供不均匀监督时变得显著困难。现有FCIL方法通常通过输入空间合成来保存旧知识，但在异构任务流下表现脆弱且难以跨模态迁移。此外，现有方法在监督不平衡和阶段错位情况下容易因重放质量问题导致性能退化，仅增加重放数量无法解决此问题。

Method: 提出PRO框架，用投影排练编排替代合成输入重放。具体包括：1）移除外部预训练，在相同warmup条件下评估所有方法；2）服务器维护紧凑的类级投影记忆；3）客户端在当前样本和旧投影记忆上执行平衡的伪多任务训练。进一步提出PRO-MAX扩展，通过邻域加权记忆对齐增强PRO，同时保持服务器轻量化原则（服务器仅聚合模型更新和记忆统计信息）。

Result: 在图像、文本和图基准测试中，PRO和PRO-MAX在异构任务流下显著提升了知识保留和最终效用，同时在同构FCIL中保持竞争力。即使基线方法获得扩展的重放预算，它们在监督不平衡和阶段错位下仍会退化，表明单独增加重放数量无法解决重放质量失效问题。弱任务诊断进一步显示，较大的重放错配与较大的下游退化相关，而本方法能更好地保持投影记忆与演化表示的对齐。

Conclusion: 投影排练编排比输入空间合成重放更有效，能够更好地处理异构联邦类增量学习中的挑战。该方法在保持服务器轻量化的同时，通过维护与演化表示对齐的投影记忆，有效解决了监督不平衡和阶段错位导致的性能退化问题，证明了重放质量比数量更关键。

Abstract: Federated class-incremental learning (FCIL) becomes substantially harder when clients observe different label subsets, progress through tasks at different stages, and provide uneven supervision for the same semantic concepts. Existing FCIL methods often preserve old knowledge through input-space synthesis, but they can be fragile under heterogeneous task streams and difficult to transfer across modalities. To alleviate such issues, we propose PRO, a framework that replaces synthetic input replay with projected rehearsal orchestration. To remove external pretraining, we evaluate all methods under the same warmup. After this, PRO maintains compact class-level projected memories on the server and allows clients perform balanced pseudo multi-task training over current examples and old projected memories. To handle stronger representation drift, we further introduce PRO-MAX, which augments PRO with neighborhood-weighted memory alignment while preserving the same server-light principle that the server only aggregates model updates and memory statistics. Across image, text, and graph benchmarks, PRO and PRO-MAX improve retention and final utility under heterogeneous streams while remaining competitive in homogeneous FCIL. Even when baselines are given expanded replay budgets, they degrade under supervision imbalance and stage misalignment, indicating that replay quantity alone does not resolve replay-quality failures. Additional weak-task diagnostics further show that larger replay mismatch is associated with larger downstream degradation, while our method keeps projected memories better aligned with the evolving representation.

</details>


### [242] [Robust Transformer-Based One-Step Stock Index Forecasting via Shifted Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15701)
*Tien Thanh Thach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Transformer架构用于股票指数预测，结合余弦退火学习率调度和新型移位数据增强技术（SDA），在VN30和标普500数据集上取得了最佳预测性能，证明数据增强比增加模型复杂度更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer模型直接应用于金融时间序列预测面临显著挑战，主要由于金融数据存在噪声信号强、短期记忆动态特征以及分布漂移等固有特性，导致预测效果不佳。

Method: 提出一种改进的Transformer架构用于单步股票指数预测；引入先进的余弦退火学习率调度策略；设计新型移位数据增强技术；在VN30和标普500两个基准股票指数数据集上进行综合评估。

Result: 余弦退火学习率调度相比广义逆幂调度器显著提升了预测精度；移位数据增强大幅降低了预测误差和运行间变异性，同时增强了对超参数选择的鲁棒性；余弦退火与SDA的组合在两个数据集上均取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 数据增强在基于Transformer的金融预测中比增加模型复杂度更为重要，为在噪声金融环境中进行稳健的股票指数预测提供了实用且计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers have shown remarkable success in sequence modeling, yet their direct application to financial time series remains challenging due to noisy signals, short-memory dynamics, and distributional shifts. This paper proposes a modified Transformer architecture for one-step stock index forecasting, combined with advanced learning-rate scheduling and a novel Shifted Data Augmentation (SDA) technique. We evaluate the proposed framework on two benchmark stock index datasets, VN30 and S&P 500. Experimental results demonstrate that cosine annealing with warmup consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the generalized inverse-power scheduler. Furthermore, SDA substantially reduces forecasting errors and run-to-run variability while improving robustness to hyperparameter selection. The combination of cosine annealing scheduling and SDA achieved the best performance on both datasets, indicating that data augmentation can play a more important role than increasing model complexity in Transformer-based financial forecasting. These findings provide a practical and computationally efficient approach for robust stock index forecasting in noisy financial environments.

</details>


### [243] [How to Score Experts for One-Shot MoE Expert Pruning: A Unified Formulation and Selection Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15716)
*Zongfang Liu,Jinghui Zhang,Zijian Ma,Guangyi Chen,Xin Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的MoE专家剪枝框架，并发现了基于三个因素的选择原则：任务无关剪枝应优先使用基于激活的准则，而任务特定剪枝可保留路由频率和门权重信息。提出的新准则MAN和MSAN在16个基准上表现优异，性能提升最高达8.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家剪枝方法主要依赖启发式准则，没有单一准则在所有情况下最优，缺乏针对不同部署目标选择剪枝准则的原则。一次性专家剪枝中如何建立准则选择原则仍然是一个重要但未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 引入一个统一的MoE专家一次性剪枝框架，围绕三个核心因素组织：路由频率、门权重和激活强度。基于该框架推导出准则选择原则，并提出两个新的任务无关准则：平均激活范数（MAN）和均方激活范数（MSAN）。

Result: 在四个代表性MoE模型和16个多样化基准测试中，MAN和MSAN在任务无关设置中表现稳定强劲，获得前两名平均排名，相比最强基线平均性能提升高达8.8分。

Conclusion: 统一的剪枝框架为现有启发式准则提供了系统性视角，准则选择原则为不同部署场景提供了明确指导，新提出的MAN和MSAN准则为任务无关剪枝提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models reduce per-token computation through sparse expert activation, yet deployment still requires storing the full expert pool, making one-shot expert pruning a practical approach for reducing memory usage. Although effective, existing criteria are largely heuristic, and no single criterion is universally optimal. Thus, establishing a principle for selecting pruning criteria suited to different deployment objectives remains an important yet largely underexplored problem in one-shot expert pruning. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation for one-shot MoE expert pruning organized around three factors: routing frequency, gate weighting, and activation strength. The formulation yields a criteria selection principle: task-agnostic pruning should favor routed-token-averaged, gate-free activation-based criteria, whereas task-specific pruning can benefit from retaining routing-frequency and gate-weight information. Beyond this principle, the formulation also provides a systematic view of existing heuristic criteria and gives rise to two new task-agnostic criteria, Mean Activation Norm (MAN) and Mean Squared Activation Norm (MSAN). Across four representative MoE models and 16 diverse benchmarks, MAN and MSAN are consistently strong in the task-agnostic setting, obtain the top-two average ranks, and improve average performance by up to 8.8 points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [244] [InstantForget: Update-Free Backdoor Unlearning with Inference-Time Feature Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15730)
*Zhenyu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了InstantForget方法，在无需更新模型参数的推理阶段，通过检测异常特征并将其重置为中性表示来实现后门遗忘，在CIFAR-10上平均ASR降至0.071。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有更新无关的后门遗忘方法通常依赖投影假设，但作者审计发现该假设仅在BadNets上有效，在WaNet、Blended和SIG等更隐蔽的后门攻击上失败（ASR分别为0.683、0.888、0.941），且失败原因无法用谱紧致性、空间局部性或子空间错位解释。

Method: 提出InstantForget方法：基于logit-triplet gap分析发现失败由目标边界、目标logit下降和非目标logit上升的差距预测；采用清洁校准的门控重置策略，使用Mahalanobis分数标记异常特征，仅将被标记特征移向中性的非目标表示，无需触发样本或参数更新。

Result: 在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18上，四个非自适应触发器平均ASR降至0.071，检测AUROC达0.981，可迁移至八个测试骨干网络中的六个；但在WaNet、ModelNet10点混合、两种骨干几何结构及自适应特征紧致性攻击下失败。

Conclusion: InstantForget证明了推理时后门遗忘的可行性，但存在明确边界：对隐蔽性强的后门攻击和自适应攻击效果有限，为后续研究提供了明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Backdoor unlearning aims to remove a malicious trigger behavior from a deployed model while preserving clean utility. We study the update-free inference-time setting, where model parameters remain frozen. First, we audit a common projection assumption under oracle paired clean and triggered features. Projection succeeds mainly on BadNets and leaves WaNet, Blended, and SIG at 0.683, 0.888, and 0.941 ASR on CIFAR-10 ResNet-18. This failure is not explained by spectral compactness, spatial locality, or subspace misalignment. It is predicted by a logit-triplet gap involving the target margin, target-logit drop, and non-target logit rise. We then introduce InstantForget, a clean-calibrated gated reset that flags anomalous features with a Mahalanobis score and moves only flagged features toward a neutral non-target representation. With one fixed operating point selected on held-out triggered validation, InstantForget reduces average ASR to 0.071 across four non-adaptive CIFAR-10 triggers without triggered samples or parameter updates at deployment. It also reaches 0.981 detection AUROC and transfers to six of eight tested backbones. Reported failures under WaNet, ModelNet10 point blend, two backbone geometries, and adaptive feature-compactness attacks define the method's scope.

</details>


### [245] [Unsupervised Learning for Missing Modalities in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15743)
*Hassan Ismkhan,Hamid Bouchahcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UL4M4框架，通过无监督学习在任务无关的方式下填补多模态学习中缺失的特征嵌入，有效解决了模态缺失问题，在超过50%模态缺失的情况下首次实现F1-Micro分数稳定高于0.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习在实际应用中经常面临模态缺失的挑战，现有方法难以处理多样化且高度不完整的模态缺失模式，需要一种能够灵活处理任意数量模态缺失、且不依赖于下游任务的解决方案。

Method: 提出UL4M4框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)模态特定归一化和新颖的部分模态距离度量，实现对不完整观测的公平聚类，保持不同维度和模态数量下的尺度不变性；(2)基于聚类中心的迭代贪婪填补过程，用于在训练或推理时填补任意缺失的模态。该方法使用冻结的编码器，填补模块与下游任务解耦，可轻松集成到任何融合/预测架构中。

Result: 在多样化和高度不完整的实验设置下，UL4M4展现出强大的鲁棒性，在超过50%模态缺失的情况下首次实现F1-Micro分数稳定高于0.7，在不同聚类大小上结果稳定，显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: UL4M4为多模态学习中的模态缺失问题提供了一个有效、灵活且鲁棒的解决方案，能够处理任意数量的模态和任意缺失模式，具有广泛的应用前景和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the missing-modality challenge in multi-modal learning by introducing Unsupervised Learning for Missing Modalities in Multi-Modal Learning (UL4M4), a flexible framework that imputes missing feature embeddings in a task-independent manner before supervised prediction. We propose modality-specific
  normalization and a novel partial-modality distance metric to enable fair clustering of incomplete observations, capturing cross-modal structures while preserving scale-invariance across varying dimensionalities and modality counts. Cluster centers from this unsupervised stage guide an iterative greedy imputation process for any
  missing modalities during training or inference, supporting arbitrary numbers of modalities and arbitrary missing patterns per sample. The imputation module is lightweight, uses frozen encoders, and decouples from the downstream task, allowing easy integration with any fusion/prediction architecture. Extensive experiments under diverse and highly incomplete regimes demonstrate UL4M4's robustness, achieving, to
  the best of our knowledge, the first consistent F1-Micro scores above 0.7 on challenging missing configurations even when more than 50\% of modality slots are missing. Results are also stable across cluster sizes and significantly outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available here: https://github.com/h-ismkhan/Multimodal-Learning-with-Missing-Modalities-via-Unsupervised-Learning.

</details>


### [246] [From Correlation to Causation in Lane Change Prediction for Automated Driving: A Causal Explanation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15756)
*Mohamed Manzour,Aditya Kumar,Augusto Luis Ballardini,Miguel Ángel Sotelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于因果推理的换道预测框架，不仅实现了高预测精度（F1-score > 95%），还提供了可解释的因果链分析，区分了直接影响和中介效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有换道预测方法主要学习观测变量与未来操作之间的统计关联，忽略了输入变量之间的因果依赖关系，限制了模型的可解释性，特别是当物理相关变量（如纵向间隙、相对纵向速度、碰撞时间TTC）被作为独立的扁平输入处理时。

Method: 提出一个基于因果推理的框架，结合了语言特征构建、专家约束的因果发现、深度结构因果建模（DECI）、基于干预的效应分析、反驳测试和递归因果链解释。该方法不仅预测未来操作，还识别直接贡献于预测的候选变量、影响它们的上游因素以及效应传播的因果链。

Result: 在车道标线穿越事件前3秒内，平均F1-score超过95%。通过基于干预的效应分析，在学习的因果结构下区分了有影响力的变量和弱影响力变量，进一步区分了候选直接贡献者和中介效应，并生成了对比性因果链解释。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是构建了一个机制感知的换道预测流程，超越了基于相关的分类方法，为操作预测提供了更具可解释性的因果推理能力。该方法不仅能够解释为什么预测的操作被支持，还能说明为什么替代操作缺乏支持。

Abstract: Lane-change prediction is a central task in intelligent vehicles, where early maneuver anticipation can support safer decision-making. However, many existing approaches mainly learn statistical associations between observed driving variables and future maneuvers, while overlooking the causal dependencies among the input variables themselves. This limits interpretability, especially when physically related variables such as longitudinal gap, relative longitudinal velocity, and Time-To-Collision (TTC) are treated as independent flat inputs. This article presents a causal-inference-based framework for lane-change prediction and explanation. The proposed approach combines linguistic feature construction, expert-constrained causal discovery, deep structural causal modeling with Deep End-to-end Causal Inference (DECI), intervention-based effect analysis, refutation testing, and recursive causal-chain explanation. The objective is not only to predict the future maneuver, but also to identify candidate variables that directly contribute to the prediction, the upstream factors influencing them, and the causal chains through which these effects propagate. The framework achieves average F1-scores above 95% during the first three seconds before the lane-marking crossing event. Beyond prediction accuracy, the framework uses intervention-based effect analysis to distinguish influential from weakly influential variables under the learned causal structure. It further distinguishes candidate direct contributors from mediated effects and generates contrastive causal-chain explanations that clarify why the predicted maneuver is favored and why the alternative maneuvers are less supported. The main contribution is therefore a mechanism-aware lane-change prediction pipeline that moves beyond correlation-based classification toward more interpretable causal reasoning for maneuver prediction.

</details>


### [247] [The Data Manifold under the Microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15760)
*Marios Koulakis,Constantin Seibold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数据几何基准测试框架，通过改造现有数据集实现对曲率、reach和体积等几何量的高精度估计，为验证深度学习理论假设提供了受控实验环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习理论与实践存在显著差距。泛化和逼近误差界限通常针对简化模型推导或过于宽松而缺乏信息量。许多理论依赖流形假设和几何正则性（如内蕴维度、曲率、reach），但现有选择呈现两极分化：分析流形几何已知但适用性有限，或真实数据集几何难以精确估计。研究进展需要对数据流形几何的深入理解和合适的基准测试。

Method: 作者构建了一个数据几何基准测试框架：1）重新设计并扩展dSprites和COIL-20数据集，增加变换维度并采用密集的轴对齐采样；2）配合有限差分估计器，在通用估计器不可靠或难以部署的条件下，以接近真实值的精度恢复曲率、reach和体积。该框架作为受控测试平台，既可用作几何估计器的校准环境，也可作为探索理论假设的沙盒。

Result: 框架能够以接近真实值的精度估计流形的曲率、reach和体积等几何量。两项应用研究展示了其实用价值：1）评估Genovese等人及Fefferman等人提出的界限的缩放行为；2）追踪β-VAE的逐层几何变化，揭示了当前界限的行为特征。研究结果表明受控基准对指导和验证未来理论具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究数据几何提供了标准化的测试环境，填补了现有基准的空白，能够有效支持几何估计器的校准和理论假设的验证，为连接深度学习理论与实践提供了实用工具。

Abstract: A significant gap exists between theory and practice in deep learning. Generalization and approximation error bounds are often derived for simplified models or are too loose to be informative. Many rely on the manifold hypothesis and on geometric regularity such as intrinsic dimension, curvature, and reach. Progress requires insight into data-manifold geometry and suitable benchmarks, yet existing options are polarized: analytic manifolds with known geometry but limited applicability, or real-world datasets where geometry is only coarsely estimable. We introduce a benchmarking framework for studying data geometry. We repurpose and extend dSprites and COIL-20 with additional transformation dimensions and dense, axis-aligned sampling, and pair them with finite-difference estimators that recover curvature, reach, and volume at near-ground-truth accuracy in a regime where general-purpose estimators are unreliable or difficult to deploy. The framework is intended as a controlled testbed, useful as a calibration environment for geometric estimators and a sandbox for probing theoretical assumptions. To illustrate its use, we present two application studies, namely assessing the scaling behavior of the bounds of Genovese et al. and Fefferman et al., and tracking the layer-wise geometry of a $β$-VAE, highlighting the behavior of current bounds and the value of controlled benchmarks for guiding and validating future theory.
  A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/koulakis/manifold-microscope.

</details>


### [248] [Visualizing Uncertainty: Spatial Maps of Missing and Conflicting Evidence in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15767)
*Dong Hyun Jeong,Feng Chen,Jin-Hee Cho,Lance M. Kaplan,Audun Jøsang,Soo-Yeon Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了不确定性激活图（UAM）框架，结合证据深度学习（EDL）与FullGrad方法，生成可解释的空间不确定性归因图，区分缺乏证据的空缺不确定性和证据冲突的不谐和不确定性，填补了不确定性量化与可解释性之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化方法仅提供标量置信度度量，无法揭示输入图像中哪些空间区域导致了不同类型的不确定性。在安全关键领域部署可靠机器学习系统时，理解模型不确定性的空间来源至关重要。

Method: 提出不确定性激活图（UAM）框架，将证据深度学习（EDL）与FullGrad类激活映射相结合。利用FullGrad的完整梯度分解特性和主观逻辑的原则性不确定性量化，通过计算信念加权的归因，分别生成空缺不确定性激活图和不谐和不确定性激活图，识别模型知识缺失区域和模糊证据区域。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，该框架有效区分了空缺不确定性和不谐和不确定性，能够精确定位导致模型不确定性的图像区域，提供直观的视觉反馈以评估模型在复杂视觉识别任务中的可靠性。

Conclusion: UAM框架成功填补了不确定性量化与可解释性之间的关键空白，为理解和诊断深度神经网络的不确定性提供了理论基础扎实且直观的可视化工具，有助于在安全关键应用中提升模型可信度。

Abstract: Understanding when and why deep neural networks are uncertain is crucial for deploying reliable machine learning systems in safety-critical domains. While existing uncertainty quantification methods provide scalar measures of model confidence, they offer limited insight into which spatial regions of an input contribute to different types of uncertainty. We propose a novel visualization framework, Uncertainty Activation Map (UAM), that combines Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) with Full-Gradient Class Activation Mapping (FullGrad) to generate interpretable spatial uncertainty activation maps. Our approach distinguishes between two fundamental types of uncertainty: vacuity, representing lack of evidence, and dissonance, capturing conflicting evidence between competing hypotheses. By leveraging the complete gradient decomposition property of FullGrad and the principled uncertainty quantification of Subjective Logic, our method produces theoretically grounded visualizations that highlight specific image regions responsible for model uncertainty. With this framework, vacuity and dissonance activation maps are generated by computing belief-weighted attributions, enabling identification of where models lack knowledge versus where they encounter ambiguous evidence. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively addresses the critical gap between uncertainty quantification and explainability, providing intuitive visual feedback to assess model reliability in complex visual recognition tasks.

</details>


### [249] [Bayesian Networks with Latent Time Embedding for Stage-Aware Causal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15784)
*Nguyen Linh Dan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BN-LTE（带有潜在时间嵌入的贝叶斯网络）框架，用于阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展的阶段感知建模。该方法通过基线生物标志物估计疾病伪时间，并按照生物学合理的AT(N)排序约束有向依赖关系，能够有效预测tau蛋白进展并识别出中期伪时间窗内淀粉样蛋白的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AD纵向模型要么将AT(N)级联表示为固定的生物标志物序列，要么作为黑盒预测任务，难以确定生物学指导的生物标志物关系何时影响未来的区域病理变化。缺乏一个既能预测tau进展又能解释阶段依赖性AT(N)级联机制的框架。

Method: 提出BN-LTE贝叶斯结构框架，包含三个关键组件：（1）从基线生物标志物谱估计疾病伪时间；（2）根据生物学合理的AT(N)排序约束有向依赖关系；（3）使用后验样条变化结构方程将初始多模态测量与未来年度化区域tau-PET变化联系起来。方法整合了因果推断技术（g-formula对比、root-adjusted AIPW）进行验证。

Result: 在ADNI数据的重复受试者不相交评估中，BN-LTE相比基线预测方法展现出更强的tau进展空间重建能力。此外，成功恢复了后验阶段变化的AT(N)约束效应，识别出中期伪时间窗内的淀粉样蛋白敏感性，该发现得到了模型隐含g-formula对比、root-adjusted AIPW、机制敏感消融实验以及跨样条和先验规范的稳健性分析的支持。

Conclusion: BN-LTE定位为一个贝叶斯结构框架，能够在观察性纵向神经影像数据中预测tau进展的同时检验阶段依赖性AT(N)级联机制，为AD进展研究提供了可解释的预测工具。代码已开源。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is often described through the amyloid-tau-neurodegeneration, or AT(N), cascade. However, most longitudinal models represent this cascade either as a fixed sequence of biomarkers or as a black-box forecasting task. This makes it difficult to determine when biologically guided biomarker relationships influence future regional pathology. In this study, we introduce Bayesian Networks with Latent Time Embedding (BN-LTE), a Bayesian structural framework for stage-aware modeling of AD progression. BN-LTE estimates disease pseudotime from baseline biomarker profiles and constrains directed dependencies according to biologically plausible AT(N) ordering. Posterior spline-varying structural equations are then used to link initial multimodal measurements with future annualized regional tau-PET change. Across repeated subject-disjoint evaluations using ADNI data, BN-LTE shows strong spatial reconstruction of tau progression compared with the included forecasting baselines. Beyond spatial reconstruction, BN-LTE recovers posterior stage-varying AT(N)-constrained effects and identifies a mid-pseudotime window of amyloid sensitivity. This window is supported by model-implied g-formula contrasts, root-adjusted AIPW, mechanism-sensitive ablations, and robustness analyses across spline and prior specifications. Overall, these findings position BN-LTE as a Bayesian structural framework for forecasting tau progression while examining stage-dependent AT(N)-cascade mechanisms in observational longitudinal neuroimaging data. Our code is available at https://github.com/danleneurocom/BN-LTE.

</details>


### [250] [Proximal Policy Optimization for Amortized Discrete Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15793)
*Anna Zykova-Myzina,Timofei Gritsaev,Daniil Tiapkin,Nikita Morozov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将近端策略优化（PPO）算法应用于生成流网络（GFlowNet）训练，相比标准GFlowNet训练目标，在收敛速度和数据效率方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet框架与熵正则化强化学习之间存在广泛的理论联系，但尚缺乏将成熟的策略梯度算法系统性地应用于GFlowNet训练的研究，特别是利用这些方法提高采样效率和收敛速度的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 基于GFlowNet与熵正则化强化学习的理论联系，推导了适用于GFlowNet训练的标准策略梯度算法等价形式，并深入研究了基线训练和优势估计等方法学问题。核心贡献是首次推导并成功将近端策略优化（PPO）应用于GFlowNet训练。

Result: 在从合成能量函数到分子图生成等多个基准测试中，PPO方法相比标准GFlowNet训练目标展现出更快的收敛速度和更高的数据效率，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 策略梯度算法（尤其是PPO）可以成功应用于GFlowNet训练，为结构化离散概率分布采样提供了更高效的训练范式，拓展了GFlowNet与强化学习方法之间的理论与实践桥梁。

Abstract: This paper explores policy gradient algorithms for training stochastic policies to sample from structured discrete probability distributions under the Generative Flow Network (GFlowNet) framework. Building on extensive theoretical connections between GFlowNets and entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, we derive equivalents of standard policy gradient algorithms for training GFlowNets, as well as experimentally explore their various methodological aspects, including baseline training and advantage estimation. Most importantly, our work is the first to derive and successfully apply proximal policy optimization to GFlowNets, showing its improved convergence speed and data efficiency compared to standard GFlowNet training objectives on benchmarks ranging from synthetic energies to molecular graph generation.

</details>


### [251] [Mean-Field Parallel Decoding for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15805)
*Tamim Zoabi,Ameen Ali,Liran Ringel,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的离散扩散语言模型解码框架，通过引入提交分数和成对交互来协调并行token更新，在保持生成质量的同时提高了并行解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型虽然能够实现并行token生成以降低解码延迟，但现有的基于边缘置信度的独立token选择方法存在局限性：单独看起来可靠的token在多个位置同时更新时可能形成不兼容的配置，限制了有效的并行性。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练的解码框架：(1) 在每次前向传播中为每个掩码位置分配提交分数；(2) 利用模型预测分布导出的成对交互来细化这些分数；(3) 通过变分松弛得到简单的固定点更新，在单次前向传播中抑制冲突的同步提交。该方法是轻量级的，不需要辅助模型或重新训练，可直接嵌入现有的扩散解码流程。

Result: 在推理和代码生成基准测试上，该方法在质量-延迟权衡方面表现出一致的改进，能够在维持竞争性生成质量的同时实现更多的并行token提交。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了离散扩散模型中并行token更新的协调问题，通过简单的固定点更新机制在单次前向传播中避免了冲突的同步提交，为离散扩散语言模型提供了一种即插即用的高效并行解码解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models enable parallel token generation, offering a pathway to low-latency decoding. However, selecting tokens independently by marginal confidence limits effective parallelism: tokens that appear reliable in isolation can form incompatible configurations when several positions are updated at once. We introduce a training-free decoding framework that coordinates these parallel updates. At each forward pass, the method assigns a commit score to each masked position and refines these scores using pairwise interactions derived from the model's predictive distributions. A variational relaxation yields a simple fixed-point update that suppresses conflicting simultaneous commitments within a single forward pass. This mechanism allows the decoder to commit more tokens in parallel while maintaining competitive generation quality. The method is lightweight, requires no auxiliary model or retraining, and drops into existing diffusion decoding pipelines without modification. Experiments on reasoning and code-generation benchmarks show consistent improvements in the quality-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [252] [Continuous Cross-Domain Traffic State Prediction via Memory-Augmented Graph Liquid Time-Constant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15807)
*Jinrong Xiang,Ming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MA-GLTC框架，通过构建时空单元实现细粒度跨域知识对齐，利用图液体时间常数网络建模连续时间下的交通演化，并设计记忆转移存储机制处理未见过的目标域模式，在五个公共交通数据集上平均预测误差最高降低10.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中部分区域因传感基础设施不足导致交通观测数据有限，跨域知识迁移成为解决数据稀缺交通预测问题的重要方案。然而现有跨域交通预测方法存在源-目标域适配粒度粗糙、处理未见目标域模式能力有限、对不规则或异构时间条件下连续交通动态建模不足等局限。

Method: 提出记忆增强图液体时间常数网络(MA-GLTC)框架：1)构建时空单元(STUs)将交通网络分解为可迁移的局部单元，实现跨域细粒度知识对齐；2)开发图液体时间常数网络(GLTC)，将图耦合循环电导引入液体时间常数动力学，使节点状态能够以泄漏、自适应时间常数和邻域感知反馈的方式演化，从而建模连续时间下的图耦合交通演化；3)设计基于记忆的转移存储(MTS)机制，用于保存源域知识、检索匹配的交通模式，并在出现未见状态时更新可靠的目标域模式。

Result: 在五个公共交通数据集上的实验表明，MA-GLTC在短期和长期预测任务中均持续优于代表性的域内和跨域基线方法。与次优方法相比，MA-GLTC分别在五个数据集上将平均预测误差降低了3.02%、0.33%、8.92%、10.09%和2.11%。

Conclusion: MA-GLTC框架有效解决了现有跨域交通预测方法的关键局限，通过细粒度知识对齐、连续时间动态建模和记忆增强机制，显著提升了数据稀缺场景下的交通状态预测性能，为智能交通系统中的跨域迁移学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic state prediction is a fundamental task in intelligent transportation systems. In practical applications, some regions suffer from limited traffic observations due to insufficient sensing infrastructure, making cross-domain knowledge transfer an important solution for data-scarce traffic prediction. However, existing cross-domain traffic prediction methods still face several limitations, including coarse-grained source-target adaptation, limited capability in handling unseen target-domain patterns, and insufficient modeling of continuous traffic dynamics under irregular or heterogeneous temporal conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a continuous cross-domain traffic prediction framework, termed Memory-Augmented Graph Liquid Time-Constant Network (MA-GLTC). Specifically, we first construct spatio-temporal units (STUs) to decompose traffic networks into transferable local units, enabling fine-grained knowledge alignment across domains. Then, a graph liquid time-constant network (GLTC) is developed to model graph-coupled traffic evolution in continuous time. Different from generic graph neural ODE-based models, GLTC introduces graph-coupled recurrent conductance into liquid time-constant dynamics, allowing node states to evolve with leakage, adaptive time constants, and neighborhood-aware feedback. Furthermore, a Memory-based Transfer Storage (MTS) mechanism is designed to preserve source-domain knowledge, retrieve matched traffic patterns, and update reliable target-domain patterns when unseen states emerge. Experiments on five public traffic datasets demonstrate that MA-GLTC consistently outperforms representative innerdomain and cross-domain baselines in both short-term and longterm prediction tasks. Compared with the second-best method, MA-GLTC reduces the average prediction errors by 3.02%, 0.33%, 8.92%, 10.09%, and 2.11%, respectively.

</details>


### [253] [Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15812)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Giuseppe Di Fatta,Giuseppe Nicosia,Panos M Pardalos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了布朗核阶梯(BKLs)框架，通过布朗核积分构造递归定义的再生核希尔伯特空间层次结构，为深度学习中组合表示的研究提供了数学上可处理的分层函数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计学习理论中，构建能够捕获层次组合表示的数学可处理函数空间仍然是一个核心挑战。现有的函数空间框架难以直接刻画深度学习中的层次结构特性，需要一个新的数学框架来研究深度神经网络中的组合表示。

Method: 引入布朗核阶梯(BKLs)，这是一种通过布朗核积分构造递归定义的再生核希尔伯特空间层次结构。从线性泛函开始，每一层通过对支撑在前一层子集上的概率测度积分布朗核获得，从而构建一个深度直接编码在层次结构中的递归函数空间模型。基于此框架定义了规范BKL空间及相关的复杂度泛函，并采用基于递归子集分解和布朗核阈值表示的组合证明技术。

Result: 证明了BKL空间形成拟Banach空间，满足依赖于深度的Hölder正则性估计，并表现出关于深度的严格单调性。进一步证明了正则化经验风险最小化的存在性结果，导出了在高维环境和层次深度方面保持一致控制的Gaussian复杂度界限。这些估计为BKL空间上的正则化经验风险最小化提供了近参数阶的超额风险保证。

Conclusion: BKL框架为研究深度学习中的组合表示提供了一个数学上可处理的层次函数空间框架，建立了严格的数学理论基础，使得深度学习中的层次结构特性能够在函数空间理论中得到严格刻画和分析。

Abstract: Constructing mathematically tractable function spaces that capture hierarchical compositional representations remains a central challenge in statistical learning theory. We introduce Brownian kernel ladders (BKLs), a recursively defined hierarchy of integral reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces generated through Brownian-kernel integral constructions. Starting from linear functionals, each layer is obtained by integrating Brownian kernels over probability measures supported on subsets of the previous layer, yielding a recursive function-space model in which depth is encoded directly through the hierarchy.
  Based on this framework, we define canonical BKL spaces together with an associated complexity functional. We establish several analytical and statistical properties of these spaces. In particular, we show that BKL spaces form quasi-Banach spaces, satisfy depth-dependent Hölder regularity estimates, and exhibit strict monotonicity with respect to depth. We further prove existence results for regularized empirical risk minimization and derive Gaussian complexity bounds that remain uniformly controlled with respect to both the ambient dimension and the hierarchy depth.
  A key ingredient of the analysis is a combinatorial proof technique based on recursive subset decompositions and Brownian-kernel threshold representations. These estimates yield excess-risk guarantees of near-parametric order for regularized empirical risk minimization over BKL spaces. Our results provide a mathematically tractable hierarchical function-space framework for studying compositional representations in deep learning.

</details>


### [254] [SILAGE: Memory-Efficient, Full-Gradient-Free Nonconvex Optimization for Nested Finite Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15832)
*Igor Sokolov,Laurent Condat,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SILAGE算法，一种针对双重有限和结构的方差缩减方法，在消除周期性全局梯度刷新的同时仅需O(n)内存，并通过嵌套功能相似性实现自适应复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模数据集的经验风险最小化问题中，数据通常呈现嵌套的双重有限和结构(N=nm个样本被划分为n个大小为m的块)。现有方差缩减方法存在权衡困境：递归估计器(如PAGE)需要周期性的全局全梯度刷新，计算开销巨大；单循环方法(如SILVER)虽避免刷新但需要O(nm)的内存来存储每个样本的控制变量，这在实践中不可行。

Method: 提出SILAGE算法，主动利用双重求和结构：通过每次迭代最多评估一个局部组梯度来消除对所有nm个分量的周期性全局全梯度刷新，同时仅需要O(n)的内存复杂度。收敛分析避免了悲观的Lipschitz常数，通过嵌套功能相似性(跨组异质性δ₁和组内异质性δ₂)使复杂度自然适应底层数据几何结构。

Result: SILAGE算法成功实现了方差缩减方法中计算效率与内存效率的平衡：(1)消除了周期性全局全梯度刷新；(2)内存复杂度从O(nm)降低至O(n)；(3)提供了紧致的收敛分析，复杂度通过嵌套功能相似性自适应于数据几何；(4)在多个实际相关场景下改进了现有最优复杂度界限。

Conclusion: SILAGE算法通过充分利用问题的双重有限和结构，有效解决了现有方差缩减方法在大规模数据场景下的扩展性瓶颈，为分布式数据孤岛、外存学习等实际应用提供了理论上更优、实践中可行的优化方案。

Abstract: Empirical risk minimization on massive datasets naturally exhibits a nested double finite-sum structure, where $N=nm$ total samples are logically or physically partitioned into $n$ blocks of size $m$ (e.g., in pooled data silos, out-of-core learning, or deliberate stratification). While variance-reduced methods achieve optimal oracle complexities for nonconvex objectives, they suffer from severe scaling bottlenecks in this centralized regime. Recursive estimators, such as PAGE, require periodic global full-gradient refreshes over all $nm$ samples, which are computationally expensive. Conversely, single-loop methods, such as SILVER, avoid such refreshes but require an impractical $\mathcal{O}(nm)$ memory footprint to store a control variate for every sample. In this paper, we propose SILAGE, a variance-reduced algorithm that addresses this trade-off. By actively exploiting the double-sum structure, SILAGE eliminates periodic global full-gradient refreshes over all $nm$ components (evaluating at most one local group gradient per iteration) while requiring only $\mathcal{O}(n)$ memory. Furthermore, we provide a tight convergence analysis that avoids pessimistic worst-case Lipschitz constants. Instead, SILAGE's complexity natively adapts to the underlying data geometry via nested functional similarities: across-group ($δ_1$) and within-group ($δ_2$) heterogeneity. Our results improve existing state-of-the-art bounds in several practically relevant regimes.

</details>


### [255] [Wasserstein Convergence of ODE-Based Samplers in Decentralized Diffusion Model via Velocity Field Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15835)
*Chencheng Tang,Xuanyu Xue,Fangyikang Wang,Chao Zhang,Hubery Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为去中心化扩散模型建立了基于ODE采样的Wasserstein-2距离收敛保证，收敛速率为O(N^{-1/2}+ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型收敛理论主要针对单一速度场的标准扩散过程。出于隐私保护和可扩展性的考虑，近期出现的去中心化扩散架构采用多个局部专家和路由机制，导致采样过程包含随机的专家切换，这种随机动态超出了现有扩散收敛分析的适用范围，因此需要建立新的收敛性理论。

Method: 研究了一个具有随机速度场的去中心化扩散框架及其基于ODE的采样方法。通过理论分析，在Wasserstein-2距离度量下建立了收敛性保证。

Result: 证明了N步离散化分布以O(N^{-1/2}+ε)的速率在W_2距离下收敛到解析解，其中ε刻画了神经网络的近似误差。这是首个针对去中心化扩散模型ODE采样方案的W_2收敛结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了去中心化扩散模型收敛性分析的理论空白，为具有随机专家切换机制的扩散模型提供了首个Wasserstein-2距离收敛保证，为隐私保护和可扩展的扩散模型应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved impressive empirical success in generative tasks, and their convergence theory is now relatively well understood. Motivated by privacy and scalability, recent decentralized diffusion architectures replace a single global velocity field with multiple local experts and a routing mechanism, yielding a sampling dynamics with stochastic expert switching that falls outside standard diffusion convergence analyses. In this work, We study a decentralized diffusion framework with stochastic velocity fields and ODE-based sampling. We establish a convergence guarantee in Wasserstein-2 distance, showing that the distribution of the $N$-step discretization converges to the analytical solution at rate $\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/2}+\varepsilon)$ in $W_2$, where $\varepsilon$ captures the neural approximation errors. To our knowledge, this is the first $W_2$ convergence result for decentralized diffusion models with an ODE-based sampling scheme.

</details>


### [256] [David vs. Goliath in Next Activity Prediction: Argmax vs. LSTM, Transformer, and LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15868)
*Hans Weytjens,Ingo Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在下一活动预测任务中，简单的基于计数的argmax基线能够达到或接近十亿参数大语言模型的性能，预训练并未带来一致性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测性流程监控领域已从经典机器学习发展到深度学习架构（LSTM、Transformer）再到大语言模型，但现有研究缺乏一个能够直接对比LLM、Transformer、LSTM和简单基线方法在序列建模设置下性能的系统性基准测试。

Method: 在7个真实事件日志上进行系统性基准测试，比较词汇适配的LLM、从头训练的Transformer、LLM蒸馏的Transformer、LSTM以及简单的基于计数的argmax基线方法。

Result: 实验结果呈现出"大卫战胜歌利亚"的发现：预训练相比从头训练未带来一致的性能提升，模型规模对性能影响甚微，且在大多数数据集上简单的argmax基线能够匹配或接近十亿参数LLM的性能表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前NAP任务中模型复杂度与性能之间的不匹配现象，表明在该领域应用大语言模型时需要重新审视预训练优势和规模效应的传统认知。

Abstract: Next activity prediction (NAP) is a cornerstone of predictive process monitoring (PPM), enabling organizations to move from retrospective analysis to proactive process steering. The PPM field has progressed from classical machine learning through deep learning architectures such as LSTMs and Transformers to large language models (LLMs). Despite growing model complexity, no benchmark jointly compares LLMs, Transformers, LSTMs, and simple baselines in a direct sequence modeling setting for NAP. In this paper, we fill this gap with a systematic benchmark. We compare vocabulary-adapted LLMs, Transformers trained from scratch, LLM-distilled Transformers, and LSTMs against a simple counting-based argmax baseline across seven real-life event logs. Our results tell a David vs. Goliath story: pretraining confers no consistent improvement over training from scratch, model size shows little effect on performance, and on most datasets the argmax baseline matches or approaches the performance of billion-parameter LLMs.

</details>


### [257] [Intelligence Is Not the Bottleneck: Validating an LLM First-Pass Manuscript Score Against Peer-Review Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15887)
*Costa Georgantas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了AIPR系统在ICLR会议投稿评审中的有效性。该系统无需微调，仅通过提示学习即可对论文进行评分。在300篇投稿的测试中，AIPR的整体评分能够有效区分被接收和被拒绝的论文（AUROC 0.82），且表现稳定可靠。虽然精心设计的流程相比简单提示仅有边际提升，但前者提供了更有依据的评审意见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）辅助同行评审的研究主要关注生成的评论文本质量，而非系统赋予的数值分数的有效性。作者旨在验证一个名为AIPR的系统能否仅通过提示学习（无需微调）准确预测学术论文的接收结果，从而为LLM在学术评审中的实际应用提供实证依据。

Method: 研究采用预注册假设的冻结流程，在ICLR会议的300篇公开投稿上进行测试。AIPR系统读取提交的手稿，输出五个0-100分的质量维度和一个加权总分。研究者将其预测分数与实际的接收决定层级和审稿人评分进行对比分析，并将完整流程的表现与仅使用单段提示的基线模型进行对照。

Result: AIPR的整体评分能有效区分被拒绝和被接收的投稿（AUROC 0.82，95% CI 0.78-0.87），分数随接收层级单调递增并与审稿人平均评分呈正相关。预测信号在低分段最强（低分论文被拒率远高于基准），且不存在口头报告论文。有效性主要来自模型本身：简单提示基线的表现接近完整流程（p = 0.09，未达到预定义显著性标准）。但工程化流程显著提升了稳定性（重复运行的标准差从2.8降至0.7）并提供了基于证据的结构化评审。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM通过提示学习即可生成有效的论文质量评分，无需针对评审数据进行微调。虽然复杂流程相比简单提示的优势有限，但工程化设计显著提升了系统的可靠性和评审的可解释性。该系统可辅助而非替代人类决策，为LLM辅助学术评审提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly proposed to assist peer review, yet most evaluations judge the prose of machine-generated review text, not the validity of the numeric score a system assigns. We validate AIPR, which reads a submitted manuscript and emits five 0-100 quality dimensions and a weighted overall score, against the public decision outcomes of a major machine learning venue. AIPR grades by prompting alone, with no fine-tuning on reviews or decisions. Across 300 ICLR submissions with public decision tiers and reviewer ratings, graded under a frozen pipeline with hypotheses pre-registered before any score met any outcome, the overall score separates rejected from accepted submissions (AUROC 0.82, 95% CI 0.78-0.87), rises monotonically across tiers, and tracks the mean reviewer rating. The signal is strongest where we claim it: the lowest-scoring fifth is rejected far above the base rate, with oral papers absent. The validity comes mostly from the model: a one-paragraph prompt on the same model discriminates almost as well as the full pipeline (the small gap favours the pipeline but does not meet the pre-declared criterion, p = 0.09). What the engineering adds is reliability and a grounded review: AIPR's score barely moves across repeated runs (0.7 vs. 2.8 points within-paper SD) where the bare prompt swings, and the same pass returns a rubric-structured, evidence-grounded review rather than a bare number, with the human keeping the decision.

</details>


### [258] [Scalar-pathway fidelity improves physical accuracy in short-range equivariant interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15892)
*Jia Bi,Alin Marin Elena,Samuel Pinilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对等变神经网络势函数中的标量路径提出改进方案（PAN池化和PGS混合器），在金属、共价、离子及分子体系上使MACE的力和能量误差降低19-27%，应力误差降低27-28%，仅需约5%的额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络势函数在局部几何表示方面已有改进，但其可部署的能量面最终通过不变标量通道表现，这些标量通道的聚合和光谱分辨率仍缺乏充分研究。标量路径的保真度可能成为提升短程等变原子间势函数性能的关键设计维度。

Method: 提出两种轻量级、保持对称性的标量路径修正方法：物理感知邻域（PAN）池化添加配位敏感的幅度调制；物理引导光谱（PGS）混合器用径向和锥形光谱基增强边和读出标量特征。这些方法仅作用于ℓ=0通道，保持等变张量骨干不变。以MACE作为高体序机制骨架进行验证，并在Allegro和NequIP上测试可移植性。

Result: 在金属Ag、共价Si、短程离子LiF/Li-F子集和MD17/rMD17分子体系上，标量路径修正使MACE力误差降低22-27%，能量误差降低19-22%；在有应力标签的体系上，应力误差降低27-28%，额外推理FLOPs成本仅约5%。在Allegro和NequIP上也获得方向一致的性能提升，表明该方法在不同短程等变骨干架构间具有可移植性。

Conclusion: 标量路径保真度是短程等变原子间势函数的一个实用设计维度。通过简单的对称性保持修改即可显著提升预测精度，且该方法可移植到不同的等变神经网络架构中，为势函数设计提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Accurate interatomic potentials enable molecular dynamics of materials, molecules, and interfaces beyond density-functional-theory length and time scales. Equivariant neural network potentials have improved the representation of local geometry. However, their deployable energy surfaces ultimately manifest through invariant scalar channels, whose aggregation and spectral resolution remain comparatively underexamined. Here we use Physics-Aware Neighborhood (PAN) pooling and Physics-Guided Spectral (PGS) mixers as controlled scalar-pathway probes: lightweight, symmetry-preserving modifications that act only on \(\ell=0\) channels while leaving the equivariant tensor backbone unchanged. Using MACE as a high-body-order mechanistic scaffold, PAN adds coordination-sensitive amplitude modulation, whereas PGS augments edge and readout scalar features with radial and tapered spectral bases. Across metallic Ag, covalent Si, a short-range ionic LiF/Li--F subset, and MD17/rMD17 molecules, this scalar-pathway correction reduces MACE force errors by 22--27\% and energy errors by 19--22\%; on systems with stress labels, stress errors decrease by 27--28\%, at approximately 5\% additional inference-FLOPs cost. Directionally consistent gains in Allegro and NequIP further indicate that the correction is portable across distinct short-range equivariant backbones, although effect sizes remain architecture-dependent. These results identify scalar-pathway fidelity as a practical design dimension for short-range equivariant interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [259] [Topological Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15897)
*Kacper Wyrwal,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了拓扑流匹配方法，通过拉普拉斯漂移项注入拓扑信息，在保持流匹配优势的同时有效处理结构化数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配方法将结构化空间（如脑图上的fMRI数据）视为欧氏空间中的点，忽略了其定义域的丰富拓扑特征，无法充分利用数据的几何结构信息。

Method: 将流匹配解释为求解退化薛定谔桥问题的框架，通过在参考过程中引入拉普拉斯导出的漂移项来注入拓扑信息，从而捕获底层域的结构特征，同时保持流匹配的稳定、无模拟目标和确定性样本路径特性。

Result: 在多种结构化数据集上验证了方法的有效性，包括脑部fMRI、洋流、地震事件和交通流数据，证明了对拓扑信息的有效利用。

Conclusion: 拓扑流匹配作为标准流匹配的直接替代方案，能够有效处理结构化数据，在保持原有优势的同时充分利用数据的空间拓扑特征，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Flow matching is a powerful generative modeling framework, valued for its simplicity and strong empirical performance. However, its standard formulation treats signals on structured spaces, such as fMRI data on brain graphs, as points in Euclidean space, overlooking the rich topological features of their domains. To address this, we introduce topological flow matching, a topology-aware generalization of flow matching. We interpret flow matching as a framework for solving a degenerate Schrödinger bridge problem and inject topological information by augmenting the reference process with a Laplacian-derived drift. This principled modification captures the structure of the underlying domain while preserving the desirable properties of flow matching: a stable, simulation-free objective and deterministic sample paths. As a result, our framework serves as a drop-in replacement for standard flow matching. We demonstrate its effectiveness on diverse structured datasets, including brain fMRIs, ocean currents, seismic events, and traffic flows.

</details>


### [260] [On-Policy Distillation with Curriculum Turn-level Guidance for Multi-turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15912)
*Gengsheng Li,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Ruiqi Liu,Tianyu Yang,Jie Sun,Qiyong Zhong,Haiyun Guo,Junfeng Fang,Dan Zhang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided-OPD算法，通过混合教师和学生生成的动作并逐步撤出教师干预，有效解决了多轮智能体蒸馏中学生误差累积导致教师监督失效的问题，在三个基准上平均提升25.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体（如规划、工具调用、环境交互的Agent）通常依赖大模型，推理成本高昂。自然的解决方案是通过On-Policy Distillation (OPD)将能力迁移到小模型，但OPD在此场景下存在特征性失败模式：学生模型的小误差会在多轮交互中累积，使轨迹偏离教师熟悉的状态分布，导致教师在学生最需要指导的地方反而提供不可靠的监督。

Method: 提出Guided On-Policy Distillation (Guided-OPD)算法：1) 在每个rollout中混合教师生成和学生生成的turns；2) 按照课程学习方式调度教师的干预概率，使其逐渐衰减至零。强指导在早期保持轨迹接近教师分布，随后逐步撤出以恢复推理时使用的纯on-policy模式。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop三个基准测试上，从Qwen3-30B-A3B教师蒸馏Qwen3学生模型。Guided-OPD相比vanilla OPD平均提升Score 21.1%、Success Rate 25.5%，且对更小的学生模型增益更大。

Conclusion: Guided-OPD通过渐进式撤出教师指导的有效策略，成功解决了多轮智能体蒸馏中的误差累积问题，为将大模型Agent能力高效迁移到小模型提供了实用方案，在更小规模学生模型上优势更明显。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents that plan, invoke tools, and interact with environments offer a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks, yet their capabilities typically rely on very large models whose inference cost is prohibitive in practice.On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is a natural recipe for transferring such capabilities to smaller students, but we find that it suffers a characteristic failure mode in this setting: small student errors compound across turns and push the trajectory out of the teacher's familiar state distribution, so the teacher's supervision becomes least reliable precisely where the student needs it most.We propose Guided On-Policy Distillation (Guided-OPD), a simple yet effective algorithm that mixes teacher- and student-generated turns within each rollout and schedules the teacher's intervention probability along a curriculum that decays to zero.Strong guidance keeps early trajectories close to the teacher distribution and is then gradually withdrawn to recover the purely on-policy regime used at inference.On ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, distilling Qwen3 students from a Qwen3-30B-A3B teacher, Guided-OPD improves Score by 21.1\% and Success Rate by 25.5\% over vanilla OPD on average, with larger gains on smaller students.

</details>


### [261] [Reinforcement Learning for LLM-based Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15917)
*Amit Arnold Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用GRPO强化学习方法微调1.5B-14B参数的LLM，使其具备通过Wikipedia工具和新闻摘要获取实时信息的能力来预测未来事件。1.5B参数模型经训练后预测性能超越Claude Sonnet 3.5，并探讨了LLM预测能力的扩展性及判断性预测在可验证/不可验证领域分类法中的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM在知识截止日期之外的预测能力，探索如何让模型通过外部工具获取当前信息来预测真实事件；评估不同规模LLM的预测扩展能力；分析判断性预测中固有认知不确定性（如掷骰子的随机性）的影响，并将其纳入可验证/不可验证的领域分类体系。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)这一高效的强化学习方法，对参数规模从1.5B到14B的预训练LLM进行微调。模型配备Wikipedia修订工具和新闻摘要功能以获取实时信息。设计了模拟不同预测动态特征的训练问题，并记录了研究过程中的失败尝试。

Result: 经过GRPO训练的Qwen 2.5 1.5B模型在预测任务上超越了Claude Sonnet 3.5，评价指标为与市场共识概率的交叉熵。实验结果揭示了LLM预测能力的扩展规律，并阐明了判断性预测的分类归属及固有不确定性的影响。

Conclusion: GRPO是一种有效的样本和内存高效强化学习方法，能够显著提升小规模LLM的预测性能，使其超越大型商业模型。该研究为LLM预测能力的扩展性提供了实证依据，并为判断性预测的理论分类和不确定性处理提供了新见解，同时记录的研究路径有助于避免后续研究的常见陷阱。

Abstract: We use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a recently devised sample and memory efficient reinforcement learning method, to finetune pretrained LLMs in the range of 1.5B to 14B parameters equipped with the ability to get current information through the use of a Wikipedia revisions tool, or news summaries, to forecast real events beyond the knowledge cutoff of the LLM, as well as problems made to simulate different aspects of the dynamics of that training.
  We use the results of these experiments to comment on the scaling capability of LLMs for forecasting, as well as classify how judgmental forecasting fits into the verifiable/unverifiable domain taxonomy, considering the impact of the inherent aleatoric uncertainty when forecasting future events (e.g. the roll of a die).
  As a result of the GRPO training, we manage to bring a 1.5B parameter transformer (Qwen 2.5 1.5B) to forecasting performance superior to Claude Sonnet 3.5 over the same dataset as measured by cross entropy from the market agreed probabilities. We also discuss various dead ends on the path to this result.

</details>


### [262] [An Exploratory Study of Blood Glucose Estimation from Photoplethysmography Signals using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15927)
*Ruhani Bhatia,Vijval Ekbote*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发布了一个配对数据集，包含智能手表采集的连续PPG信号和CGM设备记录的血糖值，并进行了初步的机器学习探索，结果表明PPG信号中可能存在预测血糖的潜在信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病和极端血糖水平是全球性重大健康问题。现有的连续血糖监测（CGM）技术虽有效，但传统的侵入式方法需要刺破皮肤，存在刺激和硬结等风险，因此迫切需要可大规模部署的准确非侵入式CGM方法。

Method: 利用可穿戴设备（智能手表）中的传感技术，以非侵入方式连续监测光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号。构建了一个配对数据集，同时包含智能手表采集的连续PPG信号和CGM设备记录的血糖值，并基于该数据集进行了初步的机器学习和深度学习模型实验探索。

Result: 发布了一个配对数据集，并完成了初步实验探索。初步结果表明PPG信号中可能存在预测血糖的信号，但需要来自更多个体的更大规模数据进行进一步探索验证。

Conclusion: 本研究为基于PPG信号的非侵入式血糖估计提供了数据基础和初步可行性验证，展示了利用可穿戴设备进行无创血糖监测的潜力，但需更多数据支持进一步研究。数据集已公开发布于Zenodo平台。

Abstract: Diabetes and extreme blood sugar levels are some of the major health problems faced by humans today across the world. While Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) has emerged as an effective technology for management of diabetes as well as for monitoring blood sugar levels, this technology has traditionally been invasive (that is, requiring the piercing of the skin) and carries the risk of irritation, induration, etc. This highlights the need for accurate and non-invasive CGM methods that can be deployed at scale. With the emergence of various sensing technologies and their integration in wearables like the smart-watch, we now have the capability to continuously monitor body signals like the Photoplethysmogram (PPG) in a non-invasive manner. Having the ability to continuously monitor blood glucose through CGMs and continuously monitor PPG signals through a smart-watch offers an opportunity to get dense data on these two, opening the possibility of building machine learning and deep learning based models to estimate blood glucose level from PPG signals. In this work, we first present a paired dataset comprising continuous PPG signals from a smartwatch along with glucose values recorded using a CGM device. We also present the results of some preliminary experimental explorations performed on our dataset. These preliminary results suggest that some predictive signals may exist, though more exploration is needed with more data from a larger number of individuals. The dataset can be accessed at https://zenodo.org/records/20577959

</details>


### [263] [Causal-Privacy Audit Workflow for Synthetic and Distilled Data in Dropout Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15940)
*Hanghang Zheng,Xiwei Zhuang,Zhong Wang,Hong Liu,Xiao Chen,Jingwen He,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了CaP-Eval工作流，用于评估合成学生数据在决策支持中的因果保真度和隐私风险，发现DPGNet在保持因果结构方面表现最佳，但生成数据需同时审计方向、大小、重叠度和隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成和蒸馏学生数据越来越多地用于隐私意识的学习分析，但其在面向决策的机构支持中的适用性仍不确定。在辍学支持中，生成的数据不仅要保持预测效用或分布相似性，还要保持用于指导建议、付款计划援助和奖学金相关决策的财务状况证据。

Method: 本研究引入了CaP-Eval，这是一个面向决策的因果-隐私审计工作流，用于在固定的估计量、时间感知调整设计、估计器集合和经验隐私治理屏幕下评估生成的学生数据。该工作流在预测效用、处理效应保真度、对替代估计器的鲁棒性和本地训练记录接近度方面比较了原始数据、蒸馏数据、对抗合成数据、统计合成数据和DPGNet隐私导向生成数据。

Result: DPGNet和蒸馏数据比对抗和高斯Copula基线更可靠地保留了原始财务状况处理效应结构。DPGNet在不同的epsilon水平上保持了完全的方向和等级一致性；epsilon=10产生了最小的非原始IPW和DML偏差，而epsilon=1和epsilon=5放大了几个财务状况对比。蒸馏数据保持高度保真度，但保留了最强的本地训练记录接近度信号。TabularGNet在定性方向上保持了一致性，但存在适度衰减；高斯Copula压缩了效应大小。

Conclusion: 预测效用、隐私导向、经验披露信号和因果保真度存在分歧；生成的学生数据在用于决策之前，需要进行方向、大小、重叠度和发布治理风险的联合审计。

Abstract: Synthetic and distilled student data are increasingly used to enable privacy-conscious learning analytics, yet their suitability for decision-facing institutional support remains uncertain. In dropout support, generated data must preserve not only predictive utility or distributional resemblance, but also the financial-status evidence used to guide advising, payment-plan assistance, and scholarship-related decisions. Method: This study introduces CaP-Eval, a decision-facing causal-privacy audit workflow for evaluating generated student data under a fixed estimand, timing-aware adjustment design, estimator set, and empirical privacy-governance screen. The workflow compares original, distilled, adversarial synthetic, statistical synthetic, and DPGNet privacy-oriented generated data on predictive utility, treatment-effect fidelity, robustness to alternative estimators, and local training-record proximity. Results: DPGNet and distilled data preserved the original financial-status treatment-effect structure more reliably than the adversarial and Gaussian Copula baselines. DPGNet preserved full direction and rank agreement across epsilon levels; epsilon = 10 produced the smallest non-original IPW and DML deviations, while epsilon = 1 and epsilon = 5 amplified several financial-status contrasts. Distilled data remained highly faithful but retained the strongest local training-record proximity signal. TabularGNet preserved qualitative directions with moderate attenuation, and Gaussian Copula compressed effect magnitudes. Conclusions: Predictive utility, privacy orientation, empirical disclosure signals, and causal fidelity diverged; generated student data require joint audits of direction, magnitude, overlap, and release-governance risk before decision use.

</details>


### [264] [Scalar-Stepsize Nonuniform Monte Carlo Optimistic Policy Iteration: A Certified Counterexample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15978)
*Yuanlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个反例，证明了在非均匀更新频率下，蒙特卡洛乐观策略迭代可能无法收敛，给出了Tsitsiklis提出的问题的否定答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsitsiklis证明了蒙特卡洛乐观策略迭代在均匀更新结构下的收敛性，并指出非均匀更新频率是一个微妙的障碍。本文旨在对非均匀更新情况给出确定的否定答案。

Method: 作者构造了一个三状态、两动作的折扣MDP模型，分析非均匀更新频率下的状态值递归。通过研究对角缩放的贪婪策略平均场，识别出非恒定的吸引性混合周期轨道。

Result: 在带有有界无偏几何视界估计器和Robbins-Monro步长的情况下，随机递归以正概率被困在周期轨道附近，因此无法收敛。这揭示了几何障碍：均匀采样产生径向残差收缩，而标量非均匀采样各向异性地扭曲残差动力学，可能产生切换吸引环。

Conclusion: 非均匀更新频率确实可以导致异步状态值递归的收敛失败，这为Tsitsiklis的猜想提供了确定的反例证明，揭示了标量步长与非均匀采样之间的根本不兼容性。

Abstract: Tsitsiklis proved convergence of Monte Carlo optimistic policy iteration under a uniform update structure and identified nonuniform update frequencies as a delicate obstruction. We give a certified negative answer for the natural scalar-stepsize, unnormalized asynchronous state-value recursion with fixed nonuniform state-selection probabilities. In a three-state, two-action discounted MDP, the nonuniform update frequencies induce a diagonally scaled greedy-policy mean field with a certified nonconstant attracting hybrid periodic orbit. With a bounded unbiased geometric-horizon estimator and Robbins--Monro stepsizes, the original stochastic recursion remains trapped near the cycle with positive probability and therefore fails to converge. The example pinpoints a geometric obstruction: uniform sampling gives radial residual contraction, whereas scalar nonuniform sampling anisotropically distorts the residual dynamics and can generate switched attracting cycles.

</details>


### [265] [Do Safety Monitors Stay Reliable After an Update? Benchmarking and Predicting Activation-Monitor Staleness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15980)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统测试了激活监控器在模型更新后的可靠性，发现量化类更新通常能保持监控器性能，而微调类更新常导致监控器失效，建议微调后默认重新验证监控器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活监控器作为部署安全堆栈的重要组成部分，但部署后的模型经常经历量化、微调、LoRA适配或适配器合并等更新，而监控器却保持冻结状态。研究者需要验证这种隐式契约是否成立：即在基础模型上训练的激活监控器在常规模型更新后是否仍然可靠。

Method: 对多种安全相关的监控器、模型深度、更新系列和开源权重模型进行系统测试，比较量化类更新和微调类更新对冻结探针性能的影响。进一步研究监控器类型的依赖性，并开发基于部署前特征的性能退化预测方法。

Result: 发现量化类更新基本保持冻结探针性能，而微调类更新经常导致探针过时；监控器脆弱性高度依赖监控器类型（隐私/PII探针最受影响，拒绝-合规探针相对稳定）；QLoRA特别有害而单独NF4量化相对良性；性能退化可从部署前特征预测。

Conclusion: 微调应默认触发激活监控器重新验证，预测方法可帮助优先检查最可能失败的监控器，从而优化重新验证预算的分配。

Abstract: Activation monitors-lightweight probes trained on a language model's internal representations-are an increasingly common layer in deployment safety stacks. Deployed models however are rarely static: they are quantized, fine-tuned, adapted with LoRA, or served with merged adapters while the monitor remains frozen. We present the first systematic test of whether this implicit contract holds: whether activation monitors trained on a base model remain reliable after these routine model updates. Across multiple safety-relevant monitors, model depths, update families, and open-weight models, we find a sharp split: quantization-style updates largely preserve frozen probe performance, while fine-tuning-style updates frequently make probes stale. Fragility is highly monitor-dependent, with privacy/PII probes most affected and refusal-compliance probes comparatively stable, showing that retraining a behavior need not stale its corresponding monitor. QLoRA is especially damaging despite NF4 quantization alone being relatively benign, suggesting that quantization becomes riskier when combined with adaptation. We further show that degradation is predictable from pre-deployment features, enabling revalidation budgets to be triaged toward the monitors most likely to fail. These results suggest that fine-tuning should trigger activation-monitor revalidation by default, while prediction can help prioritize which monitors to check first.

</details>


### [266] [Decomposing one-class support vector machine into an ensemble of one-data support vector machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16002)
*Toshitaka Hayashi,Dalibor Cimr,Hamido Fujita,Richard Cimler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加速单类支持向量机(OCSVM)的策略，通过分解数据集并对单个数据点训练模型，再通过集成学习组合，实现了比传统OCSVM更快的速度，同时保持相似的分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单类支持向量机(OCSVM)是最具竞争力的单类分类(OCC)算法之一，但在处理大规模数据集时存在可扩展性问题，训练速度较慢。

Method: 提出了一种OCSVM加速策略：(1)将数据集分解为单个样本；(2)为每个数据点单独训练OCSVM模型；(3)应用集成学习组合所有模型来计算数据集的OCSVM模型；(4)通过数据缩减策略（在训练样本平均值上训练OCSVM模型）实现进一步加速。

Result: 使用Python包进行实验对比表明，所提出的策略比传统OCSVM更快，同时达到了相似的分类结果。此外，该方法可以在样本和模型之间建立一对一的对应关系。源代码已上传至GitHub。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了OCSVM在大规模数据集上的可扩展性问题，提出了一种有效的加速策略，在保持分类性能的同时显著提升了训练速度。

Abstract: One-class classification (OCC) is a classification problem in which the training data contains only one class. The one-class support vector machine (OCSVM) is one of the most competitive OCC algorithms. However, OCSVM has scalability issues with large-scale datasets. This paper proposes the acceleration strategy of OCSVM. The idea is to decompose the dataset into samples and train OCSVM models for single data points. Subsequently, ensemble learning is applied to combine all models to compute the OCSVM model for the dataset. In addition, further acceleration is achieved through a data-reduction strategy with an OCSVM model trained on the average of the training samples. The experiment compared the proposal and traditional OCSVM using the Python package. The proposed strategy is faster than traditional OCSVM, while achieving similar classification results. Moreover, the proposed strategy can create one-to-one correspondence between samples and models. Source code is uploaded at https://github.com/ToshiHayashi/ODSVM

</details>


### [267] [IBAD: Interpretable Behavioral Anomaly Detection on Human Mobility Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16023)
*Bita Azarijoo,John Krumm,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IBAD框架，通过潜在狄利克雷分配(LDA)学习可解释的日常移动行为模板，并使用分层自监督模型检测行为异常，证明了行为原型可跨地理和人口背景迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动看似高度多样化，但个体的日常移动实际上可以由少数重复的行为模板(如通勤、学校活动、照护、夜生活、办事模式等)来解释。现有研究缺乏对这些可解释行为模板的学习及异常检测方法。

Method: 提出IBAD框架：(1)使用潜在狄利克雷分配(LDA)发现全局行为模板；(2)采用分层自监督模型从软行为模板中学习个体正常行为；(3)引入拼接基准测试创建受控的行为不匹配。该框架关注个体在跨位置执行的活动而非特定位置。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上的实验表明：(1)日常行为可有效分解为少量可解释模板；(2)学习到的行为原型能在不同地理和人口背景间迁移；(3)IBAD在所有设置中保持稳健的竞争性能。

Conclusion: IBAD成功实现了可解释的行为异常检测，将个体表示为行为模板混合的分布，证明了行为模板的可迁移性，为人类移动模式分析提供了新的可解释框架。

Abstract: Human mobility appears highly diverse, yet much of a person's daily mobility can be explained by a small set of recurring behavioral templates, such as commuting, school-centered activities, caregiving, nightlife, or errand patterns. We present \texttt{IBAD} (\underline{I}nterpretable \underline{B}ehavioral \underline{A}nomaly \underline{D}etection), a framework that learns interpretable daily mobility templates and represents each individual as a distribution over mixtures of these templates. Rather than focusing on specific locations, IBAD characterizes activities that individuals perform across locations. This approach first discovers global behavioral templates using Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA), then employs a hierarchical self-supervised model to learn normal behavior of individuals from their soft behavioral templates. We also introduce a \emph{splicing benchmark} that creates controlled behavioral mismatches between an individual's historical profile and injected mobility patterns. Experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets show that daily behavior can be effectively decomposed into a small number of interpretable templates. Crucially, we show that the learned behavioral archetypes \emph{transfer} across distinct geographic and demographic contexts. Furthermore, IBAD maintains a robust competitive performance across all settings. For reproducibility purposes, the code is accessible at ~\href{https://github.com/USC-InfoLab/IBAD}{https://github.com/USC-InfoLab/IBAD}.

</details>


### [268] [The Information-Theoretic Benefit of Shared Representations under Orthogonality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16028)
*Thomas Dittrich,Oliver Potocki,Philipp Grohs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从描述长度角度证明了，在满足正交性约束的组合式架构中，当任务共享潜在硬特征时，联合逼近相比单独逼近具有严格更优的编码效率，为现代多任务深度学习的参数效率提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构日益呈现多任务、多模态特点，通常采用预训练基础模型与任务特定微调模型相结合的方式。经验上利用问题间的相似性可显著提升整体性能，但联合逼近相比单独逼近的参数复杂度优势在理论上尚未被充分理解，特别是在模型需满足等变性、守恒律或正交性等结构约束的现代深度学习场景中。

Method: 作者通过构造一类正交函数类进行分析：使用Rademacher-Haar小波级数实现共享硬特征，结合Sawtooth-Walsh读出函数强制输出坐标正交。Rademacher-Haar小波的二叉树结构将逼近难度集中在公共特征分量上，读出函数则作为任务特定头部。基于信息论框架，在一致范数下分别建立了单独逼近类和联合逼近类的描述长度下界和上界。最后通过将问题归约到三角波逼近，在Heaviside激活函数的神经网络模型中实现了这种分离。

Result: 证明了联合逼近与单独逼近在最优逼近速率上存在尖锐差距。即使在对输出坐标施加正交性约束的情况下，当任务共享潜在硬特征时，组合式架构中的联合逼近严格需要更少的比特数。这一分离结果在具体神经网络模型中得到实现。

Conclusion: 研究从描述长度效率角度为组合式多输出架构提供了理论洞察，揭示了神经网络如何在几何约束下保持表达能力，阐明了共享潜在特征的联合逼近相比单独逼近具有严格的参数效率优势。

Abstract: Modern deep learning architectures are increasingly multi-task and multi-modal, using a pretrained foundation model combined with task-specific, fine-tuned models. Empirically, exploiting similarity across different problems, instead of solving them individually, can significantly improve overall performance. While the generalization and sample complexity properties of multitask learning have been widely studied, the parametric complexity of joint approximation in comparison to separate approximation remains less well understood. The question is particularly relevant in modern deep learning, where models are increasingly required to satisfy structural constraints such as equivariance, conservation laws, or orthogonality. We prove lower and upper bounds on the description-length for separate and joint approximation classes, respectively, in uniform norm. We build a class of orthogonal functions by composing a shared hard feature, realized by a Rademacher-Haar wavelet series, with Sawtooth-Walsh readouts to enforce orthogonality of output coordinates. The dyadic tree structure of the Rademacher-Haar wavelet concentrates the approximation hardness in the common feature component, while the readouts act as task-specific heads. Using an information-theoretic framework, we obtain a sharp gap between the optimal approximation rates achievable by joint and separate coding. Finally, we realize this separation in a neural network model using Heaviside activations via reduction to triangle-wave approximation. Our results show that even under an orthogonality constraint joint approximation requires strictly fewer bits in compositional architectures, provided the tasks share a latent hard feature. This provides theoretical insight into the description-length-efficiency of compositional multi-output architectures and clarifies how neural networks can retain expressivity under geometric constraints.

</details>


### [269] [Inference-Time Decision Calibration for Temporal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16034)
*Arthur Chagas,Arthur Buzelin,Yan Aquino,Pedro Bento,Gisele L. Pappa,Wagner Meira,Cristiano Arbex Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时序分类的“表示-校准”分解框架，通过冻结原生分类器并添加保守残差多尺度分支和分支感知校准器，区分表示不足与决策层证据利用不足，在多个数据集上验证了该方法在不同场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序分类中的错误通常被认为是表示学习失败，但实际上也可能源于可用证据如何转化为决策的问题。现有方法难以区分缺失的时间证据和决策层面未被充分利用的证据，这促使研究者开发一种能够分离这两种错误来源的方法。

Method: 提出表示-校准分解框架：(1)保持训练好的原生分类器冻结；(2)添加保守残差多尺度分支作为辅助证据；(3)设计事后分支感知校准器在决策时重新组合原生和残差证据。该方法无需重新训练骨干网络即可区分表示不足与决策层证据利用不足的问题。

Result: 在FI-2010、PTB-XL、UCI-HAR、MHEALTH和HARTH五个数据集上的实验表明：增益强烈依赖于场景。残差多尺度证据在噪声大或表示受限的设置中最有用（特别是短时域FI-2010和较弱的循环骨干网络），而分支感知校准在原生和辅助逻辑包含互补证据时发挥作用。接近饱和的设置从两种干预中获益有限。

Conclusion: 时序分类不应仅被理解为表示学习问题，还应被视为信任、组合和校准多视图证据的问题。这为时序分类研究提供了新的视角，强调了决策层面证据处理的重要性。

Abstract: Temporal classification errors are often treated as representation failures, but they can also arise from how available evidence is converted into decisions. This paper proposes a representation--calibration decomposition for temporal classification. We keep a trained native classifier frozen and separate two inference-time interventions: a conservative residual multi-scale branch that adds auxiliary logits to the native prediction, and a post-hoc branch-aware calibrator that recombines native and residual evidence at decision time. This design distinguishes missing temporal evidence from underused decision-level evidence without retraining the backbone. Across FI-2010, PTB-XL, UCI-HAR, MHEALTH, and HARTH, we find that gains are strongly regime-dependent. Residual multi-scale evidence is most useful in noisy or representation-limited settings, especially short-horizon FI-2010 and weaker recurrent backbones, while branch-aware calibration helps when native and auxiliary logits contain complementary evidence not fully exploited by the raw decision rule. Near-saturated settings show limited gains from either intervention. These results suggest that temporal classification should be understood not only as representation learning, but also as the problem of trusting, combining, and calibrating evidence from multiple views.

</details>


### [270] [Circuit Tracing in Autoregressive Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16044)
*Darin Tsui,William Deinzer,Daniel Saeedi,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProGenMech，一个用于生成式蛋白质语言模型的机械可解释性框架，通过跨层转码器(CLTs)重建层间生成计算，并在因果生成和零样本适应度估计任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于稀疏自编码器和转码器的机械可解释性方法主要关注蛋白质表示学习模型，无法捕捉自回归生成所需的计算机制，且对生成式蛋白质语言模型的底层生成机制理解不足。

Method: 提出ProGenMech框架，将跨层转码器(CLTs)扩展到ProGen3模型（一个稀疏混合专家模型，支持因果生成和跨度填充）；开发了零样本电路发现框架来识别负责蛋白质生成和适应度预测的稀疏潜在电路。

Result: ProGenMech在因果生成和零样本适应度估计任务中优于局部转码器基线，能够有效恢复ProGen3的概率分布和功能评分行为；在跨度填充任务中匹配原始模型的生成分布；发现的电路揭示了与保守序列模式和蛋白质适应度景观相关的生物学有意义模体和功能区域。

Conclusion: ProGenMech为可解释和可控的蛋白质生成奠定了基础，成功建立了一个能够忠实恢复层间生成计算的机械可解释性框架。

Abstract: Protein language models (pLMs) can generate novel protein sequences with properties beyond those observed in nature, yet the mechanisms underlying protein generation remain poorly understood. Existing mechanistic interpretability methods based on sparse autoencoders and transcoders primarily focus on protein representation learning models and do not capture the computation required for autoregressive generation. Here, we introduce ProGenMech, a mechanistic interpretability framework for generative protein language models that extends cross-layer transcoders (CLTs) to ProGen3, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts model trained for both causal generation and span infilling. Unlike per-layer approaches, CLTs reconstruct each layer using sparse latent variables from all preceding layers, enabling faithful recovery of inter-layer generative computation. We further develop a zero-shot circuit discovery framework to identify sparse latent circuits responsible for protein generation and fitness prediction. In causal generation and zero-shot fitness estimation tasks, ProGenMech outperforms local transcoder baselines in recovering ProGen3's probability distribution and functional scoring behavior, while matching the original model's generative distribution in span infilling tasks. Moreover, the recovered circuits reveal biologically meaningful motifs and functional regions associated with conserved sequence patterns and protein fitness landscapes, establishing a foundation for interpretable and steerable protein generation.

</details>


### [271] [Active Learning with Low-Rank Structure for Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16045)
*Vincent Cohen-Addad,Sasidhar Kunapuli,Vahab Mirrokni,Mahdi Nikdan,David P. Woodruff,Samson Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低秩近似和残差采样的新数据选择框架，在理论和实验上均优于现有的聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据选择方法（如k-center和k-means聚类）依赖于数据具有内在几何结构的假设，但许多现代数据集更具有全局代数结构，更适合用低秩近似或主成分分析来捕捉。因此需要一种新的数据选择方法来更好地利用这种代数结构。

Method: 提出基于低秩近似和残差采样的数据选择框架，通过行子集选择和损失保持核心集构造的视角来形式化。在满足温和正则性条件（可解释为代数或角度Lipschitz连续性）的嵌入表示下，选择加权数据子集。

Result: 证明可以选择$\tilde{O}(k + \frac{1}{\varepsilon^2})$个数据点的加权子集，其平均损失能在$(1+\varepsilon)$相对误差内近似整个数据集的平均损失（附加$\varepsilon Φ_k$项，其中$Φ_k$是嵌入矩阵的最优rank-k近似代价）。在真实数据集上的实验表明该方法优于均匀采样和基于聚类的敏感性采样。

Conclusion: 基于低秩近似的数据选择方法能够更好地处理具有全局代数结构的现代数据集，在理论和实践中都展现出优于传统聚类方法的性能。

Abstract: In the data selection problem, the objective is to choose a small, representative subset of data that can be used to efficiently train a machine learning model. Sener and Savarese [ICLR 2018] showed that, given an embedding representation of the data and suitable geometric assumptions, heuristics based on $k$-center clustering can be used to perform data selection. This perspective was further explored by Axiotis et. al. [ICML 2024], who proposed a data selection approach based on $k$-means clustering and sensitivity sampling. However, these methods rely on the assumption that the dataset exhibits intrinsic geometric structure that can be effectively captured by clustering, whereas many modern datasets instead possess global algebraic structure that is better exploited by low-rank approximation or principal component analysis.
  In this paper, we introduce a new data selection framework based on low-rank approximation and residual-based sampling, formulated through the lens of row subset selection and loss-preserving coreset construction. Given an embedding representation of the data satisfying mild regularity conditions, which can be interpreted as algebraic or angular notions of Lipschitz continuity, we show that it is possible to select a weighted subset of $\tilde{O}\left(k + \frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ data points whose average loss approximates the average loss over the full dataset within a $(1+\varepsilon)$ relative error, up to an additive $\varepsilon Φ_k$ term, where $Φ_k$ denotes the optimal rank-$k$ approximation cost of the embedding matrix.
