Abstract: Accurate state of health (SOH) estimation is a critical diagnostic service for lithium-ion battery management. However, reliance on labor-intensive manual feature engineering and opaque black-box models hinders scalable industrial deployment. To address this, we introduce TC-SOH: a modular, plug-and-play service architecture for autonomous, end-to-end SOH prediction. TC-SOH employs a temporal-contrastive mechanism and a cross-window prediction pretext task to extract degradation-relevant representations directly from raw operational data. To improve transparency, we connect model efficacy with representation diagnostics: visualization, sensitivity analysis, redundancy analysis, bidirectional probing, future-SOH probing, and temporal shuffling show that learned features overlap with selected expert descriptors while retaining additional SOH-relevant variation, and that ordered temporal context improves subsequent-SOH prediction. Across four public datasets, TC-SOH outperforms the considered physics-informed and data-driven baselines, reducing MAPE by 1.91 times and RMSE by 2.13 times.

</details>


### [309] [SDS-LoRA: Overcoming Anisotropic Gradient Scaling in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16454)
*Junghun Oh,Sungyong Baik,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LoRA中由奇异值驱动的各向异性梯度缩放问题，提出SDS-LoRA方法通过结构解耦奇异值来改善梯度传播，从而提升模型适配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文从几何视角研究LoRA参数化的局限性。作者发现当全量微调梯度反向传播到低秩矩阵时，会经历由奇异值驱动的各向异性缩放。这种现象导致梯度向主导奇异方向倾斜并抑制其他方向，从而扭曲全量微调梯度，降低低秩矩阵梯度的有效秩，并加剧与全量微调之间的性能差距。

Method: 提出SDS-LoRA（Singular value Decoupled Scaling LoRA），一种新的低秩参数化方法。该方法在结构上将奇异值从反向传播过程中解耦，确保全量微调梯度仅通过低秩矩阵子空间的正交基进行反向传播，独立于其尺度。收敛性分析证明，LoRA的收敛率会随低秩矩阵条件数恶化而下降，而SDS-LoRA保持独立。

Result: 在自然语言和视觉基准测试上的实验结果表明，SDS-LoRA改善了损失收敛性，缩小了与全量微调的性能差距，显著提升了适配性能。收敛分析验证了该方法对条件数的独立性，而标准LoRA会受到条件数影响。

Conclusion: SDS-LoRA通过结构化解耦奇异值，有效解决了LoRA中各向异性梯度缩放的根本问题，为大型预训练模型的高效微调提供了更优的参数化方案，在保持参数效率的同时显著缩小了与全量微调的性能差距。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient adaptation of large pre-trained models to downstream tasks by parameterizing weight updates with low-rank matrices. In this paper, we investigate the limitations of the LoRA parameterization from a geometric perspective. Specifically, we show that when a full fine-tuning gradient is backpropagated to the low-rank matrices, it undergoes anisotropic scaling driven by their singular values. We argue that this phenomenon is undesirable because it distorts the full fine-tuning gradient by skewing it toward dominant singular directions while suppressing others. Our analyses demonstrate that anisotropic gradient scaling reduces the effective rank of the low-rank matrices' gradients and results in suboptimal alignment between the full fine-tuning gradient and its low-rank approximation in LoRA, thereby exacerbating the gap to full fine-tuning. To address these limitations, we propose a new low-rank parameterization, SDS-LoRA, which structurally decouples singular values from the backward pass. Our method ensures that the full fine-tuning gradient backpropagates only through the orthonormal bases of the low-rank matrices' subspaces, independent of their scales. Convergence analysis demonstrates that while LoRA's convergence rate degrades with the condition number of the low-rank matrices, SDS-LoRA remains independent of it. Experimental results across natural language and vision benchmarks show that SDS-LoRA improves loss convergence and reduces the gap to full fine-tuning, significantly enhancing adaptation performance.

</details>


### [310] [SPRI: SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization for Data-Constrained MoE Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16456)
*Weiqiao Shan,Ruixiang Mao,Yuang Li,Yuhao Zhang,Yingfeng Luo,Tong Zheng,Chen Xu,Yucheng Qiao,Chunxiang Jin,Yi Yuan,Jingdong Chen,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPRI方法，通过SVD分解预训练FFN权重生成残差并分配给不同专家，在数据受限的监督适应场景下有效实现MoE升级循环，显著提升多语言语音翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE升级循环方法在数据受限的监督适应场景下表现不佳，主要由于专家同质化或对预训练参数的过度扰动，需要在利用预训练权重结构的同时引入充分的专家多样性。

Method: 提出SVD分区残差初始化(SPRI)，从预训练FFN权重中提取SVD分解残差并分配给路由专家，引入基于预训练频谱结构的可控专家多样性；同时设计两阶段训练策略提升适应稳定性。

Result: 在CoVoST2数据集的15个英到多语言翻译方向上，相比全量微调稠密模型平均BLEU和COMET分别提升2.58和3.32分，相比最优MoE升级循环基线分别提升3.39和4.34分。

Conclusion: SPRI通过利用预训练权重的频谱结构实现可控专家多样性，在数据受限场景下有效解决了MoE升级循环的核心挑战，为资源高效的模型稀疏化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable efficient scaling, but training them from scratch remains prohibitively expensive. MoE upcycling mitigates this cost by converting pretrained dense models into sparse MoE models. However, existing upcycling methods typically rely on large-scale continued training and often perform poorly under data-constrained supervised adaptation, due to either homogeneous experts or overly disruptive perturbations to pretrained parameters. In this setting, effective upcycling must leverage pretrained weight structure while introducing sufficient diversity among routed experts. To this end, we propose SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization (SPRI), which distributes SVD-partitioned residuals derived from pretrained feed-forward network (FFN) weights across routed experts, introducing controlled expert diversity grounded in pretrained spectral structure. We further introduce a two-stage training strategy to improve adaptation stability. We evaluate SPRI on multilingual speech-to-text translation, where limited supervised data challenges MoE upcycling and multiple target languages provide natural routing heterogeneity. On CoVoST2 across 15 En-to-XX directions, SPRI improves average BLEU and COMET over fully fine-tuned dense models by 2.58 and 3.32 points, respectively, and outperforms the prior best MoE upcycling baseline by 3.39 BLEU and 4.34 COMET points.

</details>


### [311] [Privacy from Symmetry: Orthogonally Equivariant Transformers for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16461)
*Alexander Yukhimchuk,Andrey Shulga,Mladen Kolar,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了正交混淆方法，通过在客户端用秘密正交矩阵变换嵌入，并设计了具有O(d)等变性的ConjFormer架构，在保持模型性能的同时有效防止服务器端的嵌入反演攻击，将token恢复率从超过35%降至最高1.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地运行大语言模型不现实，敏感文本推理被迫依赖第三方服务。现有分割推理方案仅将token保留在客户端并发送隐藏表示，但这些表示仍可通过最近邻搜索结合公开嵌入表被恢复，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出正交混淆过程：客户端在传输前用秘密正交矩阵乘以嵌入；设计ConjFormer架构，通过标量RMSNorm归一化和所有线性权重的块状正交共轭实现精确的O(d)等变性，使服务器端完全在旋转基下执行前向传播，无法观测未旋转的隐藏状态。

Result: 在基于PubMed微调的GPT-2和Llama 3.2 1B模型上，正交混淆消除了直接余弦最近邻反演，token恢复率从前10名超过35%降至最高1.3%，微调后困惑度仅增加0.4%。

Conclusion: 在架构层面强制对称性可以为隐私保护的LLM推理提供实用防御，无需噪声注入或重型密码学机制，实现了隐私与性能的有效平衡。

Abstract: Running large language models locally is often impractical, pushing inference on sensitive text to third-party providers. Split inference partially mitigates this by keeping tokens on the client and sending only hidden representations, but these representations can still be recovered via nearest-neighbor search against the public embedding table. We propose an orthogonal obfuscation procedure in which the client multiplies embeddings by a secret orthogonal matrix before transmission. To enable correct inference under arbitrary rotations, we introduce ConjFormer, a transformer variant that is exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant via a lightweight normalization change (scalar RMSNorm) together with blockwise orthogonal conjugation of all linear weights. As a result, the server performs the full forward pass entirely in the rotated basis and never observes unrotated hidden states. Experiments on GPT-2 and Llama 3.2 1B models fine-tuned on PubMed show that orthogonal obfuscation eliminates direct cosine nearest-neighbor inversion and reduces token recovery from over 35% top-10 to at most 1.3%, while increasing perplexity by only 0.4% after fine-tuning. These results indicate that enforcing symmetry at the architectural level can provide a practical defense for privacy-preserving LLM inference without noise injection or heavy cryptographic machinery.

</details>


### [312] [Learning aligned EEG representations with subject-specific encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16462)
*Bruna J. Lopes,Gabriel Schwartz,Sylvain Chevallier,Raphael Y. de Camargo,Bruno Aristimunha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 跨受试者EEG解码中，使用受试者特定编码器配合共享分类器的混合架构，能够作为学习到的对齐机制，在性能上优于传统的欧氏对齐(EA)预处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨受试者EEG解码虽然能获得更多训练数据，但也面临强烈的受试者间分布偏移问题。研究旨在探索是否可以仅通过任务监督和架构设计来学习受试者对齐的表示，而非依赖传统的预处理对齐方法。

Method: 提出混合编码器架构，用受试者特定编码器替代共享EEG编码器，后接通用分类器；在四个运动想象数据集上与标准EEGNet、AttentionBaseNet和CTNet基线模型（结合欧氏对齐EA）进行对比实验。

Result: 混合编码器将EA的作用内化：移除EA后验证损失曲线和潜在距离分析变化很小；受试者特定头部增强类别区分度并将每个受试者置于其自身潜在流形附近，改善了大多数受试者的性能，但对方法敏感的子集效果有限。

Conclusion: 受试者特定编码器可作为EEG解码的学习对齐机制，但针对未见受试者的头部选择仍是待解决的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG decoding promises more training data, but it also exposes neural networks to strong inter-subject distribution shifts. We study whether task supervision and architecture alone can learn subject-aligned representations. We replace a shared EEG encoder with subject-specific encoders followed by a common classifier, and compare this hybrid model with standard EEGNet, AttentionBaseNet, and CTNet baselines with Euclidean Alignment (EA) on four motor-imagery datasets. EA improves shared encoders by recentering subject covariances, but the hybrid encoder largely internalises this role: validation-loss curves and latent-distance analyses change little when EA is removed. Subject-specific heads increase class distinctiveness and place each subject close to its own latent manifold, improving most subjects while leaving a method-sensitive subset. These results support subject-specific encoders as a learned alignment mechanism for EEG decoding and identify head selection for unseen subjects as the remaining bottleneck.

</details>


### [313] [BRICKS-WM: Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16489)
*Shaowei Zhang,Jiahan Cao,Xunlan Zhou,Shenghua Wan,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRICKS-WM框架，通过模块化设计解耦世界模型中的智能体动力学和背景动力学，实现了跨智能体的世界模型复用，在保持性能的同时避免了针对新智能体重新训练整个世界模型的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的强化学习方法通常采用单体式潜在动力学模型，将环境动力学耦合在一个整体过程中。这种耦合严重限制了可复用性：即使环境保持不变，更改智能体也需要从头重新训练整个世界模型。基于物理世界由独立实体组成的观察，作者认为全局动力学可以建模为通过潜在接口交互的独立动力学模块的组合。

Method: 提出BRICKS-WM（Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models）框架，用于结构化世界模型的模块化组装。核心方法是将潜在状态空间分解为被驱动的智能体模块和外部背景模块，通过学习到的潜在接口进行桥接。与之前优先考虑视觉分割的以对象为中心的方法不同，BRICKS-WM在过渡动力学中强制实施功能分离，确保背景动力学对智能体动力学保持不可知性。

Result: 实验表明，BRICKS-WM从头训练时能够达到与强单体基线相当的强化学习控制性能，并且能够在不同智能体之间复用冻结的背景动力学模型。

Conclusion: BRICKS-WM成功实现了世界模型的模块化分解，通过功能性的动力学分离而非单纯的视觉分割，解决了传统单体式世界模型的可复用性问题。该方法在保持控制性能的同时，使得背景动力学可以跨智能体复用，显著提高了模型重用效率。

Abstract: Model-based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable success in continuous control by leveraging latent world models. However, prevailing approaches typically rely on monolithic latent dynamics, entangling environment dynamics into a coupled process. This coupling severely limits reusability: altering the agent necessitates retraining the entire world from scratch, even if the environment remains constant. To address this, we introduce BRICKS-WM (Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models), a framework for the modular assembly of structured world models. Driven by the insight that the physical world is composed of independent entities, we posit that global dynamics can be modeled as a composition of distinct dynamical modules interacting via latent interfaces. As a minimal instantiation, we factorize the latent state space into an actuated Agent module and an external Background module, bridged by a learned latent interface. Unlike prior object-centric methods that prioritize visual segmentation, BRICKS-WM enforces a functional separation in transition dynamics, ensuring that background dynamics remains agnostic to the agent's dynamics. Empirically, BRICKS-WM achieves control performance comparable to strong monolithic baselines when trained from scratch, and enables the reuse of frozen background dynamics across agents.

</details>


### [314] [daVinci-kernel: Co-Evolving Skill Selection, Summarization, and Utilization via RL for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16497)
*Dayuan Fu,Mohan Jiang,Tongyu Wang,Dian Yang,Jiarui Hu,Liming Liu,Jinlong Hou,Pengfei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了daVinci-kernel，一个基于强化学习的GPU内核优化框架，通过动态技能库实现技能发现与利用的耦合，在KernelBench上显著超越先前最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化领域需要在保证功能正确性的前提下提升执行效率，现有方法缺乏系统性的技能发现与复用机制，亟需一种能够自动发现、验证和重用优化技术的框架。

Method: 采用共享单一LLM主干的三智能体架构：1）技能选择智能体通过BM25和LLM重排序检索相关技术；2）策略智能体基于选定技能生成多轮CUDA/Triton内核；3）技能总结智能体将成功轨迹提炼为可复用技能。通过结构化SFT冷启动初始化，经执行验证的可复现加速技能方可入库，最终使用多轮REINFORCE和各智能体优势估计进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 在KernelBench上，daVinci-kernel-14B在Fast₁阈值下三个难度级别分别达到37.2%、70.6%和32.2%，均超越此前最强RL训练模型Dr.Kernel-14B。

Conclusion: daVinci-kernel成功实现了GPU内核优化中技能发现与利用的有效耦合，验证了动态技能库机制和三智能体协作架构在自动化内核优化任务中的有效性。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization represents a paradigm where functional correctness is assumed and execution efficiency is the objective. We present daVinci-kernel, a reinforcement learning framework that couples skill discovery with skill exploitation through a dynamically evolving skill library. daVinci-kernel jointly trains three agents sharing one LLM backbone: a Skill Selection Agent that retrieves relevant techniques via BM25 and LLM reranking, a Policy Agent that generates multi-turn CUDA/Triton kernels conditioned on selected skills, and a Skill Summary Agent that distills successful rollouts into reusable skills. Candidate skills are added only after execution-based verification confirms reproducible speedups. All three agents share a single LLM backbone, are initialized via a structured SFT cold start on diversity-filtered data, and are then jointly optimized end-to-end with multi-turn REINFORCE and per-agent advantage estimation. On KernelBench, daVinci-kernel-14B achieves 37.2%, 70.6%, and 32.2% on Level 1, Level 2, and Level 3 under the Fast$_1$ threshold, outperforming the strongest prior RL-trained model, Dr.Kernel-14B.

</details>


### [315] [Tail-Shape Estimation in LLM Evaluation Is Fragile: A Protocol for Diagnosing False Positives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16511)
*Luca Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于验证大语言模型评估中尾部形状声明的预注册协议，并在毒性评估任务中发现了先前研究存在的三类假阳性问题，证明尾部指数估计比已有文献显示的更加脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究建议将LLM评估从基于均值的指标转向尾部感知指标（如条件风险价值和尾部指数估计）。然而，尾部指数参数（用于描述尾部重度的指标）是否能在均值和标准尾部幅度统计量之外提供额外的判别信息，尚未得到严格验证。作者旨在检验尾部形状估计的有效性，并建立一套严格的验证标准。

Method: 作者预注册了一套包含四个关卡的验证协议：(1)可采性要求、(2)拟合优度检验、(3)阈值稳定性检验、(4)效应量要求。任何声称存在正尾部形状的研究都需通过这些门槛。该协议被应用于标准的LLM毒性评估设置，使用两种结构不同的评分器家族进行测试。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)协议成功识别出三种不同模式的假阳性情况，这些情况在未经严格检验的分析中会被当作真实结果发表；(2)两种评分器的头条尾部形状声明均被拒绝；(3)验证了协议能够有效捕获不稳健的尾部形状估计。

Conclusion: 在所检验的LLM毒性评估设置中，尾部形状估计比近期文献所建议的更加脆弱和不稳定。作者推荐将所提出的预注册协议作为类似场景下尾部指数声明的起点，以确保研究结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent work motivates moving large language model (LLM) evaluation from mean-based to tail-aware metrics, including conditional value-at-risk and tail-index estimates of reward-model error. We ask whether the canonical extreme-value-theory tail-index parameter, which isolates how heavy a tail is from how large the tail mass is, adds discriminative information beyond the mean and a standard tail-magnitude statistic in LLM evaluation. We pre-register a protocol covering admissibility, goodness-of-fit, threshold-stability, and effect-size requirements for any positive tail-shape claim. The protocol is the contribution of this paper; the empirical study below is a demonstration of what its gates catch. Applied to a standard LLM toxicity-evaluation setup under two structurally different scorer families, the protocol catches three distinct modes of false positives that a naive analysis would have published, and rejects the headline tail-shape claim on both scorers. We conclude that tail-shape estimation in the LLM toxicity-evaluation setups we examined is more fragile than the recent literature suggests, and recommend the protocol as a starting point for tail-index claims in similar setups.

</details>


### [316] [Direction-Conditioned Policies via Compositional Subgoal Scoring for Online Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16515)
*Swaminathan S K,Damiya Gondha,Theyanesh Eswaramoorthy Rajahkrishnan,Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direction-Conditioned Policies (DCP)，一种在线目标条件强化学习方法，通过将目标达成分解为子目标评分和方向条件策略两个共享InfoNCE表示的组件，解决了标准方法在目标远离数据分布时几何信息不足的问题，在9个环境中优于Contrastive RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman理论表明最优目标条件动作仅通过当前状态的目标到达距离梯度依赖目标，但标准在线GCRL仍将行动者条件化于原始目标——当目标远离数据分布时该信号在几何上缺乏信息量。

Method: DCP将目标达成分解为两个共享InfoNCE表示ψ的组件：(1)子目标评分步骤，选择与最终目标g在ψ_g中对齐的访问状态z_t；(2)方向条件行动者，消费从ψ(s_t)到ψ(z_t)的单位方向d_t和幅度r_t。两组件联合训练，部署时干净分解（子目标评分被移除，方向条件保持用g替代z_t）。

Result: 证明了三个理论结果：(1)HJB下的方向充分性：控制仿射动力学下最优动作仅通过价值梯度依赖目标；(2)定量界：在温和条件下，训练和部署时行动者的条件输入在表示误差和测地松弛范围内一致；(3)方向条件失败的可控子空间刻画。在9个环境中，DCP在大多数最终指标上优于Contrastive RL，在操作和障碍交互任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: 对比表示表现为编码环境拓扑的在线拟度量，学习的ψ距离景观的定性分析支持该方法；AntSoccer的单个失败案例定位到理论预期的学习梯度病理。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman theory implies that the optimal goal-conditioned action depends on the goal only through the gradient of the goal-reaching distance at the current state, yet standard online GCRL still conditions the actor on the raw goal -- a signal that is geometrically uninformative when the goal is far from the data distribution. We propose Direction-Conditioned Policies (DCP), a fully online method that decomposes goal-reaching into two components sharing one InfoNCE representation $ψ$: a subgoal-scoring step that selects a visited state $z_t$ aligned with the final goal $g$ in $ψ_g$, and a direction-conditioned actor that consumes the unit direction $d_t$ and magnitude $r_t$ from $ψ(s_t)$ to $ψ(z_t)$. The two components train jointly, factor cleanly at deployment (subgoal scoring is removed, while direction conditioning remains with $g$ in place of $z_t$), and admit independent modification at the same $(d_t,r_t)$ interface. We prove three results. First, direction sufficiency under HJB: the optimal action under control-affine dynamics depends on the goal only through the value gradient. Second, a quantitative bound showing that, under mild conditions on the learned representation and assuming the scoring rule returns an on-path $z_t$, the actor's conditioning input at training and at deployment coincide up to representation error and geodesic slack. Third, a controllable-subspace characterization of when directional conditioning fails. Across nine environments, DCP improves over Contrastive RL on most final metrics, with the largest gains on manipulation and obstacle-interaction tasks; a qualitative analysis of the learned $ψ$-distance landscape shows the contrastive representation behaves as an online quasimetric encoding environment topology, and the single failure case (AntSoccer) localizes to a learned-gradient pathology that the theory anticipates.

</details>


### [317] [How Post-Training Shapes Biological Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16517)
*Lukas Fesser,Hanlin Zhang,Michelle M. Li,Eric Wang,Bryan Perozzi,Shekoofeh Azizi,Sham M. Kakade,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了生物科学推理模型的后训练流程，通过100多个模型的受控实验发现：各训练阶段对泛化能力影响显著不同——CPT通过生物学语言对齐提升性能，SFT虽提高域内表现但导致域外性能下降，RL配合合理奖励可改善泛化；最优方案是简短SFT配合较大RL资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物科学推理模型采用语言模型与生物学基础模型结合的方式，通过后训练流程构建。然而，各个后训练阶段如何塑造推理与泛化能力缺乏深入理解，模型性能提升与过拟合专业化之间的界限尚不清晰。需要系统性地研究不同训练阶段对模型泛化性能的影响机制。

Method: 在基因组学、转录组学和蛋白质三个领域构建并评估了超过100个生物推理模型。通过受控实验设计，系统性地变换主干网络、持续预训练(CPT)、监督微调(SFT)和强化学习(RL)四个维度，同时测量域内(ID)和域外(OOD)性能表现。

Result: 发现各训练阶段对泛化能力产生截然不同的影响：CPT通过与生物学语言对齐提升下游性能；SFT持续提升域内性能，但导致域外性能早期达峰后随训练分布拟合而下降；RL应用于强SFT检查点并配合对齐奖励时，能改善域外性能并部分恢复泛化能力。生物推理能力并非随监督或计算资源单调递增，而取决于训练阶段的组合方式。

Conclusion: 在固定后训练预算约束下，最优的域内-域外权衡策略是采用简短的SFT阶段配合较大的RL资源分配，并实现各阶段间的非对称适应能力。研究揭示了后训练流程的阶段组合对生物推理模型泛化性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning models for biology combine language models with foundation models trained on multimodal biological data, including DNA, RNA, and proteins. These models are built through post-training, yet how each stage shapes reasoning and generalization remains poorly understood. We study when post-training improves performance and when it induces over-specialization. Across genomics, transcriptomics, and proteins, we train and evaluate more than 100 biological reasoning models under controlled variation in backbone, continued pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL), measuring both in-domain (ID) and out-of-domain (OOD) performance. We find that each post-training stage reshapes generalization in a distinct way rather than contributing uniform gains. CPT improves downstream performance by aligning models with biological language. SFT consistently increases ID performance but causes OOD performance to peak early and decline as models fit the training distribution. RL, when applied to strong SFT checkpoints with aligned rewards, improves OOD performance and partially recovers generalization. These results show that biological reasoning does not improve monotonically with additional supervision or compute. Instead, performance depends on how training stages are composed. Under fixed post-training budgets, the strongest ID-OOD trade-off comes from brief SFT, larger RL allocations, and asymmetric adaptation capacity across stages.

</details>


### [318] [Neural Bayesian Anomaly Mitigation: A Robust Loss that Doubles as an Unsupervised Contamination Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16524)
*S. A. K. Leeney,W. J. Handley,H. T. J. Bevins,E. de Lera Acedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NBAM是一种基于贝叶斯潜在开关混合模型的新型鲁棒损失函数，不仅能容忍数据污染，还能无监督地识别污染样本及其结构，在 CIFAR-10 非对称标签污染实验中表现优于现有鲁棒损失方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒损失函数（如Huber、Student-t和广义交叉熵）虽能使监督模型容忍污染数据，但无法识别哪些观测值被污染。作者旨在开发一种既能保持鲁棒性又能识别污染样本的通用损失函数。

Method: 提出神经贝叶斯异常缓解（NBAM）方法：基于贝叶斯潜在开关混合模型，边际似然定义鲁棒监督损失，关联后验定义无监督污染分类器。引入学习到的输入依赖先验π_φ(x)捕获污染的空间局部性，使已知污染附近的样本更可能被标记，同时自动产生奥卡姆惩罚以防止过度标记。

Result: 在CIFAR-10非对称标签污染实验中，NBAM无需监督即可恢复污染过程的结构：污染后验能够分离干净样本与污染样本，学习的异常头识别每个标签翻转对的方向。在污染率0.2-0.6范围内，NBAM优于四种鲁棒损失基准方法。

Conclusion: NBAM成功实现了鲁棒训练与污染识别的统一框架，可作为标准训练损失的即插即用替代品应用于任何监督学习流程，同时提供校准的逐样本污染后验概率，为数据污染问题提供了更完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Engineered robust losses such as Huber, Student-$t$, and generalised cross-entropy make supervised models tolerant of contamination but cannot answer which observations are corrupted. We introduce Neural Bayesian Anomaly Mitigation (NBAM), a general-purpose drop-in loss derived from a Bayesian latent-switch mixture model: the marginal likelihood defines a robust supervised loss, and the associated posterior defines an unsupervised contamination classifier. Like Huber or Student-$t$, NBAM can replace the standard training loss in any supervised pipeline; unlike them, it additionally learns a structured contamination model and returns a calibrated per-sample contamination posterior. A learned input-dependent prior $π_φ(x)$ captures the spatial locality of contamination, so that samples near known corruptions are more likely to be flagged, while an Occam penalty emerges automatically and regularises against over-flagging. On CIFAR-10 with asymmetric label contamination, NBAM recovers the structure of the corruption process without supervision: the contamination posterior separates clean from corrupted samples, and the learned anomaly head identifies the direction of every label-flip pair. Alongside these capabilities, NBAM outperforms the four robust-loss baselines considered here at contamination rates 0.2-0.6.

</details>


### [319] [Assessing Reliability of Symbol Detection in Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16535)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中概念检测的可靠性问题，发现联合训练可能导致模型在瓶颈层编码任务特定的捷径，使解释不可靠。通过交换独立训练的概念检测器和分类头，作者提出了一种可靠性感知的训练策略，显著缓解了概念泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）作为可解释人工智能工具，通过人类可解释的符号进行预测。然而，高任务准确性并不能保证这些符号被忠实检测：联合训练的CBM可能在瓶颈层编码任务特定捷径，导致解释不可靠。作者旨在研究概念检测的可靠性问题。

Method: （1）通过交换共享相同符号词汇表的独立训练概念检测器和分类头来评估概念检测可靠性；（2）利用性能下降、概念级指标和符号级不确定性估计来识别易发生虚假激活的概念；（3）提出可靠性感知训练策略：使用多个分类头优化共享概念检测器，并对依赖全局或实例级不可靠符号的行为进行惩罚。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011数据集（全概念监督）上，检测器和分类头几乎可以自由交换（准确率下降不到1个百分点，相对保留率超过99%，无概念检测低于机会水平）；在受控合成任务中，随着概念监督权重降低，模型保持近乎完美的任务准确率，但交换准确率和与真实概念的一致性下降到机会水平。可靠性感知训练显著缓解了泄漏问题，在泄漏状态下将交换准确率大约提高了一倍。

Conclusion: CBM中的概念检测可靠性是一个关键但常被忽视的问题。联合训练可能导致概念编码捷径而非真实语义。提出的可靠性感知训练策略能有效缓解概念泄漏，提升模型的可解释性和可靠性。在低概念监督场景下，该方法尤为有效，能够显著改善概念检测的忠实性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are a relevant tool for explainable Artificial Intelligence because they make their predictions through human-interpretable symbols. However, high task accuracy does not guarantee that these symbols are detected faithfully: jointly trained CBMs may encode task-specific shortcuts in the bottleneck, making their explanations unreliable. In this paper, we study concept-detection reliability by swapping independently trained concept detectors and classification heads that share the same symbolic vocabulary. We use the resulting performance degradation, concept-level metrics, and symbol-wise uncertainty estimates to identify concepts that are especially prone to spurious firing. Finally, we propose a reliability-aware training strategy in which a shared concept detector is optimized with multiple classification heads and penalized for relying on globally or instance-wise unreliable symbols. On CUB-200-2011 with full concept supervision, detectors and heads are almost freely interchangeable (swap drop below one accuracy point, relative retention above $99\%$, and no concept detected below chance), whereas on a controlled synthetic task we show that, as the concept-supervision weight is reduced, models keep near-perfect task accuracy while swapped accuracy and agreement with the ground-truth concepts collapse to chance. Our reliability-aware training substantially mitigates this leakage, roughly doubling swap accuracy in the leaky regime.

</details>


### [320] [MIRAGE: Auditing Anti-Muslim Bias in Frontier LLMs Across Reasoning, Agentic, and Time-Coupled Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16562)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Tamim Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIRAGE基准测试，评估1200个提示词在直接补全、思维链推理和代理决策三种条件下的反穆斯林偏见。结果显示：思维链推理加剧偏见12-34%，代理决策存在9-22个百分点的偏差，现有缓解方法在代理场景中效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管发现大型语言模型存在反穆斯林偏见已有五年，但大多数评估仍局限于单轮提示补全场景，无法反映前沿LLM的实际部署方式（如思维链推理和代理决策），因此需要更贴近实际部署条件的评估方法。

Method: 提出了MIRAGE（穆斯林身份推理与代理生成评估）基准测试，包含1200个提示词，涵盖三种部署条件：直接补全、思维链推理和模拟代理决策，涉及内容审核、贷款筛选、难民索赔摘要和招聘筛选四个领域，在六个前沿模型上进行评估。

Result: 三个主要发现：(i) 思维链推理相对直接补全加剧穆斯林-暴力联想12-34%；(ii) 代理决策在穆斯林与匹配的非穆斯林案例间存在9-22个百分点的偏差；(iii) 偏见与检索的新闻语境紧密耦合，近期冲突检索下偏见增加18-27%；现有的基于提示的缓解方法在代理场景中转移效果差。

Conclusion: 当前基于提示的缓解方法在代理场景中效果有限，能抑制直接补全偏见但无法有效解决代理决策偏差，表明需要针对实际部署场景开发更有针对性的缓解策略。发布MIRAGE基准和开源评估框架以支持针对性缓解研究。

Abstract: Five years after the discovery of persistent anti-Muslim bias in large language models, most evaluations remain confined to single-turn prompt completion, a setting that no longer reflects how frontier LLMs are deployed. We introduce \textbf{MIRAGE} (Muslim-Identity Reasoning and Agentic Generation Evaluation), a benchmark of 1{,}200 prompts spanning three deployment-realistic conditions: direct completion, chain-of-thought reasoning, and simulated agentic decision-making across content moderation, lending triage, refugee claim summarization, and hiring screens. Across six frontier models, we find that (i) chain-of-thought reasoning \emph{amplifies} rather than suppresses Muslim-violence associations by 12--34\% relative to direct completion, (ii) agentic decisions exhibit a 9--22 percentage-point asymmetry between Muslim and matched non-Muslim cases on identical evidence, and (iii) bias is sharply time-coupled to retrieved news context, increasing 18--27\% under recent-conflict retrieval. Existing prompt-based mitigations transfer poorly across our three conditions, suppressing direct-completion bias while leaving agentic asymmetry largely intact. We release MIRAGE and an open evaluation harness to support targeted mitigation research.

</details>


### [321] [RepNet: Tackling spectral bias in deep neural networks via parameter reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16575)
*Yong Wang,Tao Zhou,Xuhui Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RepNet，一种通过重参数化第一隐藏层权重和偏置来控制初始斜率和划分点分布的深度神经网络模型，有效解决了传统DNN在捕捉高频和多尺度特征时的频谱偏置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在科学计算中取得了显著成功，但在捕捉振荡和多尺度行为时经常遭受频谱偏置的限制。通过研究浅层ReLU神经网络在拟合高频函数时的失败原因，发现初始斜率尺度和网络诱导的划分点分布是解决快速振荡的关键因素。

Method: 提出RepNet重参数化DNN模型，核心思想是对第一隐藏层的权重和偏置进行重参数化，从而有效控制初始斜率尺度并提供适当的初始划分点分布。将重参数化的权重和偏置作为可训练参数，使DNN能够在训练过程中实现自适应频率缩放。此外，推导了重参数化DNN输出和斜率幅度的定量估计以指导初始化。

Result: 数值实验包括多尺度一维和四维函数逼近、结合物理信息神经网络(PINNs)的正向和逆向PDE问题以及算子学习，结果表明RepNet在捕捉高振荡特征方面相比普通DNN提高了预测精度，仅带来少量额外计算成本。

Conclusion: RepNet为克服频谱偏置和将DNN应用于多尺度问题提供了一种有效且灵活的方法，证明了通过重参数化技术可以显著改善深度神经网络在高频问题上的表现。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable success in scientific computing, yet they often suffer from spectral bias in capturing oscillatory and multiscale behaviors. In this study, we investigate this limitation by examining the failure of shallow ReLU neural networks in fitting high-frequency functions. This observation identifies two important factors in resolving rapid oscillations: the initial slope scale and the distribution of partition points induced by the networks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose RepNet, a reparameterized DNN model for ReLU and tanh networks designed for high-frequency and multiscale problems. The key idea is to reparameterize the weights and biases in the first hidden layer, which enables effective control of the initial slope scale and provides an appropriate distribution of the initial partition points. Furthermore, treating the reparameterized weights and biases as trainable parameters allows the DNN to achieve adaptive frequency scaling during training. In addition, we derive quantitative estimates for the output and slope magnitudes of the reparameterized DNN to guide the initialization of the proposed method. Numerical experiments, including multiscale one- and four-dimensional function approximation, forward and inverse PDE problems in combination with physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), and operator learning, demonstrate that RepNet improves the predicted accuracy of vanilla DNNs in capturing highly oscillatory features with slightly additional computational cost. These results indicate that RepNet provides an effective and flexible approach for overcoming spectral bias and applying DNNs to multiscale problems.

</details>


### [322] [On the Entropy Formula for Real, Complex, and Quaternionic Deep Linear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16579)
*Luis Contreras,Marco Nahas,Tejas Kotwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将实数Deep Linear Network的熵公式扩展到复数和四元数情况，获得了统一公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Menon和Yu建立了实数Deep Linear Network (DLN)的熵公式，但该结果仅局限于实数域。为了完整理解DLN的熵性质，需要将理论推广到复数和四元数域。

Method: 通过数学推广方法，将Menon-Yu的实数DLN熵公式扩展到复数域$\mathbb{C}$和四元数域$\mathbb{H}$，推导出适用于所有三种数域的统一形式。

Result: 成功获得了覆盖实数域$\mathbb{R}$、复数域$\mathbb{C}$和四元数域$\mathbb{H}$上Deep Linear Network的统一熵公式。

Conclusion: 该统一公式不仅推广了Menon-Yu的结果，还为理解DLN在不同数域上的熵性质提供了统一的数学框架。

Abstract: We extend the entropy formula of Menon and Yu for the real Deep Linear Network (DLN) to its complex and quaternionic analogues, obtaining a unified formula for DLNs over $\mathbb{R}$, $\mathbb{C}$, and $\mathbb{H}$.

</details>


### [323] [Multi-Modal Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Network with Mixture of Experts for Soil Organic Carbon Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16580)
*Daniele Mos,Felipe Drummond,Anton Bossenbroek,Soufiane el Khinifri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpTGNN，一个多模态时空图神经网络，用于预测表层土壤有机碳(SOC)。通过异构图表示、基础模型特征提取和混合不确定性量化，在非洲数据集上达到R²=0.762，显著优于XGBoost基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOC预测方法存在两大局限：(1)手工特征结合经典ML或单模态深度模型，缺失丰富的光谱和时序信息；(2)基于网格的架构忽略了野外测量的不规则空间结构。这些限制阻碍了SOC预测精度的提升。

Method: SpTGNN将土壤测量表示为异构图节点，包含三种边类型（空间邻近性、光谱相似性、高程），使用关系图注意力学习各关系的独立模式。采用微调的TerraMind编码器从Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1和DEM信号提取节点特征，结合环境协变量和学习的位置、时间嵌入。通过稀疏专家混合(MoE)模块融合四个信息流。使用异方差回归(偶然不确定性)和深度集成(认知不确定性)进行不确定性量化，并加入Moran's I惩罚项正则化空间自相关。

Result: 在全球SOC数据集上进行评估（全球约49k样本，非洲约26k，欧洲约14k）。5成员深度集成在非洲测试集上达到R²=0.762，RMSE=3.51±0.48 g/kg，MAPE=22.9%，优于表格XGBoost基线；最佳单检查点验证R²达0.864。集成不确定性量化达到后校准ECE为0.031(混合)和0.026(β-NLL)。消融实验证实异构图、MoE融合和微调骨干网络均有实质性贡献。

Conclusion: SpTGNN是首个统一基础模型特征提取、异构图注意力和分解不确定性量化的SOC估算框架，为农业可持续性、土地利用政策和施肥规划提供了更准确的SOC预测工具。该方法在多区域数据集上验证了有效性，为未来土壤碳预测研究奠定了新基础。

Abstract: Top-soil organic carbon (SOC) prediction is fundamental to agricultural sustainability, land use policy and fertilization planning. Existing approaches face two limitations: they pair hand-crafted covariates with classical ML or single-modal deep models that miss rich spectral and temporal information, and grid-based architectures ignore the irregular spatial structure of field measurements. We introduce SpTGNN, a multi-modal spatio-temporal graph neural network addressing both. SpTGNN represents soil measurements as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with three edge types (spatial proximity, spectral similarity, elevation), and applies relational graph attention to learn separate patterns per relation. A fine-tuned TerraMind encoder extracts node features from Sentinel-2, Sentinel-1 and DEM signals, combined with per-sample environmental covariates and learned positional and temporal embeddings. A sparse Mixture-of-Experts module fuses the four streams via top-$k$ routing. Uncertainty is captured by pairing heteroscedastic regression (aleatoric) with deep ensembles (epistemic), and a Moran's $I$ penalty regularizes spatial autocorrelation. We evaluate on a global SOC corpus split into three regional instances ($\sim$49k samples globally, Africa $\sim$26k, Europe $\sim$14k). Our 5-member deep ensemble reports $R^2=0.762$, RMSE $=3.51\pm0.48$ g/kg and MAPE $=22.9\%$ on the Africa test split, improving over a tabular XGBoost baseline; the best single checkpoint reaches validation $R^2=0.864$. Ablations confirm the heterogeneous graph, MoE fusion and fine-tuned backbone each contribute substantively, and the ensemble UQ stack achieves post-calibration ECE of $0.031$ (hybrid) and $0.026$ ($β$-NLL). To our knowledge, this is the first framework to unify foundation-model feature extraction, heterogeneous graph attention and decomposed uncertainty quantification for SOC estimation.

</details>


### [324] [Infant Spontaneous Movement Noise Improves Exploration in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16590)
*Francisco M. López,Markus R. Ernst,Francisco Cruz,Matej Hoffmann,and Jochen Triesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受婴儿自发运动启发，提出一种在训练过程中逐步增加探索噪声时间自相关性的机制，实验证明该方法能产生结构化的探索行为并提升深度强化学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习中常用的探索方式是时间上不相关的白噪声，但近期研究表明时间相关的有色噪声能产生更平滑的轨迹并提高状态空间覆盖效率。作者探究受婴儿自发运动启发的动作噪声是否也能改善探索效率，发现婴儿末端执行器速度的功率谱密度遵循有色噪声过程，且频谱指数随年龄增长而增加。

Method: 作者提出一种机制，在强化学习训练过程中逐步增加探索噪声的时间自相关性，以匹配婴儿运动的统计特性。这种渐进式的噪声策略模仿了婴儿从低频谱指数到高频谱指数的发育模式。

Result: 在多个强化学习环境中的实验表明，受婴儿启发的噪声能产生结构化的探索行为，与传统的探索策略相比可以提高学习效率。这种噪声策略在探索过程中展现出更好的状态空间覆盖能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明人类运动和认知发育模式可以为人工代理的学习机制设计提供有益指导，生物启发的探索策略在深度强化学习中具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Exploration in deep reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly implemented as temporally uncorrelated white noise. However, recent works show that temporally correlated colored noise can improve exploration efficiency by producing smooth trajectories with better coverage of the state space. We inquire whether action noise inspired by infant spontaneous movements can also improve exploration in deep RL. We find that the power spectral densities of babies' end-effector velocities follow a colored noise process where the spectral exponent increases with age. Inspired by this developmental pattern, we introduce a mechanism that progressively increases the temporal auto-correlation of exploration noise during RL training, matching the infant statistics. Experiments across several RL environments show that infant-inspired noise produces structured exploratory behavior and can improve learning efficiency compared to conventional exploration strategies. These findings suggest that human motor and cognitive development can provide useful guidance for designing learning mechanisms in artificial agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/trieschlab/baby-noise-rl.

</details>


### [325] [PhysGuard: Fisher-Guided Gradient Projection for Sim-to-Real Neural PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16602)
*Changjian Zhou,Junfeng Fang,Negin Yousefpour,Peng Wu,Bin Yan,Guillermo A Narsilio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PhysGuard框架，通过约束微调方向来保护神经算子中关键的物理特征，有效解决了仿真到实验数据的域适应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子模型在从仿真数据迁移到实验测量数据时存在sim-to-real gap。标准的微调方法虽然能减小这一差距，但可能会破坏预训练阶段学习到的物理相关表示。现有的知识保持适应方法主要针对视觉或语言任务，而神经算子需要保护的是核心尺度的物理结构而非语义或视觉特征，因此需要专门针对物理特性的保护方法。

Method: 提出PhysGuard框架，包含以下关键组件：(1) 利用仿真数据计算经验Fisher信息矩阵来识别物理关键参数方向；(2) 将微调更新限制在不干扰这些关键方向的子空间内；(3) 采用分层Gram矩阵表述使该方法对百万参数模型高效；(4) 自适应阈值自动确定保护子空间大小。该方法通过频谱探测实验验证了主导Fisher方向与低频输出结构强相关。

Result: 在四种神经算子架构和不同物理系统的基准测试中，PhysGuard在大多数评估指标上表现优异。在严重域迁移情况下，相比标准微调方法，低频误差降低高达32%，同时保持了适应性。该方法能够有效识别并保护物理关键特征，显著提升了sim-to-real适应性能。

Conclusion: PhysGuard为神经算子的sim-to-real适应提供了一个有效的物理保护框架，通过基于Fisher信息矩阵的参数方向约束，成功保护了核心物理结构，在保持模型适应性的同时显著提升了在实验数据上的预测精度。该方法为物理驱动的模型迁移学习提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Neural operator models trained on simulation data often lose accuracy when applied to experimental measurements due to the sim-to-real gap. Standard fine-tuning with limited real data can reduce this gap, but it may also damage the core physics-relevant representations learned during pretraining. Although knowledge-preserving adaptation has been widely investigated in vision or language tasks, it remains unclear whether these methods are suitable for neural operators whose architectures and protected knowledge are fundamentally different. Neural operators need to preserve core-scale physical structures rather than semantic or visual features. We propose PhysGuard, a physics-preserving framework for accurate sim-to-real adaptation of neural operators. Specifically, PhysGuard uses the empirical Fisher Information Matrix computed on simulation data to identify physics-critical parameter directions, then restricts fine-tuning updates to directions that do not interfere with them. A layer-wise Gram-matrix formulation makes this efficient for models with millions of parameters, while an adaptive threshold automatically determines the protected subspace size. A spectral probe experiment shows that the dominant Fisher directions are strongly associated with low-frequency output structures. Experiments on benchmark across four neural operator architectures and different physical systems show that PhysGuard performs strongly on most evaluation metrics compared to baselines. The benefits are most evident under severe domain shift, where it reduces low-frequency error by up to 32\% compared to standard fine-tuning while maintaining adaptability. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZhouChaunge/PhysGuard.

</details>


### [326] [TCHG: Tri-Trust Conditioned Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Reliable Dynamic Trust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16611)
*Bohao Liao,Boyu Deng,Qipeng Song,Jieling Wang,Jingchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCHG框架，将信任证据分解为三个独立通道并赋予不同功能角色，通过解耦异质信任证据提升图神经网络在信任预测任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图神经网络的信任预测方法将异质信任信号统一表示，未将其分解为独立证据通道，无法利用不同证据在信任建模中应发挥的独特作用，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 提出三信任条件异构图学习框架（TCHG），将信任证据分解为三个通道：实体可靠性控制消息准入、交互行为可靠性调节传播强度、情境信任通过上下文条件算子选择调节传播模式；针对三个证据通道时间演化尺度差异，维护具有非均匀衰减率的独立时间状态，防止快速变化的情境信号覆盖缓慢累积的实体可靠性；同时进行信任概率预测和输出概率校准。

Result: 在多个公开信任数据集上的大量实验表明，TCHG相比代表性的信任预测方法和异构图基线，实现了有效且可靠的信任预测，在稀疏或冲突证据下提升了预测置信度。

Conclusion: 通过将异质信任证据解耦为功能独立的证据通道并赋予其在图传播中的差异化控制作用，TCHG有效解决了现有方法无法区分利用不同信任证据的问题，为信任预测及相关应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Trust prediction infers latent user-user trust relations and provides important support for social recommendation, fake-review and manipulation detection, and risk identification. Graph neural networks have become a prominent approach to trust prediction because of their ability to learn network structures and complex trust dependencies. However, existing methods often rely on a unified representation of trust signals and do not disentangle heterogeneous trust evidence into separate evidence channels, failing to exploit the distinct roles that different evidence channels should play during trust modeling. To address this gap, this paper argues that trust evidence should not be treated as an undifferentiated input, but should be decomposed and used as functional control factors over graph propagation. We propose TCHG, a tri-trust conditioned heterogeneous graph learning framework that decomposes trust evidence into three channels and assigns them distinct functional roles in propagation: entity reliability governs message admission, interaction-behavior reliability modulates propagation strength, and contextual trust adjusts the propagation mode through context-conditioned operator selection. Since the three evidence channels evolve at different temporal scales, TCHG maintains independent temporal states with non-uniform decay rates to prevent rapidly changing contextual signals from overwriting slowly accumulated entity reliability. It further predicts trust probability and calibrates the output probability, improving predictive confidence under sparse or conflicting evidence. Extensive experiments on multiple public trust datasets show that TCHG achieves effective and reliable trust prediction compared with representative trust prediction and heterogeneous graph baselines.

</details>


### [327] [Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16620)
*Soham Bhattacharjee,Dushyant Singh Chauhan,Salem Lahlou,Martin Takac,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的推理时扩展方法EGLR，通过引入确定性的层递归轴$L$来补充传统的随机采样轴，在几乎不增加计算成本的情况下，将采样空间从单一温度轴扩展为$L\times T$笛卡尔空间，在MATH-500上相比纯温度方法提升8.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法仅依赖随机token级采样来生成rollout多样性，这种单一轴采样空间具有根本性限制。作者识别出第二个完全确定性且互补的轴：层跨度$L$，即在高不确定性token处递归重新应用冻结模型顶部解码层的层数。不同的$L$选择会产生不同的rollout，解决不同的问题子集。

Method: 提出Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion (EGLR)：一种无训练的解码过程，在高不确定性token处递归重新应用顶部$L$层，最多进行$K_{\max}$次迭代，直到下一个token分布收敛。结合$T$个温度采样，EGLR将单一轴随机rollout池转变为$L\times T$笛卡尔采样空间，同时保持几乎相同的每次rollout成本。

Result: 在8个指令微调模型和6个数学推理基准上验证，$L$轴与温度轴真正互补。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上的MATH-500测试中，联合$L\times T$ oracle达到91.6%，比纯温度oracle(83.4%)高8.2个百分点，比纯层oracle(81.2%)高10.4个百分点。扩展的rollout池为自洽性、best-of-$N$验证器和GRPO等下游过程提供了更丰富的候选。

Conclusion: 层递归轴$L$为推理时扩展提供了一个新的确定性方向，不依赖随机噪声，与温度采样互补，能够显著提升模型推理性能，为消耗rollout的下游过程开辟了新的改进空间。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has become the dominant lever for improving language-model reasoning, but existing methods derive rollout diversity from a single source: stochastic token-level sampling. We argue that this single-axis sampling space is fundamentally limiting, and identify a second, fully deterministic and complementary axis: the layer span $L$ at which a frozen model's top decoder layers are recursively re-applied at high-uncertainty tokens. Different choices of $L$ produce distinct rollouts that solve different subsets of problems, with no stochasticity. We instantiate this axis through Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion (EGLR), a training-free decoding procedure that re-applies the top-$L$ layers for at most $K_{\max}$ iterations until the next-token distribution converges. Combined with $T$ temperature samples, EGLR turns a single-axis stochastic rollout pool into an $L\times T$ Cartesian sampling space at almost the same per-rollout cost. We characterize this space across $8$ instruction-tuned models and $6$ math reasoning benchmarks, and show that the $L$-axis is genuinely complementary to temperature: on MATH-500 with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, the joint $L\times T$ oracle reaches $91.6\%$, $+8.2$ percentage points beyond the temperature-only oracle ($83.4\%$) and $+10.4$ points beyond the layer-only oracle ($81.2\%$), confirming that the two axes capture genuinely complementary problems. The expanded rollout pool provides richer per-prompt candidates for any downstream procedure that consumes rollouts, including self-consistency, best-of-$N$ with verifiers, and group-relative RL training (GRPO), opening a new direction for inference-time scaling that does not rely on stochastic noise.

</details>


### [328] [SPICE: Synergy and Partial Information Based Curriculum Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16639)
*Ankush Pratap Singh,Houwei Cao,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPICE框架，一种基于部分信息分解（PID）理论的多模态课程学习方法，通过分解冗余、独特和协同信息成分动态调整样本学习顺序，实现了随训练过程自适应演化的多模态学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态课程学习策略假设样本的相对复杂度在整个训练过程中保持不变，无法适应模型的演化过程；而实际上每个模态的信息量在不同样本和训练阶段差异很大，需要一种能够动态适应模型演化的课程学习方法。

Method: 基于部分信息分解（PID）理论，将多模态交互分解为冗余、独特和协同信息成分，实现对样本复杂度的可解释动态表征；在此基础上设计渐进式课程，使模型从学习跨模态共享线索过渡到模态特定模式，最后学习复杂协同交互；利用单模态和多模态预测得出的PID信息估计实时优化样本排序。

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试上的实验表明，该方法相比传统训练和最先进基线方法均有一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: PID信息分解与自适应样本排序相结合的多模态课程学习方法有效，能够通过可解释的信息成分分解和动态课程演化显著提升多模态学习性能。

Abstract: Multimodal learning exploits complementary information across heterogeneous modalities. The informativeness of each modality can vary widely across samples and training stages. Existing multimodal curriculum learning strategies often assume that the relative complexity of samples remains unchanged throughout training and therefore cannot adapt to model evolution. We propose SPICE (Synergy and Partial Information based Curriculum Evolution), a novel progressive curriculum framework for multimodal interaction learning. Guided by Partial Information Decomposition (PID) theory, our approach decomposes multimodal interactions into redundant, unique, and synergistic information components, enabling an interpretable and dynamic characterization of sample complexity. Building on this decomposition, we design a progressive curriculum that evolves throughout training, allowing the model to transition from learning shared cross-modal cues to modality-specific patterns and, finally, to complex synergistic interactions. Adapting to model evolution, sample ordering is refined in real-time using PID information estimates derived from unimodal and multimodal predictions. Experiments across multiple multimodal benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over conventional training and state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of PID information decomposition and adaptive sample ordering for multimodal curriculum learning.

</details>


### [329] [Distribution Alignment for One-Shot Federated Learning via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16655)
*Daniele Berardini,Vito Paolo Pastore,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SLOT-Align，一种针对单次联邦学习的几何感知特征协调框架，通过最优传输对齐解决联合域偏移和标签偏移问题，无需修改现有方法即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次联邦学习（OSFL）中，客户端与服务器仅交互一次，异构客户端数据分布的影响被放大。域偏移与标签偏移的联合作用导致特征表示错位，且无法通过迭代优化纠正。现有方法依赖知识蒸馏、服务器端生成或集成聚合，但假设表示已对齐或仅单独处理域/标签偏移，难以应对复杂的联合偏移场景。

Method: 提出SLOT-Align框架：使用共享冻结编码器提取紧凑特征统计量；通过Bures-Wasserstein重心构建全局参考；利用闭式测地线最优传输映射对齐局部表示。该方法计算高效，无需修改训练过程，可直接与依赖冻结编码器的现有OSFL流程结合。

Result: 在多个基准数据集、预训练骨干网络及OSFL方法上的广泛实验表明，SLOT-Align在联合域偏移和标签偏移下持续提升模型准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SLOT-Align为OSFL提供了一种高效、通用的特征协调方案，有效解决了联合域标签偏移下的表示错位问题，具有良好的兼容性和实用价值。

Abstract: One-Shot Federated Learning (OSFL) addresses extreme communication regimes in which clients interact with the server only once, amplifying the impact of heterogeneous client data distributions. In particular, the interaction of domain shift and label shift across clients induces misaligned feature representations that cannot be corrected through iterative optimization. Existing OSFL methods rely on distillation, server-side generation or ensemble-based aggregation, but assume aligned representations or address domain and label shift separately. We introduce SLOT-Align (Single-round, Learning-free Optimal Transport Alignment), a geometry-aware feature harmonization framework for OSFL. SLOT-Align uses a shared frozen encoder to extract compact feature statistics, constructs a global reference via Bures-Wasserstein barycenters, and aligns local representations using closed-form geodesic optimal transport maps. The method is computationally efficient and can be combined with existing OSFL pipelines relying on frozen encoders without modifying their training procedures. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, pretrained backbones, and OSFL methods show that SLOT-Align consistently improves accuracy and robustness under joint domain and label shift.

</details>


### [330] [Near-Optimal Stochastic Linear Bandits with Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16656)
*Ofir Schlisselberg,Mengxiao Zhang,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带延迟反馈的随机线性老虎机问题，发现延迟是否依赖于损失会显著影响问题难度：损失无关延迟仅带来维度无关的加性遗憾惩罚，而损失相关延迟则引入了依赖维度平方根的根本性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟反馈场景下，线性老虎机问题与多臂老虎机（MAB）的行为差异尚不清楚。现有研究未能清晰刻画延迟与线性结构如何交互影响遗憾界限，特别是缺乏对不同延迟模型（损失无关、损失相关、延迟即收益）的系统分析。

Method: 针对三种延迟模型进行理论分析：(1)损失无关延迟模型，建立延迟独立于实现损失的场景；(2)损失相关延迟模型，分析延迟依赖于损失的情况；(3)延迟即收益模型，作为损失相关延迟的特例。通过构造上界和下界证明来刻画延迟惩罚的本质。

Result: (1)损失无关延迟：遗憾惩罚仅为加性，随机延迟下与期望延迟成比例，对抗性延迟下与最大未观测数成比例，且均与维度无关；(2)损失相关延迟：证明了上下界匹配（对数因子内），延迟惩罚依赖于维度的平方根，比MAB显著更难；(3)延迟即收益模型：MAB中仅依赖最优臂延迟的最优保证在线性老虎机中无法实现。

Conclusion: 本文给出了延迟反馈与线性泛化交互作用的精确刻画：损失无关延迟下线性结构与MAB表现类似，仅产生维度无关的加性惩罚；而损失相关延迟下，线性结构带来根本性困难，遗憾惩罚与维度相关。这一发现明确了线性老虎机在延迟反馈场景下的本质难点。

Abstract: We study stochastic linear bandits with delayed feedback under several delay models and establish near-optimal regret guarantees. Our results identify when delayed linear bandits exhibit the same qualitative behavior as multi-armed bandits (MAB), and when the linear structure creates fundamentally new challenges. Specifically, (1) for \emph{loss-independent delays}, where the delay does not depend on the realized loss (but potentially depends on the arm), we show that delays incur only an additive regret penalty. Under stochastic delays, this penalty scales with the expected delay, while under adversarial delays, it scales with the maximum number of outstanding observations. Notably, both delay penalties are dimension-free, improving upon the state-of-the-art results; (2) for \emph{loss-dependent delays}, we show that linear bandits are substantially harder than MAB: unlike in MAB, we prove matching (up to log factors) upper and lower bounds in linear bandits, whose delay penalty depends on the square root of the dimension. (3) for the \emph{delay-as-payoff model}, a special case of loss-dependent delay, we show that the optimal MAB guarantee, which depends only on the delay of the optimal arm, is also unattainable in linear bandits. Together, these results provide a sharp characterization of how delayed feedback interacts with linear generalization.

</details>


### [331] [Beyond Defensive Reporting: Machine Learning for Active Anti-Money Laundering Control in Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16663)
*Dara Goldar,Geir Kjetil Ferkingstad Sandve,Martin Jullum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究机器学习在保险理赔反洗钱检测中的应用，发现结合欺诈标签可显著提升检测效果，最佳模型能在前2-6%的理赔中捕获近三分之二的洗钱案件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险理赔洗钱对保险公司构成欺诈赔付、声誉和监管风险，但现有研究缺乏对此类洗钱预防方法的探讨，亟需从被动报告转向主动预防。

Method: 利用挪威主要保险公司的生产数据，训练梯度提升决策树模型检测被报告为可疑洗钱的理赔；引入预算加权捕获率指标评估模型；探索保险欺诈标签作为辅助训练信号的效果。

Result: 融入欺诈调查标签显著提升洗钱检测性能；最佳模型在前2-6%的高风险理赔中可捕获近三分之二的洗钱案件。

Conclusion: 机器学习可有效协助保险公司提前标记可疑洗钱理赔，实现主动预防；欺诈与洗钱行为模式存在关联性，欺诈标签可作为有效的辅助训练信号。

Abstract: Money laundering through insurance claims poses a threat to insurers both through fraudulent payouts and reputational and regulatory risk. Despite this, little research has examined how such laundering can be prevented. This paper examines whether machine learning can help insurers flag suspicious claims before payout, shifting the focus from passive reporting to active prevention. Using production data from a major Norwegian insurer, we train gradient-boosted decision tree models to detect claims later reported to authorities for suspected money laundering. Because fraud and laundering may share behavioural patterns, we also examine whether insurance fraud labels can serve as an auxiliary training signal. We compare different learning setups using the Budget-Weighted Capture Rate, a metric introduced in this paper to measure how many laundering cases are captured when only a small share of claims can be manually reviewed. The results show that incorporating fraud-related investigation labels substantially improves laundering detection. The best-performing model captures nearly two-thirds of laundering cases within the top-ranked 2 to 6 percent of claims selected for investigation. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of machine learning for money laundering detection in insurance claims.

</details>


### [332] [Multimodal Evaluator Preference Collapse: Cross-Modal Contagion in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16682)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多模态AI评估中存在严重的评价者偏好崩溃（EPC）和跨模态传染现象：单一策略权重达48.4%，跨模态偏好转移导致策略反转。跨模型评估架构是主要风险因素，自评估可提供近完全免疫（97%零传染）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理使用语言模型评估自身输出时会涌现系统性偏差，而评价者偏好崩溃（EPC）在多模态设置中被急剧放大，且存在跨模态传染的新现象——一种模态上的评估者偏好会转移到并破坏另一种模态的策略选择，需要深入研究这一问题的严重程度、传播机制和风险因素。

Method: 使用GPT-4o评估DeepSeek-chat在文本和视觉任务上的表现，设计四阶段隔离训练范式测量传染系数，通过四个评估器配置进行统计验证（N=53次独立重复，15,592次API调用），引入传染矩阵并发布MM-EPC实验框架。

Result: 单策略（step_by_step）吸收48.4%权重（是纯文本自评估的3.2倍）；跨模型评估产生强对称双向传染（γ_{T→V}=1.176，γ_{V→T}=1.089）；高轮次导致单一策略主导（70%零传染）；自评估提供近完全免疫（97%运行零传染，均值γ=0.033）。未发现显著方向不对称性。

Conclusion: 跨模型评估器架构是偏好传染的主要风险因素，自评估机制可有效免疫跨模态传染。研究为多模态AI系统的评估设计提供了关键见解，建议避免跨模型评估架构以防止偏好传染。

Abstract: When AI agents use language models to evaluate their own outputs in a feedback loop, systematic biases emerge. We show that Evaluator Preference Collapse (EPC) is dramatically amplified in multimodal settings. Using GPT-4o to evaluate DeepSeek-chat across text and visual tasks, we find that a single strategy (step_by_step) absorbs 48.4% of all weight -- 3.2x the collapse observed in text-only self-evaluation -- while three visual-domain strategies receive only 9.1% combined weight. We then demonstrate a novel phenomenon we term cross-modal contagion: evaluator preferences acquired on one modality transfer to and corrupt strategy selection on another. Through a four-phase isolation training paradigm, we measure contagion coefficients and document strategy inversion -- the optimal strategy for a modality reverses after cross-modal exposure. A Phase 3 statistical validation across four evaluator configurations (N=53 total independent repetitions, 15,592 API calls) reveals a clear hierarchy: cross-model evaluation (GPT-4o, N=8) produces strong but symmetric bidirectional contagion (mean gamma_{T->V}=1.176, gamma_{V->T}=1.089, Delta=-0.088, p=0.575, Cohen's d=0.29); high round counts (DashScope, 50 rounds) cause collapse to single-strategy dominance (70% zero contagion); and self-evaluation provides near-complete immunity -- 97% of runs (N=30, DeepSeek-chat) yield exactly zero contagion (mean gamma=0.033, 95% CI [-0.031, 0.010], p=0.642, d=0.07). No evaluator condition shows statistically significant directional asymmetry. We introduce the contagion matrix indexed by evaluator identity, release the MM-EPC experimental framework, and identify cross-model evaluator architecture as the primary risk factor for preference contagion.

</details>


### [333] [Adaptive inference and function vectors in deep transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16694)
*Ravin Raj,Gautam Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个深度Transformer的平均场交互系统理论，揭示了Transformer通过内部状态表示（“函数向量”）实现分布式推理的机制，并在上下文回归任务中验证了潜在上下文变量的非高斯、分层结构与Transformer深度之间的非平凡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer被广泛应用于学习复杂变量间的相关性，但其内部机制仍不明确。为了理解Transformer如何实现分布式推理以及深度和前馈块对其能力的影响，作者提出了一个理论框架来解释Transformer的内部工作机制。

Method: 作者将深度Transformer建模为一个受通信、局部性和深度约束的平均场交互系统，提出了“函数向量”概念，理论分析了Transformer如何在不同层次上推断潜在上下文变量。通过约束线性注意力Transformer在上下文回归任务中进行实验验证。

Result: 理论预测并实验验证了潜在上下文变量的非高斯、分层结构与Transformer深度之间存在非平凡关系。实验表明，深度架构能够实现自适应推理，前馈块和深度使Transformer能够实现比之前描述的更丰富的上下文学习算法类别。

Conclusion: 深度Transformer可以通过内部状态表示实现分布式推理，其深度和前馈块显著增强了上下文学习能力，能够处理具有复杂层次结构的潜在变量，揭示了Transformer强大的表示学习能力的理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers are widely used as a general-purpose substrate for learning complex correlations between a large collection of coupled variables, but their internal mechanisms have remained mysterious. We introduce a theory of a deep transformer as a mean-field interacting system that implements distributed inference, subject to constraints on communication, locality and depth. We show that such a system can exploit internal state representations ('function vectors') to infer a latent context variable at increasingly finer scales over its layers. In an in-context regression task, the theory predicts a non-trivial relationship between non-Gaussian, hierarchical structure in the latent context variable, and transformer depth. Predictions are tested using constrained linear attention transformers and demonstrate adaptive inference in deep architectures. Feedforward blocks and depth enable transformers to implement a much richer class of in-context learning algorithms than previously described.

</details>


### [334] [Learning Policy from a Single Trajectory in Average-Reward Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16729)
*Jongmin Lee,Ernest K. Ryu,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为弱连通平均奖励MDP建立了单轨迹有限样本复杂度保证，提出了无需先验知识的无模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程的有限样本分析工作有限，且大多依赖遍历性或生成模型等严格假设。本文旨在为弱连通平均奖励MDP从单轨迹学习角度提供首个有限样本复杂度保证。

Method: 研究弱连通MDP中单轨迹的动力学特性，基于此分析开发了新颖的无模型方法，包括基于价值和基于策略的两类方法。

Result: 在弱连通MDP中，基于价值和基于策略的方法分别实现了Õ(1/ε²)和Õ(1/ε⁴)的单轨迹有限样本复杂度保证。此外，提出了首个不需要问题相关量先验知识的无模型方法（针对连通MDP）。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了弱连通平均奖励MDP单轨迹学习的理论空白，建立了首个有限样本复杂度保证，并开发了实用的无模型算法。

Abstract: While there is an extensive body of work characterizing the sample complexity of discounted cumulative-reward MDPs, finite sample analyses for average-reward MDPs have been limited, and most existing works rely on restrictive assumptions such as ergodicity or access to a generative model. In this work, we establish the first finite sample complexity guarantees from a single trajectory for weakly communicating average-reward MDPs. To this end, we study the dynamics of a single trajectory in weakly communicating MDPs and based on this analysis, we develop novel model-free methods. Notably, our value-based and policy-based methods provide finite sample complexity guarantees of $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$ and $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^4)$ from a single trajectory in weakly communicating MDPs, respectively. Furthermore, we introduce the first model-free method that requires no prior knowledge of problem-dependent quantities for communicating MDPs.

</details>


### [335] [MyPCBench: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16748)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Andrew Keunwoo Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MyPCBench，一个针对计算机使用型智能体的个性化桌面环境基准测试。该环境包含17个模拟的Web应用和完整的桌面栈，以Michael Scott为典型用户角色，定义了184个任务。测试了6个模型，其中Claude Opus 4.6表现最佳，解决率为55.4%，是唯一超过50%的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的计算机使用型智能体基准测试是在非个性化环境中评估模型，而实际部署中的个人助理需要跨用户的整个数字生活工作，包括上下文、历史数据和登录账户，特别是在Web任务上差距最大——现有的实时Web评估无法测试需要登录或个人信息的网站，而这正是真实个人助理需要处理的场景。

Method: 构建了MyPCBench环境：在Linux桌面环境中部署17个模拟的真实世界Web应用和完整的桌面栈，所有应用都以一个典型用户角色（Michael Scott）进行数据填充。基于OpenClaw社区的真实请求定义了184个任务，并为六个闭源和开源模型提供了统一的计算机+bash工具接口进行测试。

Result: 在六个测试模型中，Claude Opus 4.6表现最好，完全解决了55.4%的任务，也是唯一一个解决率超过50%的模型。模型失败主要集中在跨多个应用的任务和长轨迹任务上，这些场景对个性化助手的压力最大。

Conclusion: MyPCBench填补了个性化桌面环境评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在处理跨应用任务和长轨迹任务时的局限性。研究发布了环境、任务集和智能体框架，为未来开发真正的个人助理型智能体提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for computer-use agents evaluate models in impersonal environments. This leaves a gap between evaluation and deployment where personal assistants are expected to work across a user's whole digital life, including their context, historical data, and logged-in accounts. This gap is widest on web tasks, where live web evaluations cannot exercise sites that require logging in or personal information, the kind of site a real personal assistant has to drive. We introduce MyPCBench, which tests computer-use agents as personal assistants on a Linux desktop populated with 17 simulated real-world web applications and a full desktop stack, all seeded for one canonical persona, Michael Scott from The Office. We define 184 tasks in this environment, each inspired by a real request drawn from the OpenClaw community, and benchmark six closed and open-weight models with a uniform computer+bash tool surface. We find that the best model, Claude Opus 4.6, fully solves 55.4\% of the tasks, the only model above 50\%. Model failures cluster on tasks that span many applications and on long trajectories, where personalization stresses an assistant the most. We release the environment, task set, and agent harness at https://mypcbench.com.

</details>


### [336] [Maximum Entropy Inverse Reinforcement Learning for Mean-Field Games with Average Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16759)
*Şevket Kaan Alkır,Naci Saldı,Berkay Anahtarcı,Can Deha Karıksız*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间无限视界平均奖励准则下的平均场博弈逆强化学习问题，通过最大因果熵原理和职业测度框架，为有限维线性奖励和无限维RKHS奖励提供了收敛性保证的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均场博弈中，专家演示来自于未知奖励下的平稳平均场均衡，需要通过逆强化学习恢复能够解释观测行为的策略。现有方法主要针对折扣奖励准则，而平均奖励准则下缺乏折扣因子收缩性，这为算法设计和收敛性分析带来挑战。

Method: 采用统一职业测度框架，强制与专家平均场项和长期特征期望保持一致性。对于有限维线性奖励，给出凸对偶重构和显式对数划分目标，利用平滑性和曲率性质设计恒定步长梯度下降算法。对于无限维RKHS奖励，开发拉格朗日松弛方法，引入基于次要化的随机核实现软贝尔曼算子的严格收缩性，建立对数似然分数的Fréchet可微性和Lipschitz平滑性。

Result: 为两类奖励建立了理论保证的算法框架。有限维情形证明了恒定步长梯度下降的收敛性；无限维情形通过构造收缩算子解决了折扣因子缺失问题，建立了梯度上升算法的收敛保证。恶意软件传播和消费者选择模型的数值实验表明，恢复的策略与专家行为高度匹配。

Conclusion: 成功将逆强化学习扩展到平均奖励准则下的平均场博弈，通过职业测度框架和构造性方法克服了理论障碍，为处理无限视界平均奖励问题提供了新的理论工具和计算方法。

Abstract: We study inverse reinforcement learning for discrete-time, infinite-horizon mean-field games (MFGs) under an average-reward criterion. Expert demonstrations are assumed to arise from a stationary mean-field equilibrium under an unknown reward, and the goal is to recover a policy explaining the observed behaviour via the maximum causal entropy principle. We formulate the inverse problem by enforcing consistency with the expert mean-field term and long-run feature expectations, treating two reward classes within a unified occupation-measure framework. For finite-dimensional linear rewards, we give a convex dual reformulation with an explicit log-partition objective, and prove smoothness and curvature properties justifying constant-step-size gradient descent. For infinite-dimensional RKHS rewards, we develop a Lagrangian relaxation whose inner-maximising policy is characterised by a soft Bellman equation. The main obstacle is the absence of a discount-factor contraction. We resolve this by introducing a minorisation-based sub-stochastic kernel that yields a strict contraction of the soft Bellman operator. We establish Fréchet differentiability and Lipschitz smoothness of the log-likelihood score, leading to a gradient ascent algorithm with convergence guarantees. Two numerical examples, a malware-spread MFG and an RKHS-based consumer-choice model, show that the recovered policies closely match expert behaviour.

</details>


### [337] [A Validated LBM Dataset and Pipeline for Surrogate Modeling of Turbulent 3D Obstructed Channel Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16765)
*Lukas Schröder,Shubham Kavane,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的管道，用于生成雷诺数Re=1,000-10,000范围内三维通道流动的训练数据，并使用格子玻尔兹曼求解器进行了严格验证，为神经代理模型的标准化比较提供了基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估三维湍流中的神经算子需要经过验证的数据集和物理基准，目前缺乏针对复杂几何形状湍流的标准训练数据和评估体系。

Method: 构建可复现的数据生成管道，采用具有累积量碰撞算子的格子玻尔兹曼求解器，在1024x512x512分辨率下进行网格收敛性研究，并通过斯特劳哈尔数、阻力系数和湍流脉动等实验测量值进行严格验证。

Result: 成功建立了经过实验验证的三维通道流动数据生成管道，可以生成绕任意几何形状的湍流训练数据，为神经算子的标准化比较提供了可靠基础。

Conclusion: 该验证管道能够支持傅里叶神经算子和U-Net变体在预测、超分辨率和误差修正任务上的系统评估，未来将比较数值求解器与神经代理的计算效率，为湍流中神经算子的实际应用提供标准化基准。

Abstract: Evaluating neural operators for 3D turbulent flow requires validated datasets with physical benchmarks. We present a reproducible pipeline generating training data for 3D channel flows around generated geometries at Re=1,000-10,000. Our lattice Boltzmann solver with cumulant collision operators is rigorously verified against experimental measurements (Strouhal number, drag coefficients, turbulent fluctuations) with comprehensive grid convergence studies at resolution 1024x512x512. Building upon an established framework, this validated pipeline enables standardized surrogate model comparison. We outline planned systematic evaluation of Fourier Neural Operator and U-Net variants on forecasting, super-resolution, and error correction tasks, using physics-informed metrics to assess turbulent energy cascade representation. Future work will compare computational efficiency between numerical solvers and neural surrogates, exploring practical application. We seek community feedback on our validation approach, planned benchmark methodology, and evaluation priorities for neural operators in turbulent flows.

</details>


### [338] [Taming Curvature: Architecture Warm-Up for Stable Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16768)
*Sameera Ramasinghe,Ajanthan Thalaiyasingam,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Yan Zuo,Karol Pajak,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速的在线曲率估计方法，并基于观察到的训练不稳定性与曲率激增的相关性，设计了"架构预热"策略，通过逐步增加网络深度来稳定大规模Transformer训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer训练经常出现瞬态损失尖峰和发散问题，导致计算资源浪费。尽管稳定性边缘(EoS)理论提供了通过曲率理解和控制优化稳定性的工具，但现有的曲率控制方法在大规模训练中因曲率估计的复杂性而不受欢迎。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于热启动幂迭代和Hessian-向量乘积的快速在线估计器，用于估计最大（预条件）Hessian特征值（即曲率）；2. 基于观察到的训练不稳定性与预条件曲率激增重合、曲率随深度增长的发现，提出架构预热策略：逐步增长网络深度以控制预条件Hessian并稳定训练。

Result: 所提出的曲率估计方法在十亿参数规模下实现了可行的逐迭代曲率跟踪，且精度更高；实验验证了架构预热方法能够有效减少训练不稳定性，相比现有最先进的稳定化技术效果更好，且不降低收敛速度。

Conclusion: 本文成功将曲率跟踪应用于大规模Transformer训练，揭示了曲率与训练不稳定性的关系，并提出了实用的架构预热方法来稳定训练过程，为大规模模型训练提供了新的工具和方法。

Abstract: Training billion-parameter Transformers is often brittle, with transient loss spikes and divergence that waste compute. Even though the recently developed Edge of Stability (EoS) theory provides a powerful tool to understand and control the stability of optimization methods via the (preconditioned) curvature, these curvature-controlling methods are not popular in large-scale Transformer training due to the complexity of curvature estimation. To this end, we first introduce a fast online estimator of the largest (preconditioned) Hessian eigenvalue (i.e., curvature) based on a warm-started variant for power iteration with Hessian-vector products. We show theoretically, and verify empirically, that the proposed method makes per-iteration curvature tracking feasible at billion parameter scale while being more accurate. Using this tool, we find that training instabilities coincide with surges in preconditioned curvature and that curvature grows with depth. Motivated by these observations, we propose architecture warm-up: progressively growing network depth to carefully control the preconditioned Hessian and stabilize training. Experiments on large Transformers validate that our approach enables efficient curvature tracking and reduces instabilities compared to existing state-of-the-art stabilization techniques without slowing down convergence.

</details>


### [339] [GD$^2$PO: Mitigating Multi-Reward Conflicts via Group-Dynamic reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16771)
*Haotian Liu,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Siyuan Huang,Xinpeng Liu,Pengyu Cheng,Jiajun Song,Ruijin Ding,Junfeng Li,Zhechao Yu,Mengyu Zhou,Hongteng Xu,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GD²PO算法，通过冲突感知过滤机制和查询级重加权解决多奖励强化学习中的奖励冲突问题，显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练强化学习方法在处理多维奖励时存在多奖励冲突问题：单次rollout在某些奖励维度上产生正向优势，在其他维度上产生负向优势，导致反向信号相互抵消，降低训练效率。

Method: 提出Group-Dynamic reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GD²PO)：1）设计冲突感知过滤机制，屏蔽存在严重奖励分歧的rollouts，防止冲突信号相互抵消；2）引入查询级重加权机制，根据整体奖励一致性动态调整每个查询的更新强度。

Result: 在工具调用和人类偏好对齐等多种多奖励场景的实验中，GD²PO持续且显著地优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: GD²PO通过有效解决多奖励冲突问题，成功保留了有效RL优势的幅度，显著加速了学习效率，为多维奖励强化学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As LLMs advance, post-training reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly relies on multi-dimensional rewards to cultivate comprehensive capabilities. This shift demands new algorithms capable of optimizing diverse and potentially competing objectives simultaneously. To address this, existing methods such as Group reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GDPO) decompose the overall score into independent reward groups, then compute the RL loss separately within each group. However, this strategy still encounters multi-reward conflicts: a single rollout can yield positive advantages on certain reward dimensions but negative ones on others, causing opposing signals to cancel each other out during aggregation, further hindering RL training efficiency. Inspired by Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO), which improves RL training efficiency by filtering out ineffective rollouts with near-zero advantages, we propose Group-Dynamic reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GD$^2$PO). Specifically, GD$^2$PO employs a conflict-aware filtering mechanism to mask out rollouts suffering from severe reward-wise disagreement. By preventing conflicting signals from canceling each other out, this masking strategy preserves and enhances the magnitude of effective RL advantages, thereby significantly accelerating learning efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce query-level reweighting to dynamically adjust the update intensity of each query based on its overall reward consensus. Experiments on various multi-reward scenarios, including tool calling and human preference alignment, demonstrate that GD$^2$PO consistently and significantly outperforms existing baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/GD2PO.

</details>


### [340] [We Need Explanation Cards to Connect Explanation Algorithms to the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16786)
*Eric Günther,Balázs Szabados,Kristof Meding,Gunnar König,Sebastian Bordt,Ulrike von Luxburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解释卡片（Explanation Cards）来增强算法解释，通过补充鲁棒性、有效性信息及明确的解释说明，弥合解释呈现与实际提供内容之间的差距，使算法解释更适用于实际场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法解释存在两大问题：一是解释的含义往往不符合直觉，需要专业知识才能正确解读；二是常用的解释算法无法有效揭示复杂决策函数的行为。这导致解释表面传达的信息与实际提供的信息之间存在差距，影响其实用性。

Method: 提出解释卡片（Explanation Cards），在标准解释基础上补充三项内容：（1）鲁棒性和有效性等互补信息；（2）清晰的解释说明指南；（3）明确解释的适用范围和局限性。以反事实解释和SHAP为例，展示如何构建解释卡片。

Result: 解释卡片能够使原本信息量不足的解释变得实用，同时帮助识别解释无效的情况；将解释责任从用户转移到提供者，提供者需明确说明解释能得出什么结论、不能得出什么结论；为欧盟AI法案的可解释性条款提供了实际操作方案。

Conclusion: 解释卡片是使算法解释适应实际应用场景的重要一步，能够有效提升解释算法的实用性，为AI系统的透明度和问责制提供了一种可行的实现路径。

Abstract: Algorithmic explanations are intended to help stakeholders understand opaque algorithmic decisions, but in practice, they often fall short. First, the meaning of algorithmic explanations is often not what one might intuitively expect, so expert knowledge is required to interpret them correctly. Second, recent work has shown that popular explanation algorithms are uninformative about the behavior of complex decision functions. Together, these issues create a gap between what explanations appear to convey and what they actually provide. In this work, we propose Explanation Cards for Explanation Algorithms, which augment standard explanations with complementary information about robustness and validity, as well as clear instructions for interpretation. The complementary information can render otherwise uninformative explanations practically useful, while also helping to detect cases where they are not. Importantly, the interpretation instructions in explanation cards shift responsibility from users to providers: Rather than expecting users to recognize what can and cannot be concluded from an explanation, providers must make this explicit upfront. Using counterfactual explanations and SHAP as examples, we demonstrate how providers can construct explanation cards and that these cards provide users with the guidance needed for sound interpretation. We further argue that explanation cards offer a practical means of operationalising the explainability provisions of the EU AI Act. Overall, explanation cards are a significant step toward making explanation algorithms fit for real-world use cases.

</details>


### [341] [Decision-Weighted Flow Matching for Contextual Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16790)
*Jize Xie,Haomiao Wu,Qiang Chen,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策加权流匹配(DW-FM)框架，通过重新加权速度回归目标来校正条件生成模型与下游决策之间的目标失配，在CVaR随机优化任务上显著降低决策遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型在随机优化场景生成中存在目标失配问题：标准训练目标关注整体分布拟合，但下游决策对决策敏感区域的误差更为敏感，而对统计常见区域的误差相对不敏感，这种不一致会导致次优决策。

Method: 提出决策加权流匹配(DW-FM)框架，保留标准流匹配的简洁性，同时利用决策敏感的端点信息对速度回归目标进行重新加权。通过损失诱导决策差异和伴随传输论证，理论上建立下游遗憾与路径速度失配的联系，推导出遗憾对齐的理想替代目标和实用的端点加权目标。

Result: 在三个CVaR情境随机优化基准上验证（合成投资组合、半真实金融、交通CVaR任务），DW-FM相比标准基线在下游遗憾指标上取得显著改进，证明了遗憾对齐训练的有效性。

Conclusion: DW-FM成功解决了生成模型训练与下游决策目标失配的核心问题，为决策导向的场景生成提供了理论基础和实践框架，在保持方法简洁性的同时提供了遗憾保证，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Conditional generative models are increasingly used as scenario generators for stochastic optimization, but standard training objectives emphasize uniform distributional fit rather than the downstream decisions induced by generated scenarios. This creates an objective mismatch: errors in statistically common regions may have little effect on decision regret, whereas errors in decision-sensitive regions can substantially change the optimal action. We propose Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM), a regret-aligned training framework that preserves the simplicity of standard flow matching while reweighting its velocity-regression objective using decision-sensitive endpoint information. Theoretically, we connect downstream regret to pathwise velocity mismatch through a loss-induced decision discrepancy and an adjoint transport argument, yielding an ideal regret-aligned surrogate and practical endpoint-weighted objectives with regret guarantees. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of DW-FM on three CVaR-based contextual stochastic optimization benchmarks spanning synthetic portfolio, semi-real financial, and traffic-CVaR tasks, where DW-FM improves downstream regret over standard baselines.

</details>


### [342] [Deep Q-Learning on Hölder Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16846)
*Qian Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间随机控制中Q学习的算子理论基础。证明了Bellman更新将输入映射到各向异性正则类，在状态变量上平滑而在动作变量上保持Lipschitz依赖。基于此提出了张量积DeepONet架构，并给出了显式的近似和资源界限以及时间步趋近于0时的刚度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于价值的强化学习中，每个Q学习或DQN更新都基于Bellman最优性目标。作者旨在连续扩散设置中分离并研究这一目标的正则性和近似复杂性，以填补连续时间随机控制中Q学习理论的空白。

Method: 在一致椭圆性和Hölder正则系数的假设下，分析Bellman更新算子的映射性质。证明其将输入映射到各向异性正则类。基于混合正则性特征，设计张量积DeepONet架构，推导显式的近似误差界限和资源界限。

Result: 证明Bellman更新使状态变量平滑，但对动作变量仅保持Lipschitz依赖性，形成紧致的Bellman迭代族。得到了显式的近似和资源界限，并揭示了当时间步趋近于0时的刚度-复杂度权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本研究在Bellman目标正则性和连续随机控制近似层面为Q学习理论做出了直接贡献，建立了连续时间设置下的理论基础。但作者明确指出，这不构成对具有探索、经验回放和随机梯度更新的实际采样Q学习的完整收敛定理。

Abstract: We study the operator-theoretic core of Q-learning in continuous-time stochastic control with continuous states and actions. In value-based reinforcement learning, each Q-learning or DQN update is built from a Bellman optimality target; our analysis isolates this target in a diffusion setting and studies its regularity and approximation complexity. Under uniform ellipticity and Hölder-regular coefficients, we show that a Bellman update maps bounded inputs into an anisotropic regularity class, smoothing the state variable while leaving only Lipschitz dependence on the action variable. This yields a compact family of Bellman iterates and motivates a tensor-product DeepONet architecture adapted to the mixed regularity of the problem. We then derive explicit approximation and resource bounds, together with a stiffness--complexity trade-off as the time step $δ\to 0$. The resulting theory makes a direct contribution to Q-learning theory at the level of Bellman target regularity and approximation in continuous stochastic control. At the same time, we do not claim a full convergence theorem for practical sampled Q-learning with exploration, replay, and stochastic gradient updates.

</details>


### [343] [HawkesNest: A Multi-Axis Synthetic Benchmark for Spatiotemporal Pattern Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16863)
*Yahya Aalaila,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HawkesNest，一个基于多变量Hawkes过程的生成器对齐基准测试框架，用于可控的时空点过程模型评估。通过定义四个复杂性维度（时空纠缠、背景异质性、跨类型交互、域拓扑），实现了对STPP模型的诊断性压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时空点过程（STPP）模型评估依赖于不透明的现实世界数据集，其中潜在的生成结构未知，模型失败难以归因。缺乏可控的基准测试框架来系统性地评估模型在不同结构复杂度下的表现。

Method: 基于多变量Hawkes过程构建HawkesNest基准生成器。定义四个复杂性轴：时空纠缠、背景异质性、跨类型交互、域拓扑。每个轴关联一个从潜在数据生成机制计算的确定性指标。通过在固定全局速率、稳定性和仿真预算下改变这些轴，实现对STPP模型在已知结构困难度下的诊断性压力测试。验证指标的单调性和近正交性。

Result: 验证了四个复杂性指标在受控扫描下单调且近似正交。发现Hawkes系列基线模型在联合异质性-纠缠复杂度下性能退化，即使它们在结构上与Hawkes数据生成骨干对齐。进一步发现神经模型AutoSTPP对时空纠缠的孤立增加仍然脆弱。

Conclusion: HawkesNest为STPP模型提供了一个可解释、可控的评估框架，能够揭示模型在特定结构复杂度下的脆弱性。该基准有助于诊断模型失败原因、指导模型改进，并为STPP模型的系统评估建立了新标准。

Abstract: Evaluation of spatiotemporal point process (STPP) models relies heavily on opaque real-world datasets, where latent generative structure is unknown and model failures are difficult to attribute. We introduce HawkesNest, a generator-aligned benchmark for controlled spatiotemporal pattern complexity built on a multivariate Hawkes backbone. HawkesNest defines four complexity axes: space--time entanglement, background heterogeneity, cross-type interaction, and domain topology. Each axis is associated with a deterministic index computed from the latent data-generating mechanism. By varying these axes while holding global rate, stability, and simulation budget fixed, HawkesNest enables diagnostic stress tests of STPP models under known structural difficulty. We verify that the indices are monotone and nearly orthogonal under controlled sweeps. We illustrate its use by showing that Hawkes-family baselines degrade under joint heterogeneity--entanglement complexity, even though they are structurally aligned with the Hawkes data-generating backbone. We further show that HawkesNest exposes neural-model sensitivity: AutoSTPP remains vulnerable under isolated increases in space--time entanglement. Code. Available at https://github.com/YahyaAalaila/HawkesNest

</details>


### [344] [Integrated Marketing Attribution: A Bayesian Framework for Privacy-Safe Granular Measurement Anchored in MMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16878)
*Meghana R. Bhat,Ankit Umare,Utsav Aggarwal,Richard Vecsler,Arunkumar Mani,Karthik Nair,Chandhu Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出集成营销归因框架（IMA），结合营销组合模型（MMM）与贝叶斯归因模型，在保护隐私的前提下实现活动层级的精准归因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种主流营销测量方法存在缺陷：MMM虽保护隐私且适合渠道级规划，但粒度太粗无法优化具体营销活动；MTA虽能提供细粒度归因，但在日益严格的隐私限制下可靠性下降。行业亟需既能保护隐私又能提供活动层级洞察的解决方案。

Method: 提出集成营销归因（IMA）统一框架，将MMM与特定渠道的贝叶斯归因模型相结合。该框架利用MMM提供的先验信息，从聚合数据中推导活动层级的效果，既保持了与MMM的一致性，又实现了细粒度的隐私安全归因。

Result: IMA框架成功弥合了MMM与MTA之间的鸿沟，在不依赖用户级追踪的情况下，从聚合数据中提取出活动层级的归因洞察，实现了隐私保护与细粒度分析的平衡。

Conclusion: IMA为零售营销测量提供了一种新的范式，在满足隐私合规要求的同时，实现了营销活动层级的精准优化，为营销人员提供了更实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Retail marketing measurement increasingly requires granular campaign-level insights without relying on user-level tracking.
