However, the two dominant approaches, Marketing Mix Modeling (MMM) and Multi-Touch Attribution (MTA), often produce fragmented insights. MMM is privacy-safe and robust for channel-level planning but is too coarse for campaign optimization, while MTA provides granular attribution but has become less reliable under increasing privacy restrictions. We propose Integrated Marketing Attribution (IMA), a unified framework that combines MMM with channel specific Bayesian attribution models to derive campaign-level effects from aggregated data. By leveraging MMM-informed priors, IMA delivers granular, privacy-safe attribution while preserving consistency with MMM.

</details>


### [345] [Upper Bounds on the Generalization Error of Deep Learning Models via Local Robustness and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16883)
*Abdul-Rauf Nuhu,Parham M. Kebria,Vahid Hemmati,Mahmoud N. Mahmoud,Edward Tunstel,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的泛化边界方法，通过在输入子区域内根据稳定/不稳定样本数量缩放鲁棒性项，解决了现有鲁棒性泛化边界过于宽松的问题，在ImageNet上获得了最紧的非空泛化边界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于鲁棒性的泛化边界在实际应用中往往过于宽松，大大超过真实错误率，限制了其实用性。虽然这一问题常归因于不确定项，但很大程度上源于鲁棒性项本身——特别是对于0-1损失，现有方法将鲁棒性项视为全局度量，忽略了其在输入空间不同子区域间的变化。

Method: 提出一种新的泛化边界，通过根据每个子区域内稳定和不稳定样本的数量对鲁棒性项进行缩放来克服现有局限。该方法同时融合了数据依赖和模型依赖因素，在保持实际相关性的同时提供更紧的真实错误上界。

Result: 在ImageNet数据集上的实验表明，该方法在一系列鲁棒深度神经网络中始终保持非空性，并且在现有方法中实现了最紧的估计，与经验性能密切吻合。

Conclusion: 通过考虑输入空间子区域内鲁棒性项的局部变化，可以获得更紧、更实用的泛化边界，为安全关键应用中深度学习模型的泛化性能评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Generalization is a critical property of data-driven models, particularly deep learning models deployed in safety-critical applications. Robustness-based generalization bounds have gained attention as a principled way to link robustness properties to generalization performance, often in a data-dependent manner. However, most existing bounds suffer from vacuousness in practical settings, yielding loose upper bounds that greatly exceed the actual error rates and limiting their usefulness for real-world evaluation. While this issue is often attributed to the uncertainty term, a substantial part of the problem originates from the robustness term itself, particularly for the 0-1 loss. Existing approaches typically treat the robustness term as a global measure, ignoring its variation across different sub-regions of the input space. In this work, we propose a generalization bound that addresses this limitation by scaling the robustness term according to the number of stable and unstable samples within each sub-region. Our bounds incorporate both data- and model-dependent factors while maintaining practical relevance (yielding tighter upper bounds on true error). Experiments on models trained on the ImageNet dataset show that our bounds remain consistently non-vacuous and achieve the tightest estimates among existing methods, closely aligning with empirical performance across a range of robust deep neural networks.

</details>


### [346] [Beyond Weights and Gradients: A Taxonomy of Federated Learning Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16891)
*Alvaro Javier Vargas Guerrero,Xinguang Wang,Quang Manh Doan,Guy Nagels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了联邦消息的数学定义和分类法，将联邦学习中的信息交换分为三类，并通过分析202篇近期论文揭示了自2021年以来联邦学习向多样化消息传递范式转变的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着联邦学习快速发展，传统的模型权重和梯度交换已无法涵盖现代有效载荷（如合成数据和联邦分析）的全部范围，现有定义存在不足，需要建立能够同时考虑效用和隐私的形式化框架。

Method: 提出了联邦消息的形式化数学定义，建立了包含三种类别的分类法：模型结构、统计摘要和数据条件表示，并从计算需求、通信成本和隐私风险三个维度对这些类别进行评估。

Result: 通过对202篇近期发表文献的系统综述，发现自2021年以来联邦学习领域出现了显著转变，从标准的深度学习更新转向更专业的信息共享方式，呈现出多样化消息传递范式的发展趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来研究提供了结构化路径，有助于根据不同的硬件和安全要求优化联邦系统，推动联邦学习在计算效率、通信开销和隐私保护之间的平衡发展。

Abstract: Federated Learning is rapidly evolving beyond the exchange of traditional model weights and gradients, yet existing definitions fail to capture the full scope of modern payloads like synthetic data and federated analytics. This paper addresses the gap by proposing a formal mathematical definition of a federated message that accounts for both utility and privacy. We introduce a taxonomy that organizes these exchanges into three categories: model structures, statistical summaries, and data-conditioned representations. By evaluating these groups based on computational demands, communication costs, and privacy risks, we provide a clearer understanding of the trade-offs involved in decentralized training. Our review of 202 recent publications highlights a significant shift since 2021 toward diverse messaging paradigms, signaling a move away from standard deep learning updates toward more specialized information sharing. This framework provides a structured path for future research to optimize federated systems for varying hardware and security requirements.

</details>


### [347] [Fantastic Pretraining Optimizers and Where to Find Them II: Hyperball Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16899)
*Kaiyue Wen,Xingyu Dang,Kaifeng Lyu,Tengyu Ma,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hyperball是一种优化器包装器，通过固定权重矩阵和优化器更新的Frobenius范数，解决了矩阵优化器（如Muon）在大规模模型上性能增益缩小的问题，在1.2B参数模型上实现了20-30%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于矩阵的优化器（如Muon）可以显著加速语言模型预训练，但在使用标准常数解耦权重衰减时，随着模型规模和数据规模的增长，其相对于AdamW的优势会逐渐缩小。先前理论表明，使用权重衰减训练会导致权重范数达到仅依赖于训练超参数的平衡状态，而权重衰减决定了角度学习率（即权重矩阵方向变化的速度）。

Method: 提出Hyperball，一种简单的优化器包装器。给定基础优化器（如Adam或Muon），Hyperball将权重矩阵的Frobenius范数及其对应的优化器更新设置为固定常数。这种方法将权重衰减的作用从控制权重范数转变为控制角度学习率。

Result: 在高达1.2B参数的Qwen3风格模型上，Muon Hyperball相比权重衰减基线实现了20-30%的token等效加速。Hyperball还改善了不同宽度和深度之间的学习率迁移性。

Conclusion: Hyperball通过固定权重矩阵和优化器更新的Frobenius范数，成功解决了矩阵优化器在大规模模型上性能增益缩小的问题，同时提升了学习率的迁移性，为大规模语言模型预训练提供了一种有效的优化方法。

Abstract: Matrix based optimizers such as Muon can substantially speed up language model pretraining, but their gains over AdamW are observed to shrink as model size and data scale grow when using standard constant decoupled weight decay. We propose Hyperball, a simple optimizer wrapper that addresses this issue. Given a base optimizer such as Adam or Muon, Hyperball sets the Frobenius norms of weight matrices and their corresponding optimizer updates to fixed constants. On Qwen3 style models up to 1.2B parameters, Muon Hyperball achieves 20--30% token equivalent speedup over weight decay baselines. Hyperball also improves learning rate transfer across widths and depths compared to decoupled weight decay. This method is motivated by prior theory showing that training with weight decay leads to an equilibrium weight norm that only depends on the training hyperparameters. Through this mechanism, the weight decay then decides the angular learning rate, i.e. how fast the direction of the weight matrix changes.

</details>


### [348] [Factorized Neural Operators Decompose Dynamic and Persistent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16900)
*Hao Tang,Yuechen Duan,Jiongyu Zhu,Zimeng Feng,Hao Li,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分解神经算子(FaNO)，通过分解频谱表示为等变动态响应和不变持久响应，有效捕捉物理系统中的多尺度异构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统通常表现出异构机制，快速演化的动力学与持久结构共存。现有神经算子通常依赖单一主导归纳偏置，将不同的物理响应耦合到共享表示中，难以有效捕捉这种多尺度物理行为。

Method: 引入跨域统一格林函数框架，提出分解神经算子(FaNO)，将频谱表示分解为等变动态响应分支和不变持久响应分支。等变分支捕捉快速变化的瞬态动力学，不变分支提取相干的持久结构。

Result: FaNO的两个算子分支自发地专门化为不同的物理角色，在不同尺度和域间保持一致。该方法提高了预测精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化能力，在长期自回归推演、跨分辨率外推和物理区域转变下保持一致预测。

Conclusion: 可扩展的物理建模应超越单一归纳偏置公式，转向更好地反映物理系统异构组织的分解算子表示，这有助于加速机器学习在科学计算和发现中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Physical systems often exhibit heterogeneous mechanisms, where rapidly evolving dynamics coexist with persistent structures. Capturing such multiscale physical behavior remains challenging for existing neural operators, which typically rely on single dominant inductive bias and therefore couple distinct physical responses into a shared representation. We introduce the Unified Green's Function Framework across domains and propose the Factorized Neural Operators (FaNO), which decompose spectral representations into equivariant dynamic responses and invariant persistent responses, leading to better interpretability and generalization. Mechanistically, we show that the two operator branches spontaneously specialize into distinct physical roles that remain consistent across scales and domains: the equivariant branch captures rapidly varying transient dynamics, whereas the invariant branch extracts coherent persistent structures. This factorized mechanism of FaNO improves prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency and cross-scale generalization across physical systems and domains. In particular, it maintains consistent predictions under long-horizon autoregressive rollout, cross-resolution extrapolation and physical-regime shifts. These findings suggest that scalable physical modeling may benefit from moving beyond single-inductive-bias formulations toward factorized operator representations that better reflect the heterogeneous organization of physical systems, accelerating the reliable deployment of machine learning for scientific computing and discovery.

</details>


### [349] [Demystifying Variance in Circuit Discovery of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16920)
*Frank Zhengqing Wu,Francesco Tonin,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了电路发现方法中的三种方差问题，提出了改进方法CEAP，揭示了重述方差暗示LLM难以操控的深层问题，并澄清了样本方差的良性本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的电路发现方法EAP-IG虽然在忠实度指标上表现良好，但存在严重的变异性问题：重采样方差（使用同分布新数据时电路变化）、重述方差（提示重述时电路偏移）和样本方差（单个样本的忠实度波动大）。这些方差问题影响了电路发现方法的可靠性和实用性，需要深入研究其根源。

Method: 1. 提出CEAP方法：在EAP-IG基础上改进，提供理论保证，用于减少重采样方差。
2. 实验分析重述方差：研究不同模板的提示如何激活不同电路。
3. 验证稀疏性作用：测试稀疏性是否能够解决重述方差问题。
4. 理论分析样本方差：研究选择性贡献缩放机制对忠实度评分的影响。

Result: 1. CEAP方法显著降低了重采样方差。
2. 重述方差源于不同模板的提示倾向于激活模型中不同的电路，这使得找到一个能解释和控制任务行为的综合电路极具挑战性。
3. 稀疏性方法无法解决重述方差问题。
4. 样本方差大多是良性的：极差的忠实度分数往往源于忠实度的定义方式，而非电路本身的缺陷；选择性贡献缩放机制影响了忠实度的大小。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM可能本质上难以操控，因为表达同一任务的不同提示模板会激活不同的电路，难以找到统一解释的综合电路。这为机械可解释性研究提供了重要见解，同时澄清了方差问题的性质，为未来方法改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Circuit discovery is a key technique in mechanistic interpretability to pinpoint the model components that are crucial for performing a given task. Although the current state-of-the-art method (EAP-IG) performs well on the metric of (un)faithfulness, it suffers from substantial variability. This includes resampling variance, where the circuit changes when we probe with a new batch of data from the same distribution; rephrasing variance, where the discovered circuit shifts when the prompts are rephrased; and sample-wise variance, where a circuit with low population unfaithfulness exhibits large fluctuations in unfaithfulness across individual samples.
  This paper studies the roots of these variances. We demonstrate that CEAP, our new circuit discovery method that improves upon EAP-IG with a theoretical guarantee, can substantially lessen resampling variance. We further show that rephrasing variance arises because prompts with different templates tend to activate different circuits in the model. This leads us to argue that it may be challenging to find a comprehensive circuit that explains and controls the model's behavior on a task, which can be expressed in countless templates, suggesting that LLMs may be inherently hard to steer. We show that sparsity, which has been claimed to form more compact and interpretable task circuits, fails to solve this problem. Regarding sample-wise variance, we argue that it is largely benign: extremely poor unfaithfulness scores often stem from how unfaithfulness is defined, rather than from defects in the measured circuits. We show that the magnitude of unfaithfulness is affected by selective contribution scaling, a neural mechanism that accounts for the extremely poor scores sometimes observed.

</details>


### [350] [A Unified Causal-Origin Taxonomy of Distributional Shifts in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16933)
*Ardianto Wibowo,Paulo E Santos,Amer Baghdadi,Matthew Stephenson,Karl Sammut,Jean-Philippe Diguet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中分布偏移的统一因果起源分类法，通过POMDP分解交互过程，区分内部（智能体驱动）和外部（环境驱动）偏移，并引入了评估框架来衡量偏移影响和适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习系统在操作条件与先前遇到的条件不同时性能会下降，这反映了底层数据生成过程中的分布偏移。这种偏移可能发生在训练和评估之间（如ID和OOD泛化），或在环境动态随时间演变的非平稳设置中。然而，这些观点之间的形式关系尚不清楚，现有工作主要关注缓解策略而非智能体-环境交互中偏移的因果起源。

Method: 将监督学习中的经典数据集偏移原理转移到强化学习中，通过生成交互过程重新表述分布偏移。使用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）将交互分解为结构组件，包括状态分布、观测过程、策略、奖励和转移动力学，以及偏移时间边界。提出的分类法区分了内部（智能体驱动）和外部（环境驱动）的分布偏移，偏移时间边界视角进一步刻画了显式、隐式和混合偏移。

Result: 建立了一个统一的因果起源分类法，成功将ID/OOD泛化和非平稳性统一为底层过程中的结构变化。该公式化方法支持对分布偏移下鲁棒性的系统分析，并提供了衡量偏移影响和适应的评估框架。

Conclusion: 通过将分布偏移建立在强化学习因果起源结构的基础上，本工作支持对分布偏移下鲁棒性的系统分析，为理解和处理强化学习中的分布偏移问题提供了统一的理论框架和实用的评估工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) systems often degrade when operating conditions differ from those previously encountered, reflecting distributional shifts in the underlying data-generating process. Such shifts may occur between training and evaluation, as in In-Distribution (ID) and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization, or within non-stationary settings where environment dynamics evolve over time. However, the formal relationship between these views remains unclear, and existing work mainly focuses on mitigation rather than the causal origin of shift within the agent-environment interaction. This work develops a unified causal-origin taxonomy that characterizes sources of distributional shift in RL and relates ID/OOD generalization to non-stationary settings. We transfer the classical dataset-shift principle from supervised learning to RL by reformulating distributional shift in terms of the generative interaction process. Using a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP), we decompose the interaction into structural components, including the state distribution, observation process, policy, reward, and transition dynamics, together with the shifted-time boundary. The proposed taxonomy distinguishes internal, agent-driven, and external, environment-driven, distributional shifts. The shifted-time boundary perspective further characterizes explicit, implicit, and hybrid shifts. This formulation unifies ID/OOD generalization and non-stationarity as structured changes in the underlying process. We also introduce an evaluation framework for measuring shift impact and adaptation through performance degradation and recovery metrics. By grounding distributional shift in the causal-origin structure of RL, this work supports systematic analysis of robustness under distributional shift.

</details>


### [351] [Scalable Circuit Learning for Interpreting Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16939)
*Naiyu Yin,Dennis Wei,Tian Gao,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CircuitLasso，一种基于稀疏线性回归的高效电路学习方法，在保持结构准确性的同时大幅降低计算成本，成功揭示SAE特征间的关系并在领域泛化任务中验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性研究中，原始神经元具有多义性导致学到的电路难以解释；虽然稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征缓解了该问题，但其高维度使得现有的基于干预的电路学习方法在计算上不可行。

Method: 提出CircuitLasso方法，基于稀疏线性回归构建可扩展的电路学习框架，避免了传统干预方法的高计算成本。

Result: 在基准数据上，CircuitLasso恢复的电路结构准确性与最先进的基于干预的方法相匹配，但计算成本仅为其一小部分；能够高效揭示SAE特征间的关系，展示人类可解释的语义特征如何在模型中传播并影响预测。

Conclusion: 通过领域泛化任务验证了学习到的电路的实用性，利用其洞察以显著降低的成本实现了相当的性能，证明了该方法在机制可解释性研究中的有效性。

Abstract: A prominent research direction in mechanistic interpretability is learning sparse circuits over LLM components to reveal how they jointly produce model behavior. However, raw neurons are polysemantic, making learned circuits hard to interpret. Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features alleviate this, but their high dimensionality makes existing intervention-based circuit learning methods computationally prohibitive. We propose CircuitLasso, a scalable circuit-learning approach based on sparse linear regression. CircuitLasso recovers circuits whose structural accuracy matches that of state-of-the-art intervention-based methods on the benchmark data, at a fraction of the computational cost. For interpretability, CircuitLasso efficiently uncovers relationships among SAE features, showing how human-interpretable semantic features propagate through the model and influence its predictions. Finally, we validate the utility of our learned circuits by leveraging their insights to achieve comparable performance at substantially lower cost on a domain-generalization task.

</details>


### [352] [Phantoms and Disclosures: a Causal Framework for Auditing Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16952)
*Kareem Amin,Rudrajit Das,Alessandro Epasto,Adel Javanmard,Dennis Kraft,Mónica Ribero,Sergei Vassilvitskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可定制的经验性审计框架，用于检测和解释生成式AI模型在合成数据生成过程中的隐私泄露问题，无需模型访问权限即可区分真实披露和虚假披露，提供了比现有方法更严格的隐私泄露下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI和大语言模型的快速应用，合成数据被作为敏感真实世界数据集的隐私保护替代方案而受到关注。然而，生成高实用性的合成数据往往存在记忆和复述训练语料库中私有信息的风险，需要有效的审计方法来检测和解释此类数据泄露。

Method: 该框架将输入数据划分为训练集和保留集，通过严格的统计假设检验来确定观察到的披露是否与严格的隐私基线（如零学习或特定的差分隐私边界）一致。该机制能够区分"真实披露"（系统直接复现用户信息）和"虚假披露"（系统偶然生成用户数据），且无需模型访问、无需金丝雀插入、无需参考模型训练，仅需要合成输出和保留控制集。

Result: 该框架有效地实现了成员推理攻击，提供了比现有基于数据的审计方法更严格的隐私泄露经验下界。该方法具有模型无关性，适用于任何合成数据生成机制，且比影子模型或基于金丝雀的替代方案所需的计算资源少几个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的审计框架为合成数据生成中的隐私泄露检测提供了一种高效、模型无关的解决方案，能够在不依赖模型内部访问的情况下提供有意义的隐私保证验证，为隐私保护的合成数据生成实践提供了重要的审计工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative AI and Large Language Models (LLMs) has spurred interest in synthetic data as a privacy-preserving alternative to sensitive real-world datasets. However, generating high-utility synthetic data often carries the risk of memorizing and regurgitating private information from the training corpus. In this work, we present a customizable empirical auditing framework designed to detect and explain such data disclosures. Our framework introduces a mechanism to distinguish between "true disclosures"-where the system directly reproduces a user's information-and "phantom disclosures''-where the system incidentally generates a user's data. By partitioning input data into training and holdout sets and applying rigorous statistical hypothesis testing, we determine if observed disclosures are consistent with strict privacy baselines, such as zero-learning or specific Differential Privacy (DP) bounds. Crucially, this approach requires no model access, no canary insertion, and no reference model training -only the synthetic output and a held-out control set. We demonstrate that this framework effectively functions as a membership inference attack, providing empirical lower bounds on privacy leakage that are tighter than prior data-based auditing methods. Our approach is model-agnostic, applies to any synthetic data generation mechanism, and requires orders of magnitude fewer computational resources than shadow-model or canary-based alternatives.

</details>


### [353] [Beyond the Smile: A Hybrid Convolutional VAE for Crypto Volatility Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16961)
*Sadanand Singh,Allam Reddy,Manan Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于加密货币隐含波动率曲面的卷积变分自编码器，结合二次微笑重新拟合的混合预测器，在BTC和ETH期权数据上实现了显著的预测精度提升，在高遮蔽率下误差降低八倍，并能自动识别市场异常事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币期权市场存在波动率曲面数据不完整的问题，传统的参数化微笑重新拟合方法在数据缺失严重时表现不佳。研究者希望开发一种能够有效补全缺失数据、保持无套利约束且能捕捉跨市场共享特征的深度学习模型，以改善波动率曲面的预测和异常检测能力。

Method: 提出了卷积变分自编码器模型，在6×7期限-德尔塔网格上进行参数化，训练数据为2023年5月至10月期间的6034个完整币安期权曲面（BTC和ETH）。模型结合确定性期限路由规则与二次微笑重新拟合，形成混合预测器。采用了联合训练策略以学习两个市场间的共享波动率曲面流形。

Result: 模型在10-50%遮蔽率下实现了0.94-1.56波动率点的隐藏单元曲面补全RMSE。混合预测器在50%遮蔽率下达到0.83波动率点误差，相比单独使用微笑重新拟合的7.00波动率点降低了八倍。在结构性相关孔洞模式下（模拟整个期限行权价被撤出），学习模型误差保持在1.5-1.9波动率点，而重新拟合方法误差达9.6-13.1波动率点。BTC和ETH联合训练使模型性能相对单市场训练提升9-27%。模型在列出行权价处保持无日历套利和蝴蝶套利。重建误差成功标记了10月下旬ETF预期反弹和2023年8月17日闪崩等异常事件。

Conclusion: 该研究表明生成式模型在波动率曲面补全任务中具有显著优势，特别是在传统参数化方法失效的高遮蔽率场景下成为唯一可行的预测器。BTC和ETH之间存在显著的共享波动率曲面流形，联合训练可有效提升模型性能。模型还展现了无监督异常检测的潜力，为加密货币衍生品定价和风险管理提供了实用工具。所有训练和评估基础设施已开源以支持可重现的后续研究。

Abstract: We present a convolutional variational autoencoder for cryptocurrency implied-volatility surfaces, together with a deployable predictor that combines it with a quadratic smile re-fit through a deterministic per-tenor routing rule. Trained on 6,034 fully-filled hourly Binance Options surfaces of BTC and ETH spanning May-October 2023 and parameterised on a common $6 \times 7$ tenor-delta grid, the model attains a hidden-cell surface-completion RMSE in the 0.94-1.56 vol-point range across both markets and mask rates 10-50%. The hybrid predictor attains 0.83 vol points at 50% masking against 7.00 for the smile re-fit alone, an eightfold reduction obtained at no additional inference cost. Under structurally-correlated hole patterns that emulate the withdrawal of an entire tenor of strikes, the smile re-fit incurs 9.6-13.1 vol points of error while the learned model remains at 1.5-1.9, isolating a regime in which the generative model is the only viable predictor. Joint training on BTC and ETH improves the in-distribution model on both markets by 9-27% relative to the better-performing single-symbol counterpart, indicating a substantially shared vol-surface manifold across the two largest cryptocurrencies over the observation window. The hybrid is calendar- and butterfly-arbitrage-free at the listed strikes, a property that the parametric smile re-fit alone fails at high mask rates. The per-snapshot reconstruction error of the trained model flags the late-October ETF-anticipation rally and the August $17$, $2023$ flash crash as elevated-error periods without supervision. All training and evaluation infrastructure is released to support reproducible follow-on work.

</details>


### [354] [Scalable Pairwise Kernel Learning with Stochastic Vec Trick](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16979)
*Napsu Karmitsa,Tapio Pahikkala,Antti Airola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPaiK，一种针对成对学习场景的可扩展核学习方法。通过引入随机广义vec技巧（sGVT），该方法在保持核方法表达能力的同时大幅降低了计算和内存需求，使得大规模成对核学习成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对学习（pairwise learning）是预测对象对结果的一种特殊监督学习形式。然而，传统核方法在成对设置中面临计算复杂度高和内存消耗大的问题，限制了其在大规模数据集上的应用。因此，迫切需要开发一种既保持核方法强大表达能力，又能有效处理大规模数据的成对学习方法。

Method: 本文提出了SPaiK（Scalable Pairwise Kernel learning），核心创新是随机广义vec技巧（stochastic generalized vec trick, sGVT）。sGVT是稀疏Kronecker积乘法算法的随机扩展，能够高效地进行大规模成对核训练。该方法通过随机化策略，有效降低了计算复杂度和内存需求，同时保持了核方法的表达能力。

Result: 作者在七个真实的药物-靶点亲和力数据集上对SPaiK进行了评估，并与现有的成对学习最先进方法进行了对比。实验结果表明，SPaiK能够处理此前核方法无法触及的大规模数据集，并在性能上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SPaiK成功解决了成对核学习在大规模数据上的可扩展性问题，通过sGVT创新性地平衡了计算效率与模型表达能力。该方法为药物发现等需要成对学习的实际应用场景提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise learning is a specialized form of supervised learning that focuses on predicting outcomes for pairs of objects. In this work, we introduce SPaiK, a new scalable kernel learning method tailored for pairwise settings. Our approach preserves the expressive power of kernel methods while substantially reducing computational and memory requirements. The key innovation is the stochastic generalized vec trick (sGVT), a stochastic extension of the sparse Kronecker product multiplication algorithm, which enables efficient large-scale training with pairwise kernels. By incorporating sGVT, SPaiK makes it possible to apply kernel-based pairwise learning to datasets of a size previously out of reach. We evaluate the performance of SPaiK on seven real-world drug-target affinity datasets and compare the results with state-of-the-art methods in pairwise learning.

</details>


### [355] [Analytic Torsion and Spectral Gap Capture Persistent-Laplacian Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16990)
*Jernej Grlj,Aaron D. Lauda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将持久拉普拉斯算子压缩为三个数学不变量（Betti数、谱间隙、解析挠率）的紧致谱表示方法，在保持或提升预测性能的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久拉普拉斯算子虽比持久同调提供更丰富的几何表示，但其完整特征谱在学习任务中面临高维度和不同过滤尺度下"变长"问题的挑战，限制了其应用。

Method: 将持久拉普拉斯算子蒸馏为三个数学不变量：Betti数、谱间隙和解析挠率，构建固定长度的紧致特征空间来替代完整特征谱。

Result: 在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上验证，该紧致表示能捕获完整特征谱的核心预测信号，某些情况下性能更优，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频特征值引入的噪声。

Conclusion: 这三个数学不变量为谱几何与拓扑学习之间提供了有原则的、固定长度的接口，证明了紧致谱表示的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: While persistent Laplacians (PL) offer a richer geometric representation of data than persistent homology, utilizing their full eigenspectrum for learning tasks is often hampered by high dimensionality and the ``varying length'' problem across different filtration scales. We propose a compact spectral representation that distills the persistent Laplacian into three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, the spectral gap, and analytic torsion. Across benchmark datasets including MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT, we demonstrate that this reduced feature space captures the essential predictive signal of the full spectrum, and in some cases outperforms it, while significantly reducing computational overhead and preventing the noise introduced by higher-frequency eigenvalues. Our results suggest that these invariants provide a principled, fixed-length interface between spectral geometry and topological learning.

</details>


### [356] [From Tokens to Policy: Causal and Interpretable Heterogeneous Treatment Effects Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17010)
*Riccardo Cadei,Frank Otchere,Nyasha Tirivayi,Gustavo Angeles Tagliaferro,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NEXIS方法，将异质性处理效应（HTE）识别重构为马尔可夫毯发现问题，利用多模态预处理测量数据，在两个非洲反贫困项目中实现了可解释且可操作的政策优化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HTE识别方法在表达性和可解释性之间存在权衡，且当存在未测量的异质性驱动因素时，可能导致虚假的HTE表征，缺乏因果解释。随着多模态多视角预处理测量数据的丰富和最小人工监督的可扩展表示方法的发展，通过潜在交互因子进行因果表征成为可能。

Method: 将HTE识别重构为充分且对齐的预处理表示上的马尔可夫毯发现问题，提出了神经曝光交互搜索（NEXIS）方法。该方法是一个迭代过程，具有可证明的一致性选择性质，并经过实证验证。结合卫星图像等多模态数据来捕获以前未测量的环境效应修饰因子。

Result: 在非洲两个反贫困项目中部署了NEXIS方法，通过卫星图像捕获了以前未测量的环境效应修饰因子。方法成功识别了潜在的交互因子，并产出了新颖、可解释且具有指导意义的政策优化指南。

Conclusion: NEXIS为受控实验中的HTE因果表征提供了可行解决方案，通过结合多模态预处理数据，能够发现先前未测量的效应修饰因子，从而为政策优化提供可解释和可操作的指导，推动项目的下一轮迭代优化。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Treatment Effect (HTE) identification is crucial to explain the impact of an intervention and optimize our policies accordingly. Existing approaches trade expressivity for interpretability, but, if some active heterogeneity drivers are unmeasured, methods at both ends of this spectrum allow for spurious HTE characterization with no causal reading. In this work, we focus on controlled experiments and argue that an oracle HTE causal characterization via the latent interactors is now within reach, thanks to (i) more extensive pre-treatment measurements, i.e., multi-modal and multi-view, and (ii) scalable representations with minimal human supervision. We then re-frame HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem on a sufficient and aligned pre-treatment representation, and introduce Neural EXposure Interaction Search (NEXIS), an iterative procedure with provable and empirically validated consistent selection. We deploy NEXIS on two anti-poverty programs in Africa, augmenting each with satellite imagery capturing previously unmeasured environmental effect modifiers, leading to novel, interpretable and prescriptive guidelines to optimize the programs' next iterations.

</details>


### [357] [Filtered Conformal Ellipsoids for Graph-Native Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17014)
*Yannick Limmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出过滤共形椭球方法，用于多变量时间序列的联合预测集构建。通过结合状态空间滤波器的预测协方差与分裂共形校准，实现了适应坐标间依赖性的同时保持有效的覆盖保证。实验表明，在中等规模图原生交通数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列的联合预测集需要控制单一事件并适应跨坐标依赖性。现有方法要么依赖高斯尾部概率进行覆盖保证（假设过强），要么无法有效捕捉变量间的复杂依赖关系。需要一种方法既能利用学习到的预测协方差，又不依赖于高斯分布假设来保证覆盖率。

Method: 提出过滤共形椭球方法：(1) 使用冻结的状态空间滤波器输出单步预测均值和协方差；(2) 对马氏距离分数应用分裂共形校准；(3) 滤波器选择椭球形状，共形校准选择标量半径。核心创新是在可观察预测律商中分析收缩性，而非原始隐藏状态，从而处理过滤分数的依赖性问题。在稳定贝叶斯高斯投影滤波器、协方差边界和有限时域可观察性Fisher条件下，证明学习到的发射分布律的收缩性。

Result: 理论结果：在依赖性条件下，获得契比雪夫型近似覆盖边界；在额外几何混合浓度假设下获得更锐利的伯恩斯坦型边界；在高斯神谕可实现性下获得近神对数体积比较。实验结果：在中等规模图原生交通基准(METRLA-20和PEMSBAY-50)上，学习的滤波器产生比静态协方差和非滤波基线更锐利的椭球；在全图规模和非图原生数据集上，因子和copula基线可能更强。

Conclusion: 过滤共形椭球方法成功地将学习到的预测协方差与分布无关的覆盖保证相结合。该方法在图原生中等规模数据集上表现优异，证明了方法的有效性。理论分析揭示了在依赖性环境下共形预测的覆盖保证机制，为多变量时间序列预测提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: Joint prediction sets for multivariate time series should control a single event while adapting to cross-coordinate dependence. We study filtered conformal ellipsoids: a frozen state-space filter emits a one-step predictive mean and covariance, and split-conformal calibration is applied to the resulting Mahalanobis scores. The filter is used to choose the ellipsoid shape; conformal calibration chooses the scalar radius, so the construction benefits from a learned predictive covariance without relying on Gaussian tail probabilities for coverage. The main difficulty is that filtered scores are dependent and learned recurrent filters need not contract in their raw hidden state; we therefore analyse contraction in an observable predictive-law quotient that identifies hidden states producing the same future sequence of emitted Gaussian laws. Under a stable Bayes Gaussian-projection filter, covariance bounds, and a finite-horizon observability Fisher condition, small excess Gaussian negative log-likelihood implies contraction of the learned emitted laws. Combined with a threshold-autocovariance envelope this yields a Chebyshev-type approximate coverage bound for filtered split-conformal prediction under dependence; a sharper Bernstein-type bound requires an additional geometric-mixing concentration assumption. Under Gaussian oracle realisability we also obtain a near-oracle log-volume comparison within the class of conditionally valid Gaussian ellipsoid rules. We instantiate the framework with a GCN-GRU filter with diagonal-plus-low-rank covariance. On moderate-size graph-native traffic benchmarks (METRLA-$20$ and PEMSBAY-$50$), the learned filter gives sharper at-target ellipsoids than static-covariance and non-filter baselines; at full-graph scale and on non-graph-native datasets, factor and copula baselines can be stronger.

</details>


### [358] [ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17024)
*Violet Xiang,Amrith Setlur,Chase Blagden,Nick Haber,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExpRL提出了一种基于RL的中期训练方法，将参考答案作为奖励脚手架而非模仿目标，通过LLM评分器对推理过程进行细粒度奖励，在数学推理任务上优于SFT和稀疏奖励RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理能力提升依赖于稀疏奖励强化学习，但其成功很大程度上取决于基础模型的覆盖范围。实践中常通过中期训练（mid-training）在精选推理轨迹上训练模型以获得基本技能（如分解、验证、自我修正），但这种方法需要人工指定学习内容，且对于需要组合这些基本技能的困难问题效果有限。因此需要一种更自动化的方法来扩展模型的推理能力覆盖范围。

Method: 提出ExpRL（Reward-Enhanced RL）方法：1）利用大规模人类问答数据集；2）不将参考答案作为模仿目标，而是作为奖励脚手架；3）参考答案对策略模型隐藏，仅用于构建问题特定的评分标准；4）策略模型从原始问题提示采样推理轨迹；5）LLM评分器将采样的推理轨迹与参考答案对比，分配结果级或过程级密集奖励；6）这种设计能够强化部分进展、有用的中间简化过程和有效的推理行为。

Result: 在挑战性数学推理任务上，ExpRL相比SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法提供了更强的RL初始化效果；作为后续稀疏奖励RL的初始化，ExpRL训练的模型表现更好；额外的混合领域实验表明ExpRL可以扩展到纯数学领域之外的设置。

Conclusion: ExpRL成功实现了基于RL的自动化中期训练，通过将参考答案转化为密集奖励信号而非模仿目标，有效解决了稀疏奖励强化学习中的覆盖问题，为LLM推理能力提升提供了新的范式，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.

</details>


### [359] [Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17035)
*Xiaolin Li,Ning Wang,Ninghui Li,Wenhai Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了差分隐私（DP）能增强联邦学习（FL）对后门攻击鲁棒性的假设，揭示了DP反而会掩盖恶意更新的统计特征、导致防御失效，并据此提出RING攻击方法，在中等隐私预算下对六种防御达到90.3%的平均攻击成功率（较基线提升最高26.08倍），暴露了DP-FL的根本安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为差分隐私（DP）能增强联邦学习对后门攻击的鲁棒性，但作者通过实证分析发现DP-FL中存在根本性矛盾：遵守DP会在掩盖恶意更新统计特征的同时，使现有防御机制失效，从而引发了对DP在FL中安全性的重新审视。

Method: 提出RING攻击方法，将其作为扰动层显式利用DP的掩盖效应来隐藏恶意贡献，同时最大化攻击影响；受损客户端通过协同构建对抗扰动，在聚合时重建强后门信号而不触发异常检测，该方法对底层后门技术具有无关性和可组合性。

Result: 在四个图像和文本数据集、非独立同分布设置下，RING在中等隐私预算下对六种最先进防御方法达到90.3%的平均攻击成功率，相比基线策略提升最高26.08倍；缓解该威胁需要付出显著的效用代价，揭示了DP-FL部署中的根本安全缺口。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在联邦学习中非但不能增强安全性，反而会通过掩盖后门信号特征导致防御失效，形成根本性的安全漏洞；缓解该威胁需要显著的效用权衡，这对DP-FL的实际部署提出了严峻的安全挑战。

Abstract: Prior research suggests that differential privacy (DP) inherently enhances the robustness of federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. Through an empirical analysis of two baseline attack strategies, we uncover a fundamental tension in DP-FL: while bypassing DP allows state-of-the-art defenses to detect and filter malicious updates, complying with DP inadvertently masks their distinguishing statistical characteristics. Consequently, existing defenses become ineffective as DP reduces the raw backdoor signal. Building on this masking effect, we propose RING, a novel attack that explicitly exploits DP to conceal malicious contributions while maximizing attack impact. By collaboratively crafting adversarial perturbations, compromised clients reconstruct a strong backdoor signal during aggregation without triggering anomaly detection. RING operates as a perturbation layer that is agnostic to the underlying backdoor technique, making it broadly applicable and composable with existing attacks -- a property that significantly amplifies the threat it poses to DP-FL. Extensive evaluations across four image and text datasets under non-iid distributions show that RING achieves an average attack success rate of 90.3% against six state-of-the-art defenses under a moderate privacy budget, an improvement of up to 26.08x over baseline strategies. Finally, we evaluate potential countermeasures and find that mitigating this threat incurs significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security gap in the deployment of differentially private FL.

</details>


### [360] [Exact Posterior Score Estimation for Solving Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17048)
*Abbas Mammadov,Ozgur Kara,Kaan Oktay,Iskander Azangulov,Adil Kaan Akan,Hyungjin Chung,James Matthew Rehg,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了精确后验得分（EPS）方法，通过推导线性高斯逆问题的闭式后验得分，将后验采样转化为去噪问题，在保持预训练结构的同时显著提升性能并减少计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流模型用于解决线性逆问题时，只能提供无条件得分而非后验得分。现有方法要么使用近似的测量匹配校正来引导预训练去噪器，要么训练条件恢复模型但放弃了先验的去噪结构。需要一种既能利用预训练模型优势，又能准确进行后验采样的方法。

Method: 本文推导了在线性高斯逆问题下，一般高斯插值的精确后验得分闭式表达式，证明后验采样可简化为在算子依赖的移位枢轴下、具有各向异性噪声协方差的去噪问题。基于此提出了精确后验得分（EPS）训练目标，保留了标准预训练的输入/输出结构，可以从头训练或从预训练去噪器微调。推理时使用与底层主干相同的采样器，无需似然梯度或投影。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet数据集上的五个线性逆问题实验中，EPS在保真度、感知质量和分布指标上均优于免训练和基于训练的基线方法，同时使用的去噪器评估次数比基于梯度的后验采样器减少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: EPS方法成功地将后验采样问题转化为去噪问题，既保持了预训练模型的结构优势，又实现了精确的后验采样，为线性逆问题提供了高效准确的解决方案，在性能和计算效率方面都取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models learn powerful data priors by training a denoiser to reverse Gaussian corruption. To use this prior to solve a linear inverse problem, one needs to sample from the posterior, but the score that the prior provides is the unconditional score, not the posterior score. Existing methods either steer a fixed pretrained denoiser with approximate measurement-matching corrections, or train a conditional restoration model that abandons the denoising structure of the prior. We derive the exact posterior score in closed form for linear Gaussian inverse problems under general Gaussian interpolants, and show that posterior sampling reduces to a denoising problem at an operator-dependent shifted pivot under an anisotropic noise covariance. We turn this identity into Exact Posterior Score (EPS), a denoising training objective that preserves the input/output structure of standard pretraining and can therefore be trained from scratch or fine-tuned from a pretrained denoiser. At inference, EPS uses the same sampler as the underlying backbone, with no likelihood gradients or projections. We evaluate EPS on five linear inverse problems across FFHQ and ImageNet, where it outperforms training-free and training-based baselines on fidelity, perceptual, and distributional metrics, while using roughly an order of magnitude fewer denoiser evaluations than gradient-based posterior samplers.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [361] [From MWM to iSLIP: A Linear-Algebraic Tutorial on Input-Queued Switch Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14771)
*Xiaotong Yuan,An Guo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在N×N SoC交叉开关背景下，统一使用队列矩阵Q、匹配矩阵P和李雅普诺夫能量函数V这三种数学对象，解释了最大权重匹配（MWM）、可容许流量下的队列稳定性以及iSLIP的行-列解耦机制。实验对比表明，谱调度和熵正则化最优传输（OT）在吞吐量和延迟上接近MWM，而iSLIP在非均匀可容许高负载流量下存在吞吐瓶颈（约80%），队列无限增长且延迟比MWM高两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度算法分析缺乏统一的理论框架，且缺少对近似算法在高负载下性能极限的定量评估。本文旨在建立共享数学语言来统一理解不同调度目标与机制，并精确量化iSLIP等近似算法在高负载非均匀流量下的性能退化。

Method: 使用队列矩阵Q、匹配矩阵P和李雅普诺夫能量函数V作为统一数学框架；构建C++离散事件模拟器，以匈牙利算法求解的精确MWM作为性能基准；对比三种近似算法：iSLIP（r=3轮/周期）、谱调度（r=3轮/周期）和熵正则化OT（r_sink=10轮Sinkhorn归一化）；在四种流量模式下测量吞吐量和平均信元延迟。

Result: 谱调度和熵正则化OT在大多数测试条件下，吞吐量和延迟均紧密跟踪MWM；iSLIP在非均匀可容许高负载流量下（unbalanced w=0.5, ρ_load≥0.9）吞吐量上限约80%，瓶颈队列无限增长，延迟比MWM高两个数量级；在均匀流量下iSLIP无此崩溃现象，ρ_load=0.99时延迟约为MWM的3.7倍；谱调度和OT的性能提升需要额外O(r·N²)量级的每周期计算开销。

Conclusion: 谱调度和熵正则化OT是接近最优的近似调度算法，适用于高负载场景；iSLIP在非均匀高负载流量下存在严重的吞吐量瓶颈和延迟退化问题，均匀流量下性能尚可；高性能算法的硬件可行性（芯片面积、功耗、时序收敛）仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: This paper uses three objects -- the queue matrix Q, the matching matrix P, and the Lyapunov energy function V = ||Q||^2 -- as a shared mathematical language to explain, within a single framework, the scheduling objective of maximum weight matching (MWM), queue stability under admissible traffic (per-port loads strictly below 1), and the mechanics of iSLIP's Grant-Accept row-column decoupling together with the long-run average service matrix P-bar. The setting throughout is an N-by-N SoC crossbar, where each clock cycle permits at most one cell transfer per input-output port pair. For the experimental comparison, we built a C++ discrete-event simulator and used exact MWM (solved by the Hungarian algorithm) as the performance reference. All three approximate algorithms are given a fixed iteration budget: r = 3 rounds per cycle for iSLIP and for spectral scheduling, and r_sink = 10 Sinkhorn normalization rounds for entropy-regularized optimal transport (OT). Throughput and average cell delay are measured across four traffic patterns. Spectral scheduling and entropy-regularized OT track MWM closely in both throughput and delay across most tested conditions. iSLIP, by contrast, hits a throughput ceiling of roughly 80% under non-uniform admissible traffic at high load (unbalanced pattern w = 0.5, rho_load >= 0.9), with bottleneck queues growing without bound and delays reaching two orders of magnitude above MWM. Under uniform traffic this breakdown does not occur: at rho_load = 0.99 iSLIP delay is about 3.7x that of MWM. The performance gains of spectral scheduling and OT come at an additional per-cycle compute cost on the order of O(r*N^2) multiply-accumulate or exponential operations; whether this overhead is feasible in real hardware -- in terms of die area, power, and timing closure -- remains to be evaluated.

</details>


### [362] [Towards Ubiquitous 6G Computing and Networking Convergence: Architecture and Mechanism for Cross-Domain Resource Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15073)
*Yang Li,Xing Zhang,Yan Zhang,Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G网络中计算网络融合(CNC)提出分层跨域协调架构和基于分层多智能体强化学习的编排机制，显著降低系统能耗并提升任务满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络将支持沉浸式通信、AI与通信融合、感知与通信融合等六大应用场景，这些场景需要大量计算支持，且用户需求日益细分、多样化和个性化。传统网络切片无法满足新兴服务场景的异构计算和网络需求。移动计算网络融合(CNC)通过将计算资源深度嵌入移动网络基础设施，提供针对多样化用户需求的集成计算网络服务，成为一种根本不同的范式。

Method: 首先回顾CNC在移动网络视角下的演进，调研现有研究并按移动网络架构进行分类。基于这些洞察，提出分层跨域协调架构，以及基于分层多智能体强化学习的编排机制。

Result: 性能评估表明，所提出的架构和机制显著降低了系统能耗，同时提升了任务满意度率。

Conclusion: 本文为6G移动网络中的CNC编排提供了新的架构和机制方案，并讨论了开放性挑战和未来研究方向，为6G网络支持多样化计算密集型应用场景奠定基础。

Abstract: The 6G network will support six major application scenarios, such as immersive communication, integrated AI and communication, and integrated sensing and communication. Many scenarios necessitate significant computational support. Moreover, user demands are becoming increasingly segmented, diverse, and personalized. Traditional network slicing alone is insufficient to meet the heterogeneous computing and networking demands of emerging service scenarios. Mobile computing network convergence (CNC) introduces a fundamentally different paradigm from the conventional cloud computing plus communication network model by deeply embedding computing resources into the mobile network infrastructure and enabling integrated computing-network services tailored to diverse user demands. In this article, we investigate orchestration architectures and mechanisms for CNC in 6G mobile networks. We begin by reviewing the evolution of CNC from a mobile network perspective and surveying existing studies, which we categorize according to mobile network architectures. Building on these insights, we propose a hierarchical, cross-domain coordination architecture and an orchestration mechanism based on hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning. Performance evaluations demonstrate that the proposed architecture and mechanism significantly reduce system energy consumption while enhancing task satisfaction rate. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [363] [Sub Terahertz LEO Satellite Communication: Vision, Opportunities, and Challenges toward the First Prototype in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15410)
*Sergi Aliaga,Vitaly Petrov,Andrew Benincasa,Albert Diez Comas,Ali J. Alqaraghuli,Jose V. Siles,Ken R. Duffy,Marc Sanchez Net,Tommaso Melodia,Josep M. Jornet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将亚太赫兹(sub-THz, 100-300GHz)通信技术扩展至100公里级卫星链路的可行性，分析了硬件与信号处理技术的成熟度，并介绍了TeraLink项目的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十年亚太赫兹无线技术从早期的小众应用迅速发展到近期实现公里级100Gbit/s+无线回传链路，基于此发展势头，研究将其扩展至低地球轨道(LEO)卫星通信系统的潜力。

Method: 评估新兴亚太赫兹硬件和信号处理技术的成熟度；分析其作为现有毫米波和光学卫星技术补充方案的独特作用；讨论实现该系统的研究与工程挑战；介绍TeraLink国际研发项目及NASA CSLI空间任务的路线图。

Result: 亚太赫兹通信技术具备支持LEO卫星长距离运行的潜力；可作为现有毫米波和光学卫星技术的补充，提供额外容量、更强韧性和架构灵活性；TeraLink项目正在推进首个亚太赫兹LEO卫星通信空间硬件原型。

Conclusion: 亚太赫兹卫星通信系统在技术上可行，但仍有诸多研究挑战需要解决；TeraLink项目将为未来亚太赫兹卫星通信的实际部署奠定基础。

Abstract: The landscape of sub-terahertz (sub-THz, 100GHz - 300GHz) wireless technology evolved drastically over the last two decades - from only a few niche use cases in sensing and ultra-short-range communications in early 2000s toward operational multi-kilometer range 100GBbit/s+ wireless backhaul links demonstrated recently. Building on this momentum, this article explores the feasibility of extending sub-THz communications to 100-km-scale satellite links. We first assess the technological readiness of emerging sub-THz hardware and signal-processing techniques, highlighting their potential to support long-range operation in low-Earth-orbit (LEO) systems. We then outline the unique role that sub-THz links can play as a complementary solution to existing millimeter-wave and optical (``laser'') satellite technologies, offering additional capacity, improved resilience, and new architectural flexibility. We further discuss open research and engineering challenges toward implementing such sub-THz satellite communication systems in practice. We finally outline the key state-of-the-art solutions and the roadmap of TeraLink, an ongoing international R&D project aiming to build and launch, through an approved NASA CSLI space mission, the first hardware prototype of sub-THz LEO satellite communications in space.

</details>


### [364] [Single-Connection Mixed-Criticality Transport with CATS: Bounded Guarantees, Three Structural Limits, and a QUIC Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16924)
*Syed Muhammad Aqdas Rizvi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出CATS方案：在TCP连接上实现传输层优先级，解决了混合关键性应用中关键消息类的饥饿和公平性问题，并指出只有基于QUIC的多流独立方案才能从根本上实现延迟保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合关键性应用（如卫星终端、工业遥测等）需要在单个连接上复用低延迟关键消息和批量流量，传统单一FIFO连接会导致关键流量饥饿，而并行连接方案存在资源消耗大、受限于NAT/策略、无法保证单流公平性等问题。

Method: 设计CATS（Conductor驱动的非对称传输方案）：发送端实现的、对接收端透明的TCP传输层优先级机制。Conductor为每个消息分配优先级类别和即时序列号，采用基于信用的整形器。并进一步提出CATS-over-QUIC方案，利用独立流和每流隔离实现延迟保证。

Result: CATS能够同时提供：确定性的非饥饿保证、单流公平性、可证明的有界每类延迟。同时发现TCP上存在三个结构性障碍限制带内优先级：有序序列空间（队首阻塞）、共享拥塞窗口（跨类耦合）、每流粒度的网络QoS。这些障碍解释了为何公平队列和L4S无法帮助单连接，而并行连接需以增加流为代价降低尾延迟。CATS-over-QUIC通过独立流实现端点自隔离，在单个公平流上获得延迟保证。

Conclusion: 在TCP单连接上无法根本性解决优先级延迟问题，只有采用QUIC等多流协议，通过独立流配合聚合耦合拥塞控制，才能在单个公平流上实现确定性的延迟保证和优先级隔离。

Abstract: Mixed-criticality applications, such as satellite terminals, industrial telemetry, embedded systems, tactical, and other constrained links, often multiplex a small, latency-critical message class and bulk traffic over a single commodity transport connection. A single FIFO connection can starve the critical class under load. The obvious alternative, opening parallel connections, costs an additional five-tuple (often blocked by carrier-grade NAT, port budgets, and operator policy) and is not always available; when the critical class is light, two connections can also be bandwidth-fair only in aggregate rather than single-flow fair.
  We present CATS (Conductor-driven Asymmetric Transport Scheme), a sender-side, receiver-transparent transport-layer priority scheme over TCP: a Conductor assigns each message a priority class and just-in-time sequence numbers, using a credit-based shaper. CATS provides the one combination its alternatives cannot: deterministic non-starvation together with single-flow fairness, plus a provable bounded per-class delay. We then show that, crucially, CATS-over-TCP is not a tail-latency mechanism, and why. Three structural barriers bound in-band priority: the in-order sequence space (head-of-line blocking), the shared congestion window (cross-class coupling), and the per-flow granularity of network QoS (in-band priority is invisible to it). These barriers explain why fair-queuing and even the modern low-latency standard L4S cannot help a single connection, and why two parallel connections reduce the latency tail at the cost of an additional flow. We give CATS-over-QUIC as the principled escape: independent streams with per-stream isolation under aggregate-coupled congestion control self-isolate at the endpoint, attaining the guarantees on one fair flow. An ns-3 evaluation and QUIC proof-of-concept support the findings.

</details>


### [365] [IoT-Zoo: A Container-Based Framework for Heterogeneous IoT Device Profiles and Reproducible Traffic Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15653)
*Vagner E. Quincozes,Diego Kreutz,Silvio E. Quincozes*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IoT-Zoo，一个基于容器的物联网测试平台，通过异构的、数据集驱动的设备配置文件支持可重复的实验，解决了现有平台设备类型有限、缺乏真实环境异构性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物联网网络和安全解决方案验证平台通常通过复制有限的设备类型来实现可扩展性，这限制了配置文件的多样性，无法捕捉现实世界物联网环境的异构性，难以提供真实且可重现的实验数据。

Method: 基于Containernet构建了IoT-Zoo容器化测试平台，采用数据集驱动的异构物联网设备配置文件，自动化部署多领域场景，支持MQTT和RTSP等真实应用协议，提供单命令接口进行环境配置和自动流量捕获（PCAP）。

Result: IoT-Zoo平台能够生成一致的流量基线，通过支持异构设备配置文件和真实应用协议，成功捕捉了真实物联网环境的异构性，同时显著降低了评估网络和安全解决方案所需的操作工作量。

Conclusion: IoT-Zoo为物联网网络和安全解决方案的验证提供了一个实用、可扩展且可重现的实验平台，通过异构设备配置和自动化流量捕获有效解决了现有平台在设备多样性和操作复杂性方面的局限性。

Abstract: The validation of networking and security solutions for the Internet of Things (IoT) requires realistic and reproducible experimental data. However, existing platforms often achieve scalability by replicating a limited set of device types, which restricts profile diversity and fails to capture the heterogeneity of real-world IoT environments. In this paper, we present IoT-Zoo, a container-based testbed designed to support reproducible experimentation through heterogeneous, dataset-driven IoT device profiles. Built upon Containernet, IoT-Zoo automates the deployment of multi-domain scenarios and supports real application protocols such as MQTT and RTSP. The platform provides a single-command interface for environment provisioning and automated traffic capture (PCAP), enabling the generation of consistent traffic baselines and reducing the operational effort required to evaluate networking and security solutions.

</details>


### [366] [SINR-Aware Base Station Deployment in Wide Area IoT Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15952)
*Sachin Kadam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了广域物联网传感器网络中的SINR感知基站部署问题，提出了一种具有(1-1/e)近似保证的多项式时间贪心算法，在保证目标SINR阈值的前提下以最低成本实现完全覆盖，计算时间比元启发式算法快190倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网应用的快速扩展，需要在干扰受限条件下有效部署基站以实现大范围地理区域的连续连接。现有技术通常采用基于距离或二元的覆盖模型，这些抽象方法忽略了密集部署中同频干扰对通信质量的影响，无法满足实际部署需求。

Method: 将SINR感知基站部署问题建模为组合优化问题，目标是从预定义的候选基站集合中确定最小成本子集，确保每个物联网传感器至少被一个基站覆盖且满足目标SINR阈值。通过理论分析证明所提出的覆盖函数具有单调性和子模性，从而设计了具有(1-1/e)近似保证的多项式时间SINR感知贪心算法。

Result: 在真实配水网络数据集上的数值评估表明，所提出的SINR感知贪心算法能够实现近最优的基站部署。与遗传算法（GA）和粒子群优化（PSO）算法相比，该方法实现了完全传感器覆盖，部署成本在最优秀的元启发式解的12.3%以内，同时计算时间减少了高达190倍。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了考虑同频干扰影响的基站部署问题，证明了SINR感知覆盖函数的子模性质，为NP-hard的基站部署问题提供了理论近似保证和高效实用的解决方案，在保证部署质量的同时显著降低了计算开销，为广域物联网网络规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of Internet of Things (IoT) applications necessitates the effective deployment of base stations (BSs) to enable consistent connectivity across large geographic areas under interference-limited conditions. Existing techniques typically use distance-based or binary coverage models; however, these abstractions fail to account for the influence of co-channel interference on the quality of communication in dense deployments. In this paper, we investigate the Signal-to-Interference-plus-Noise Ratio (SINR)-aware Base Station Deployment (BSD) problem in wide-area IoT sensor networks. The objective is to determine a minimum-cost subset of BSs from a predefined set of candidate BSs such that every IoT sensor is covered by at least one BS and a target SINR threshold is satisfied. The problem is formulated as a combinatorial optimization problem, which is NP-hard. Theoretical analysis establishes that the proposed coverage function is monotone and submodular, enabling the SINR-aware greedy algorithm to achieve a (1-1/e)-approximation to the optimal solution while maintaining a polynomial-time computational complexity. Numerical evaluations on a real water distribution network dataset demonstrate that the proposed SINR-aware greedy algorithm achieves near-optimal base station deployment while significantly reducing computational effort. Compared with the Genetic Algorithm (GA) and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithms, the proposed approach attains complete sensor coverage with deployment costs within 12.3% of the best-performing metaheuristic solution while requiring up to 190 times lower execution time.

</details>


### [367] [Hidden Degradation Costs in Energy-Cost-Only HEMS Optimisation: Study on Battery and PV Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16051)
*Dawood Butt,Nandor Verba*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对住宅光伏储能系统，发现仅优化能源成本的模型预测控制（MPC）方法会忽略电池退化成本，导致退化成本最高可达能源节约成本的1060%，表明必须在优化目标中纳入退化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住宅储能系统的模型预测控制（MPC）优化策略通常仅以最小化净能源成本为目标，忽略了电池退化成本。这导致优化策略激进、循环次数高，实际部署时可能造成显著的经济损失，因此需要量化并评估退化成本的影响。

Method: 构建了基于滚动时域混合整数线性规划（MILP）的英国住宅家庭能源管理系统（HEMS）基线模型，使用REFIT数据集的需求数据。通过3×3敏感性分析（三种电池容量×三种光伏阵列规模），并采用Naumann应力模型和雨流循环计数法进行事后退化成本估算。

Result: 研究表明，对于每种电池容量，退化成本基本保持恒定。退化成本最高可超过能源成本节约的1060%。仅优化能源成本的方法会系统性低估真实的系统总成本。

Conclusion: 仅以能源成本为目标的优化方法存在严重缺陷，系统性低估系统总成本，证明了在控制策略中纳入退化感知的必要性，为开发退化感知控制策略提供了有力依据。

Abstract: Residential battery energy storage systems (BESS) are increasingly deployed alongside photovoltaic (PV) generation to reduce household energy costs under volatile time-of-use (TOU) tariffs. Model predictive control (MPC) is a widely adopted optimisation strategy for home energy management systems (HEMS), typically formulated to minimise net energy cost, subject to physical and operational constraints. However, battery degradation is rarely embedded in the optimisation objective, meaning its cost is unquantified and aggressive; high-cycle-count strategies could incur significant losses once deployed to physical systems. This paper presents a receding-horizon mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) baseline for a UK residential HEMS, using demand data from the REFIT dataset. A 3 by 3 sensitivity study is conducted across three battery sizes and three PV array sizes, with post-hoc degradation cost estimated using the Naumann stress model and rainflow cycle counting. Results show that degradation remains constant for each battery size and can exceed energy cost savings by up to 1,060 %. These results demonstrate that energy-cost-only optimisation systematically underestimates the true system cost, motivating a degradation-aware control formulation.

</details>


### [368] [Impact of ADAS and V2X Penetration Rates on Cooperative Active Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16453)
*M. C. Lucas-Estañ,B. Coll-Perales,M. I. Khan,J. Gozalvez,O. Altintas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析了ADAS和V2X渗透率对协同主动安全应用有效性的影响，发现两者结合部署能显著提升高速公路紧急制动场景下的交通安全，且随着V2X部署加速，协同效应更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同主动安全是联网自动驾驶的关键应用，其有效性取决于车辆提前识别交通安全风险的能力。ADAS传感器受感知范围和视野限制，而V2X通信范围更广但受渗透率影响，需要研究两者对交通安全的影响。

Method: 以高速公路场景下的紧急制动操作为用例，分析ADAS和V2X渗透率对协同主动安全应用有效性的影响，研究两者独立部署和联合部署的效果。

Result: ADAS和V2X各自都能提升交通安全，但两者结合部署能进一步放大安全增益，这种协同效应在V2X部署速度加快时变得更加明显。

Conclusion: ADAS与V2X的协同部署能够产生互补优势，显著提升协同主动安全应用的效果，为智能网联汽车的渐进式部署策略提供了重要依据。

Abstract: A major driver of connected and automated driving is cooperative active safety. The effectiveness of cooperative safety applications depends on the ability of vehicles to detect traffic safety risks in advance. Such risks can be identified either through ADAS (Advanced Driving Assistance Systems) or via V2X (Vehicle to Everything) communications. More vehicles are gradually being deployed with ADAS, but ADAS sensors can be limited by their sensing range and field of view. On the other hand, V2X can experience communication ranges beyond the ADAS sensing range, but its impact is highly dependent on the V2X penetration rate. This paper analyzes the impact of ADAS and V2X penetration rates on the effectiveness of cooperative active safety applications considering an emergency braking maneuver use case in a highway scenario. Results show that while ADAS and V2X each enhance traffic safety, their combined deployment further amplifies these gains, with the effect becoming more pronounced as V2X is deployed more rapidly.

</details>


### [369] [Predictive Dynamic Scheduling for Deterministic Communications in Beyond 5G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16471)
*Syed Morsleen Riaz,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Baldomero Coll-Perales,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型预测式动态调度方案，通过预测流量动态分配资源来支持确定性通信，避免了现有半静态调度方案因预测不准确导致的资源预留低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络需要支持新兴时敏应用并提供确定性的服务质量保障，现有基于流量预测的半静态调度方案存在预测不准确导致的资源预留低效问题，无法有效满足有界时延需求。

Method: 提出一种预测式动态调度方案，利用流量预测为满足时延要求的传入请求动态分配资源，同时避免使用未来预测分组可能需要的资源，从而克服半静态调度中的资源预留低效问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该预测式动态调度方案在混合业务流和不同服务质量要求的场景下，能够有效支持确定性通信。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的预测式动态调度方案有效解决了现有半静态调度方案的局限性，为下一代无线网络提供确定性服务保障提供了有效的资源管理解决方案。

Abstract: Next generation wireless networks must sustain deterministic service levels to support emerging time-sensitive applications. The ability to guarantee bounded latencies depends on the efficient management of radio resources. Several studies propose leveraging the native intelligence of future networks to develop predictive schedulers capable of efficiently managing resources. However, existing proposals focus on semi-static scheduling, where resources are reserved based on traffic predictions, and these reservations are susceptible to inefficiencies due to prediction inaccuracies. This study advances the state of the art with a novel predictive dynamic scheduling scheme that avoids such inefficiencies, and leverages traffic predictions to allocate resources to incoming requests that meet their latency requirements while avoiding resources likely to be needed by future predicted packets. Our results demonstrate that the proposed predictive dynamic scheduling effectively supports deterministic communications in scenarios with mixed traffic flows and varying QoS requirements.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [370] [Unified KV Pooling to Accelerate Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14779)
*Minchul Kang,Changyong Shin,Jinwoo Jeong,Jaerim Park,Woohyun Kim,Bonyul Gu,Dongwoo Kang,Gyeongsik Yang,Chuck Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一KV池化和KV直通技术，解决了长上下文LLM服务中KV缓存卸载的延迟问题，将TTFT降低约4.1倍，使其满足10秒以内的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM服务需要将KV缓存卸载到主机内存和SSD，但现有机制存在显著效率问题：服务延迟高达30.7秒，超出典型TTFT要求（10秒）3倍以上。主要原因包括：(1)检索通过主机内存和SSD串行化，导致其他主机内存模块和SSD未被充分利用；(2)基于SSD的KV检索有84%的时间消耗在内核文件系统中，而非实际设备访问。

Method: 提出两项关键技术：(1)统一KV池化（unified KV pooling）：将多个主机内存模块和SSD聚合为单一逻辑池，根据设备带宽分布KV缓存；(2)KV直通（KV-passthrough）：绕过内核文件系统，通过SPDK从用户空间直接访问SSD上的KV缓存。

Result: 在LLaMA 3.1-8B、GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen3-30B-A3B上的评估显示：统一KV池化将长上下文场景下的TTFT相比最先进技术降低约4.1倍，均控制在10秒以内；通过消除文件系统开销，阻塞I/O时间最多减少23.2倍。

Conclusion: 统一KV池化和KV直通技术有效解决了长上下文LLM服务中KV缓存管理的性能瓶颈，显著降低了服务延迟和I/O开销，使长上下文推理能够满足实际应用的时延要求。

Abstract: Long-context LLM serving requires offloading KV caches to host-memory and SSDs, but existing mechanisms are not designed for such long contexts. We observe significant inefficiencies in current KV caching in long contexts: high serving latency ~30.7 s, exceeding the typical TTFT requirement of 10 s by more than 3x. Our in-depth analysis explains two major reasons: (1) retrieval is serialized through host-memory and SSD, leaving other host-memory modules and SSDs underutilized, and (2) SSD-based KV retrieval spends 84% of its time in the kernel filesystem rather than actual device access. To address the problems, we propose unified KV pooling, which aggregates multiple host-memory modules and SSDs into a single logical pool and distributes KV caches across devices based on their bandwidth. To eliminate the filesystem overhead, we design KV-passthrough, which bypasses the kernel filesystem and directly accesses SSD-resident KV caches from user space via SPDK. Across evaluations on LLaMA 3.1-8B, GPT-OSS-20B, and Qwen3-30B-A3B, unified KV pooling reduces TTFT in long-contexts ~4.1x over state-of-the-art techniques, all making under 10 s. It also reduces blocked I/O time by up to 23.2x by eliminating filesystem overhead.

</details>


### [371] [Running hardware-aware neural architecture search on embedded devices under 512MB of RAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14824)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件感知的神经网络架构搜索（HW NAS）方法，可在嵌入式设备上运行，生成面向低端MCU的微型CNN，在TinyML基准数据集上达到SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬件感知神经架构搜索方法通常依赖外部服务器，难以直接在资源受限的嵌入式设备（如低端微控制器MCU）上运行。而在物联网和可穿戴机器人等场景中，需要在边缘设备上直接部署模型，同时保护隐私并降低对云端的依赖。

Method: 提出一种新颖的硬件感知神经架构搜索方法，该方法考虑目标计算平台的可用资源，能够直接在各种嵌入式设备上执行搜索过程，生成面向低端微控制器（MCU）的微型卷积神经网络架构。

Result: 在Visual Wake Word数据集（标准TinyML基准）上的人类识别任务中达到了最先进的结果，并在多种嵌入式设备上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法使嵌入式设备（如网关）能够在本地针对采集数据定制CNN架构，无需外部服务器支持，既保护了隐私又开拓了物联网和可穿戴机器人等领域的新应用场景。

Abstract: This document proposes a novel approach to hardware-aware neural architecture search (HW NAS) that considers the resources available on the computing platform running it, enabling its execution on various embedded devices. The presented HW NAS produces tiny convolutional neural networks (CNNs) targeting low-end microcontroller units (MCUs), typically involved in the Internet of Things (IoT) or wearable robotics, opening new use cases. A gateway could run it to tailor CNNs' architecture on the acquired data without using external servers, ensuring privacy. The proposed technique achieves state-of-the-art results in the human-recognition tasks on the Visual Wake Word dataset, a standard TinyML benchmark, on several embedded devices.

</details>


### [372] [KATANA: A Fast, Low-Power Mapping of Kalman Filters onto Edge NPUs for Real-Time Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14992)
*Bodhisatwa Kundu,Anish Rooj,Sumit Saha,Abhradeep Sarkar,Arghadip Das,Arnab Raha,Mrinal K. Naskar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了KATANA框架，首次将卡尔曼滤波器（LKF/EKF）映射到商用NPU上，在Intel Core Ultra处理器上实现了高达97.9%的动态能耗降低，帧率可达408.73 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时跟踪系统（如雷达监视、反无人机、自动驾驶）运行在边缘平台上，受限于严格的功耗和实时性约束。现有卡尔曼滤波器主要在CPU上串行执行多目标跟踪，或使用定制FPGA/ASIC加速器延长设计周期。现代AI-PC SoC集成了低功耗、数据并行的NPU，研究能否将卡尔曼滤波器映射到现有矩阵引擎上，同时满足实时和低功耗需求。

Method: KATANA框架应用三种代数图重写优化：（1）通过预计算负投影矩阵H_neg进行减法到加法的重构；（2）静态形状张量融合；（3）块对角批量并行化。这些优化确保100%的操作在DPU矩阵引擎上执行，实现了端到端的LKF和EKF到商用NPU的映射。

Result: 在Intel Core Ultra Series 2上，优化的批量EKF在13.43 W有功功率下达到223.35 FPS，LKF在14.05 W下达到408.73 FPS。相比CPU实现，动态能耗降低高达97.9%。

Conclusion: KATANA成功证明了卡尔曼滤波器可以高效映射到商用NPU上，无需专用加速器即可同时满足实时性和低功耗要求，同时释放CPU和GPU用于主要工作负载，为边缘平台的实时状态估计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: State estimation is the closed-loop core of every real-time tracking system, from radar surveillance and counter-UAV defense to autonomous driving and robotics. These deployments run on edge platforms, where defense systems mount on vehicles and drones, and civilian pipelines live on cars and handheld devices. Here, every additional watt of compute erodes mission duration or operational range. Two hard constraints follow: each new measurement must be fused before the next control cycle, and the total compute must fit within a strict battery and thermal power envelope. The Linear and Extended Kalman Filters (LKF, EKF) are dominant estimators on these systems, but today they execute almost exclusively on CPUs, which serialize multi-object tracking (MOT) updates, or on custom FPGA/ASIC accelerators that lengthen design cycles. Contemporary AI-PC SoCs, like the Intel Core Ultra Series 1 and 2, integrate a low-power, data-parallel Neural Processing Unit (NPU). We therefore ask whether the Kalman filter can be mapped onto this existing matrix engine to meet real-time and low-power budgets simultaneously, avoiding a dedicated accelerator and keeping the CPU and GPU free for primary workloads. We present KATANA, an NPU-aware optimization framework delivering the first end-to-end mapping of the LKF and EKF onto a commercial NPU, alongside a cross-platform characterization on shipping AI-PC silicon. KATANA applies three algebraic graph rewrites: subtract-to-add reformulation via a precomputed negative-projection matrix H_neg, static-shape tensor fusion, and block-diagonal batched parallelization, ensuring 100% of operations execute on the DPU matrix engine. On the Series 2, the optimized batched EKF reaches 223.35 FPS at 13.43 W active power, and the LKF reaches 408.73 FPS at 14.05 W, delivering up to a 97.9% reduction in dynamic energy versus the CPU implementation.

</details>


### [373] [PANDA: An LLM-Enhanced Performance-Driven Analog Design Framework Bridging Design Intent and Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15052)
*Haoyi Zhang,Weijian Fan,Xiaohan Gao,Bingyang Liu,Runsheng Wang,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PANDA框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）增强模拟电路设计自动化，实现了从设计意图到最终版图的跨阶段协同优化，将设计周期从数天或数周缩短至数小时，同时提升了设计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟电路设计在拓扑、尺寸调整和版图阶段高度依赖人工干预，且现有自动化方法往往孤立处理各阶段，缺乏跨阶段依赖关系的有效管理，导致设计效率低、周期长。

Method: 提出PANDA框架，通过引导式拓扑合成、子结构感知的尺寸调整和约束驱动的版图生成，主动管理跨阶段依赖；引入LLM增强，将自动化从以算法为中心的执行转向以设计意图为中心的协同设计。

Result: PANDA框架成功连接高层设计意图与最终版图，实现了跨阶段协同优化，将设计周转时间从数天或数周显著缩短至数小时，并有效提升了设计性能。

Conclusion: PANDA框架通过LLM增强的跨阶段协同设计，突破了传统模拟电路设计自动化的局限，为高效、高性能的模拟电路设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional design of analog circuits heavily relies on manual interventions across topology, sizing, and layout, with prior automation addressing stages in isolation. In this work, we propose PANDA, an LLM-enhanced framework that bridges high-level design intent to final layout by actively managing cross-stage dependencies through guided topology synthesis, substructure-aware sizing, and constraint-driven layout generation. This shifts automation from algorithm-centric execution to intent-centric co-design, reducing turnaround time from days or weeks to hours while improving design performance.

</details>


### [374] [A Spatio-Temporal Expert Prefetching Framework for Efficient MoE-based LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15453)
*Yingnan Zhao,Razvan Bunescu,Ahmed Louri,Avinash Karanth,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ST-MoE，一个时空专家预取框架，通过利用专家选择模式的可预测性，将专家加载与计算重叠，有效降低了MoE LLM推理延迟并提升能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLM通过稀疏激活专家提升了模型容量，但动态专家激活模式导致推理时专家按需加载产生显著延迟，成为性能和能效瓶颈。

Method: 分析发现专家请求在相邻MoE层和连续解码token间存在强相关性。据此提出ST-MoE框架，包含轻量级运行时预测机制和可重构硬件设计，在保持原始路由行为的前提下主动预取专家。

Result: ST-MoE成功预测未来专家激活，将专家加载与计算重叠，显著提升了MoE推理性能和能效，同时保持了模型推理精度。

Conclusion: 利用MoE模型中专家选择的时空相关性进行预测性预取是有效的，ST-MoE为MoE LLM的高效推理提供了可行的软硬件协同解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) based large language models (LLMs), such as Qwen and DeepSeek, have recently emerged as an effective approach to improving model capacity without proportionally increasing computational cost. By replacing the conventional feed-forward network in dense LLMs with a set of experts and activating only a subset of them for each input token, MoE models significantly increase the total number of parameters while keeping the per-token computation relatively manageable. However, this dynamic and irregular expert activation pattern also introduces substantial expert loading overhead during inference, since the required experts must be fetched on demand according to token-dependent routing results. As a result, expert loading latency becomes a major source of performance and energy inefficiency. To this end, we first perform a comprehensive analysis of expert selection behavior in various MoE-based LLMs and applications, including language understanding and code generation. Our analysis reveals that, within each application domain, expert requests exhibit strong correlation across both adjacent MoE layers and consecutive decoding tokens, making future expert activations predictable. Based on this insight, we propose ST-MoE, a spatio-temporal expert prefetching framework that proactively stages experts ahead of use to overlap expert loading with ongoing computation. ST-MoE combines a lightweight runtime prediction mechanism that preserves the original routing behavior with a reconfigurable hardware design that efficiently supports dynamic expert prefetching. The combined effect of the prediction mechanism with the supporting hardware significantly improves MoE inference performance and energy efficiency while preserving model inference accuracy.

</details>


### [375] [In-DRAM Signature Generation Using Simultaneous Multiple-Row Activation: An Experimental Study of Off-The-Shelf DRAM Chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15470)
*Umut Baser,Ismail Emir Yuksel,F. Nisa Bostanci,Konstantinos Sgouras,Ataberk Olgun,Emre Hakan Demirli,Zhiheng Yue,Harsh Songara,Oguz Ergin,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出SiMRA-PUF，利用商用DDR4 DRAM芯片的同时多行激活（SiMRA）机制生成物理不可克隆函数（PUF）响应。对112个现代DDR4 DRAM芯片的实验表明，该方法在可靠性和唯一性方面表现优异，且比现有DRAM-based PUF评估延迟降低5.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用现货（COTS）DRAM芯片广泛应用于各种计算系统，但其是否能够作为可靠的物理不可克隆函数（PUF）使用尚不明确。本研究旨在探索利用DRAM的同时多行激活（SiMRA）机制生成设备特定签名的可行性，以构建安全且高效的DRAM-based PUF。

Method: 研究团队在112个现代DDR4 DRAM芯片（来自10个模块）上进行了严格的实验表征。通过同时激活不同数量的DRAM行（2、4、8、16和32行），生成设备特定的签名作为PUF响应。研究分析了不同激活行数、DRAM芯片密度与芯片版本以及温度对SiMRA生成响应的影响，并从可靠性、唯一性和评估延迟三个维度评估SiMRA-PUF的性能。

Result: 实验得出8项关键发现，其中两项主要成果尤为突出：第一，SiMRA-PUF在2、4、8、16和32行激活下，平均组内Jaccard指数分别达到89.02%、89.81%、93.03%、94.06%和94.86%，平均组间Jaccard指数分别为3.98%、2.37%、3.44%、2.92%和3.24%，证明生成的签名在设备内可重复且设备间具有唯一性。第二，基于2行激活的SiMRA-PUF比最先进的DRAM-based PUF评估延迟降低了5.75%。

Conclusion: 本研究首次成功在商用DDR4 DRAM芯片上利用同时多行激活机制实现了物理不可克隆函数（PUF）。SiMRA-PUF在可靠性和唯一性方面均达到优异水平，且评估延迟低于现有方法。研究团队已开源实验基础设施和数据集（https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/SiMRA-PUF），为后续研究提供了重要基础。该成果证明了商用DRAM芯片作为安全硬件原语的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate that it is possible to generate unique, repeatable, and device-specific signatures suitable for use as Physical Unclonable Function (PUF) responses in commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) DRAM chips by leveraging simultaneous multiple-row activation (SiMRA). Based on a rigorous experimental characterization of 112 modern DDR4 DRAM chips (from 10 modules), we introduce SiMRA-PUF, the first DRAM-based PUF that uses SiMRA-generated signatures as PUF responses. We analyze SiMRA-PUF in terms of reliability, uniqueness, and evaluation latency for varying numbers of simultaneously activated DRAM rows (i.e., 2, 4, 8, 16, and 32), DRAM chip density & die revision, and evaluate how temperature affects the similarity of SiMRA-generated responses. Among our 8 key experimental observations, we highlight two major results. First, SiMRA-PUF provides average intra-Jaccard indices of 89.02%, 89.81%, 93.03%, 94.06%, and 94.86%, and average inter-Jaccard indices of 3.98%, 2.37%, 3.44%, 2.92%, and 3.24% for 2-, 4-, 8-, 16-, and 32-row activations, respectively, showing that SiMRA-generated signatures are both repeatable within a device and unique across devices. Second, 2-row activation-based SiMRA-PUF provides 5.75% lower evaluation latency than the state-of-the-art DRAM-based PUF. We open-source our infrastructure and datasets at https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/SiMRA-PUF.

</details>


### [376] [LLM4RTL: Tool-Assisted LLM for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15500)
*Jing Jin,Robert Chu,Ning Yan,Masood S. Mortazavi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM4RTL系统，通过JRCRC数据增强流水线和工具辅助架构，用小模型实现了与GPT-4O相当的RTL代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源Verilog/RTL代码生成缺乏高质量的训练样本用于微调或低秩适配，现有LLM在基于规则的推理和逻辑推理方面存在常见弱点，影响了RTL代码生成的质量。

Method: 提出JRCRC（judge-renew-check-renew-check）流水线，利用不同成本和能力的分层商业LLM来过滤和优化代码生成样本；开发工具辅助架构，通过预处理工具动态帮助LLM从表格数据格式推断逻辑关系。

Result: 在VerilogEval基准测试中取得了显著的整体性能提升，超越了多个最先进方法；使用小得多的LLM实现了与GPT-4O相当的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的LLM4RTL系统通过数据质量提升和工具辅助推理相结合，证明了小规模LLM在RTL代码生成任务上可以达到大模型性能，为硬件设计的LLM应用提供了成本效益更高的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have facilitated impressive progress in software engineering, code generation, tooling, and systems. Concurrently, a significant body of research has developed which explores a growing variety of methods and systems for applying LLMs to hardware and chip design (e.g., systems for RTL code generation based on functional description). However, when it comes to open Verilog/RTL code-generation, we need high-quality training samples to build specialized and more effective LLM systems through fine-tuning or low-rank adaptation. Here, we propose a ``judge-renew-check-renew-check'' (JRCRC) pipeline which updates a current public dataset using a hierarchy of state-of-the-art commercial LLM models differing in their costs and capabilities in RTL code generation. This approach achieves a cost-effective mechanism for filtering and refining code-generation samples into a higher-quality training dataset. Our experiments also identify some common weaknesses of LLMs in rule-based reasoning and logic, and consequently, in RTL code-generation. Having identified these weaknesses, we develop an architecture for incorporating pre-processing tools to dynamically assist the LLMs in inferring logical relationships from tabular data formats. With our tools-assisted architecture for RTL code generation, we achieve significant overall performance gains in the VerilogEval benchmark and outperform many state-of-the-art methods. Our LLM4RTL system achieves performance comparable to that of GPT-4O using a significantly much smaller LLM.

</details>


### [377] [Approaching Shannon Bound with Lossless LLM Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15789)
*Hongshi Tan,Yao Chen,Gustavo Alonso,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非对称数字系统(ANS)的LLM权重无损压缩框架，通过发现LLM权重的有效熵比存储位宽低2-10倍，实现了接近香农极限的无损压缩，在实际系统中将最大批处理大小提升至4.8倍，吞吐量提升至1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)参数规模已达到万亿级别，权重存储需求进入TB级别，与GPU内存容量形成严重不匹配。尽管无损压缩在其他领域广泛应用，但在LLM系统中仍未充分利用。研究旨在探索LLM权重的内在信息熵特性，开发适合GPU推理的高效无损压缩方法。

Method: 通过对1.5B到405B参数规模的模型进行全面熵分析，发现LLM权重的有效熵比存储位宽低2-10倍。基于此发现，提出了一种基于非对称数字系统(ANS)的分块级、即时无损解压框架，将解码过程与GPU推理中的GEMM分块模式对齐，并集成到支持多GPU的SGLang服务框架中。

Result: 在多种LLM数值格式下，该方法实现了距离香农极限仅0.01-0.1比特位率的压缩性能，几乎消除了所有统计冗余。在Qwen-14B模型上，最大批处理大小从47提升至75，吞吐量提升至1.2倍；在Mixtral-176B模型上，可行批处理大小从20提升至95(4.8倍)，吞吐量提升至1.6倍。相比最新无损压缩方法NeuZip和DFloat11，吞吐量进一步提升至11倍。

Conclusion: LLM权重存在显著的信息冗余，其有效熵远低于存储位宽暗示的水平。提出的ANS分块级无损压缩框架能够高效利用这种低熵特性，在保持无损的前提下实现接近理论极限的压缩效果，显著提升LLM推理系统的内存利用效率和吞吐性能，为解决LLM内存瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now scale to trillions of parameters, driving weight storage into the terabyte regime and creating an acute mismatch with GPU memory capacity. Although lossless compression is widely effective in other domains, it remains underutilized in LLM systems. Through a comprehensive entropy study across models from 1.5B to 405B parameters and numeric formats ranging from bf16 to int4 and AWQ/SQ8, we find that LLM weights contain far less intrinsic randomness than their stored bitwidth implies, their effective entropy is 2-10x lower, indicating that up to a 10x footprint reduction is theoretically achievable without altering any weight values. Leveraging this insight, we introduce a tile-level, on-the-fly lossless decompression framework based on Asymmetric Numeral Systems that aligns decoding with the GEMM tiling pattern of GPU inference. Our design achieves bit-rates within 0.01-0.1 bits of the Shannon limit across a wide range of LLM numerical formats, demonstrating that nearly all statistical redundancy is eliminated. Integrated into the SGLang serving framework with multi-GPU support, our approach increases the maximum batch size of Qwen-14B from 47 to 75, improving throughput by up to 1.2x. On Mixtral-176B, the feasible batch size increases from 20 to 95 (4.8x), yielding up to 1.6x throughput improvement. Compared to state-of-the-art lossless compression approaches NeuZip and DFloat11, our design further improves throughput by up to 11x.

</details>


### [378] [EPIC: A System Framework for Efficient Egocentric Perception on Embodied AR Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15859)
*Tianhua Xia,Haiyu Wang,Jiajing Zheng,Su Chen,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EPIC系统，这是一个面向智能AR眼镜的高效自我中心感知算法-硬件协同优化框架，通过利用视线、姿态和惯性信号推断用户意图并仅保留感知输入中最具信息量的部分，平均减少27.5倍内存占用和24.3倍能耗，同时保持智能辅助准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能AR眼镜正向支持基础模型辅助的智能系统演进，但“感知优先”的设计造成了严重瓶颈。持续捕获、处理和存储高分辨率自我中心视频等感知数据会带来巨大的功耗和内存开销，在资源受限的AR眼镜上难以持续维持。

Method: 提出EPIC（Efficient Egocentric Perception）算法-硬件协同优化框架。该框架利用视线、姿态和惯性信号来推断用户意图，并仅保留高分辨率感知输入中最具信息量的部分，从而大幅降低感知开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，与完整视频基线方案相比，EPIC平均减少27.5倍的内存占用和24.3倍的能耗，同时在自我中心视频理解任务上保持了智能辅助的准确性。

Conclusion: EPIC成功解决了智能AR眼镜上感知优先设计带来的资源瓶颈问题，实现了高效的自我中心感知，为具身智能在智能眼镜上的关键应用场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern smart AR glasses are evolving into intelligent systems that support foundation model-based assistance through continuous perception of the user and surrounding environment. However, this perception-first design creates major bottlenecks. Continuously capturing, processing, and storing rich perceptual streams, especially high-resolution egocentric video, imposes substantial power and memory overhead, which is difficult to sustain on resource-constrained AR glasses. In this work, we propose EPIC, an efficient egocentric perception system for embodied intelligence on smart AR glasses. EPIC is an algorithm-hardware co-optimization framework that leverages gaze, pose, and inertial signals to infer user intent and retain only the most informative parts of high-resolution perceptual input, greatly reducing perception overhead. Our results show that EPIC reduces memory footprint by $27.5\times$ and energy consumption by $24.3\times$ on average compared with full video baseline solution, while preserving intelligent assistance accuracy on egocentric video understanding tasks, a key application scenario for embodied intelligence on smart glasses.

</details>


### [379] [Google's Training Supercomputers from TPU v2 to Ironwood: Architectural Stability, Scale, Resilience, Power Efficiency, and Sustainability Across Five Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15870)
*Norman P. Jouppi,Sridhar Lakshmanamurthy,Cliff Young,David Patterson*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文总结了谷歌TPU从第二代到Ironwood共五代的发展历程，展示了其如何演变为可扩展、弹性和高效能的AI训练超级计算机，并识别了成功训练加速器的六大特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度神经网络工作负载（如Transformer）的快速演变，需要总结TPU架构如何在八年内适应这些变化，并为未来的AI训练加速器设计提供指导。

Method: 通过回顾和分析五代TPU（从TPU v2到Ironwood）的架构演进，重点关注HBM容量和带宽、节点性能、超级计算机性能、光电路交换机、内置自测试和硬件重放等关键技术特征。

Result: 在八年间实现了单节点HBM容量和带宽10倍增长、峰值节点性能100倍增长、超级计算机性能3600倍增长；通过提高每瓦性能和降低每次浮点运算的碳排放显著减少了环境影响；TPU的稳定架构成功适应了快速变化的深度学习工作负载。

Conclusion: 识别出可能定义本十年成功训练加速器的六大特征，证明了TPU的可扩展、弹性、高效和可持续的超级计算机设计理念的有效性。

Abstract: This paper (to appear in the July/August 2026 issue of IEEE Micro magazine) summarizes five generations of Google s TPUs, from TPU v2 to Ironwood, highlighting their evolution as scalable, resilient, power-efficient, sustainable supercomputers for AI training. It details the TPU s stable architecture, which has surprisingly easily accommodated the rapidly changing deep neural network workloads, such as the rise of Transformers. Key advancements over eight years include 10x increase in HBM capacity and bandwidth per node, a 100x increase in peak node performance, and a 3600x increase in supercomputer performance. The paper also discusses the role of optical circuit switches, built-in self test, and hardware replay in enhancing resilience and how TPU's environmental impact is reduced with substantial improvements in performance per Watt and in carbon emissions per floating point operation. It concludes by identifying six features that may well characterize successful training accelerators of this decade.

</details>


### [380] [AIA: A Customized Multi-core RISC-V SoC for Discrete Sampling Workloads in 16 nm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16143)
*Shirui Zhao,Nimish Shah,Wannes Meert,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款基于RISC-V的16nm多核SoC芯片（AIA），通过定制指令、近邻数据共享和专用编译链加速边缘设备上的MCMC采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率模型中的MCMC采样方法对于安全关键应用至关重要，但存在计算成本高、难以并行化的问题，在传统CPU/GPU平台上执行效率低下。

Method: 设计了一个包含RISC-V主处理器和16个定制RISC-V核心的2D网格架构的近似推理加速器（AIA）。每个核心集成了：（1）用于Knuth-Yao采样和非线性函数插值的定制指令与数据通路；（2）直接访问相邻核心寄存器文件的机制以减少数据移动开销；（3）开发了专用编译链以实现高效的空间映射和调度。

Result: 成功流片了基于Intel 16nm工艺的多核RISC-V SoC，该架构通过硬件定制和编译优化有效提升了MCMC算法的并行执行效率。

Conclusion: AIA为边缘设备提供了高效的近似推理加速能力，通过架构与编译的协同设计克服了传统平台在MCMC采样上的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Probabilistic models (PMs) are essential in advancing machine learning capabilities, particularly in safety-critical applications involving reasoning and decision-making. Among the methods employed for inference in these models, sampling-based Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques are widely used. However, MCMC methods come with significant computational costs and are inherently challenging to parallelize, resulting in inefficient execution on conventional CPU/GPU platforms.
  To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AIA, a multi-core RISC-V System-on-Chip (SoC) design fabricated using Intel's 16 nm process technology.
