Our Approximate Inference Accelerator (AIA) is specifically designed to empower edge devices with robust decision-making and reasoning abilities. The AIA architecture incorporates a RISC-V host processor to manage chip-to-chip data communication and a 2D mesh of 16 custom versatile RISC-V cores optimized for high-efficiency approximate inference.
  Each core features (i) custom instructions and datapath blocks for non-normalized Knuth-Yao (KY) sampling, as well as for the interpolation of non-linear functions (e.g., logarithmic, exponential), and (ii) direct data access to the register file of each neighboring core, to reduce the data movement costs of frequent data exchanges between nearby cores. To further capitalize on the parallelism potential in MCMC algorithms, we developed a specialized compile chain that enables efficient spatial mapping and scheduling across the cores.

</details>


### [381] [When Proofs Meet Hardware: Comparing NTT and SumCheck in Zero-Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16146)
*Jianqiao Mo,Alhad Daftardar,Barath GaneshKumar,Kaiyue Guo,Hong Wang,Benedikt Bunz,Siddharth Garg,Brandon Reagen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一架构框架下对NTT和SumCheck两种证明原语进行了硬件系统级直接比较，发现两者各有优劣，SumCheck在高次多项式表现更好，NTT在低次多项式且内存受限时可能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零知识证明领域，关于SumCheck协议与数论变换(NTT)的效率比较存在长期争论：密码学界认为SumCheck渐进效率更高(O(N) vs O(N log N))，而硬件加速器设计者认为NTT更硬件友好(具有局部性和数据重用)，SumCheck则受限于顺序依赖轮次。然而，这两种基于不同原语的证明系统在硬件系统层面的权衡仍未被充分理解。

Method: 在ZeroCheck协议( zkSNARKs的通用构建块)背景下，构建了统一的架构框架，实现了NTT和SumCheck两种原语的优化系统。在相同的片上SRAM和片外带宽预算约束下进行公平比较，系统性地评估两种原语在不同工作负载下的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明不存在普适的最优选择：对于高次多项式，SumCheck性能优于NTT；对于低次多项式，性能取决于内存可用性——在给定SRAM预算下，NTT通过利用数据重用，在中等工作负载下可能提供更好的性能。

Conclusion: 研究桥接了密码协议设计与硬件架构，为理解基于NTT和SumCheck的零知识证明系统的证明成本提供了实用指导，证明了选择证明原语时需要综合考虑多项式次数和硬件资源约束。

Abstract: In the ZKP community, it has long been discussed that the SumCheck protocol is asymptotically more efficient than the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), requiring only $O(N)$ arithmetic versus $O(N \log N)$. At the same time, hardware accelerator designers propose that NTT is more hardware-friendly, benefiting from locality and data reuse, while SumCheck suffers from sequential, dependent rounds. Despite these competing intuitions, the hardware-system-level trade-offs between NTT- and SumCheck-based proving primitives remain insufficiently understood.
  Beyond individual accelerator design, this work presents, to our knowledge, the first hardware-system-level direct comparison of NTT- and SumCheck-based proving primitives under a unified architectural framework. We study them in the context of the ZeroCheck protocol, a common building block in zkSNARKs. We implement optimized systems for both primitives. Both are evaluated under the same level on-chip SRAM and off-chip bandwidth budgets. Our results show that there is no universal winner. Generally, SumCheck outperforms NTT for high-degree polynomials. For low-degree polynomials, performance depends on memory availability: under given SRAM budgets, NTT might deliver better performance for medium-sized workloads by exploiting data reuse.
  These findings, bridging cryptographic protocol design and hardware architecture, offer practical guidance for understanding the proving cost of NTT- and SumCheck-based zero-knowledge proof systems.

</details>


### [382] [AIA: A 16nm Multicore SoC for Approximate Inference Acceleration Exploiting Non-normalized Knuth-Yao Sampling and Inter-Core Register Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16148)
*Shirui Zhao,Nimish Shah,Wannes Meert,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\name{}的概率图模型近似推理加速器，通过定制化RISC-V核心阵列和非归一化Knuth-Yao采样器，实现了比现有MRF加速器快2倍且能效提升1.45倍的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率图模型（PGMs）为机器学习提供了推理和决策能力，其常用的采样式马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）近似推理方法计算密集且难以并行化，在现代CPU/GPU平台上执行效率低下。

Method: 设计包含RISC-V主机和16个定制化RISC-V核心的2D网格架构；每个核心配备创新的非归一化Knuth-Yao采样器和插值单元；通过寄存器文件实现核间直接数据访问；开发定制编译器链进行空间映射和调度优化。

Result: 在0.9V电压下达到1277 MSample/s的采样率，在0.7V电压下实现20 GSamples/s/W的能效；相比现有最先进的马尔可夫随机场（MRF）加速器，速度提升高达2倍，能效提升1.45倍。

Conclusion: 该设计成功实现了高效的并行MCMC推理加速，并通过在贝叶斯网络基准上的映射验证了设计的灵活性，为边缘设备上的概率推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic graphical models (PMs) are popular to empower machine learning with the ability of reasoning and decision-making. To perform approximate inference in PMs, sampling-based Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms are commonly employed. Unfortunately, MCMC is compute-intensive and hard to run in parallel, resulting in inefficient execution on modern CPU/GPU platforms. This paper proposes \name{}, an Approximate Inference Accelerator designed to empower decision-making and reasoning at the edge. \name{} consists of a RISC-V host, and a 2D mesh of 16 customized RISC-V cores optimized to efficiently support PM inference, each featuring (i) a novel non-normalized Knuth-Yao sampler and interpolation unit; and (ii) core-to-core direct data access via the register file, which provides solutions for compute-intensive operations. To fully exploit the parallel potential of Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms, a customized compiler chain has been developed for effective spatial mapping and scheduling on the chip. \name{} can generate 1277 MSample/s at 0.9V and 20 GSamples/s/W at 0.7V which is up to 2$\times$ faster and 1.45x more energy efficient compared to the previous state-of-the-art Markov Random Field (MRF) accelerator. We further map Bayesian Networks benchmark onto \name{} to show the flexibility of our design.

</details>


### [383] [Embedded Arena: Iterative Optimization via Hardware Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16190)
*Zhihan Zhang,Alexander Le Metzger,Jiuyang Lyu,Chun-Cheng Chang,Jiayi Shao,Yujia Liu,Emmanuel Azuh Mensah,Edward Wang,Kurtis Heimerl,Gregory D. Abowd,Shwetak Patel,Natasha Jaques,Vikram Iyer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个硬件在环的LLM智能体框架，用于自动优化嵌入式设备上的AI模型部署。该方法通过实时硬件反馈迭代优化模型和固件，在7次迭代内超越了人类专家的性能，实现了250-400倍的模型压缩，并在真实场景（麋鹿检测和儿童语音转录）中成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式设备（如野生动物监测站和临床可穿戴设备）由于延迟、通信或隐私限制需要本地AI推理。针对异构微控制器的模型优化需要同时满足内存、功耗和温度等物理约束并保持精度，这种多维优化目前依赖专家手动完成，成本高昂且效率低下。研究动机是探索LLM智能体是否能利用真实硬件反馈自主完成这一复杂的多轮优化流程。

Method: 提出硬件在环（hardware-in-the-loop）智能体竞技场框架：智能体迭代优化模型和固件，包括编译、烧录和在真实硬件上测量性能指标，形成闭环优化。前沿模型（如Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro）在没有硬件反馈时完全失败（部署成功率0%），而硬件在环方法在3次迭代内实现首次成功部署，7次迭代内超越人类专家。

Result: 实现了250倍视觉模型压缩（精度损失<3.3%）和400倍音频模型压缩（特征错误率损失<6%），使得商业MCU能够通过太阳能采集实现无电池运行。在实际系统中验证：麋鹿检测相机陷阱达到96.7%准确率；儿童发育研究的语音转录可穿戴设备达到8.44% FER。

Conclusion: 硬件在环的智能体协同优化方法首次成功实现了LLM自主导航嵌入式AI部署的复杂流程，并在实际应用中证明了其有效性。该方法不仅自动化了专家级的多维优化工作，还在性能上超越了人类专家，为资源受限设备上的AI部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Embedded devices from wildlife monitoring stations to clinical wearables require local AI inference due to latency, communication, or privacy constraints. Optimizing models for heterogeneous microcontrollers (MCUs) requires simultaneously satisfying hard physical constraints on memory, power, and temperature while preserving accuracy, a multidimensional optimization that is today performed manually by experts. We ask whether an LLM agent can autonomously navigate this complex, multi-turn pipeline guided by real hardware feedback, and introduce a hardware-in-the-loop agent arena in which the agent iteratively refines both model and firmware -- compiling, flashing, and measuring on real hardware -- to enable closed-loop optimization. Frontier models, including Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro, fail entirely without hardware feedback (0% deployment success), whereas our hardware-in-the-loop formulation achieves the first successful deployment within three iterations and can surpass human expert results within seven. This agentic co-optimization achieves 250x compression for vision models with <3.3% accuracy loss and 400x for audio with <6% Feature Error Rate loss, enabling battery-free operation on a commercial MCU via solar harvesting. We demonstrate practical impact in two real-world systems: an elk-detection camera trap (96.7% accuracy) and a phonetic-transcription wearable (8.44% FER) for child development research.

</details>


### [384] [NeuronFabric: A Software Reference Architecture for On-Chip Transformer Training with Local Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16440)
*Evgeny Ukladchikov*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuronFabric，一种面向Transformer训练的软件参考架构，通过BF16W混合精度方案在FPGA/ASIC上实现本地Adam优化器更新，在334K参数模型上验证了数值正确性和内存节省效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公开加速器架构通常将训练计算与优化器状态更新分离，或依赖外部内存和主机协调。为了在FPGA和ASIC等硬件平台上实现完整的本地训练能力，需要一种能够在片上存储器限制下完成前向传播、反向传播和Adam优化的统一架构设计。

Method: 提出NeuronFabric软件参考架构，使用C#实现完整的前向传播、反向传播和Adam优化器。引入BF16W混合精度策略：权重以BF16格式存储以节省内存，而Adam优化器的一阶和二阶矩保持FP32精度以确保数值稳定性。在334K参数的自回归Transformer模型（d=88, H=4, f=264, L=4, vocab=256）上进行验证，使用Shakespeare语料库进行字符级语言建模训练。

Result: BF16W配置在80K样本训练后评估损失为1.5426，与FP32 GPU参考实现的1.5224相近，并生成连贯的字符级文本。内存方面，FP32模型需要约4.0MB（匹配Xilinx ZCU102的BRAM容量），BF16W变体仅需约3.34MB，为激活值存储留出空间。量化了BF16W的内存节省效果，并描述了词汇表预算约束问题。

Conclusion: NeuronFabric作为公开架构披露和软件参考实现，成功验证了BF16W混合精度方案在片上训练中的可行性和数值正确性。该工作为后续FPGA和ASIC硬件实现奠定了基础，下一步将进行FPGA训练的实测验证。本文不包括FPGA实际测量数据。

Abstract: Publicly documented accelerator architectures generally separate training computation from optimizer-state updates or rely on external memory and host orchestration. This paper presents NeuronFabric, a software reference architecture intended for future FPGA and ASIC implementations of transformer training with local Adam updates. A complete C# prototype implements forward pass, backpropagation, and Adam optimization without external machine-learning frameworks. The goal is to validate numerical correctness and memory requirements before hardware implementation. The evaluated model is a 334K-parameter autoregressive transformer (d=88, H=4, f=264, L=4, vocab=256) trained on the Shakespeare corpus. The BF16W configuration achieves evaluation loss 1.5426 after 80K samples, compared with 1.5224 for an FP32 GPU reference, while producing coherent character-level text. The paper introduces BF16W, which stores weights in BF16 while retaining Adam optimizer moments in FP32. This reduces memory requirements for on-chip training. A 334K-parameter FP32 model with Adam moments requires approximately 4.0 MB, matching the BRAM capacity of a Xilinx ZCU102 device. The BF16W variant requires approximately 3.34 MB, leaving memory available for activation storage. We describe the vocabulary-budget constraint observed during earlier experiments, quantify BF16W memory savings, and outline FPGA training as the next stage of development. No FPGA measurements are included in this paper. This publication serves as a public architectural disclosure and software reference implementation for future FPGA and ASIC exploration of the NeuronFabric architecture.

</details>


### [385] [Towards Delta Aware Training: Efficient DNN Weight Storage for Resource-Constrained FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16516)
*David Peter Federl,Lukas Einhaus,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增量压缩的新方法，通过使用较低位宽的权重增量压缩技术在资源受限的FPGA上部署神经网络，固定参考增量方案相比连续增量表现更优，实现了约50%的权重压缩和7.992M MACs/s的最大吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的FPGA上部署深度神经网络面临内存和计算能力有限的挑战，需要开发有效的压缩技术来减少内存占用并提升部署效率。

Method: 提出一种新型压缩技术，通过以较低位宽保存权重增量来减少内存占用，并训练网络适应压缩后的增量。研究两种增量方案：连续增量和固定参考值增量。开发专用硬件加速器，采用增量压缩的乘累加(MAC)算子，在AMD Spartan-7 S15 FPGA上实现。

Result: 在FashionMNIST数据集和多层感知机上的实验表明：固定参考增量压缩方案优于连续增量方案，使用4位权重增量实现约78.6%的验证精度，相比8位定点网络精度损失约8.3%；硬件加速器实现近50%的权重压缩，最大吞吐量达7.992M MACs/s。

Conclusion: 所提出的增量压缩技术能够有效减少资源受限FPGA上神经网络的内存占用，固定参考增量方案是更优选择，该方法在精度损失可接受的范围内显著提升了部署效率。

Abstract: The deployment of embedded deep neural networks on resource-constrained field programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) is challenging due to limited memory and computational capacities. We introduce a new compression technique to reduce the memory footprint by saving weights in deltas with lower bitwidth and training the network to cope with compressed deltas. Two delta schemes are investigated: consecutive deltas and deltas with a fixed-reference value. We evaluate both on the FashionMNIST data set with a multi-layer-perceptron. The results indicate that fixed-reference delta compression outperforms the consecutive variant, achieving a validation accuracy of approximately 78.6 %, with 4 bit weight deltas, representing an accuracy loss of roughly 8.3 % compared to a fixed-point network with 8 bit. Our specialized hardware accelerator with a delta-compressed multiply-and-accumulate operator compresses weights by nearly 50 % and achieves a maximum throughput of 7.992M MACs/s on an AMD Spartan-7 S15 FPGA.

</details>


### [386] [TreeGRNG: Binary Tree Gaussian Random Number Generator for Efficient Probabilistic AI Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16599)
*Jonas Crols,Guilherme Paim,Shirui Zhao,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出TreeGRNG，一种用于贝叶斯神经网络的低成本低功耗高斯随机数生成器，能耗降低3.7倍，吞吐率提升5.8倍，并支持灵活的概率分布调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络(BNNs)通过监控决策不确定性增强神经网络可信度，但在极边缘设备推理时，每个神经元都需要集成高斯随机数生成器(GRNG)。现有GRNG算法严重依赖大量算术运算和庞大的查找表，给超低功耗硬件实现带来巨大挑战。

Method: 提出创新的二叉树随机数生成器(TreeGRNG)，使用超低成本的常数比较器替代算术单元；进一步利用高斯分布特性进行硬件感知优化。

Result: 优化后的TreeGRNG在分布精度上超越现有技术，实现每样本能耗降低3.7倍，单位面积吞吐率提升5.8倍。相比现有技术，TreeGRNG具有更高灵活性，可轻松调整采样概率分布形状，超越传统GRNG能力。

Conclusion: TreeGRNG为边缘设备上的贝叶斯神经网络推理提供了高效、低功耗的解决方案，并为未来概率AI设计开辟了新方向。设计已开源。

Abstract: Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) offer opportunities for greatly enhancing the trustworthiness of conventional neural networks by monitoring the uncertainties in decision-making. A significant drawback for BNN inference at the extreme edge, however, is the imperative need to incorporate Gaussian Random Number Generators (GRNG) within each neuron. State-of-the-art GRNG algorithms heavily depend on multiple arithmetic operations and the use of extensive look-up tables, posing significant implementation challenges for ultra-low power hardware implementations. To overcome this, this paper presents an innovative binary tree random number generator (TreeGRNG) allowing the use of ultra-low-cost constant comparators instead of arithmetic units. We further enhance the TreeGRNG proposal with a set of hardware-aware optimizations exploiting the Gaussian properties. The optimized TreeGRNG surpasses the State-of-the-Art (SoTA) in terms of distribution accuracy while achieving a 3.7$\times$ reduction in energy per sample and boosting the throughput per unit area by 5.8$\times$. Moreover, our TreeGRNG proposal possesses a distinct advantage over the current SoTA in terms of flexibility, as it easily enables designers to adjust the shape of the sampled probability distribution, extending beyond the capabilities of traditional GRNGs, opening the horizon towards future probabilistic AI designs. The TreeGRNG design is available open-source in the link

</details>


### [387] [DataGuard: Guaranteeing Private Training in Systolic-array Based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16809)
*Pawan Kumar Sanjaya,Christina Giannoula,Nikhil Shreekumar,Ian Colbert,Alec Dewulf,Mehdi Saeedi,Ihab Amer,Gabor Sines,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出DataGuard，一种基于硬件的机制，确保联邦学习中只有满足差分隐私要求的数据才能离开设备，防止第三方应用违反隐私预算，硬件开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习（FL）部署通常假设第三方FL应用可以正确实现差分隐私（DP）算法，但实际上第三方应用拥有对敏感数据的完全访问权限，可能会违反数据所有者定义的隐私预算，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出DataGuard，一种基于硬件的机制，在硬件层面保证只有满足DP要求的计算结果才能离开设备，无需信任第三方应用即可确保隐私预算不被超出。

Result: 在四种加速器上针对多种机器学习模型进行仿真评估，DataGuard的面积开销小于0.01%，性能降低小于0.3%。

Conclusion: DataGuard能够以极小的硬件开销和性能损失，有效解决联邦学习中第三方应用不可信导致的隐私预算违规问题，为隐私保护提供了可靠的硬件保障。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) and federated learning (FL) have emerged as important privacy-preserving approaches when using sensitive data to train machine learning (ML) models. FL ensures that raw sensitive data does not leave the users' devices by training the model locally on the device. DP ensures that the model does not leak any information about an individual by clipping and adding noise to the gradients before updating the model. It provides formalism to constrain privacy loss during training to a privacy budget determined a priori by the owner of sensitive data. However, real-life deployments of FL algorithms typically assume that a third-party FL application can be trusted to correctly implement DP algorithms. Thus, the third-party application is given full access to sensitive data. In this work, we propose DataGuard, a hardware-based mechanism that guarantees that the only data that can leave the device is the result of computation that meets DP requirements. DataGuard can thus be used to ensure that the privacy budget defined by the data owner is not exceeded during FL training without the need to trust a third-party application. We evaluate DataGuard in simulations of four accelerators for various ML models and demonstrate only small area overheads of less than 0.01\% and performance slowdowns of less than 0.3\%.

</details>


### [388] [Architecture Carbon Tool v3: Enabling Sustainability-aware Silicon System Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16889)
*Vincent T. Lee,Bilge Acun,Zachary Lewis,Carole-Jean Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ACT3（Architecture Carbon Tool v3），这是一个可扩展和可定制的建模平台，用于支持可持续性感知的硅系统架构设计和优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着半导体器件制造和运行的碳成本日益受到关注，可持续性逐渐成为系统架构设计的新指标。与功耗和性能建模工具类似，实现可持续性感知的硅系统设计和优化需要新一代的电子设计自动化和架构建模工具。

Method: 本文对Architecture Carbon Tool v3 (ACT3)进行了更新。ACT3相比之前的版本，提供了更丰富的建模能力、增强的辅助数据和分析遥测功能，以及一阶设计空间探索能力。论文概述了这些扩展功能，并通过多个案例研究展示了ACT3的基本实用性。

Result: ACT3成功提供了可扩展和可定制的建模平台，支持研究和高级开发工作。相比前版本，ACT3在建模能力、分析遥测和设计空间探索方面都有显著提升，为可持续性感知的架构设计铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 论文最后识别了研究和开发的机会，期望研究社区能够继续改进硅系统的可持续性建模和设计方法论。ACT3为可持续性感知的架构设计空间探索提供了基础平台。

Abstract: As the carbon cost of manufacturing and operating semiconductor devices has come into sharper focus, sustainability has gradually emerged as a new system architecture design metric. Like power and performance modeling tools, enabling sustainability-aware silicon systems design and optimizations will require a new generation of electronic design automation and architectural modeling tools. Towards this end, we present an update to the Architecture Carbon Tool v3 (ACT3) which aims to provide an extensible and customizable modeling platform for research and advanced development to pave the path towards sustainability-aware architectural design space exploration. Compared to previous versions of ACT, ACT3 provides significantly richer modeling capabilities, enhanced collateral and analysis telemetry, and first order design space exploration capabilities. This technical brief provides an overview of these expanded capabilities and illustrates ACT3's basic utility across several case studies. Finally, we identify opportunities for research and development where we expect the research community can contribute towards continuing to improve sustainability modeling and design methodology for silicon systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [uringscope: Portable, Low-Overhead Observability for io_uring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15137)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了uringscope，一个基于CO-RE eBPF的单二进制、语言无关的io_uring可观测性工具，解决了现有工具无法观测io_uring请求流的问题，开销低且能检测正确性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: io_uring将I/O提交和完成移入共享内存环，虽然快速但不可见。strace只能看到环设置，内核跟踪点不是稳定ABI，导致现有工具只能在狭窄的内核范围内工作。

Method: 1）构建精确的请求生命周期模型并从内核事件重建请求流；2）使用BTF探测程序变体、CO-RE字段风格和位置无关读取技术可移植地附加到不稳定的跟踪点表面；3）实现轻量级正确性模式检测提交边界危害。

Result: 在设备绑定的NVMe工作负载上，uringscope的聚合模式开销仅为吞吐量的0.7%至9.9%，比所有全保真度替代方案都更便宜。工具能够检测提交边界危害并提供内置诊断功能。

Conclusion: uringscope成功解决了io_uring可观测性问题，在低开销和高保真度之间取得了良好平衡，并为运维人员提供了从测量到命名病理的诊断能力。

Abstract: io_uring moves I/O submission and completion into shared-memory rings. This makes it fast, and it also makes it invisible. strace sees only the ring setup, and the kernel tracepoints that expose the request flow are not stable ABI, so the few tools built on them work only on narrow kernel ranges. We present uringscope, a single-binary, language-agnostic observability tool for io_uring built on CO-RE (Compile Once, Run Everywhere) eBPF. uringscope makes four contributions. The first is a precise model of the request lifecycle and a method to reconstruct per-request flows from kernel events. The second is a technique for attaching portably to an unstable tracepoint surface, using BTF-probed program variants, CO-RE field flavors, and position-independent reads. The third is an evaluation of the tradeoff between overhead and fidelity: on device-bound NVMe workloads uringscope's aggregate mode costs 0.7 to 9.9% of throughput, which is cheaper than every full-fidelity alternative we measured. The fourth is a lightweight correctness mode that reuses the same reconstruction to detect submission-boundary hazards, together with a built-in doctor that turns the measurements into named pathologies with evidence, for operators who are debugging a tail-latency incident rather than browsing histograms.

</details>
